_Dasunan ALLAH Mai Rahama Mai Jin Ƙai_


                         🅿️ *1*


Daƙarfin gaske ya bugi ƙaton table ɗin dake gabansa wanda yake shaqe da tarin takardu, sannan ya ɗago rinannun idanunshi waɗanda suka cika da zallan ɓacin rai marar misaltuwa ya zubasu akan p.a wanda yake gurfane agabansa cike da ɓarin jiki dana zuci.

"Me kake son kacemin _P.A_ kana nufin aikin da mukai ya lalace"!! yafaɗi maganar fuskarsa cike da tarin bakin ciki marar misaltuwa.

Wanda aka kira da p.a ne ya qara rissinar da kansa a qasa tare da kallon uban gidan nasu cikin rawar jiki.

"Wallahi tallahi nima ɗazu habu wakili yake sanar dani cewar aikin da mukai a Ɗawisu l.g ya lalace sannan kuma ansamu wasu ɓata garin samari sun haura cikin makarantar ƴammatah ta ɗawisun har sunyiwa guda uku fyaɗe" yakai maganar yana sadda kansa a qasa batare daya kalli uban gidan nashiba.

Rintse idanunshi yay harna wajan mintuna biyar kafin ya warasu yana bin _p.a_ dasu cike da tarin ɓacin rai ya ɗaga masa hannun alamun yabar masa office.

Cike da sassarfa _p.a_ ya miqe ya nufi hanyar barin office ɗin.

Tare da qara rissinawa yace. ALLAH ya taimaki _Megirma *MAS*!_ yakuma huci zuciyar ka mutumin qwarai fari mai farar zuciya sukari bakai farin banza ba yaro mai jini ajika wanda duniya take yayi ALLAH yacigaba da dafa maka adukkan lamuranka yasa maqiyanka suzama fadawanka!! 

Yana kaiwa nan yaja masa qofar office ɗin ya fita sanin halin _mai girma *MAS* mutum marar son yawan kirari ko fadanci.


Da sauri ya koma da baya ya faɗa saman kujerar office ɗin nasa ya ɗaga kansa sama yana kallon rufin p.o.p office ɗin cike da bakin ciki yana ayyana yadda zewa ma'aikatan daya baiwa wannan contract ɗin rashin mutunci danshi mutum ne tsayayye sam baya son,   son zuciya shiyasa baya zama da duk wani mutum marar amana.

Cike da qwarin gwiwa ya ɗauki wayarsa wadda take yashe a saman table ɗin yafara neman layi.....

Ringing biyu aka ɗaga daga can ɓangaren tare da cewa."ALLAH ya taimaki _Maigirma *MAS*........" 

Bai tsaya jiran cigaba da maganar na cikin wayan tashiba yafara magana cike da ɓacin rai "Haba sardauna yaya zakuyi mana haka mu baku aiki amman kuyi mana almun dahana kasan dai halina kuma kasan waye ni natsani rashin gaskiya arayuwata wannan dalilin yasa waccan tafiyar ban baku aiki ko ɗaya ba" yakai maganar yana komawa ya lafe a saman kujerar daya ke kai.

Cike da bakin ciki wanda aka kira da sardauna ya mayar da abinda ya taso masa zuciyarsa tare da qaqalo murmushin qarya sannan ya soma bawa  _Maigirma MAS_ hakuri tare da cewa shima bai san haka yaransa sukai aikinba amman gobe monday zeje yagani, 


Batare daya tanka masa ba ya katse kiran cikin rashin jin dadi.

Kiran sakataren sa yay a waya ya sanar masa.  ya cewa _p.a_ ya kai masa jaka mota ya tashi.                  Cikin muntuna biyar ya haɗa tarkacan sa yabar office ɗin ransa duk ajagule.

Dagudu gourd's  ɗinshi suka rufa masa baya tare da saurin bude masa marufin wata shegiyar mota ya faɗa cikin gajiya yana gyara links ɗin hannun rigarsa.

Acikin mintunan da basu wuce goma ba motarsa tabi hanyar babban titin cikin garin ƊAWISU.


Gabaki ɗaya kowa yaga yadda manyan motocin bayan motar daya ke ciki, suke gudu yasan babu lafiya dan haka jama,a sukaita matsawa suna bawa motocin hanya waɗanda aqalla zasuyi guda goma.


Bai sanar da ƴan jarida ko wani cewar ze fita ba dagashi sesu habu p.a da sauran gourds ɗinshi haka suka nufi cikin garin ɗawisu.

Wanda zuwa lokaci kaɗan zancan zuwan bazatan da *ƊAN MAJALISA  MAI GIRMA SANATA MUHAMMAD ANWARR SAMEER  (  MAS )* yay cikin garin ɗawisu ya gama cika gari wanda yasa ma'aikatan sardauna constuructions suka shiga ɗimuwa 

Abakin tafkeken qaton gate ɗin makarantar *ƊAWISU BORDING SCHOOL* ya dakatar da drivern sa da kansa ya buɗe marufin motar tare da ziro da santala santalan fararen qafafunshi waje.

Cikin azama duk wasu masu bashi tsaro sukayo caa! akansa tare da rufa masa baya wanda ya taka da qafafunshi har cikin makarantar.

Da gudu - gudu principal ɗin makarantar tayo wajansa wadda zancan zuwansa ya kaɗa mata ciki zubewa tai agabansa tana kwasar gaisuwa sannn ta miqe tai masa jagora har wajan aikin daya bayar na kwangilar sola.

Zagaye yay sosai acikin makarantar sannan ya koma can wajan office ɗin principal inda aka miko masa farar kujera  ya zauna akanta.

Da rikitattun idanunsa waɗanda suke kama dana maijin bacci ya dubi. p.c yana jefa mata tambayoyinsa masu kama dana ɗan jarida akan yadda akai har garada suka hauro cikin makarantar batare da malamai ko ita sun luraba wanda alhakin kula da students ɗin akansu yake.

Gumine ya tsurgo mata sannan ta soma basa haquri akan wannan lamari tare da shaida masa qaddara ce babu yadda zasuyi.

Taune qasan laɓansa yay yana jin tsigar jikinsa tana miqewa babu abinda yafi tsana a duniya kamar zina babban abinda yake ɗaga masa hankali yaji anyiwa ƴammata ko yara fyaɗe wallahi jiyake inda zega waɗannan garadan da babu abinda ze hana ya kashe tsinannu.

Ɗago idonsa yay ya kalli p.c sannan ya nemi data turo masa dukkan wasu masu ɗankwali na cikin makarantar da kuma mamomin ɗakuna dan yana son sanin asalin yadda lamarin ya faru.

Cike da sauri p.c ta baxa sakonsa sannan ta koma ta tsugunna agabansa tana jimanta abun aranta.
                    

                          Hostel!

Dagudu wata baqar budurwa mai ɗan tsaho ta, taho wajan wata farar yarinya mai ɗan tsaho kaɗan marar jiki wadda take ɗaure da ɗaurin kirji ajikinta tana wanke hause ware masu kalar blue.

Cikin haki ta karaso gabanta.
Ɗaka mata duka tai tare da cewa.
"F.K kizo kece shugabar makaranta wallahi *ƊAN MAJALISARMU* yazo akan zancan haurowar da yaya gardi yayi kuma ya nemi da akira duk wasu masu ɗan kwalin cikin makarantar nan yanxu haka p.c tana can tana nemanki ashe kekina nan kina wanki.

Wadda aka kira da f.k ita ta ɗago da sauri tana kallon wadda take mata maganar cike da sanyin hali "kai lebour ni wallahi harkin bani tsoro wallahi canake wani gardin ne ai wannan murya haka"

Dariya lebour tai sannan ta kalli F.K malama nasanki da sanyin jiki sefa kinyi da gaske sabida kiran gaggawane dan ALLAH kada ki tsaya wannan yangar taki.

Tsaki F.K tai sannan ta tsame hannunta ta wanke ta nufi hanyar ɗakinsu.

Kwanarsu taje ta dauki qaton hijabi har qasa sannan ta dubi walida wadda suke kwana ɗaya da ita cikin nutsuwa "walida zanje kiran p.c dan ALLAH ki tsaman idomie ta kada ta jiqe takai maganar a nutse" wadda ke zaune daga gefe tana karatu ta ɗago ido ta kalli f.k.

"Waidaman feenah baki ci abincin nan ba tun ɗazu nifa narasa kanki tunda muka dawo daga wannan hutun naga kin sauya sanyin jikinki ya qaru kodai su baffa ne suka kuma...." kallon walidan tai cikin basarwa "nidai idan naxo mayi magana dan ALLAH ki tsaman" takai maganar tana futa daga cikin kwanar tasu.

Hanyar area class tai cikin sanyin jiki wai ahakama tana sauri.

Adai dai bakin office ɗin p.c taji sautin bugun zuciyarta yana qaruwa fiye da nada wanda qaddara tasa ta gamu dashi batare data shirya ba.

Addu,a tacigaba dayi hartaja qafafunta zuwa bakin office ɗin.

Seda ta kutsa kanta sabida yawan jama,a ga security's kota ina ga malamansu haka ta samu ta qarasa wajan da aka ce musu su taru ta tsugunna tana saukar da kanta a qasa.

Yawan bugun zuciyarta da rawar jikinta dasuka tsananta sune suka saka babu shiri ta haɗe jikinta waje ɗaya tana tsukewa kamar maijin shigar sanyi.

Kafin taji wani daddaɗan mayen kamshin turare ya cika mata hancinta wanda take tunanin ina ta sanshi?

Ɗago kanta sama tai wanda adai dai wannan lokacin shima yake neman waje akan kujerar da aka ware masa  dan ya zauna.

Cike da tsoro ta ware idanunta duka waje meya kawosa nan?".......



*ƊAN MAJALISA*

                         🅿️ *2*


Lumshe manyan idanunta tayi tare da sake warasu akansa tana jin wata iriyar nutsuwa ta shigeta kafin taji sautin bugun zuciyarta yafi nada qaruwa sosai ta shagala da kallonsa wanda takejin kamar ta dawwama a haka tana kallonsa batare data gajiyaba.
Cikin zuciyarta kuma matsanancin mamakine fall tayaya yazo cikin makarantarsu? ita data gansa a ƊAWISU GENERAL HOSPITAL  har takai masa files ɗin kaka, anya kuwa shine kodai aljanine tayaya yazo a ƊAN MAJALISAR SU? Kuma ta gansa a matsayin likita wannan lamari ya cuɗa mata kanta ya hanata sukuni tabbas tana da tarin tambayoyi aranta amman babu mai bata amsarsu dan haka ta kuma jan jikinta ta tsuke tana ayyana koma dai waye shi ita taji ta gani!!
Ta cikin wutsiyar idanunta take kallon yadda yake motsa ɗan madai daicin bakinsa yanawa ɗaya daga cikin masu tsaronsa magana wadda ba lallai na nesa dashi yaji miyake cewaba.
Mintuna kusan goma suna tsugunne kafin taga p.c ta miqe tsaye tanawa baba vise magana wanda kafin hankalinta ya dawo kanta taji yana kiran sunanta.
"Nafeesa Kabeer" taji baba vise  ya kira sunanta yana kuma umartarta data matso gaba ana son ganinta.
Cikin rawar jiki f.k ta miqe daman can ita bamai yawan magana bace ba sannan kuma batada sakewa acikin mutane tun fill azal balle yanzu dataga taron maza damasu ɗammara wannan dalili yasa tafara karanto addu,oi cikin bakinta harta samu ta qarasa gaban p.c ta sake tsugunnawa.

P.c ce ta umarceta akan ta matsa gabansa wanda faɗin hakan da p.c tai yakusan sakata ta ɗimauce bada ban qarfin addu,aba da tabbas seta faɗi a wajan.
Cike da sanyin jiki dana zuciya ta qarasa gabansa wanda yake zaune akan kujera ya ɗora qafa ɗaya kan ɗaya gefensa masu tsaronsa ne duk sun riqe bindigogi se wasu mutane daban dake bayansa suma a tsaye.

Agabansa ta tsugunna tare da mayar da kanta a qasa tana zana qasar gabanta da yatsun hannunta tana jin wani sanyi yana ratsata ga wata iriyar faɗuwar gaba data qaru acikin kirjinta.
Wata qwalla ta matso mai zafi tare da saka gefen hijabinta ta share a fakaice tana kallonsa yadda yay kamar bai san da ɗan adam a wajanba seda taga wani mutum ya risina yay masa raɗa a kunnensa sannan ya ɗago ya jefeta da wani shu'umin kallo mai kama dana raini da qasqanci.

"Ke"! yafaɗa muryarsa a daqile kamar wani basarake cike da saurin jiki p.c ta daka mata tsawa "ke nafisa ba magana ake maki ba" cikin sanyinta ta ɗago idanunta waɗanda suka soma yin jaa! sabida damuwar data taso mata ta diresu akansa tana kallonsa batare data tanka masa ba kuma batare data ɗauke idanunta daga kan nasa ba.

Cike da mamaki _mai girma MAS_ Yake kallon yarinyar gaban nasa wanda take yaji wani ɓacin rai ya ziyarcesa jiyana mata magana ta mayar dashi kamar wani wawa tsaki yaja kaɗan tare da lumshe idanunsa waɗanda yake jifanta da kallonsa na rainin hankali dasu kafin ya furzar da iska mai zafin gaske ganin tana son ta ɓata masa lokaci.
"Ke wace irin wawiya ce ana maki magana kinyiwa mutane banza hala bebiyace"! ya faɗi maganar muryarsa adake!!
F.k wadda ta shagala a kallonsa lumshe idanunta tai cikin muryarta mai dadin sauraro mai kama data mai jin sanyi ta fara magana "ALLAH ya baka haquri banji kaba" tana faɗin maganar taja bakinta tai gum jikinta duk ya dauki rawa sakama kon wani wawan kallon wulaqanci daya mako mata.

Ɗan tsaki kaɗan yaja cike da halin ko in kula yafara yi mata bayani akan yadda zasuna kula da ɗakunansu suna tsare kansu batare da sun jira malamai sunzo ba a yayin da garada suka hauro masu.
Cikin tattausan lafazi yake mata bayanin wanda idan ka kallesu seka rantse wata magana suke ta soyayya.
Abinda yafi bata mamaki yadda taga duk magana ɗaya seya lumshe idanunsa sannan yake faɗinta dalla dalla kuma yake mata maganar yadda zata fahimta sedai kuma ta lura kamar yanada ɗan saurin magana kaɗan.
Har yay yagama bayanin nasa bata fuskanci komaiba illa wata karin damuwar daya kuma jefata aciki tare da shiga ruɗun rashin makama akansa haka ta miqe jiki na rawa tai masa godiya tabar wajan.
Kafin ta qarasa wajan da aka tsaidasu taji maganganu sun karade wajan akan yay masu kyautar maqudan kuɗi.
Sallamarsu akai akan su tafi se an nemusu, inda shikuma ya miqe aka rufa masa baya ya nufi wajan ganin aikin daya zuba ai acikin makarantar.

                          HOSTEL

Tana tafe tana haɗe hanya kamar wata zararriya haka ta qarasa cikin ɗakinsu akwanar su ta yada zango tare da tube hijabin jikinta, da gudu walida tazo ta rungumeta "wayyo shugabar makaranta naji abin alkairi _mai girma MAS_ yabamu kyautar kuɗi masu yawa sannan yasa a dauke waɗanda akaiwa fyaɗe a fitar dasu waje aga lafiyansu mudai ALLAH yasa a rarrabamana kuɗinnan kinsan halin malaman nan kowa kansa yake gyarawa"
Ganin kamar f.k bata hayyacinta yasa walida taɓata "F.k lafiyanki kuwa?" ɗago kanta tai ta kalli walida da idanunta waɗanda sukai jawur! sannan ta mayar da kanta saman gado ta kifa tana kuka kamar babu gobe...
"Subuhanallahi Nafeesa kabeer lafiyanki?" walida ta kira complt sunanta, tare da kai hannunta ta ɗagota ta jawota jikinta tana rarrashinta "Dan ALLAH feena badan niba kigayamin abinda yake damunki bana son wannan zurfin cikin naki na fuskanci tunda muka dawo wannan hutun kin rage walwala ko muqamin da aka bakine bakyaso ko agidane su baffa sukai maki wani abun?" ko idanun kakane ciwonsu suka motsa?" walida ta jera mata wannan tambayoyin tana jiran amsarsu.
Seda tai kukanta mai isarta sannnn ta ɗago fuskarta tai jawur idanunta ya qara girma.

"Walida na shiga uku!! walida zuciyata batamin adalciba takaini inda zan wahala takaini inda za,a wulaqantani wallahi walida sonshi nake da gaske!!!
"Miyasa aduk sanda zan gansa seya gayamin baqar magana miyasa duk sanda zan gansa senaji kamar na mutu walida sonsa ze kasheni idan na rasu kicewa kaka akan sonshine" takai maganar cikin gushewar tunani tana kuka kamar zata shiɗe.
Cikin damuwa walida ta ɗagota "feena waye wannan ki gayamin a ina yake waye mai sa,a dayay nasarar samun kamilar mace kamarki?" takai maganar tana tallafo feena ajikinta.
Jada baya feeanah tayi tana jan numfashinta.
"Walida baya sona kuma nasan baze taɓa sona ba naganshi tun kafin nasan shiɗin ƊAN MAJALISSARMU ne naso shi tunkafin nasan shiɗinne walida na haɗu dashi ranar talata da safe acikin ƊAWISU HOSPITAL naje kai files ɗin idanun kaka sabida munji sanawar za,a tallafawa marasa lafiyar idanu mutum ɗari a wannan rana shine da mukaje muka samu shiga aka bani file ɗin kaka nakaiwa babban likitan wajan daxe ɗauki hoton idanun dasauransu naje office ɗin wanda naga anrubuta *DR. ANWARR S.* ajiki da manyan harufa da  sauri na tura qofar office ɗin danna shigar da, qafafuna sedai kash nai rashin sa,a domin hango wani haɗaɗɗan kamilallan mutum danai zaune da glass a idanunsa yana rubutu da sauri da sauri da tulin takaddu a gabansa,ban tsaya kare masa kallo ba sedai gani guda nai masa yasami nasarar sace zuciyata, dirin tashin masifarsa acikin dodon kunnena shiya saka na yada file ɗin na gudu daga wajan ."shinke mahaukaciya ce daxaki shigomin office babu permision!! naji saukar amon daddaɗar muryarsa da sauri akaina yana surfan masifa wallahi walida tunda na baro asibitin haryau ban kuma ganinshiba se yau kuma kullum senayi mafarkinsa" takai maganar tana cigaba da kukanta.

Sannan ta cigaba.....
"Seyau kawai na gansa wai ashe shine ɗan majalissarmu mutumin danaji kowa yana yabonsa da fadar alkairinsa amman nikam senaga kamar ya tsaneni yauma seda ya kirani da wawiya, walida yaya zanyi nasanshi na shaqu dashi harma ya soni sannan ina son nasan tayaya ya zama ɗan majalisarmu bayan ni a likita na sanshi.........!!



*ƊAN MAJALISA*

_Dasunan ALLAH Mai Rahama Mai Jin Ƙai_

                         🅿️ *3*


Kuka! Take kan jiki kan qarfi hartana jin numfashinta yana kokarin ɗaukewa, cike da tausayawa walida ta saka hannunta ta ɗago ta daga jikinta sannan ta soma magana.
"Dan ALLAH feenah kiyi shuru hakan nan mana akan namiji kike asarar hawayenki, wanda baima san kinayi ba, haba feenah kefa macece mai aji mai kamun kai mai ilimi da nutsuwa wadda take ganiyar quruciyarta akan me zaki zauna kina kuka akan sa" walida takai maganar tana goge mata hawayen dake sauka akan fuskarta.
"Walida dole nai kuka! idan ban kuka mai kikeso nayi?" shin wai kuka! a so qaryane?" tayaya bazanyi kuka akansa ba alhalin kukana shize saka naji sanyi acikin zuciyata" takai maganar tana janye fuskarta daga hannun walida.
Gabaki ɗaya ta riga tagama karyawa walida zuciyarta babu abinda walida take se hawaye ayayin dataga aminiyar tata tana kuka akan son wanda baima san da zamanta ba sam.
"Naji feenah na yarda so ba qarya bane ba, kuma so yana saka mutum yay fiye da kuka ma, amman ina horonki da kiyi haquri sannan kiyi addu,a matukar ALLAH yay zaku zauna waje guda dashi babu mai hanawa, dan ALLAH kibar kukan nan haka kisaka dangana aranki muyi karatunmu sosai batare da matsala ta afkuba".
Walida takai maganar cike da nuna kulawa ga aminiyar tata.
"Naji walida zanyi shuru na daina kuka amman tunani kam yanzu ma na soma wallahi nikadai nasan yadda zuciyata take masifar sonshi" takai maganar tana share hawayenta.
Cike da murna walida tace,"na yarda wallahi harnaji dadi dakuwa zaki ɗagamin hankali insha ALLAH zesoki so kamar yadda kike masa kuma da yardar ALLAH idan ya nazeer yazo kawomin provision zan ɗan tambayesa akan koze sanshi tunda ba ɓoyayye bane agarinnan" haka walida taitawa feena tattausan kalamai masu kwantar da rai harta samu ta miqe taje ya haɗa indomien data barwa walida ta tsame mata.
Koda ta zauna cin indomien kawai hango kyakykyawar fuskar gayen take wadda take qara dulmiyata acikin tafkin sonshi da begensa.
Tana gama cin indomie ɗin ta fita taje ta ɗebo wankin data gama ɗazu ta dawo kwanarsu tana ninke hause ware ɗin cike da sanyin jiki.



                       ************

Cike da qarfin gwiwa yake taka qafafunsa waɗanda suke zube a cikin wani irin azababben takalmi mai rufaffan sama,  cikin nagarta da qarfin da ALLAH madaukakin sarki yabasa yake duba ayyukan daya bawa kamfanin SARDAUNA CONSTRT. wanda zasu zubawa makarantar kwana ta ƴammata dake cikin qaramar hukumar ɗawisu wadda take cikin jerin qananan hukumomin dayake wakilta na'urorin hasken sola da kuma gyaran banɗakunan da zasuyi waɗanda aka sauya musu shadda da sauran, su..............................Rai bace ya juyo yana kallon habu p.a cike da ladabi da biyyayya p.a ya rissinar da kansa a qasa yana faman baza idanunsa kamar wanda za,a cewa katt! ya arce.
Fuskarsa hartai jawur abinka da farin mutum cikin kakkausar murya ya qara duban p.a a karo na biyu "wannan wani irin sakarci ne p.a haka na baku nace ku basu?" yafaɗa cikin wata iriyar murya kamar ba tasaba.
"I say! haka na baku tayaya nace ku gyara masu toilet's sakamakon kokensu danaji akan idan ruwa ya sauka macijai suna fito masu sannan kuzo ku wani liqe gefe da gefe batare dakunyi abinda nace ba ai wannan shashanci ne, to.wallahi tallahi na baka daga nan zuwa gobe ku kammala komai idan ba haka ba!! yaja qwafa tare da baza babbar rigar saman kayan jikinsa yabar wajan a fusace....!
Gabaki ɗaya su p.a sun sha jinin jikinsu sabida sunsan waye ANWARR mutum mai tsatstsauran ra'ayi ɗan aqida na bala'i sam baya wasa da duk wani abu mai mahimmanci a rayuwarsa musamman tallafawa alummah da kawo gyara acikin qauyuka da biranan dake qarqashin kujerarsa, daya baka aiki kai ha'inci gwara ka saci kuɗinsa ka gudu sarai sunsan shi zumane shi ga zaqi ga harbi.
Gumi ke faman tsatstsafowa habu p.a cikin zulumi ya kalli, na kusa dashi "Anas kana kallon yadda mai gida ya fusata fa wallahi idan bamuyi wasa ba wannan tafiyar ze zubar damu ya kama wasu" cikin zulumi anas ya dubi p.a "kaine ai sam haryau baka kula da halin MAI GIRMA MAS! ba shi baya son maci amana wallahi nidai babu ruwana bazaka jamin asarar na cefane ba idan ma zakaje kabawa sardauna ragowar kudin aiki acigaba da mai kyau ka basa idan kuma ba hakaba babu ruwana" anas yakai maganar tare da saurin barin wajan ya nufi wajan motar uban gidan nasu a guje ganin yadda jama,ar gari ke faman shigowa aguje sakamakon labarin daya je masu akan bangon sukari ya shigo unguwar tasu.

Atsaye yake fuskarsa babu yabo kuma babu fallasa dama kuma can Tun fil azal shi mutum ne ba mai son hayaniya ba, kuma shi bai fiya son far'aba sedai zakaga idan anyi abun dariya yay murmushi koya kauda kai wannan shine tsarinsa amman dariya bata daga cikin halinsa. cike da son talakawansa yake ɗaga masu kyakykyawan farin hannunsa mai kama dana larabawa kafin ya ɗan tabo babban yaronsa wanda duk wata tafiya yake dashi yace yaje mota ya fito da kuɗi ya rarrabawa jama,ar dake wajan sannan yaja kafarsa ya nufi mota wadda cikin mintuna ɗaya security's ɗinshi suka bude masa site ɗin baya ya faɗa.
Cikin sauri wata ƴar dattijuwa ta matso gabansa har masu tsaronsa suna hanata qarasawa wajan nasa da sauri ya ɗaga masu hannu ta cikin glass ɗin motar sannan ya zuge tare da zuba mata idanu yana kallonta.
Saukar muryarta akan dodon kunnensa yasa ya qara waro manyan idanunsa yana kallonta.
"ALLAH Yay maka albarka MUHAMMADU! ALLAH yasa kagama da duniya lafiya yadda kake taimako kaima ALLAH ya taimaka maka ubangiji ya tsare gabanka da bayanka ALLAH yabawa matarka ciki ka sami ɗan kanka.......cike da mamaki ya ɗago idanunsa yana kallonta.
Jin wata maganar daga bakin dattijuwar ya kuma sakashi a ɗimuya.

Cikin ɓacin rai ya doki gaban site din da oska yake kokarin zama cike da masifa yafara magana "Oska waya baka damar korarta shin kasan mitake cemin koko kasan itan wacece?"!! ya bugawa oska wata firgitacciyar tsawa!!

Kafin wani lokaci tuni yafara jin wani irin mugun sanyi wanda seda ya umarci oska akan ya kashe A/C'n dake cikin motar.
Tattausan hannunshi yasa ya qara jan babbar rigar dake jikinsa ya qara curewa guri ɗaya yana mai lumshe narkakkun idanuwansa, yana jin kansa kamar ba shiba.
"YA SALAM" Yafurta aƙasan laɓɓansa yana jin inama oska bai masa wannan katobararba yaya za,ai ya koreta yana hango gaskiyar wasu abubuwan agame da tsohuwar.
Addu,a yake furtawa acikin ransa yana neman tsarin ubangijinsa akan kada yasa yazama silar shigar wani ɗan adam a damuwa tun fil azal shiba mutum bane mai son yaga wani adamuwa ba sabida haka wannan kalaman suka haɗu suka ɗaga masa hankali.
Har sanda motarsa ta karya kwana ta shiga wata irin rantsatstsiyar unguwa wadda zaman faɗin yanayin kyawun unguwarma ɓata bakine.
Abakin wani katafaren gate driver ya tsaya tare da danna horn.
Cikin mintunan da bazasu gaza goma ba wasu qattin sojoji suka taho a guje tare da wangale gate ɗin.
Cike da nutsuwa motar take danna hancinta cikin compound ɗin har sanda ta shiga cikin parking lot tare da gyara tsaiwa sannan ragowar motocin suka mara mata baya.
Cikin sauri oska da sauran excort ɗinshi suka fito aguje tare da bude marufin site ɗin dayake zaune.
Seda yakai kusan mintuna 20 acikin motar batare daya fito da koda qafarsa ba.
Sannan kuma ya fito da qafarsa tare da wayoyin dake hannunshi yana mai kara ɗaya akan kunnensa.
Ɗaya site ɗin oska ya zagaya tare da ɗauko masa jakarsa sannan ya rufa masa baya shi ɗaya sauran excort's ɗin kuma suka dakata anan.
Hanyar wani haɗaɗɗan part naga oska ya nufa wanda yafi ko ina kyau  acikin gidan.
Cikin azama ya saka hannunsa ya buɗewa uban gidan nasa kofar glass ɗin wajan sannan ya ɗan dakata kaɗan tare da rissunawa seda ya bari MAS ya shiga ciki sannan ya mara masa baya.
Abakin wani corridow suka rabu oska ya aje jakar anan sannan ya rissuna yay masa sallama ya nufi juyawa.
"Oska" yaji amon muryar uban gidan nasa ya kira sunansa.
"Na am maigida! oska yay saurin juyowa yana rissunar da kansa a qasa.
"Idan kunci abinci kun huta ina son ka ɗauki mota kaje wajan hayin buzuwa ka ganomin aikin ruwan nan yaya yake tafiya amman pls kada kaje da kowa wannan yazama secret ɗina" yafaɗi maganar yana ɗan dafe kansa.
Cike da tsantsar ladabi oska ya dubi uban gidan nasa sannan yace."angama mai gida bama zan tsayaba yanzunnan zan wuce" yakai maganar yana qara jin son uban gidan nasa aransa sabida adalcinsa.
"No osk kaje ka huta tukunna mubi komai a sannu" yana kaiwa nan ya cigaba da tafiya batare daya tsaya jin me oska ze faɗa ba.
Cikin corridown yake taka qafafunsa akan wani azababban carpet mai bala'in kyau wata hanya naga ya nufa wadda zata sadaka da step ɗin bene.
Cike da sanyin jiki yake taka step ɗin har ya karasa shiga ciki.
Wani katon falo wanda ya sami damar lamushe manyan kujeru har saiti huɗu shiya shiga a tsakiyar falon ya tsaya tare da karya kwana ya nufi wata kofa wadda ta rabu guda biyu.
Ɗayar ya bude ya shiga bakinsa ɗauke da sallama.
Cike da murna wata ƴar kyakykyawar mace wadda bazata gaza shekaru 26 ba tayo gabansa tana murna.
"Sannu da zuwa dear" tafaɗa cike da farin ciki bayan ta rissuna kasa tana cire masa takalman dake qafarsa.
Seda ta cire ta ajesu a gefe ɗaya sannan ta kamo hannunsa kamar wani yaro qarami ta nufi bakin gado dashi.
Zaunar dashi tayi a bakin katon royal bed ɗin dake ɗakin.
Sannan ta tsugunna a gabansa idanunta cike da qwallah.
"Aminci da rahamar ubangiji su tabbata ga abokin rayuwata mijina abin alfaharina shin meya taɓa ka ne harka shigo gida da yanayin daban sanka dashiba, kafin naji komai daga wajanka ina son kadinga haquri sannnn ka kwatanta adalci acikin mulkin da ALLAH ya baka domin abin tambayane gareka aranar gobe qiyama"
Takai maganar tana kallonsa kamar zata masa kuka!
Cikin jarumta da tsantsar saka abu acikin zuci ya ɗago jajayen idanunsa ya kalli matar tasa dashi tare dajin tsananin qaunarta acikin ransa sabida ayanzu samun kamarta se antona mace mai ladabi da biyayya uwa uba ga tarbiya sannan tana mugun son mijin nata.
"Hidaya" ya kira sunanta.
"Karki damu ba wani abu bane kawai gajiyar office ce" yakai maganar yana jawota jikinsa.

"Ina sonki matata samun kamarki ayanzu da kamar wuya, ina rokon ubangiji daya azurtamu da zuri'a ɗayyiba"
Yakai maganar yana haɗe fuskokinsu waje guda.
Cikin damuwa ta fara magana.
"Dear nima a kullum burina kenan inga ɗanmu ko ƴarmu kasan wannan damuwarfa harta soma bayyana asaman fuskar mama!"
"Shekararmu kusan bakwai da aure dear ko ɓatan wata ban taɓa ba wallahi abin akwai damuwa" takai maganar tana matso da hawayenta.
Rungumota ya kumayi jikinsa sosai sannan yace.
"Karki damu mucigaba da addu,a ALLAH ze kawo mafita tunda dagani harke bamuda wata matsala face lokacine bai ba" yakai maganar yana mayarda kuncin zuciyarsa dan karta gane.

                         🅿️ *5*


Ya zira slipers tare da ɗaukar wayoyinshi dake yashe a saman bed ya nufi kofar fita hidaya na bayanshi, cike da ladabi da biyayya ta tako masa har zuwa falo, kana ta juya ciki bayan tayi masa fatan dawowa lafiya  kamar wanda ze tafi unguwa.
Cike da tsantsar ladabi da biyayya ma'aikatan dake farfajiyar gidan suke zubewa a qasa suna masa barka da fitowa cikin kulawa da sanin daraja da qimar ɗan adam yake ɗaga masu hannu tare da ɗan sakin killer smile kaɗan ta gefen kumatunshi.
Ya shiga part ɗinsu mama kofar falo ya danna tare da kutsa kansa aciki bakinshi dauke da sallama.
Larai mai aikinsu tana ganin shi tayi saurin takowa daga hanyar daining area inda take shiryawa su mama abinci ta tsugunna a gabanshi tare da gaisar dashi cikin ladabi da biyayya.
Cike da kulawa ya amsa tare da kama hanyar wani corridow dayay hannun hagunshi.
Wata kofa ya kama ya murda handel ɗin bayan ya shiga cikinta ya turota  sannan ya cire takalmin kafafunshi ya taka ciki bakinsa dauke da sallama.
Wani dadda ɗan kamshi mai sanyaya zuciya ya shaka acikin hancinsa ga wani sanyi mai dadi daya hade da wannan kamshi se faman tashi yake acikin falon.
Lumshe idanunshi yayi sannan ya budesu tarr a bakin kofar wani bedroom wanda kofar ta kasance kamar ta karfe amman mai glass nan ma seda yay sallama sannan ya murda ya shiga, tare da wata sallamar still a bakinshi.
Kamshin dayafi nada dadi ya shaka kana ya karasa cikin ɗakin.
Babbar mace far tass mai kimanin shekaru hamsin aduniya na hango zaune akan sallaya hannunta dauke da casbaha tana lazumi.
Kallo guda nai mata na gane itace mahaifiyar wannan bawan ALLAH.
Cike da ladabi da tsantsar biyayya ya karasa gabanta tare da zama ya tankwashe kafafunshi akan lallausan carpet din dake shimfide a tsakar dakin ya sunkuyar da kansa a qasa yana danne danne akan faffadar wayar dake hannunsa wadda aka lamushe maqudan kudade wajan siyanta.
Yakai kusan daqiqa arba'in a zaune a wajan kafin kuma ya dago kai ya kuma duban matar wadda ta daga hannunta sama tana addu,a shima tsintar kansa yay da ɗaga nasa hannun sama suka shafa addu,ar tare sannan ya ɗan saki murmushi kaɗan kana yace.
"Bar ka da rana Maamah" ya karasa maganar a rarrabe saboda yasan idan ma ya cika zaqe mata korarsa zatai 
Cikin tsananin kunya wadda mafi akasarin iyaye waɗanda suka shafi da suke nunawa ƴaƴansu na farko Maamah ta dubesa sau ɗaya takk sannan ta amsa batare data bari yaji ba.
Ganin ya sakata agaba yana kallo.tare da saka hannunshi yay tagumi seta miqe ta nufi gaban ma'adanar kayanta ta bude sannan ta ɗan juyo kaɗan.
"Bawan ALLAH katashi kaje wajan babarka ni kasakani agaba koda wani abunne?" takai maganar tana mai danne soyayyar ɗan nata acikin ranta wadda takejinta har zuciyarta amman tsananin kara da kawaici irin na fulanin usul yasa maamah kasa sakewa dashi.
Ɗago kansa yay kamar ze saka kuka shikam abin nan na maamah mamaki yake basa, shifa har tausayin kansa yakeji wallahi bada ban anty da hidaya ba daya zama maraya sabida yadda mama take masa kamar bata sanshiba.
Shiya rasa wannan irin hali na maamah amman yana kallo zata zauna dasu imran kaninsa suna hira harda dariya amman daga yazo waje zata kama sunkuyar da kai shikuma gashi ALLAH yayosa mai masifar maqon uwa.
Danne abin da ke ransa yay sannan yace.
"Dama zuwa nai in maki barka da rana sannan ina neman addu,arki akan aikin dazamu saka gobe acikin unguwar bakin rafi" yakai maganar kamar ze kuka.
Ɗan jimm tayi kamar bazatai maganaba secan kuma tace.
"ALLAH ya tsare" daga haka taja bakinta tai gumm, bata kuma magana ba amman kasan ranta cewa take ai addu,a kam kullum cikin yi maka nake ɗan albarka.
Miqewa yay tsaye tare da zube hannayanshi acikin aljihun wondon shaddar dake jikinsa, sannan ya nufi hanyar barin ɗakin duk jikinsa yay sanyi.
Har ya bude kofar ze fita yaji amon muryarta ya shiga cikin kunnenshi.
"Kacewa matarka anjima su asiya zasu shigo mata" tana gama fadin haka ta juya tacigaba da aikin gyaran cavet dinta.
Ɗan murmushi ya sauke kaɗan shi halin mamah yana kuma kara basa mamaki, wai ace hatta hidaya bata sakewa da ita, dan tsabar kunya bata yarda ko waje ɗaya su zauna da ita, haka ya karasa wajan anty wadda ya tarar da ita zaune akan kujera a falonta tana tsefewa zuby kitso cike da tsananin far'a anty ta karbesa sannan ta ture zuby daga jikinta, ta miqe taje ta wanko hannunta sanin baya son yaga gashi awaje.
"Ɗan gidana yaya rana?" anty ta tambayesa cike da kulawa.
Ga mamakina senaga ya sake kamar bashiba yana zabgawa anty labarai hatta ga fitar dayay yau seda ya gaya mata.
Haka suka dauki lokaci suna hira kafin ai kiran sallar la'asar ya miqe yay mata sallama ya  fita.

 AUTAR MANYA🌹


                         🅿️ *6*


Asalin sunanshi muhammad Anwarr Sameer wanda mafi yawancin abokansa suke masa inkiya da MAS ma'anar hakan shine,muhammad anwar samir, shiɗin ɗane na farko ga marigayi alhaji sameer mai carbi wanda a yanzu yake a matakin ɗan majalissar taraiya mai wakiltar kananun hukumomi guda goma sha huɗu acikin babban birnin jaharsu ta  ƊAWISU.

Muhammad anwar yayi karatunshi baki ɗaya a ƙasar indiya inda yayi dukkan karatunshi akan harkar aikin likitanci na ɓangaren mata, ya shafe shekaru masu yawa yana harkar karatun nasa inda kafin ya dawo mahaifinshi ya gina masa ƙaton asibiti anan cikin babban birnin ɗawisu,bayan ya dawo kasar sa ta haihuwa ya soma aiki cikin asibitin nasa batare daya bata lokaci ko sanyaba.

Cikin shekarar daya dawo kuma ALLAH yay ma mahaifinshi alhaji samir mai carbi rasuwa wanda rasuwarsa bakaramin daga hankalin dukkan zuriyar mai carbi taiba.

Alhaji samir ya rasu yabar manyan kaddarori da dama wanda acikinsu harda kamfanunnuka da sauran manya manya kaddara waɗanda sukafi gaban kwatance.

Ya rasu yabar matansa guda biyu.
Hajiya Khadija itace matarsa ta farko wadda ta kasance bafulatanar usul, tana da yara guda biyar Muhammad anwar shine na farko se mai binsa imran bayan sa akwai saifu se shahid da shahida.
  
Sannan kuma akwai hajiya hafsat wadda suke cema anty wadda mahaifin nasu ya aurota daga baya. agidan tana da yara guda biyu zubaida da zuhura.

Gabaki ɗaya zuriyar alhaji samir suna da mugun haɗin kai sam bazaka gane ɗan wancan ɗakin da ɗan wancan ba sabida yadda suke a haɗe kaf.

Hajiya khadija ( maamah) mace mai tsananin kunya da halin girma sam bata yarda wata alaqa ta hadata da babban ɗanta sabida kara ta ɗan fari.
 
Shiyasa komai na anwar yake a wajan anty wadda ta zama kamar uwar ɗakinsa sosai suke mugun good time dashi dan wani abun ma idan sunyi sedai antyn ta bawa maamah labari tai ta dariya.

Imran tare akai masu aure da anwar bayan rasuwar mahaifinsu inda yanzu haka imran yana da yara uku amman matar anwar hidaya wadda ta kasance ƴar uwar maamah ta jini ɗiyar kaninta har yau ko ɓatan wata bata taɓayi ba.


Wannan abu yana mugun ɗaga masu hankali hatta kai ga sun fara biyar likitocin wasu kasashen domin gane taka mai man matsalar gashi yanzu har kaninsa na uku yay aure shima yana da ɗan kansa amman sukam har yau shuru.


Bayan aurensa da shekara guda ya faɗa harkar siyasa wadda ba karamin kai ruwa rana yay da manyan cikin familyn suba kafin subarshi ya fara wadda tsananin tausayin halin da talakawa suke ciki yasa ya faɗa harkar bisa tilashin kansa da ra'ayin kansa, wanda ya kasance mutum tsayayye jajuryacce mai mugun tsayawa da ra'ayin kansa.


Seda yaje kasar waje yay wani course akan harkar polities na tsahon shekara ɗaya sannan ya diro kasar sa ta haihuwa wanda babu wata sanya yafara gudanar da harkarsa ta siyasa cike da ƙwarin gwiwa da kuma goyon bayan alummah.

Sosai ya bawa ƴan adawa mamaki waɗanda tashi guda ya wujijjigasu ya basu kashi tun a primary election suka san karon babu dadi.


Tashin farko seda yakai ga ya baiwa abokin karawarsa tazarar kuri'a fiye da miliyan goma wadda ta kuma ƙara masa hope akan harkar tasa takuma ƙara cusawa maƙiyanshin mugun tsanarsa shidin wanda ya zama zakaran gwajin dafi turmin tsakar gida shalugudan mahassada gamji sha sara sha sassaka murucin kan dutse bai fitoba seda ya shirya.


Akaf cikin garin na ɗawusi da sauran ƙauyuka da biranan dake cikinta babu inda sunan MAI GIRMA MAS!! bai zaga ya kewaya yayi yayoba hatta ga yaran goye zakaji suna waƙarsa wadda mawaƙa suka rera masa mata na murna maza na murna kasancewarsa mutum nagari kuma nagartacce jajurtacce mai tsatstsauran ra,ayi akan abinda yake kai.


Muhammad anwar ya kasance kyakykyawan matashi mai jini ajika farine tas mai kimanin shekaru talatin da takwas dogone irin ingarman namijin nan wanda kallo guda zaka masa kasan cewa babu wasa ko wargi a tattare dashi.

Yana da manyan idanuwa waɗanda suka kasance farare tas yana da tabon abun sallah a goshinsa fuskarsa tana zagaye da saje wanda ya kwanta yay luf se ɗan gemunsa wanda yake tsaye carr cike da gashi mai kyau wanda yake samun gyara akai akai.yana da dogon hanci tare da madaidai cin ɗan karamin baki.
Sumar kansa irin mai kanannaɗar nan ce baƙa irin wadda take samun kula gami da kashe kuɗi akai akai.
Anwar mutum ne mai tsananin tsafta kamala da kunya sannan yana da kamun kai dan shi ko lokacin daxe auren farko yawanci anty ke rakasa zance sabida baya iyawa shi acewarsa baisan mema zecewa hidayaba.
Yana da tausayi tare da son zumunci sannan yana da muguwar kyauta.
Yana da tsananin bukata wadda bai san yanada da ita ba se bayan aurensa da hidaya wadda taso basu matsala a farko kafin yanzu ta goge tadena raki.

Arayuwarsa sam baya da son hayaniya bare yawan surutu sedai shidin mutum ne mai kwakwalwar gaske wanda har mamaki yake bawa mutane akan yadda yake sauri ɗaukar abu acikin kansa ko yaya yake yana da mugun kokari kamar kamarme.

Ayanzu haka dayake matakin ɗan majilissa hakan bai hanasa kaiwa asibitinsa ziyaraba harma ya gana da marasa lafiyan cikin asibitin tare da duba lamuransu.


 Yaje majilisa ya kawo motion acikin jaharsa dama kananun hukumomin dayake wakilta saboda jajurcewarsa.

Sam baya mu'amala da matan banza sabida shi atsarinsa ma kule kule baya cikinsa, sannan shi yana da tsananin tsafta wadda ta saka a kaf duniya babu abunda yafi tsana kamar zina,


Yana da mugun kwar jini wanda ya saka babu macen daxata iya masa kallo ɗaya tai gigin ƙarawa wanda hatta mazan ma shayinsa suke basa yarda suyi four eye's dashi sabida tsabar kwar jinin da ubangiji ya basa.

Baya zama da mai ha'inci ko maci amana daya baka aiki kai ha'inci koka gudu da kudin kokai son ranka gwara ka saci kudinsa ka gudu!

Yana da tsantsar riqo da addininsa wanda kowa ya shaida sa da hakan sam baya wasa da ibada sannan baya wasa da dukka abunda ya shafi mutuncinsa.


Yana da zafin rai da saurin fushi wanda idan ba zama dashi kaiba bazaka kula da hakan ba saboda tsabar miskilanci da taurin kan daya masa yawa wanda ya haɗe da rashin magana wanda ya gada a wajan maamah mahaifiyarsa wanda zaka iya kwana dashi idan baiga dama ba baze tanka maka.

Mutum ɗaya ke iya zama dashi suyi hira itace anty.


Agaskiya Mas yana da kyawawan hali wanda yasa jama'a suke sonshi musamman idan ya lafto wata kyautar yay maka sekace bama a hayyacinsa yay ba wannan dalili yasa jama'a suke masa laƙani da bangon sukari bakai farin banza ba damuna baki mugunta!!



Yayi ɗan majilissa a karon farko ya haye yanzun ma haka a karo ba biyu ya kuma hayewa wanda ada baiyi niyyar komawa ba amman yadda yaga jama'a suna nuna masa ƙauna yasa ya kuma komawa akaro na biyu.


Yana zaune anan cikin ƊAWISU G.R.A a ƙaton gidan daya gina masu shida iyayenshi inda ya ware nasa sashen shida hidaya yakuma ware na maamah dana anty ya warewa ƙaninsa da matarsa nasu, ɗayan kanin nasama ya ware masa nasa sannan  ya zuba musu ƴan aiki da duk abinda ake buqata na jindadin rayuwa.

Wanda kuma yanzun haka su imran kaninsa su  suke jagorantar kaddarorin da mahaifinsu ya bari,shikuma yake jagorantar dukkan wani abu daya shafi gidansu dama sauran ƴan uwansu nacan ƙauye dana cikin garin ɗawisu cikin gidan kakansu mai carbi inda a yanxu haka kakarsa wadda ta haifi mahaifinshi take cikinsa da sauran ƴan uwan mahaifin nashi.

Cikin tafiyarshi ta zafin nama irinta lafiyayyan namiji mai jini ajikinshi yake taka ƙafafunshi tare da barin ɓangaren antynshi.
Harabar gidan ya nufa inda ya hangi ma'aikatan gidan maza sunata alwala a bakin famfuna domin nufar masallacin dake tsakiyar gidan wanda ake ƙoƙarin tayar da sallah.
Cikin sauri yake tattare hannun rigarshi tare da nufar wani famfo wanda yake gab da bakin masallacin ya kunnasa aikuwa nan da nan ruwa yafara gudana, batare daya ɓata lokacinshi ba ya tsugunna ya soma alwala cikin azama da himma.
Tube takalmansa yay tare da fito da ƙafafunshi masu kama dana jarirai jajur tabarakallah.
Yafara zuba masu ruwa yana wankesu yana kammalawa ya ɗan ɗebi ruwa acikin tafukan hannunshi ya shafe sumar kansa dashi sabida zafin da ake agari danya ji sanyi kasancewarsa mutum mai tsananin jin zufa.
Yakashe famfon yay saurin shiga cikin masallacin harda haɗawa da gudu! sabida jin antada sallah.
Tsahon lokuta liman yay yana gudanar da sallah a nutse kafin a sallame tare da jan addu,oi bayan an kammala kowa yay hanyar fita daga masallacin kasancewar hatta maƙota bai ɗaukewa zuwa masallacin ba kowa ze iya zuwa yay ibadarsa aciki.
Sosai yake gaisawa da mutane da ƙyar ya samu kansa.
Sannan yaja ƙafafunshi cikin kasala ya nufi ɓangarensa.
A falo ya dakata tare da zama acikin ɗaya daga cikin manya manyan kujerun falon na kamfanin royal, sannan ya zaro wayarsa ƙirar iphone wadda ake yayinta a wannan shekarar wadda take da mugun tsada.
Number oska ya shiga nema wadda ringing guda oska ya ɗauka.
Cikin mutuntaka da ladabi oska ya fara masa bayanin yadda yaje ya gano masa aikin daya bayar acan wani ƙauyen ɗawisu waishi hayin buzuwa, tare da cigaban da aka samu.
Sosai yaji daɗin yadda oska ya gaya masa akan aikin nasu, daman kuma yasan halin oska babban yaronsa wanda sukaga yau kuma sukaga gobe dashi kasancewar oska mutum mai amanar gaske.
Sallama sukai tare da katse wayar.
Layin habu p.a ya kuma kira still.
Cike da rashin gaskiya p.a ya ɗaga wayan.
"Hellow mai gida! barka da yamma?" p.a ya faɗi hakan cikin ɗari-ɗarin magana.
Cikin son ƙure ƙaryar p.a *MAS* ya soma jefa masa tambaya cikin siyasa.
"Ban ganka ɗazu acikin tawagar tahowa gida ba?" halan ko lafiya?".
*MAS* ya jefama habu p.a wannan tambaya wadda ta kusan sakasa ya saki fitsari a tsaye.
Ɗan zaro idanunshi yay kamar marar gaskiya, sannan ya soma magana..
"Am....Ammm,Mai gida! wallahi madam ce babu lafiya shine aka kirani na tafi aguje!
Lumshe idanunsa yayi masu yalwar gashi a samansu gami da haske tamkar na madara sannan yace.
"Okey ALLAH ya bata lafiya idan ka gama kazo nan gida".
Yakai maganar tamkar bai san abunda su habu ke ƙullawaba.
Wawiyar ajiyar zuciya habu ya sauke haɗi dajin wani sanyi acikin ransa ganin tamkar Anwarr bai san abunda suke yiba.
Sannan yay masa godiya ya katse wayar.
Shikuwa Muhammad anwarr ɗan kishingiɗa yayi da wani lallausan pillowa dake ajiye ata gefen kujerar dayake kai tare da ɗora hannunshi asaman ruwan cikinsa ya kamo leɓansa na ƙasa yana tsotsa tare da faɗawa tunanin yadda ze ɓullowa babban aikin dayake son ya fara na titin daya biyo takan ɗawisu zuwa bakin ruwa.

Wannan kenan!!

 AUTAR MANYA🌹


                         🅿️ *8*


Nafisa kabir shine asalin sunanta mai kimanin shekaru goma sha bakwai da haihuwa.
Suna zaune acan wani ƙauye Acikin  ɗawisu waishi fanjam!
Nafisa ƴace ga malam kabir wanda ya fito daga gidan limamin fanjam,gida mai tarin iyalai masu tarin yawa.
Malam kabir shine ɗana biyar awajan malam liman, a bayanshi kuma akwai  mata guda biyu, waɗanda suka kasance ƴaƴan kaka, mahaifiyarsu, kuma dukkan ninsu, suna zaune ne agida ɗaya wanda ya kasance ƙaton gaske irin na ƙauyen nan wanda akewa laƙabi da ɗebi da kanka.
Ƴaƴan iya bakwai, baba.lado baba.mudi baba.mati.baba bello,se malam kabiru, daga shi se sahura da hansatu.
Se ƴaƴan innah sukuma su goma ne ciff maza takwas mata biyu.
Se ƴaƴan baba waɗan da suke su shida maza uku mata uku.
Gabaki ɗaya ƴaƴan malam liman sun kasance su ashirin da ukune cif.
Se ƴaƴan ƙannan sa suma masu tarin yawa waɗan da basu da iyaka.
Kuma ko wanne yana zama acikin gidan mal liman ne yayin daze aure kawai zaka shati fegin kane ka gina yadda kakeso ka zauna da matarka.
Wanda ya kasance baka isa ka fita waje kai aureba dole sedai ka auri ƴar cikin zuriyar gidan kasan cewarsu masu ɗumbin yawa shiyasa auren bare yazama haramun acikin wannan zuriya.
Gidane ƙaton gaske mai uban tarin sassa-sassa masu yawan gaske.
Nafisa itace ɗiyar malam kabir ta farko kuma daga kanta bai sake samun wani ɗan ba har ALLAH ya dauki ransa.
Ta taso acikin maraici gaba da baya kasancewar duk gidan malam ana auren zumuncine  tun malam liman yana yare haryazo ya rasu.
Malam kabir ya kasance mutum.mai son yawan fita ƙauyukan dake kusa da nasu dama sauran kauyukan daba anan ɗin sukeba, kasancewarsa mutum mai son neman na kansa.
Lokacin da mahaifinsu yana raye yana mugun son kabiru sabida yadda yayo halinsa da komai irin nasa da kuma ladabi da biyayya hakama kaka tana son kabiru sabida yadda yake lura da ita a duk cikin ƴaƴanta.
Lokacin da kabiru ya dauko munzalin girma anan malam ya dauki habi ƴar gidan ƙaninshi ya bawa kabiru ya aura sabida duk sauran ƴaƴan kaka maza sunyi auransu da matansu acikin gidan kuma duk ƴan cikin zuriyar ne babu bare.
Fafur kabiru yaƙi amincewa da tayin mahaifin nashi sabida yadda ya kamu da son wata ƴar fulanin ruga acan wani kauye waishi shashe, dake gabansu wanda har soyayyarsu tai nisa da ƴar fulani.
Wannan shine karo na farko da kabiru ya farawa malam liman musu atun tasowarsa.
Haka zalika itama kaka wannan ne karon farko daya taɓa yi mata musu atun tasowarsa.
Hankalin kowa ya tashi sabida yadda habi take suma akansa tana itafa idan bashiba se rijiya.
Babu abinda ƴan uwan kabiru suke se dariya sunawa malam ALLAH ya ƙara sabida yadda komai yace kabiru komai yace kabiru.
Ashe su haushin abin sukeji, kuma haushin labirun sukeji hatta ga ƴan ɗakinsu su mati bakaramun haushinsa sukejiba.
Babu yadda liman ya iya haka ya ɗauki ɗan uwansa sukaje rugar suka nemowa kaburi auran ƴar fulani saboda ya duba yaga babu haramci akan hakan.
Ita kuma habi aka bawa ɗan ɗakin inna sa'idu wanda yakebin kabiru ta wajan uba.
Wannan lamarin bakaramin kawo hargitsi yay acikin wannan zuriya ba haka akaita ɗauki ba daɗi wanda seda malam ya fito yay jan ido sannan aka rabu da maganar.
Bayan wani lokaci aka daura auren auran kabiru da ƴar fulani saidu da habi,
Ankawo ƴar fulani gidan malam liman inda aka ware mata sashenta daban.
Ba ƙaramar tsangwama ƴar fulani take ganiba acikin wannan family sabida ko moɗarta basa taɓawa wai barece.
Haka zalika kaka bata shiga sabgar ƴar fulani wai acewarta surkulle sukama ɗanta yay mata musu.
Awajan malam kawai takejin dadi wanda basu dauki wani lokaciba ALLAH ya karbi ransa.
Bakaramin kuka yar fulani taiba haka tsangawamarta ta dawo sabuwa  acikin gidan bata wani jin dadi idan ba kabiru kenanba.
Cikin ikon ALLAH ta samu ciki lokacin da cikinta ya tsufa, lokacin shikuma kabiru yaje wani kauye yin sari anan ALLAH ya karbi ransa yay hatsari.
Wannan tashin hankali yasa ƴar fulani ta fara naƙuda babu shiri ta haifi ƴarta mace mai kama da ita.
Sak!
Dan tsabar rashin imani irin na ƴan uwan kabiru basu barta ta shayar da ƴartaba suka koreta wai mai bakin jini ce mayya ce tazo zata karar masu da dangi.
Lokacin baffa baban yar fulani yazo haka suka jefo masu kayanta agabansu suka kwace jinjirar suka kora ƴarfulani tare da yimata gargadi mai yawa.
Zuciya irin ta fulani yasa baffa ya hada yarsa sukai ruga yana kuka!
Wannan dalilin yasa suka tattara kayansu suka bar rigar ma baki daya sukai wata!
Riƙon jaririya ya dawo hannun kaka ga kaka kuma ta soma tsufa haka aka tatso nonon saniya aka dinga ɗirkawa ƴa har ranar suna yarinya taci suna nafisa.
Tunda nafisa ta taso take ganin tsangawa awajan kaka take ɗan samun afuwa itaman ma kuma bawai sosaiba.
Kowa ya tsaneta agidan babu mai shiga sabgarta damma kaka tana masifa wataran ne yasa ake lafa mata.
Nafisa akwai hakuri batada faɗa sam komai kuka take masa bata da hayaniya ko kaɗan gata da mugun tsoro.
Wataran kaka har munafuka take ce mata dan tsabar yadda take sum sum kullum cikin hijabi da carbi bata wasa da addini.
Gata da ƙoƙari wanda shiya kuma haddasa mata ƙiyayya acikin gidansu.
Ita kadai ke makarantar boko kuma cikin ikon ALLAH take samun cigaba.
Har ALLAH yasa taci makarantar kwana ta ɗawisu.
Wadda seda kaka tai tsaye sannan baffa babban ɗan kaka ya barta ta tafi makaranta wanda kaka take ƴan buga buga da ita da umma  sahura mai bin babanta kabiru suke tsaye akanta sabida umma sahura bata taɓa haihuwa ba, kuma ita ba,a gidan take aureba ɗan kanin kaka take aure acan cikin garin fanjam.
Kuma babu laifi yana da rufin asirinsa shiyasa take taimakon nafisa sabida daman can tasu tafi zuwa ɗaya da kabiru kasancewarta sakuwarsa.
Wataran ma ko hutu nafisa taje tana zama ne agidan umma sahura wadda nafisan ce mata ta saka mata umman sabida yadda take son nafisa komai ta samu ita komai ta samu nafi haka kawai zata hada kayan abinci taje ɗawisu takaiwa nafi sabida basuda nisa da inda take aure.
Nafisa taga rayuwa tanama kuma kan gani sabida yadda ake nuna mata ƙiyayya acikin gidansu kasancewarta bare acewarsu.
Yanzu haka suna ss 3 wanda takai da ƙyar sabida baffa yace ze mata aure da ɗansa habu, wanda babu abinda habu yasani se sata da bin mata.
Da ƙarfin addu,a ta samu ta koma makaranta amman acewarsu tana candy zasu mata aure dan duk sa'anninta na gidan sunyi aure ita ce kawai tai karatu har takai ga candy.
Walida kawarta ce tun suna jss1 har zuwa yanzu walida ita acikin garin ɗawisu take kuma iyayenta masu haline dan sosai take taimakawa nafisa dan hatta provision ɗinsu a haɗe yake.
Ita kuma nafisa take taimakon walida ta sabgar karatu dan ko iyayen walida sun san nafisa sabida yadda walida ke maganarta, su biyu suke harkarsu basa shiga sabgar kowa sosai malamai suke son nafisa wadda akewa laƙabi da f.k nafisa kabir kenan.
Yanzu haka tanada mukamin shugabar makarantar.
Babu ruwan nafisa da kowa sabida bata da magana bare tai hayaniya.
Mutum ɗaya nafisa taji tana kauna duk duniya wanda daga kallo guda taji ta kamu da sonshi shine ɗan majalisarsu mai girma mas! tunda take bata taɓa son wani abu kamarshiba yanzun haka kuka biyu take dana maraici dana sonshi wanda takeji kamar zata rasa numfashinta.

Ganinta na farko dashi.

Suna ss2 ƙarshan term sukai hutu wanda idan suka dawo zasu tafi ss3  yau sauran kwana biyu su koma hutu tana zaune a dakin kaka tana hada kayanta.
Cike da murna kaka tayo dakin.
"ke nafi" dayake haka suke ce mata agidansu.
"kiyi maza ki kintsa kayan nan naki na aikeki can bakin rumbu kije ki sayo ƙuli-ƙulinki na daka maki ni bana son wannan sanyi jikin kiyi maza"
Ɗago idanunta tai masu yalwar haske da girma.
"Tom kaka bara na kintsa" takai maganar tana ɗan kintsawa da hamzari.
Tana gama kintsawa ta yayo mayafinta ta nufi kaka ta karbi aiken sa'annan tai waje.
Lokacin datazo zata saka kanta a waje taji maganar habi matar baba sa'idu cike da habaici.
"Boko koko da ƙosai zadai muga karshan wannan dirama" takai maganar tana sheqewa da dariya.
Sarai tasan da ita take amman bata ko daga kai ta kalletaba haka ta mayar da kanta ga abinda ke gabanta domun idan da sabo ta saba da jin fiye da haka daga bakin matan gidan musamman baba habi wadda tafi kowa tsanarta.
Abakin titi ta tsaya ta hau mashin yakaita naira ashirin taje ta siyo.
Bata dawo ba se gaf da magariba.
Cike da gajiya take sako kanta a cikin gidan nasu.
Da baffa taci karo ze fita sallah.
Dakatawa yay tare da binta da kallon tsana kamar ba ciki guda suke da mahaifinta ba.
"Ke daga ina kike daga yawon iskancinki na ƴan boko kike ko?"
Rintse idanunta tai bakuma tace komai ba.
"Dakata lado dakata" muryar kaka ta karade wajan wadda take fitowa daga sashen ta zata fita.
"Ni na aiketa kadaina jifan nafi da mugayen kalamai insha ALLAHU babu ɓatacce azuriyata" takai maganar tana jan nafisa ajikinta sukai hanyar sashensu nafi na kuka!
Zaunar da ita tai akan gado tare da amsar kulin tai waje.
Itakuwa nafi kwanciya tai saboda bata sallah alokacin.
Cikin bacci sama sama taji kaka ta shigo da ɓuɗa.
"Ke nafi tashi maza gobe asubanci zamuyi cikin garin ɗawisu zanje ganin likitan idanun nan dan yada man gashi naji wannan ɗan albarkar yaron yace duk mai wata matsala gobe ya dauki katinsa na asibiti yaje can asibitin"
Tura baki tai gaba tare da goge ƙwallarta.
"Haba kak kinsanfa gobe ni gidan umma sahura zani daga nan na wuce school sannan zakice muje ki nemi wasu mana koni ɗayace jikarki"
Sakin baki kaka tai tana kallon nafi sannan kuma ta kamo hannunta tana rarrashinta.
"Hab nafisatuna wallahi kallonki nake kamar kabiruna kawai sedai watarana idan na tuna takaicin abinda ƴan uwan kabiru sukaiwa mahaifiyarki senaji haushin kaina"
Takai maganar tana lallaminta.
"Nidai kaka dan dai kawai kinsan idan kika ambaci iyayena ina jin tausayinkine amman da wallahi sedai ƴaƴan so surakaki"
Takai maganar kamar ba itaba sabida watarana ita kanta kaka ba ragama takeba.
Da shirinsu suka kwanta.
Asubar fari suka gama kintsawa suka fita.
Mashin suka samu har tasha sannan suka hau mota sukai ɗawisu.
Basu suka saukaba se takwas na safiya abakin titi kaka ta tsaya ta saya masu kosai da koko.
Fafur nafisa taƙi ci aykuwa kaka tahau mitar wai ta cika girman kai.
Da tambaya suka ƙarasa cikin asibitin wanda ya kasance babban gaske daga gabansa anrubuta.
*ANWARR HOSPITAL*! kallo guda taiwa wajan ta kauda kanta har suka kutsa kansu ciki wanda yawan jama'a da motoci shiyafi bata mamaki.
Ansha ƙauyanci wajan kaka sabida tsaruwar asibitin dan wajan bude kofar ma seda akai dariya sannan sukaje suka zauna sukabi layi.
Nurse ce ta fito jikinta sanye da farin uniform.
Wajan dasuke zaune ta ƙaraso "wacece amina liman?" cike da sauri kaka ta miƙe "nice ƴar nan"
"Okey biyoni baba" nurse ɗin ta faɗa tare da kama hanyar wani lungu.
"Ke nafi taso mana kina nufin barinki zanyi awajannan ki ɓata abanza"
Sum sum nafisa ta miƙe tabi bayan kaka sukabi wannan nurse ɗin.
Wani katafaren office wannan nurse ɗin ta shiga sannan ta nunawa kaka wajan wanda zata ita kuma ta juya.
Da far'a likitan wanda aka rubuta Dr.safwan ajikin farar coat ɗin jikinsa ya nunawa kaka wajen zama har yana tsokanar nafi.
"Kaka kozaki bani wannan ƴarfulanin jikar taki marar far'a" yafada yana kallon nafi wadda sauran ƙirisa ta fashe dan haushi.
Dariya kaka ta saki sa'annan tace.
"Ay kam ɗan nan bada ban anyi mata mijiba damun baka" takai tana dariya tabar nafi da bakin cikin yadda kaka take kokarin bada ita daga zuwa gaban mutum zata fara zance.
Wata farar takarda ya mikowa nafi.
"Tom kanwata naganki ƴar boko maza leƙa office mai number 2 kicewa dr marwan gashi injini"
Karɓa tai ta saka kanta ga fita tana mai jin haushin kaka.
Kanta a sunkuye yake sam bata tsaya duba wata number ba ta kama wata ƙofa wadda tafi ko ina na wajan kyau ta murɗa.
Cikin ikon ALLAH tana murdawa kofar ta bude.
Wadda kofar ta tafi da ƙarfin gaske ta daki bangon office ɗin wanda ya amshi wata irin tsawa mai karfin gaske.
Da baya ta koma ta raɓe ta saka hijabin jikinta ta kare fuskarta tana kallon wani mutum kamar balarabe ko bature ya mike tsaye daga can cikin office ɗin yana sakar mata wani wawan kallo.
Tsoro damuwa fargaba soyayya mai ƙarfi duka a wannan lokacin ƙaddarar faruwarsu ta dirar mata.
Tsugunnawa tai awajan tana kare jikinta sabida yadda taga ya nufota cikin tafiya mai cike da izza isa miskilanci da rashin mutunci da rainin hankali.
Agabanta ya tsaya tare da daka mata wata wawiyar tsawa!!!
"Ke mahaukaciyace!! kalmar datafi saukar dalma zafi acikin zuciyarta kenan.
"Ke wace irin daƙiƙiyace!! yakai maganar cike da zafin zuciya jiyake kamar ya rufeta da duka.
Tana ɗago kanta taganshi tsaye akanta kamar ze daketa baki ɗaya idanunshi ya rine yay ja kamar za,ai ɓarin jini acikinsu.
Da sauri ta tattare zanin jikinta ta ruga da matsiyacin gudu tabar office ɗin tana dafe saitin zuciyarta.
Anan ta jira kaka harta kammala komai sukai gida kaka na mata faɗa itakam zuciyarta ta tafi ga tunaninsa da wata mahaukaciyar soyayyarsa mai zafin gaske.
Ko gidan umma bata jeba haka ta gama hutun ta ta koma school wanda baki ɗaya ta koma wata iri hatta ga karatunta bata mayar da hankalinta akai se tunaninsa wanda kullum dashi take kwana take tashi har ALLAH ya kawoshi makarantarsu ajiya wanda ita sam batai tunanin shi bane ba.

                         🅿️ *9*


Lumshe manyan idanunta tayi masu yalwar zara zaran gashi waɗanda suka sami damar kwanciya a saman idanun nata.
Tare da matsesu tana mai zubo da wani irin hawaye mai masifar gudu! batare data buɗe idanun nataba.
Cikin azabtuwa da masifaffiyar soyayya mai saka mutum a shiga tara bama uku ba f.k tafara magana.
"Ina sonka! ina sonka!! ina sonka!!! M.A wai yaya kakeso nai da rayuwata ne?" zan mutu nima nabi abbana akan da lilin soyayyarka ina fatan kazama mijina agidan aljannah"
Takai maganar kamar mai taɓin hankali..
Shuru kwanar tasu ta ɗauka babu abinda walida take se kallon mamaki wanda tsananinsa ya hana ta ce komai.
Shafa bakinsa take da siraran yatsunta waɗanda suke ƴan madai daita masu kyaun gaske.
Lalubar paster ɗin takumayi tare da kai ɗan mitsitsin bakinta dai dai kan nasa wanda yake manne ajikin poster ɗin yaci wasu shegun suite dark blue masu azabar kyau yana tsaye ya zube hannayensa cikin aljihun wandon suite ɗin face ɗinshi sanye da wani bakin glass wanda ya boye ƙwayar idanunshi.
Ɗora bakinta tai adai dai kan nasa tare da fito da harshenta taɗan lashi laɓɓansa kaɗan dayake jikin paster sannan ta kuma mayar da ita a saman kirjinta tana sakin wani wawan kuka!
Kamar kuma an tsikareta ta miƙe tai gaban walida wadda tai tagumi tana kallon ikon ALLAH tare da mamakin lallai so ba ƙarya ba, koda kuɗi aka ce mata feenah zatai abinda tai yanzu zata musa sabida tsananin kunyar nafisa amman yanzu jibi tsabar soyayya ta sakata ta manta ma a inda take may be.
Saurkar muryar fina ita ta dawo da walida daga duniyar tunaninta.
"Walida kaini wajan yaya nura yana ina inason naji labarin M.A dan ALLAH kada na mutu"
Kamo hannun walida tai ta ɗora a saman kirjinta.
"Walida taɓa kiji yadda zuciyata take harbawa wallahi walida inaga daga kallonshi sau ɗaya may be yay min addu,a ne"
Takai maganar tana rushewa dawani irin kuka!
Kamota walida tai tare da riƙo hannunta.
"Nafisa na gaya maki yaya nura yana can waje kusa da office ɗin baba vise yana jiranmu, please dan ALLAH zomuje koma.maye insha ALLAH nasan ya nura ze sanar dake".
Sannan walida ta kuma jan numfashi.
"Tabbas ƙawata kina cikin jarrabawa mai wahalar gaske domin da alama son maso wani ya kamaki amman zan horeki daki tsawaita addu,a matukar kuna da rabon ganawa ALLAH ze haɗaki dashi".
Takai maganar tana jan hannun feenah tare da share mata hawayen dake saukar mata suka bar cikin kwanar tasu.
Tafiya mai ɗan tazara a tsakanin hostel da area classes!
Sannan suka kai wajan daya nura yake faman baza idanun ganin zuwan nasu.
Da tsokana yabi feenah wadda daman kusan yana mata hakan idan sun haɗu amman ga mamakinsa seyaa yau bata tanka shiba.
Zama sukai a ɗaya daga cikin kujerun wajan sannan feenah ta kakalo murya da ƙyar ta gaishe shi taja bakinta tai tsitt.
Ɗan murmushi walida tai cikin siyasa ta dubi ya nurah.
"Yaya yau kaga sis so slient ko?".
Bata jira cewarsa ba ta kuma cewa.
"Yau ciwon kai take yi ne sabida abinda wannan ɗan majalisar namu yay mata"
Tafaɗi maganar cikin son bugar cikin yah nura sabida bata son ta tona sirrin zuciyar aminiyar tata wadda ita kadai tasan wannan magana.
Ɗan zaro ido yay tare da cewa..
"Lil sis miya faru badai wai mai girma mas! kuke maganaba anya kuwa shine wannan ay baida lokacin mace bare na feenah"
Yakai maganar da comfidance ɗinshi sabida yasan waye mas kasancewar yaya nura ɗan afanjama komai da ruwanka.
Gyara zama walida tai sosai ta fusaknci yaya nura 
"Ai kuwa yaya nura bakaga zakin daya mata ba wai tana shugabar makaranta amman tabar gardi ya hauro mana".
Takai maganar tana zare idanun karyar data zabga.
Dariya! yaya nura yay.
"Tabb amman lallai Ƴar fillo kinci sa,a mutum kamar mai gida ya tsaya magana dake".
"Hmm yaya nura ay ba tanan gizon ke saƙaba itakuma feena tace seta rama abinda yay mata ay".
Walida tai maganar wadda hatta feenah seda ta kalli walida tare da jinjina yadda walida ta iya sarrafa zance.
"To ALLAH ya bata sa,a ita inama zata ganshi mutumin da baya da lokacin kansa matarsa mafa setai wajan kwana biyu bata gansaba sabida yawon garuruwa da kewaye".
Dum!Dum!!Dum!! gaban fina ya faɗi cike da rashin dauriya taso gwafsa masu cikin azama walida ta kaɗa mata idanu tare da kallon ya nura.
"Au yanada mata yaya?"
"Eh mana ay ya jima da aure sedai kuma ALLAH bai basa haihuwaba har yanzu anata dai nema, kuma naji ance matarsa ma ƙanwace awajansa kuma ana faɗar kirkin hajiya hidaya duk da mu bamu santaba amman babu laifi ance gidansu akwai karama da mutunci"
Yakai maganar yana duba agogon hannunta.
Sannan ya mike.
"Ƙanne na ni zan wuce gida kekuma nafisa ina son kiyada makaman yaƙinki akan mai gida! saboda shi sam ƙilama ya manta dawata ke dan shikam baya shiga harkar kowa, idan ba aikin daya saka gabansa ba sam mata basa ruɗarsa.
Naji ancema ranar biyar ga wata ze taro anan filin wasa na ɗawisu square  wanda za,a tattauna dashi sosai akan ayyukan daya gudanar sannan ze bada tallafi na mata da marayu da zawarawa saboda haka kinga shiɗin abin sone kuma abin so ga kowa dan haka shawarata agareki ki fita harkar shi tun kafin wanima yaji".
Yana kai maganar ya zaro kudi masu yawa ya basu yay masu sallama ya tafi.
Haka suka kwashi kayan nasu suka wuce hostel wanda nafisa idan banda kuka babu abinda take.
Abakin kwanarsu ta aje kayan ta faɗa saman gado tana sakin kuka kamar wadda aka cewa kaka ta rasu.
"Haba fina maye na kukan nan haka mu fawwalawa ALLAH komai ze mana maganin dukkan damuwar mu".

"Hakane feenah, amman ina son ki riƙe addu,a domin itace makami ga mumuni, sannan kuma duk wanda ya riƙe ALLAH da manzon ALLAH tofa ya gama da dukkan wata matsala.
Walida takai maganar cike da rarrashin feenah, sannan ta ɗora da cewa.
"Kina ganin wannan soyayyar da kikewa megirma mas! kamar wahala to wallahi ba wahala bace ba, hakama wata jarabtarce daga rabbil izzati, abinda nakeso dake dai shine yawaita addu,a tare da zikirin ubangiji hailala tasbihi ga sarki buwayi gagara misali wanda bashida abokin taraiyah acikin mulkinsa wanda yake abinda yaso yakuma zartar da abinda yaga dama batare da shawarar kowaba, wanda yaƙagi sammai sannan ya shimfiɗa ƙasa, ALLAH kenan  Mai annabi Muhammadu S.A.W wallahi kika duƙƙufa addu,a dakanki zakizo kina gayamin abinda kika gani.
Sedai kumafa aikin addu,a name haƙurine dole kiyi haƙuri domin ita gaggawa asalinta daga shaiɗan take ALLAH ubangiji ya haɗaki dashi yakuma cika maki burinki na alkairi amin".
Walida takai maganar tana mai kallon feenah domin taga wani kalar yanayi zata shiga.
Ga mamakinta kuwa setaga feenah ta saki ranta hartana janyo marfin lokarsu ta ɗauko carbin da madara zata haɗa ta sha.
Bayan ta zauna ta ɗauki cup taɗan zuba ruwa kaɗan ta zuba sugar da madara tare da juyawa da spoon tana kallon walida cike da ƙwarin gwiwa tafara magana.
"Tabbas walida maganarki gaskiyace nayarda da addu,a kuma wallahi bada ban ƙarfin addu,a ba da tabbas zuciyata bazatai aiki izuwa wannan lokacin batare da Muhammad a tare daniba.
Ina son Muhammad Anwarr kuma ina ƙaunarsa domin ALLAH bantaba son wani ɗa namiji kamarsa ba, sedai kash gasu baffa agida suna burin haɗani da ya habu nikuma ga wanda zuciyata take farauta".
Takai maganar tana ɗibar carbin ɗin tana kaiwa bakinta tana shafa.

"Kadafa ki manta ƙawata ita zuciya! tana tarene da abinda takeso kuma yake kyautata mata, dan haka kada ki damu mubi komai a sannu tare da jifansa da addu,a insha ALLAH zakiga kamar ba,ai wannan gwaramar ba".
"Ina fatan haka ƙawa tagari" 
Feenah ta faɗa tana sakin murmushi.

                         🅿️ *11*


Ware manyan lumsassun idanunshi yayi waɗanda lokaci guda suka juye tare da sauya kala zuwa jajur.
Cike da tsananin ɓacin rai yake sauraran na cikin wayan nashi tare da ɗan jan numfashi kaɗan ya fesar da zazzafan huci mai sauti.
Fitar amon daddaɗar muryarsa wadda ta cika da zallar ɓacin rai ita ta karaɗe falon.
"P.a mika keson gayamin ne wai?" yafaɗa cikin sautin ɓacin rai.
Bai jira ta cewar p.a ba ya cigaba da magana wadda nan da nan maganar tasa ta koma yadda take ma'ana ɗan saurin baki kaɗan.
"Wallahi nama gaya maka kaje ka samu sardaunah kuyi magana bazan lamunci cin amanaba"
Yaɗan numfasa kaɗan.
"Wannan contract dana basu bawai dan na rasa masuyimun bane ba illah kawai na duba kokenshine, wannan ma ay zancan banza ne da shashanci kace wai aiki ya lalace, suna nufi na ƙara narka wani kuɗin nawa kenan wancan sun tashi a banza?".
Yakai maganar cike da tuhuma.
Daga cikin wayar p.a dake tsaye hannunshi na rawa baki ɗaya hankalinshi ya tashi ganin mai girma mas! ze harbo jirginshi nan da nan yahau bashi haƙuri.
"ALLAH ya huci zuciyar megida!wallahi nima ɗazu ya kirani yake gayamin, amman dai mai gida da antaimaka anɗan daɗo masu kuɗin kamar yadda aka buƙata a daure a duba lamarin".
P.a yakai maganar yana zaro ido shi a dole seyaci kuɗi.
"Dakata p.a.wannan furuncin da kakemin ze iya kawo ƙarshan tafiyata dakai! danni tafiyata ban haɗata da maci amana da marar alƙawariba,sabida haka ka kula".
Yakai maganar a fusace tare da datse wayar ya wurgata can gefe.
Hannunshi wanda yake fari ƙar mai cike da yalwar gargasa ya ɗora a saman sumar kansa tare da lumashi acikinta yana hargitsawa da alamun ran ƴan maza ya ɓaci.
Ya ɗauki kusan mintina biyar a zaune yana faman lissafi acikin zuciyarsa.
Kafin kuma ya miƙe cike da zafin nama yake taka ƙafafunshi ya nufi ciki.
Kai tsaye hanyar bedroom ɗinshi ya nufa tare da buɗe ƙofar ya faɗa ciki.
Agurguje ya tube kayan jikinshi ya nufi bathroom 30 minute ya gama watsawa jikinsa ruwa.
Tare da shiryawa cikin wani farin yadi mai kyau.
Turarurruka masu sanyin ƙamshi ya fesa tare da ɗaukar makullin mota ya nufi hanyar fita.
Hango hiyada yay daga cikin daining area tana setting table.
Ɗan juyowa tayi sannan kuma ta tako cike da nutsuwa ta ƙaraso gabansa.
"Dear kardai fita zakayi?".
Tafaɗi maganar tana ɗan tura bakinta gaba kaɗan..
Shafo kumatunta yay da lallausan hannunshi tare daɗan sakin murmushi kaɗan.
"Haba gimbiya sarautar mata to maye na haɗe ran zani meating ne before ai kiran sallah"
Marairaicewa tayi cike da shagwaɓa sannan ta ɗan matso hawaye kaɗan.
"Nidai ka zauna na samu ladan baka abinci tunda yau kayi wankan baka jirani ba".
Cike da jin daɗin yadda take kyautata masa ya kamo hannunta cikin nashi.
"Ay haƙuri my love bazan jimaba insha ALLAHU".
Janye hannunta tai anasa tare da komawa baya.
"Tun break ɗin safe bamuci komai ba ina faman jiranka nai maka abinci mai daɗi shine zaka tafi bazaka tsaya kaciba nafa san meating ɗinnan watarana senai bacci baka shigoba".
Tausayinta ne ya kamasa tare dajin wata irin soyayyarta mai tsafta acikin ransa.
Kamota yay zuwa jikinsa.
"Tom ay haƙuri bazan kuma ba maza muje muci abincin".
Ya gaya mata haka tare da tallafota suka nufi daining sanin za,a takurasa da waya yasa ya kashe wayar tasama baki ɗaya.


                      Hostel

Tunda walida ta bawa f.k shawara akan addu,a shikenan nafisa ta mayar da hankalinta ga addu,a babu dare babu rana haka zalika babuji babu gani.
Dukda tana masifar jin soyayyarsa acikin ranta amman haka take daurewa tana harkokinta.
Amman takanas ta nemi radio wadda kullum take manne da ita tanajin program din da akeyi agidan radion ɗawisu wanda akewa take da.
TASKAR MAI GIRMA MAS! kusan kullum tana sauraran shirin wanda ake zaƙulo irin ayyukan dayakeyi afaɗin garin wanda wataran ake magana tare dashi.

"Nafeesah! Nafeesah!! cewar walida wadda take tsaye akanta hannunta ɗauke da sponcase wanda daga gani daga wanka ta fito.

Falo tsit ba kowa yasa ta nufi wajan wata ƙofa mai kalar golden.
Ta murɗa handle ɗin ta shiga ciki wani azababben kamshine yakaiwa hancinta cafka.
Da sassarfa ta nufi gaban sa wanda yake zaune gefen gado hannunsa dafe akan kuncinsa da alama baima san da shigowarta ba.
Agabansa ta tsugunna tare da miƙa hannunta wanda yake karkarwa ta ɗora akan hannunsa wanda yake naɗe da farar singlet ɗinsa wadda ta jiƙe da jini.
"Haka sukai maka, mai yasa wannan ɗinma bai sauka ajikinaba banyiwa kaina adalciba dana bari jinin nan ke fita ajikinka"
Yaji sautin fitar amon daddaɗar muryarta ya cika masa kunne.
Cike da mamaki ya ɗago jajayen idanunsa ya ɗora akan ƴar yarinyar dake tsugunne a gabansa tana faman kuka.
Wadda sam takasa ɓuya acikin idanun nasa.
Lumshe idanun sa ya ƙarayi tare da waresu akanta cikin dakewa.
Kallon second guda yay mata ya shaidata, tabbas itace.
Cikin jarumta ya ɗagota tare da ɗorawa a gefen gadon dayake shikuma ya tsugunna a gabanta tare da tsirata da idanunsa yana kallonta.
"Sabida me kikai hakan?"
Taji saukar muryarsa a dake acikin kunnenta.
Kallonsa tai sau ɗaya ta kawar da kanta gefe saboda ba itaba kowa ma baya ido biyu dashi sabida kwarjinin da ALLAH ya basa.
"Sabida ALLAH da kuma soyayyarka a zuciyata".
Tafaɗa a dake batare datai shakkaba.
Tsira mata idanu yay yana kallonta yanajin wani irin yanayi ajikinsa.
Cike da basar da abin ya fara ƙoƙarin ganin raunin bayanta.
Domin yafison lafiyarta ta tsira kafin wata magana ta fita abakinsa.
Saukar tattausan hanunsa a gadon bayanta yasa taɗan rintse idanunta kaɗan tanajin wani irin mugun zafi mai wuyar gaske.
Saukar da ita yay ƙasan tiles ɗin yana kokarin ƙara mayar da hannunsa gadon bayan nata domin ya zare harsashin dake ciki wanda harya fara kore alamun ze mata illah.
Cikin hikima ya kwantar da ita a lafiyayyan kirjinsa tare da fara kokarin zare mata cike da dubara.
Wata iriyar ƙara! ta saka a yayin  dataji ya zare harsashin bayan nata kafin ta kai hannunta ta kuma damƙesa tsam.
Baki ɗaya ta zuba masa nauyin jikinta duk ta cakumesa tana sakin wani irin kuka mai shiga zuciya.
Wani azababben feeling ke fisgarsa tare da wani irin mutuwar  jiki, wanda tunda yake aduniya bai taɓa jin makamancin irinshiba.
Duk yadda hannunshi ke masa zafi bai hana shi jin wata fitinanniyar sha,awa ta kamasaba.
Dafe mararsa yay wadda take barazanar fashewa.
Tare da ɗan tureta ajikinsa yana neman tsarin ALLAH daga sharrin, shaiɗan.
Sake komawa tai jikinsa tana sakin wani kukan sabida azabar datakeji.
Baki ɗaya ta zuba masa breast ɗinta ajikinsa tana goga masa su wanda ita zafin da bayanta ke mata yasaka take wannan birgimar ajikinsa shikam jiyake kamar da gayya take hakan kamar kuma tana sake kaɗa masa wata gangar sha,awar tata take.
Kamota yay sosai zuwa jikinsa  ya lumshe idanunsa yana jin wani irin mutuwar jiki.
Ya ɗora bakinsa akan nata yaƙi bata damar yin koda motsi.
Ya fara kissing ɗinta cike da ƙwarewar gaske.
Baki ɗaya ya fita daga hayyacinsa babu abinda yake se ruɗa mata jiki da salonsa.
Har sanda ya kwanatar da ita akan ƙasan tiles ɗin yabi ruwan cikinta ya kwanta yana romancing ɗinta.
Kuka kawai feenah take tana neman ɗauki wanda ko fitar sautinsa ba,aji azaba biyu ta haɗar mata.
Lokacin da hannunsa yakai kan breast ɗinta wani azababben kuka ta kuma kecewa dashi ga azabar bayanta ga azabar yadda yake jamata su kasancewar bata saba da hakanba.
Duk yadda yake bidirinsa bata hayyacinta har sanda ya sami damar rabata da martabarta  da kuma, mutuncinta
Wata ƙara! tasaki kafin ta koma luuuuu ta sume.
Jinsa yake kamar wani shaho asama sabida wani irin ni'ima dayake kwasa ajikinta itaɗin zumace madara ce baya cikin hayyacinsa ko nutsuwarsa ko tunaninsa.
Har seda ya kawo sau uku ajikinta sannan ya murgina gefenta yana mayar da numfashi kamar wanda ya sami ƴar hanu daman can.
Wani azababben zazzaɓine ya rufesa ruff haryana haɗa haƙoransa wanda tuni ya saka ya gigice ya fita ahayyacinsa da ƙyar ya mayar da wandonsa tare da komawa gefenta yana mayar da numfashi sama sama.

Takai kusan minti 30  ahaka kafin ta soma ɗaga hannunta cikin azaba da neman ɗauki.
Harta soma bude idanunta waɗanda suka koma kamar wuta dan ja!
Tare da nauyi, ta diresu akansa wanda ke yashe agefenta yana fitar da numfashi cikin azabar zazzaɓin daya rufeshi ayanzu.
Wata irin nadama da tausayin kanta nan take taji ya dirar mata, da jan ciki taja jikinta ta nufi hanyar falo.
Duk jini yana bin ƙafarta tana jin yadda ƙasanta ke faman azaba kamar ana barbaɗa  mata  barkono.
Harta samu ta fita daga falon baki ɗaya...
Cike da tsananin azaba take jan ƙafarta bayan ta dafa bango ta miƙe ta nufi hanyar bakin gate domin fita daga gidan.
Tana cigaba da kuka mai cin zuciya.....!!

Innalillahi'wainnah ilaihirraju'un......!!

                   14  - 15


Cike da tsananin azabar da tunda take acikin duniya bata jin koda makamanciyarta ba take taka ƙafafunta tare da cije bakinta da ƙarfin tsiya jitake kamar ta mutu ta koma ga mahaliccinta ta huta, ahaka harta samu ta buɗe gate ɗin da hannunta wanda yake faman karkarwa.
Buɗewatai ta fita daga gidan baki ɗaya adaddafe tayi bakin titi bayan tasha tafiya mai ɗan tazara kaɗan daga gidan zuwa bakin titi.
Ganin idanunta na faman lumshewa kamar wadda zata mutu yasa ta nemi gu ta zauna abakin titin tare da ɗagawa kowace mota dazo wucewa hannu.
Har ALLAH ya taimaketa ta sami mai napep wanda yake shiɗaya.
Cike da tausayi yaja ya tsaya a gabanta yana faman yi mata magana cikin gushewar tunani take masa bayanin inda ze kaita da ƙyar ta samu ta lallaɓa ta shiga cikij napep ɗin wanda tsohon dayake tuƙa napep ɗin yay kamar ze mata kuka.
Haka yaja yatafi cikin nutsuwa Dayake gidan umma abakin titi yake shiyasa bai wani sha walahaba ya gane ƙofar gidan ya tsaya ya sauketa tare da mata fatan alkairi.
Kuma fafur yaƙi tsayawa karɓar kuɗinta sabida yarinyar ta basa tausayi kuma daga gani acikin wani tashin hankalin take.
Itakuwa nafeesah cike da ragowar ƙarfin daya rage mata take lallaɓa jikinta harta samu ta ƙarasa bakin ƙofar gidan umma sahura.
Da dubara ta samu ta shigar da jikinta cikin gidan tanajin wata iriyar faluwar gaba wadda tazo mata baka tatam.
Atsakar gida taci karo da umma sahura da baba mijinta duk sunyi tsaye gefensu walida tsugunne tana kuka kamar ranta ze futa.
Atare suka ɗago idanunsu sukabita dashi suna kallonta cikin mamaki daga baya kuma suka rugo aguje harda mijin umma sahuran sukayo kanta.
Rumgumeta umma sahura tai tare da fashewa da wani matsanancin kuka!
"Nafisah yau kin ɗagamin hankali ina kika shiga?" inda kin mutu da yaya kikeso naida raina" takai maganar tana rungumeta ajikinta.
Ganin tana faman lumshe idanu kamar bata hayyacinta yasa mijin umma ya fita aguje ya taro napep aka cicciɓeta zuwa ɗan ƙaramin asibitin dake bakin unguwar.
Wanda zuwa wannan lokacin ita tuni tama suma bata cikin hayyacinta.
Umma kuka kawai take tana neman ɗaukin ubangiji har suka isa clinic ɗin cikin mintuna goma leburorin clinic ɗin suka zo da ɗan gadon ɗaukar marasa lafiya akai ciki da ita.
Akabarsu umma cirko cirko suna zaman jiran tsammani.
Shikuwa mijin umma sahura wajan biyan kuɗi da lissafi ya nufa domin yay booking kafin azo garesu.
Itakam umma da walida anma rasa waze baiwa wani haƙuri kowa kuka yake musamman umma datake fatan ALLAH yasa kada maganar tajewa su kaka.
Sosai likita dawata nurse suka duƙƙufa akanta abinda yasama basu nemi akira policeba sabida Mas! ya riga ya cire mata bulet ɗin amman da inda dashi dole se hukuma ta shiga sabida criminal case ne ba,a duba irin waɗannan masu mugun raunin batare da hukumaba.
Sun ɗauki manyan lokuta suna mata ɗinki tare da taimawa wajan da harsashin ya shiga wajan wankewa da liƙewa sannan nurse ɗin ta fito ta bawa baba takardar biyan kuɗi tare da rufesu da faɗan akan kada sukuma wannan gangancin na kawo marar lafiya bayan ya jigata.
Hakuri suka bata sannan baba yay hanyar pharmacy ita kuma umma ta tsaya tare da walida awajan.
Seda aka gama mata komai tsaf sannan nurseɗin ta lulluɓeta da farin mayafi na asibiti tana kokarin fita taga jini na biyo ƙafafunta......!!!

                  *16 - 17*

Cikin tsananin tsoro da kiɗima nurse ɗin tabi ƙafafunta da kallo tare da saka hannunta ta yaye mayafin rufar da akai mata,.
Cikin azama ta ɗan ɗaga rigar jikinta wadda ta kasance doguwace tare da ɗan zameta kaɗan.
"Innalillahi wa'innah ilaihirraju'un"!
Nurse ɗin ta faɗa ta ƙarfin gaske tare da saurin mayar mata da rigar ta koma baya tana share hawayen tausayinta dasuka soma mata gudu akan kuncinta.
Akiɗeme ta fita daga room ɗin tare da zuwa wajansu ummah.
Ɗan tsayawa tai agaban umma tana binta da kallon tuhuma bayan ta share hawayenta.
"Baiwar ALLAH kece mahaifiyar wannan mai raunin ko?"
Nurse ɗin ta furta hakan cikin aro dauriya.
Cikin rawar jiki ummah sahura tace.
"Eh nice ƴar nan ALLAH yasa dai ba mutuwa nafeesah tayiba"
Tafaɗa tana zubo da sabbin hawaye.
"Bata mutuba amman da ALLAH kiɗan biyoni ɗakin yanzu am pls ke ɗayafa kada kizo da wannan yarinyar".
Tanuna walida wadda ke tsaye tana zare idanu.
Ummah sahura hartana tuntube tabi bayan nurse ɗin izuwa ɗakin da aka kwantar da nafeesah.
Tana kwance samɓal tamkar gawa dan babu alamun motsi a tare da ita.
Da hannu nurse ɗin ta nunawa ummah sahura nafeesah tare da umartarta akan data ɗaga rigar ta tagani.
Jikin ummah sahura na karkarwa ta nufi gadon tasaka hannunta ta yaye rigarta abinda tagani shiyasata jada baya aguje tana runtse idanunta.
"Innalillahi wainna ilaihirraju'un" 
Umma ta faɗa tana saka kuka.
"Waya maki fyaɗe nafisah waya maki haka wani marar tsoron ubangijin ne yay maki haka nashiga uku ancuci marainiyar ALLAH"!
Takai maganar tana kuka.
Ɗan matsota tayi nurse ɗin cike da tsantsar tausayawa tace mata.
"Kiyi hakuri ummah wannan maganar bata fitarwa bace ba yanzu kiɗan bamu waje zanyi kokarin yimata ɗinki sabida gaskiya anbuɗata da yawa, sannan ki rufe bakinki kada ki gayawa kowa harta samu sauki muga yaya za,ai".
Likitan takai maganar cike da rarrashin umma sahura.
Cikin mutuwar jiki umma tabar ɗakin tana jin matsanancin tausayin nafeesah aranta.
Abakin ɗakin ta zauna ragwajab ta saka kuka kamar zata shiɗe.
Cikin sauri baba mijinta dake tafe da kayan magunguna a leda a hannunsa ya karaso gabanta ya tsugunna cikin tashin hankali yake tambayarta meke faruwa?.
Abokin kuka ba,a boye masa mutuwa atake ta zayyane masa komai inda shima nan ya rashe agabanta yasaka kukan tausayin nafeesah.
Sun ɗan dade suna koka lamarin kafin ya rarrasheta su mike su karasa wajan walida wadda ta kafesu da ido dan itakam bama tada bakin magana tunda taga ƙawarta a halin rai ko mutuwa.
Cike da tsantsar tashin hankali a kan fuskar walida ta dubi umma cikin kiɗima kana tace,.
"Ummah tun ɗazu naga kin zauna kina kuka! ƙafata ta gaza ɗaukata zuwa gareki shin lafiya dai ko,koko wani abunne ya faru da nafeesah?".
Takai maganar cikin tuhuma.
Goge hawayen daya zubo mata tayi kana ta dubi walida sa,annan tace.
"Koɗaya walida kedai mutaya yarinyar da addu,a ALLAH ya tasheta lafiya".
"Ameen ummah".
Walida ta faɗa tana ƙara share hawayenta.
Cikin ɗakin kuwa sosai nurse ɗin ta haɗo kayan dazatayi amfani dasu wurin ɗinkin nafisah wadda take jin tausayinta na ratsa mata jikinta tare kuma da la,antar wanda yay ma wannan jaririyar yarinyar wannan cin kacar.
Duƙƙufa tai sosai akanta tafara yi mata ɗinki wanda a wannan lokacin ko yatsan nafisa baya motsi sabida manyan alluran da akai mata masu saka bacci da kashe jiki.
Ta ɗauki kusan awa biyu akan tana ɗinketa wanda seda akai mata ɗinkuna har huɗu kana aka kammala cikin tausayi nurse ɗin ta lulluɓeta bayan ta zare handclov ɗin dake hannunta.
Fitowa tai daga ɗakin tare da nufar office ɗin doctor bayani tai masa tare da aje masa kayan aikinta tai wajansu umma.
Cike da hikima ta jawo umma gefe sannan tai mata bayanin komai tare da shaida mata cewar anbasu gado dole zasu ɗan kwana biyu sabida ɗinki da akai mata tare kuma da raunin dataji wanda yake son abasa kulawa harya warke.
Sallama sukai ita tai nata waje ita kuma umma ta koma taiwa baba bayani amman batai gigin faɗin zancan ɗinkiba sabida walida.
Cike da rawar jiki walida ta dubi ummah sahura sannan tace.
"Umma zanje gida yanzu na shaidawa hajiyarmu idan yaso semuzo da kayan abinci da wasu abubuwan tunda ke kina nan".
Ɗan numfasawa ta kumayi kana tace.
"Amman ummah kada kiyi saurin faɗin cewar ga dalilin dayasa feenah ta sami wannan raunin sabida kinga shi babban mutum ne mubi abin a sannu harta warke kafin asan matsaya, mudai kawai mubari akan cewar motace ta daketa a titi".
Walida takai maganar tana kallon ummah sahura.
Cikin gamsuwa baba mijin ummah sahura dama ita umma sahuran sukai na,am da maganar walida sabida basusan mita hango acikin lamarinba.
Sallama tai musu ta wuce tare da shaida musu cewar seta dawo ɗin.
Shima baba kallon umma yayi tare da ce mata bara yaje kasuwarsu ya ɗan samo kuɗi sabida koda za,a nemi wani abun.
Da kallo tabisa bayan tai masa fatan alkairi ya wuce itama ta wuce can bakin ɗakin ta zauna akan wani ɗan tudu tare da zaman jiran tsammani.
