                    18  -  19


Hanunshi yake ɗagowa wanda yake masa tsananin zafi sakamakon  harbin daya shigesa aɗazu.
Cike da tsananin jarumta wadda tazame masa ɗabi,a tabi jininsa ta mamaye ya gama ɗago hannun nashi tare da saka ɗayan ya dafe ƙasan tiles ɗin sannan ya tallafi jikinsa ya mike baki ɗaya.
Tan ƙwashe ƙafafunshi yayi bayan ya zauna ɗin tare da saka tafukan hannayenshi ya dafe kansa wanda yake barazanar tarwaste masa.
Yakai kusan minti goma a haka yana fama da kansa yana jin yana sake ɗaukar wani matsanancin zafi sassan jikinsa na kama wani irin zazzafan zazzaɓi wanda har rawa rawa jikinsa keyi.
Ɗago idanunsa yay waɗanda kallo guda zaka masu ka kawar da kanka sabida yadda sukai wani irin sauya kala zuwa jawur harwani ruwa ke kwanciya acikinsu dan tsananin jan dasuka ɗauka.
Kallon dai dai saitin da jinin yay sitinin yayi tare da ƙura idanunsa sosai awajan yana kallo.
Atake anan komai ya dawo masa sabo fill acikin ƙwaƙwalwarsa.
"Inalillahi wainnah ilahirraju'n"!!!
Yafaɗa da ƙarfin gaske tare da miƙewa tsaye zumburr kamar mazari yana kaiwa da komowa a tsakar ɗakin nasa.
"Ya ALLAH ina neman taimakon ka domin ahalin yanzu bana da wata mafuta setaka ALLAH ina roƙonka dakai gaggawar bayyanamin yarinyar nan tun kafin nazo gabanka na karbi hukuncin zi...........".yaja da ƙarfi kamar mai cutar ƙinƙina.
Kana ya koma  bakin katafaran gadonsa ya faɗa daga tsaye yana mai dafe saitin zuciyarsa.
"Ni Anwarr yau nina shiga gonar da bata halarta gareniba why!why!!why!!! maiya kaini ga hakan wani ruɗin duniyar ne yakaini ga kusantar zina abu mafi muni acikin manyan ayyukan saɓon ALLAH".
Sekuma ya mike kamar an tsikaresa ya fita daga ɗakin har harabar gidan ya leƙa tabbas babu ita babu labarinta cike da sanyin jiki ya koma ɗakin ya ɗauko mopa ya fara gyara wajan da jinin ya ɓata.
Kana ya tube kayan jikinsa yana jin tsananin ƙyamƙyamin kansa kamar ze cire fatar jikinsa haka yaja towel yay bathroom.
Ya tsaya gaban shower ya dafe bangon tiles yana jin yadda ruwan ke sauka agadon bayansa.
Ya jima a hakan kana ya taro na wankan tsarki.
Duk yadda yakai ga dauriya seda yay ƙwalla sabida yadda yake kallon aikin saɓon dayay wanda ya cika masa ransa wai yau shike wankan tsarki batare daya kusanci matarsa ba se wata ta waje.
"Kin cuceni"!!!
Ya furta a hakan a ƙasan ransa bayan ya kammala wankan bai dauki wannan towel ɗinba wani ya ɗauka dan bayajin ko ɗakin ze kuma shiga bare kayan dayay amfani dasu dan tsabar ƙyamƙyamin aikin dayay acikin ɗakin wanda ko tuna hakan bai son yay.
Agurguje yay ɗayan dakin dake gaba da wancan ɗin wanda yafi wanda yafito kyau da tsari.
Gaban ƙatuwar Wadrov ya tsaya tare da ɗaukar wasu fararen kananun kaya ya saka ajikinsa bayan ya gama wanke kansa da turare mai kamshi kana ya koma cikin bathroom ɗin ɗakin ya ɗauro alwala.
Shimfiɗa abin sallah yay tare da kallon alƙibi yanajin tsananin kunyar mahaliccinsa duk da tsananin ciwon da hannunsa ke masa amman bai hana yay nafila raka,a biyu ba yana sujjada yana hawaye yana neman yafiyar ubangijin sa.
Bayan ya idar yay salatin annabi s.a.w tare da fara tasbihi ga ALLAH yana ƙidayawa da hannunsa, harya gama kana ya soma salatin annabi s.a.w, ya jima yana salatin annabi s.a.w awajan sannan ya mike ya koma gan gadonsa yana kallon rufin ɗakin tare da ɗora hannunsa a ruwan cikinsa.
Yana tunanin shikam wani babban laifi yataɓa aikawata wanda har ALLAH ya jarabcesa da wannan masifar dan kuwa babu abinda yaki zina masifa wadda ayanzu wasu mutanan suka mayar da ita ado.
Amman shikam awajansa masiface kuma bai gusheba haka yayta jera neman yafiyar ALLAH tare da neman tsari daga sharrin sheɗan la,anan nan ALLAH,tabbas wannan ma babbar jarabawa ce garesa tinda yake baya tunanin da abinda ya tabayi wanda ya girgizashi kamar wannan kuma babu abinda ya taɓayi wanda ya sakashi nadama kamar wannan.
Waya kawota gidansa?
Miya kaita ɗakinsa?
Zuciyarsa ta basa amsa da.
Ƙaddara ta kawota gidanka, sharrin shaiɗan kuma ya kaita ɗakinka domin shiyay mata jagora har ɗakinka kaikuma ya ƙawata maka ita ya rikiɗa maka ita.
Tabbas zuciyarka seta dage domin hakan ma wata masifar ce data kunno maka tare da jarabawa mai sarƙaƙiya.
Ya aiyana hakan a zuciyarsa tare da lumshe idanunsa yanajin komai ma ya fice masa acikin ranshi.
"Ina zan ganki innemi yafiyarki sannan ki karɓi hukuncina..........!!!

Har jikinta yana kaɗawa haka Anty Aisha nurse ta ƙarasa gaban gadon cikin ɗaukin ganin hannun nafeesat daya soma motsawa kaɗan kaɗan.

Batare da westing time ba oska ya ƙaraso horn kawai ya danna daga haraban gidan.

Babu abinda yake se zuba uban ƙamshi,dayake bai saba saka kananun kayan ba shiyasa yay wani irin,azababban kyau ya boye idanunshi da glass mai kalar baki sabida baya son a irin yanayin dayake ciki idanunsa su bayyana ga mutane sabida yadda idanun nasa sukai wani irin kala.
Da kallo anty ta bisa tana yaba kyawun halitta irin na ɗan nata kana ta mike tabi bayansa ganin ya nufi hanyar fita.
Wannan karon ba irin motar da oska ya kawosa ɗazu bace wata new mordan ce mai kalar white.
Da sauri oska ya bude masa motan kana itama anty ya bude mata seda sukai setting site kana oska ya koma driver site ya zauna yaja motar yabar wajan.
Awaje ya tsaya ya koma y rufe gate ɗin sa,annan ya komo ya sake tada motar suka dauki hanya.
Seda ana kiran sallar isha"i sannan suka shigo cikin gari sabida gidan acan cikin ɗawisu yake babu jama,a sosai a unguwar anyisane daman dan sirri babu kowa a arean ma wajan idanba gidan nasaba.
Kallon agogo anty tayi tare da kallonsa sannan tace.
"Yanzu mu kira doctor kenan tunda naga munkusan karasawa gida ko?" 
Tafaɗa tana kallonsa cikin kulawa.
Ɗan rausayar da kansa yay gefe ya furzar da zazzafar iska tare da cewa.
"Nifa aunty wallahi hayaniya ce bana so kawai ya kaini wajan anty aisha nasan tana clinic ɗin maamah kuma kinga can yafi safe babu wanda ze kawo ina can ɗin tunda ƙarami ne kuma nima bana son kowa ya san ina can ɗin sabida privacy".
Sosai anty ta gamsu da bayanin nasa dan haka babu wani ɓata lokaci oska ya sauya hanya zuwa clinic ɗin da ya faɗa masan.
Lokacin da suka isa clinic din nurse aisha bata jima da rabuwa da umma ba, bakaramin mamaki taiba lokacin dataga anty kishiyar yayarta tazo office dinta tana shaida mata sakon ɗan yayar tata dan haka babu musu ta dauki kayan aikinta tai cikin ɗakin da aka ware masa.
Wanda yake ciki sosai babu ma wanda ze kawo da daki awajan.
Seda suka kai ruwa rana da ita sabida kusan sa,arsace tsiransu kaɗanne kusanma tare suka taso kafin ya yarda tai masa alluran kashe zafin wajan sannan tai treatment ɗinsa.

Itakam nafisa babu laifi ummah ta tayar da ita takaita bayi ta hada mata ruwan mai dan zafi ta shigar da ita ciki taɗan jima sannan ta fitar da ita kana ta saka zani ta goge mata jikinta sannan tai alwala sama sama ta fito waje bakaramin dauriya taiba wajan yin sallah sannan tasha tea ta koma ta kwanta tanajin kanta naɗan sarawa.
Ganin dare yayi yasa umma karo kofar tasu ita ta kwanta akasa nafisa na saman gado wadda tun dazu takejin gabanta na faduwa wanda ta rasa kona miyene.
Sam har yanzu ta kasa gasgata abinda ya faru da ita shin gaskene koko karya waiyau ita ANWAR ya raba da mutuncinta ta karfin tsiya daman soyayya haka take.
Kokaɗan ta kasa ganin laifinsa illama wata mahaukaciyar soyayyarsace ta kuma kamata wadda takejin koza,a mutu bata tunanin zata tona masa wannan asirin wallahi koda shine wataran ze ganta yace ya taɓa mata fyaɗe tofa zata karyata shi domin soyayyarsa gareta bazata taba son ganin ya tozartaba wannan dalili yasaka ayanzu dole ta boye kanta ta kuma nesanta kanta da duk inda tasan zata iya ganinsa sabida yadda ya shaidata tasan shidin, mutum ne mai mugun ƙwaƙwalwa tabbas ze iya shaidata a yayin data haɗu dashi.
Wata zazzafar ƙwallace ta kawomata mai zafin gaske ta mayar da ita tare da matse pillow a kirjinta tana ayyana yadda taji nauyinsa a kirjinta.
Tabbas shidin ya cika namiji namijin da ko acikin maza se antara shidin gwarzone a fagen komai haka zalika gwarzone a fagen saka mace ta manta ko ita wacece.

Itama shaheeda bin bayansa tai sabida duk tajita a takure shidaman yaya anwarr ɗinnan ba,a zama agabansa duk yabi yawani hade rai.
Cike da tsoro tacewa imran.
"Abban hanif zan bika nima gidan".
Dayake imran akwai tsokana juyowa yay ya kalleta.
"To maza taso autar maamah mai tsoron yaya kada ya kawo maka".
Cike da sauri tabi bayansa bayan taiwa su anty sallama.
Bayan futarsu imran anty ta dubi aisha nurse kana tace.
"Waini kam yau yana ganki haka anya kuwa maganar imran ba haka take ba?" itama takai hakan tana dariya wadda daman sun saba ƴar hakan da aisha.
Tsaki aisha taɗan ja kana ta dubi anty.
"Wallahi Anty ba haka bane ni kaɗai nasan asarar danai yau".
"Asara kamar yaya?" anty ta jefeta da tambaya.
"Wallahi wani taimako nasoyi amman ALLAH baiba"
Bata tsaya jin ta bakin anty ba ta dora da cewa.
"Wani emergency case jiya aka kawo mana wata yarinya ce".
Kafin takai wayarta ta dauki ƙara dalilin haka yasa ta dakata da maganar tare da ɗaga wayar.
Shikam tsitt yay tare da tsayar da cup ɗin a dai dai kasan bakinsa shi bai kai bakiba kuma bai ajeba.
Yana son jin mike faruwa amman ko a fuska bazaka alamun ma yana sauraransu ba.
Harta gama wayar da mijinta idanunsa na lumshe batare daya bude ba harta dora da cewa....
"Wallahi aka kawota kamar gawa bakiganta ba anty rauni taji ajikinta wanda yay alamun kamar bindiga harfa bayanta, ni wallahi dan dai mamarta tace hatsari tai ne amman dasenaga kamar case ɗinta yay dai dai da yarinyar datazo tare Muhammad jiya,.
Amman abinda yasa ban gasgata ita ɗin bane sabida, wannan itafa har fyaɗe aka mata......"
Cikin tsoro anty ta furta "fyaɗe fa Aisha".
Wadda sautin maganar ya kusan sakawa mai girma mas! ya diro daga kan gadon dayake sabida tsabar yadda maganar ta dokesa.dan dai kawai shiɗin namijin zakine ba,a gane inda ya dosa.
Rintse idanunsa yay yana sauraran muryar aisha nurse.
"Wallahi anty jikinta duk tabon harbi ga kuma fyade seda nagama cire mata ragowar karfan daya makale sannan na lura da yarinyar tana zubar da jini, wallahi abin baxe faduba anty harfa ɗinki huɗu nai mata".
Tafaɗa muryarta na shakking.
"Wallahi anty dan dai kawai wannan sunce accident ne amman danace wadda zata baiwa muhd kariyane jiya wasu ɓata garin suka far mata bayan sunga bata motsi may be".
Wata irin ƙwarewa ce tazo masa wadda ta firgita kowa da gudu anty taje ɗan karamin furjin dake dakin ta dauko ruwa ta basa yadan kurɓa sannan sukai masa sannu ya koma kan gadon ya kwanta idanunsa na kallon sama.
Seda sukaga ya koma normal sannan aisha ta cigaba.
"Kuma anty yarinyar fa marainiyace wannan matar data kawota jiya kanwar mahaifintace wallahi daga gani basu da wani gatan kirkima, yau wai nazo da shirin kawo masu abinci na tarar da an basu sallama".......
"What!!! sukaji saukar muryarsa kamar ba shiya gama tarin ƙwarewa ba.
"Wayace abata sallama dama haka akeyi?"
Ya kai maganar cikin tuhuma jikinsa har yana rawa.
Duk yadda sukai da doctor hardo aisha ta zauna ta gaya masa sannan ta ɗora da cewa ita daman taso idan yarinyar ta warke seta haɗashi da ita tasan ze shige gaba wajan ƙwatowa yarinyar ƴancinta.
"Amman kuma naga yarinyar tanada ɗan taurin kai wallahi tun jiya mukai mukai da ita akan ta faɗi wanda yay matan amman tace baxata gane ba".
"Ya salam" ya furta akasan labban sa tare da ayyana tabbas itace.......!!!
"Ina zanganki baiwar ALLAH inne mi yafiyarki inkuma hukuntaki da abinda kika mun ba halina bane ba hakan ya salam keki ka jefamu acikin wannan ƘADDARAR"
Yakai maganar cikin ransa kafin kuma kace me idanunsa har sun kaɗa Baikuma bin takansu anty ba ya faɗa duniyar tunanin mafita.
Tabbas seya nemota adik inda take wallahi baze barta haka ba.
Sallamar imran ita ta katse masu hirar su shikuma ya karbi kayan ya wuce bathroom anan ya shirya cikin ƙananun kayan da aka kawo masan kana ya ɗan ja p/capɗ ɗin kaɗan ta yadda mutane basa ganesaba kana yay hanyar waje.
Anty aisha nurse haka ta kwashi kayanta tai waje itama anty tabi bayansu ita tai office anty kuma tai can inda imran yay parking.
Shikam harma ya bude motar ya shiga kallo guda zaka masa kasan yana cikin tentsion amman tsabar miskilanci yahana ya nuna halin dayake ciki ɗin.
Anty na shiga imran yaja motar yabar clinic ɗin cikin tukin nutsuwa suka dauki hanyar gida....

32 - 33


Sunkuyar da kansa yay ƙasa yana kallon screen ɗin tafkekiyar wayar dake hannunsa mai uban kyau da tsada,wadda ta kasance iya mutanan dake kusa dashi ne sosai suka san layin dake kanta.
Number Abokinsa dr safiyan yake dialing wanda sukai karatu tare wanda yake a matsayin babban likita a babban asibitinsa na can cikin garin ɗawisu.
Kira ɗaya daga ɓangaren safiyan aka ɗaga wayan.
Sallama dr safiyan yay masa bayan sun ɗan gaisa sannan anwarr yay masa bayanin drugs ɗin dayake bukata a kawo masa sabida yadda hannunsa ke masa wani irin zafi na azaba.
Bayan sun kammala wayar ya komar da kansa baya ya kwantar yana lumshe idanunsa yana jin jikinsa fayau.
Tafiya mai ɗan tsaho sukai zuwa cikin ɗawisu g.r.a cikin nutsuwa imran ya saita hancin motar har zuwa bakin tafkeken gate ɗin gidan nasu.
Da gudu masu gadi suka taho tare da wangale masa gate ɗin,suna masa barka da shigowa.
Seda ya gyara revers ɗinsa sannan ya buɗe motar ya fito yana kokarin zagayawa baya ya budewa mai girma mas!.
Anty ce ta bude nata side ɗin ta fito shima bai jiraba ya saka ɗayan hannun nasa mai lafiya yaja marufin motar tare da ziro da kyakykyawar farar ƙafarsa guda sannan ya fito da sauran jikin..nasa ya fito baki ɗaya.
Da sauri masu tsaron gidan sukayo wajansa cikin mamaki suka zube suna masa barka da shigowa.
Kowa yana kokarin ya nuna masa yadda yake jajanta masa halin daya faɗa ajiyan na ƴan adawar da suka kai masa farmaki wajan taron jiyan.
Cike da rashin son hayaniya yake ɗaga masu hannun yana mai wucewa cikin gidan.
Bayan ya kirawo sarkin gida ya umarcesa akan yau kada ya sake ya budewa kowa gate indai ba oska dazezo anjima ya kawo masa magunguna ba..
Sabida bayason hayaniya kuma yasan muddin jama,a suka farga daya dawo gida tofa yanzu zasu damesa musamman yan jarida wanda hakan yasa tun jiya da sukai magana da shugaban jam'iyarsu ya kashe wayarsa bai kunnata ba har yanzu.
Cikin bin umarni sarkin gida ya rissuna kana ya amsawa uban gidan nasu batare dayay musuba.
Jan ƙafafunsa yay ya nufi part ɗin maama bayan sun rabu da imran wanda yay part ɗinsa itama anty part ɗinta tai domin ta samu taɗan huta.
Cikin kamala da kamewa yake sallama acikin haɗaɗɗan falon mahaifiyar tasa wanda yaci kayan more rayuwa kamar babu gobe.
Babu abinda ke tashi acikin falon se ƙamshi turaran wuta tare da sautin karatun alkur'ani mai girma cikin muryar shaik sudais wanda yake karanta suratul baƙara, ummi mai aikin mamah wadda take tsakiyar falon tana faman gyara fulallukan dasuka faɗo ita ta ɗago ta amsa masa sallamar cikin ladabi kana ta cigaba da aikin gabanta.
Cikin dakakkiyar muryarsa ta jaruman maza ya furta.
"Ke ina maamah?"
Jikin ummi mai aikin maamah wadda mahaifiyarta ita kewa mamah girki ita kuma ummin ke gyare gyaren ɗakunan maamah mahaifinta kuma shine sarkin gida ƴan kauyene suke zaune agidan can cikin b.q kuma sun jima suna tare dasu maamah dan tun Alhaji sameer yana raye suke tare shiyasa suka saba sosai da mamah ita ta kalli anwar kana tace.
"Tana sama tare da anty sakina"
Bai tankata ba yaja kafafunsa yay hanyar step ɗin cikin sauri.
Tun daga bakin kofar glass ɗin dake saman yafara zame takalman kafafunsa sannan ya saka hannu ya bude ƙofar bakinsa dauke da sallama.
Sautin muryar sakina matar imran kaninsa ke tashi da alamun hira suke da mamah amman inda hidayace mama bazata zauna da itaba.
Da gudu hanif ya taho wajansa yana gwaranci.
"oyoyo daddy" dayake haka hanif ɗin ke ce masa.
Wara hannayensa yay tare da sakin murmushi kana yace.
"Oyoyo my boy i miss you yaron daddy" yafada yana rungume yaron sam yama manta da ciwon hannunsa.
Muryar sakina yaji tana magana.
"Wallahi hanif kada dai ka famawa dadyn naka ciwonsa kaji ko".
Shikam karasowa yay tsakiyar falon yana shafa kan hanif wanda ya lafe ajikinsa sannan ya zauna a saman kujera mai fuskantar maamah.
Ganin haka yasa sakina kama gabanta bayan ta gaishesa tai masa yaya jiki.
Tana fita yaɗan zame hanif sannan ya zube a ƙasa yana gaida maamah wadda tai kamar bataga shigowarsa ba.
Cikin alkunya ta amsa masa kamar wani na gabansu.
Sannan ta aro jarumtar yi masa yaya jikin kana taja bakinta tai gum.
Kamar ze kuka haka ya zuba mata idanu yana kallonta batare daya ce mata komai ba shima.
"Mike damunka naga jikinka yay sanyi akwai abunda yake damunka ina son ka sanar min?"
Yaji saukar muryarta kamar daga sama.
Cikin mamaki yabita da kallo inda tai gefe da kanta kamar ba ita tai maganar ba.
"Babu komai daman hannun nawa ke ciwo amman nasa akawomin magani".
Yabata amsa atakaice.
"Ba wannan ba, kana addu,a dai ko,ina son ka ƙara wata akan wadda kake kaji ko?".
Sunkuyar da kansa yay sosai.
Sannan yace.
"Naji maamah insha ALLAHU zan ƙara".
"Alhamdulillahi ina son kaje kauye wajan Malam ka ƙara basa kudi yay maka addu,a sannan akuma sauke maka alkur,ani da ni zanima amman gudun kada na tafi kuma  haihuwar sakina tazo yasa banje ba tunda ita antynku abubuwa sun mata yawa".
Cikin mamaki yabita da kallo yanzu sakina matar imran harta samu cikin daya isa haihuwa amman bai kulaba kuma babu wanda ya gaya masa sabida ya lura yanzu ba,a son gaya masa maganar haihuwar wani kamar ana gudun kada ya shiga damuwa, aishi ya yarda da ALLAH yasan babu mai bayarwa seshi kuma babu mai hanawa seshi.
Amman bai maganaba ya sada kansa kawai yana cije bakinsa.
Sannan yace.
"Insha ALLAH dana samu lokaci zanje ".
Kallonsa tai sosai batare data cire idanunta akansa ba se asannan naga kamanninsu ya kuma fita sosai.
"Ka gayamin ɗayan maganar".
Yaji saukar muryarta a dodon kunnensa.
Ɗago kansa yay da mamaki
"Maamah mekenan"
Yafadi maganar a rarrabe.
"Tun jiya jikina ya bani akwai abinda ya sameka wanda ya taɓaka bayan wannan harbin wanda har cikin baccina najishi wanda ya hanani na rintsa idanuna ajiyan ka daure ka sanar dani meke faruwa dakai hakika banida ilimin sanin gaibu amman jikina ya bani akwai ƁOYAYYEN SIRRI atare dakai".
Takai maganar idanunta ƙyam akansa.
Daburcewa yay sosai alamun rashin dauriya suka bayyanar masa lallai ya yarda idan aka ce maka mahaifi kat!! kenan.
Ɗago kansa yay a raunane.
Tare da ɗaga hanif ajikinsa wanda ya soma barci.
Ajesa yay akan tumtum ya kwantar sannan yaja kafafunsa daga zaunen ya isa gabanta.
Ya saka hannunsa guda biyun ya riko nata.
Awannan karon ya kasa daurewa.
"Maamah tun jiya ina cikin wani irin hali marar misaltuwa, amman kafin na furta komai nafara duba kai nane shin ko akwai wani laifi danai ne wanda har ALLAH ya jarabceni da shiga wannan masifar, na duba amman iya hangena na kasa tunawa amman nina san ni ɗan adam ne har abada bazan taɓa fita daga siraɗin jarrabawar mahaliccina ba wadda harse na koma garesane zan dena amsa,shiyasa a kullum nake addu,ar ALLAH ya sadani da farar ƙaddara ya rabani da mummuna".
Ɗan numfasawa yay kaɗan ayayin da falon  maamah ya kuma ɗaukar tsit se karar na'urar sanyaya ɗaki kaɗai ke fita.
Sannan ya cigaba.
"Maamah kitayani addu,a ina buƙatar ƙarinta daga bakinki mai kaifi mai kuma falala daga gareni ma'ana bakin  mahaifiya, maamah nafaɗa cikin masifa amman wallahi maamah wannan ba halin ANWARR ɗinki bane kafin ki dauki mataki akaina inason ki fara duba tarbiyar da kuka ginamu akanta wadda har muma muka ɗora wasu akanta wallahi! tallahi!! na rantse da ALLAH maamah sharrin shaiɗanne........tiryan tiryan".
Yagama bata labarin abinda ya faru jiya shida nafisa idanunsa yayi wani irin kaɗawa.
Hawaye ke ɗiga daga saman kamilalliyar fuskar maamah wadda ta cika da kamala da haiba gami da tsantsar addini wanda hatta saman goshinta ya rine da baƙin tabon sallah.
"Alhamdulillahi Rabbil'Alamin".
Maamah ta furta a bayyane kana ta sunkuyar da kanta a ƙasa harna tsahon lokaci.
Sannan ta kuma furta kalmar.
"Astagfirullaha wa'atubu ilaikh".
Kana ta ɗago idanunta ta zuba masa.
"Ka kyauta ALLAH ya shirya mun kai yakuma shirya dukkan musulmi baki ɗaya,kakuma kyautawa ɗiyar mutane wadda ta saida rayuwarta akanka, ya ilahi ina zanga wannan yarinya?" ta faɗa tana kuma zubar da hawayen tausayin nafisa akan fuskarta.
"Maamah" tai saurin ɗaga hannunta alamun dakatar dashi.
"Bazan goyi bayan zalunci ba, sabida ka zalunci ɗiyar mutane,amman kuma zan yarda da ƙaddarar data sameka wanda nasan wannan ƙaddarace wallahi nasan wana haifa tabbas wannan wani alamarine daga ALLAH fatana ALLAH yasa hakan yazama alkairi gareka ALLAH kuma ya sassauta maka wannan lamari ya mayar dashi sanyi, amman tabbas ina son kanemi wannan yarinya danta yafe maka wannan hakkin nata daka ɗauka idan san samu nema ka kawomin ita".
"ALLAH ya yafe maka tare damu baki ɗaya seka dage da addu,a tare da kuma tunkarar mulkinka da gaskiya da amana dan wannama kaɗai wata ishararce ta sauka gareka wadda ALLAH ya nuna maka ita ALLAH ya rufa asiri zanta addu,a Har ALLAH ya kawo mana mafita acikin wannan lamari, wanda nakeson na horeka da ko matarka kada ka yarda taji hakan muyita addu,a dagani harkai muga mai ALLAH zeyi".
Tafaɗa tana share hawayenta.
"Nagode maamah kuma insha ALLAH zanyi dukkan abinda kika ce ALLAH ya yafe mana baki ɗaya".
Ya faɗa bayan ya miƙe yana mata sallama.
Tunda yake baita ɓa zama sunyi magana kamar wannan da itaba sabida bata yarda.
Cikin tausayi ta dubesa.
"Kajirani"
Tashi tai ta nufi cikin ɗakinta.
Minti kaɗan ta dawo da wani ƙaton galan a hannunta ta mika masa.
"Kadinga sha kafin kaci komai safe da dare, ruwan tofine jiya na zauna nai maka ALLAH ya ƙara tsarewa".
Tafaɗa cikin alkunya.
Godiya yay mata sannan ya saka hannu ya amsa yay hanyar fita.
Da kallon tausayi ta bisa sannan ta koma ta zauna ta rafka tagumi.
Shikam hanyar bangarensa yay hannunsa guda dauke da galan ɗin.......!!!!

34 - 35


Seda ya aje galan ɗin akan wani ɗan top sannan ya saka ɗayan hannunsa,mai lafiya ya murɗa handle ɗin  Ƙofar dazata sadaka da babban falon ɓangaren nasa na ƙasa.
Ɗaukar galan, ɗin yay tare da shiga cikin falon wanda kamshi da wani irin sassanyan ƙamshi ke tashi acikinsa, babu kowa afalon dan haka kansa tsaye ya nufi kwanar data karya wadda corridow ya rabata da falon.
Wata ƴar kwana ya shiga kaɗan wadda wani irin azababben step ɗin bene mai ɗan karan kyau, yabi har hanyar saman direct.
Da ƙyar yake taka step ɗin cike da gajiya haka ya ƙarasa hawa saman benen inda ya shiga cikin wani falon wanda yafi na ƙasan tsaruwa da haɗuwa gami da kayan more rayuwa.
Nufar gaban wani ɗan lungu yay wanda ya raba wasu ƙofofin bedroom guda biyu, wadda tafi sheƙin naga ya tura tare da shigar da jikinsa cikin wani haɗaɗɗen bedroom mai mugun kyau wanda yaji kayan saitin gadon royal komai fari tass, babu wani tarkace a ɗakin daga, gadon se ƙatuwar wadrov se babban dressing mirrow, gefe guda wani table ne wanda yake manne da farar sopa.
Se gaba kaɗan wani ma dai dai cin table ɗinne wanda aka aje tarkancen system akai.
Gefe guda ya aje galan ɗin tare da faɗawa kan sopa ya kwanta yana lumshe idanunsa alamun gajiya.
Sannan kuma yaɗan ɗago hannunsa mai ciwon, yafara warware farar audugar da aka naɗe masa dashi.
Ɗan rintse idanunsa yay da alamun hannun nasa nai masa zafine sannan naga ya miƙe ya nufi gaban wadrov ɗin,tare da buɗe wata ƴar loka ya ɗauko wani ɗan akwati,  tare da komawa kan sopar ya zauna.
Yana kici-kicin budewa hidaya ta turo ƙofar ɗakin ta shigo.
Hannunta riƙe da wani ɗan madai-daicin kwando, cikin nutsuwa ta ƙaraso gabansa bayan ta aje kwandon ta tsugunna agabansa.
"Ranka ya daɗe barka da shigowa yaya jikin naka ALLAH ya kiyaye gaba".
Tafaɗa face ɗinta yay kalar tausayi.
Cikin mamakin hali irin nata yake dubanta ta ƙasan, idanunsa.
"Bakiyi fushiba madam?"
Ya jefa mata tambayar sam ita bata san wani abu waishi fushi ko hayaniya ba, wannan dalilin yasa yake sara mata yadda batai tunani ba.
"Haba yallaɓai akan mi zanyi fushi da mijina dan kawai kace na zauna agida sena hau fushi dan dai fushin bashida wahala".
Takai maganar tana sinne kanta a ƙasa.
Murmushi yayi cikin jin daɗi gami da godewa ALLAH akan kyautar daya basa na mace kamar hidaya wadda ta tara abubuwan burgewa na bam mamaki.
Sannan ya saka hannunsa mai lafiyar, cike da soyayyarta, ya ɗagota zuwa jikinsa yanajin raguwar damuwar dayake ciki ta ragu fiye da yadda ya shigo da ita.
Agefensa yay mata masauki tare da ɗan zame mata kallabin kanta yana shafa kanta da tafin hannunshi.
"Kabari kaga ba lafiya garekaba, kawo na taimaka maka".
Tafaɗa tana ɗan zamewa daga jikinsa, tana kokarin karɓar ɗan akwatin dake gaban nasa.
Karɓa tai tare da budesa ta ɗauko auduga wadda taketa ɗaukar idanu dan haske.
Tafara goge masa ɗan jinin daya tsatstsafo,daga ciwon nasa kana ta naɗe masa.
Cikin nutsuwa ta sauka daga kan jikin nasa tare da adana,akwatin a inda yake sannan ta kuma zuwa ta janyo kwandon gabanta.
Ɗan madai-daicin tea flaks ta ɗauko daga cikin kwandon sannan, ta bude ta zuba ruwan coffee ɗin dayace ta haɗa, masa acikin wani ɗan cup mai kyau, sannan ta saka teaspoon tare da miƙa masa.
Da hannunta ya hade yana kokarin tallafo ƙwanƙwasonta, cikin sauri ta zille tare da mikewa.
"Bara naje na duba girki kada ya kone".
Tafaɗa tana dariya.
Ɗan tura baki yay kamar wani ƙaramin yaro sannan ya kauda kansa gefe kamar wanda yay fushi.
Cikin salo ta kawo kanta dai dai saitin face ɗinsa, ta tallafo face ɗinsa da hannunta tare da fito da harshenta taɗan lashi bakinsa kaɗan taja gemunsa sannan ta ruga aguje tana dariya.
Wata irin miƙa yay tare da komawa kan sopa ya lumshe yana jinsa awani irin mood kana ya tallafi kansa da hannunsa ya faɗa duniyar tunani, batare daya kula cup ɗin coffeen daya aje agefensa ba.

Cikin ɗan jin zafi kaɗan ta tube rigar jikinta tai saura daga ita se pant, ahaka tai gaban siff ɗin ummah sahura ta kaya, ta dauki zani ta daura sannan ta fito tsakar gidan sanin  ummah sahura na dakin baba mijinta yasa ta karasa kicin.
Ruwa mai ɗumi ta ɗiba tare da dauka tai cikin bayi.
Sosai tai kamar yadda ummah tai mata jiya, ma,ana ta gasa kanta dai dai yadda zata iya.
Tanayi tana cije bakinta sabida zafin da gaban nata ke mata harta kammala ta ɗauro alwala tai ɗaki.
Tana yin sallah ta haye gado ta kwanta da dabara saboda karta fama hannunta da bayanta.
Babu jimawa bacci mai nauyi yay awon gaba, da ita.
Koda ummah sahura tazo ganin tai bacci yasa,ta aje mata tsiren da baba ya siyo yace ta kawo mata.
Sannan ta ƙarasa ta rufe mata jikinta da bargo kana ta kashe mata ƙwan ɗakin tafita.
Bacci sosai nafeesah ta samu wanda bata farkaba se asubahi.
Koda ta miƙe  ruwan ta kuma ɗiba ta kuma gasa kanta😛( su nafi anji maza  ).
Sannan ta ɗauro alwala ta fito waje tare da nufar ɗaki.
Sallah ta tayar bayan ta idar tai azkar, sannan ta fita zuwa ɗakin baba.
