38 - 39

Ɓangarenta.


Ɓangaren nafisa kuwa baki ɗaya ta koma wata irin shuru-shuru fiyema da,da. bata fita ko ina daga tsakar gida se ɗaki tana karatun alkur'ani tare da zikirin ubangiji wannan lamarin daya faru da ita bakaramin firgita ta, yay ba sam bata doguwar magana idan ka dubeta da kyau kusan kullum bakinta motsi yake na alamun zikirin data duƙufayi tare da neman saukin ALLAH akan ciwon soyayyar anwar wanda babu laifi yanzu taɗan soma sakin jiki tafara mantawa dashi.
Sosai ta gane wautar datai na zuwa gidansa.
Wanda bata ko son tuna wannan mummunar ranar agareta.
Kwanci tashi kuma babu wuya a wajan ALLAH yanzun haka hutunsu ma ya kusan ƙarewa.
Kuma har yanzu bataje gidan kaka ba sannan kuma bata koma asibitin ba agida take zaune tana gyara jikinta yadda ya kamata kuma ALLAH ya taimaketa bata da ƙan jiki sosai wajan ya samu warkewa tafiyarta ta koma normal jikinta ma kuma haka.
Yau sun wuni da walida har dare sannan ya nura yazo ya dauki walida suka nufi gida.
Da mamakin nafisa walida ta kwan aranta ganin fafur taki yarda ay mata hiran mai girma MAS!.
Itakam nafisa walida na tafiya taja tsaki tare da komawa ɗaki ta kwanta tana jan numfashi sam bata ko son a faɗi sunansa agabanta sabida, yadda takejin wani irin soyayyansa gami da haushinsa sun cika mata zuciyanta.
Yauma da daddare ummah sahura na zaune a tsakar gida, tana sauraran redio itakam nafisa tana gefe ɗaya tana jin yadda gefen maranta yake yimata ciwo.
Ta fuskanci wannan watan bataga al'adanta ba amman bata damu ba sabida a farkon wata takeyinsa kuma gashi yanzun har wannan watan yazo tsakiya.
Amman shuru bataga komi ba, sema tsabar wani uban ciwon mara daya sakota agaba wanda baya tashi se cikin dare.
Umma sahura ke mata magana, amman sam batama jitaba.
muryan ummah sahura ne ya ƙara fita.
"Nace nafisa ko ina zamuga aisha likita oho yarinya mai mutunci, saurari kiji yadda wannan yaron arzikin yakaiwa gidan marayu tallafi, tace ɗan yayarta ne ai".
Basarwa tayi ta nuna kamar bataji mai umma take faɗi ba tare da ɗan kallonta kaɗan domin ta ɓatar da maganar.
"Umma yaufa sauran sati ɗaya mu koma makaranta, kuma naga har yanzu kaka batazo cigiyata ba kodai haushi taji".
Ɗan numfasawa umma sahura tai kana ta ce.
"A,a nikam bana tunanin haka sedai ko da malam yaje ( mijinta ) yay bata bayanin tanan zaki koma makaranta inaga shine yasa bata nemekiba, gobema nakeson kije can cikin babbar kasuwa kisiyo kayan abincinki amman inaga sema kinje babban super market ɗin dake sabon garin ɗawisu sabida kayansu sunfi kyau".
Tafaɗa tana kallon nafisa.
"Tom ALLAH ya kaimu bara naje na kwanta umma bacci nakeji".
Da kallo ta bita sannan tace.
"Nafisa zonan," komowa nafisan tai ta kara zama.
Da kallo umma sahura ta bita.
"Meyene naga kina cije baki bakijin dadine?"
Tafaɗa mata hakan cikin kulawa.
"Wallahi ni narasa miyasa mara takemin ciwo watarana har daurewa take kuma se dare yayi takemin haka".
Tafadi hakan kamar xata saka kuka.
"Yaushe zaki fara al'ada, naga rabonki dayi tun wancan watan ai?".
Ta jefa mata tambayar.
"Eh daman yana min haka se ƙarshen watan nan inaga tunda baizo a farko ba".
Tafaɗawa ummah sahura hakan cike da gamsuwa.
"Daman yana maki haka?"
"Eh mana sosai ma ko kin manta bashine wata shekara dana taɓa zuwar miki hutuba yay min haka".
"Eh kam hakane tashi kije seda safan".
Tagaya mata hakan tare da gamsuwa da abinda ta gaya matan.
Daman kuma wasu matan yanayin jininsu yana masu haka, yana ɗan rikita daga kwanakinsa.
Tana shiga ɗaki ta hau gado ta kwanta.
Ananfa ciwo yace salamu alaikum. kuka sosai take tana dafe mararta.
Ta lura sam yanzu bata wani baccin kirki sabida wannan ciwon daya sakata agaba.
Yau kimanin wata ɗaya da faruwar abinda ya sameta kuma batafi kwana biyar da dawowa daga asibitiba wannan ciwon ya sakota agaba wanda tagaza gayawa kowa illa taci kukanta da daddare da safe kuma kaga idanunta yay fam.
Da ƙyar tai sallar asubahi sannan ta koma bacci ta dunkule wuri guda tana huci sabida zazzafan zazzaɓin daya rufeta ruff.
Har wajan karfe tara tana kwance,seda ummah sahura ta gaji sannan ta shigo ɗakin ganinta a kwance yasa ta karasa gabanta ta janye mayafin data rufa dashi.
"Ke nafisa yaya kiketa baccine hala kina lafiya yau kuwa?".
Ko motsi batai ba ganin haka yasa ta barta tare da kara gyara mata mayafin ita kuma ta futa waje tacigaba da aikinta na gida.
Bata farkaba se wajan la,asar, aguje ta mike ganin lokacin sallah ya wuce, fita tsakar gidan tai ta zubda miyon daya taru abakinta sannan ta dibi ruwa tai alwala.
Takoma daka tai sallah.
Sannan ta fito.
Kicin taje ta dauki kofi ta zubo ruwan shayi tare da lemon tsami ta zauna akan turmi, tana sha hartana lumshe idanunta.
Ahaka umma tazo ta isketa wadda itama sallah tai a daki.
Tsugunnawa tai har kasa ta gaisar da ita sannan ta cigaba da shan shayin nata.
"Nafisa ga abincinki can akan tebur kiɗibi yaji yana cikin tasa ni zan fita idan kin gama ki dauki kuɗin aiken akan madubi seki rufe gidan natafi da makullin".
"To adawo lafiya".
Tafaɗa cikin kasa da murya.
Ɗan dakatawa umma tai tana kallonta.
"Waini yanaga jikinki yay sanyine ko bakya jin dadine? ɗazuma naje na tadda kinata bacci bakima san nazoba, idan wani abunne ki gayamin bana son wannan yawan shurun naki?".
Ɗan rausayar da kanta tai tare da furta.
"Wallahi babu komai umma kawai bacci na tarane ɗazu kuma na rama".
Dawowa tai gabanta ta kalleta.
"Wani irin ramuwar bacci kuma bakya baccine?".
Tajefa mata tambayar.
"Eh wallahi ni kusan kullum da ciwon mara nake kwana shiyasa ban fiye yin bacciba".
Cikin tausayi umma sahura ta bita da kallo sannan tace.
"Sannu bara malam ya dawo se yay maki rubutu".
Sunkuyar da kanta tai tare da yiwa umma sahura godiya sannan, umma tasaka kai tabar gidan.
Itakam tana gama shan shayin ta wanke kofin sannan ta ɗan ɗebo shinkafar ta zauna anan tanaci vatare data zuba yajin ba sabida bakinta kamar ya ɗaɗe da ƙuraje.
Lomar farko takai tana yatsina fuskarta ata biyu, kuwa aguje tai bakin rariya seda ta amayar da ita tass sannan taja ta kwanta a gefe tana mayar da numfashi.
Ta dade awajan ahaka kafin ta ɗauraye wajan ta mayar da kwanan kicin tare da daukar lemon tsami ta saka abakinta, jin kamar miyon ze dawo mata.
Agurguje ta shirya kanta cikin riga da skirt na atamfa sannan ta dauki mayafi baki mai ɗan shara-shara ta yafa akan atamfar ko kwalli bata saka ba haka, ta kammala shirin nata sannan ta shiga dakin umma ta dauki kuɗin naira dubu biyar ta saka acikin ƴar ƙaramar jaka, baka.
Sannan ta zira ta kalminta marar tsini ta nufi hanyar zaure.
Seda ta karanta addu,ar fita daga gida sannan taja kofar ta fita bakin titi.
Kusan mintuna goma tana faman jiran abin hawa kafin ALLAH ya kawo mai mashin, ta tara tahau bayan ta gaya masa inda zataje.
Tafiyar kusan mintuna 20 sukai sannan ta sauka abakin kasuwa, bayan ta basa kuɗinsa ta shiga cikin kasuwar.
Kwaki ta fara saya da sauran kayan ƴan makarantar kwana sannan ta fito hannunta da kaya niki niki.
Abin hawa ta kuma tara tare da gaya masa, Ɗawisu super market,babu musu ya yarda ta hau suka wuce.
Nanma abakin katon super market ɗin yaja ya tsaya bayan ta basa kudinsa taja kayanta tana mayar da numfashi ta nufi wajan.
Abakin gate din ta tsaya ganin masu tsaron wajan suna kaiwa da komowa daman ita ba wani abunne ya kawota ba illa siyan su madara da sauransu saboda wajan yana da sauki duk da, kasancewar wajan na manyane amman kayan wajan sunfi na ko ina sauki da rahusa tare da kyau da inganci, dan ance turawane ma ke bada kayan wajan.
Ganin tana faman baza idanu kamar marar gaskiya, yasa wani daga cikin securty's ɗin wajan ya ƙaraso gabanta.
"Ƴammatah yaya naga kina tsaye anan kamar kin yada wani abun?".
Ɗan tsaki taja kaɗan tana masa kallon sama da ƙasa, batare data tankasaba.
"Hellow!!" yakuma gaya mata akaro na biyu.
Awannan karon ta ɗago manyan idanunta waɗanda sukai wani irin haske gami da girma kaɗan ta dubesa dasu, kana ta furta.
"Nazo yin provision na school ne kuma senaga kamar ba,a barin shiga wajan".
Takai maganar tana aje,ledar kwakin hannunta cike da gajiya.
Kallon tausayi ya bita dashi sannan yace.
"Eh gaskiya kam bama barin shiga wajan ne sabida tsaron mai gida, daya zo shida families ɗinsa suna shopping dan da baki ɗayama zamu fitar da mutanan, ciki amman sabida tausayi irin nasa yace mubarsu shine, muka bar na ciki amman bama barin kowa ya shiga".
Yakai maganar yana kallonta.
Ɗan jimm tai sannan ta dubesa.
"Tom nagode bara na koma na siya a anguwarmu".
Ganin da ragowar zafin rana agari gashi kuma yarinyar da alamun tana da hankali yasa yaji tabasa tausayi seya dubeta.
"Kawo kayan naki semu jera ciki nai kamar tare muke sekije ki saya ko?".
Ɗan jimm ta kumayi akaro  na biyu seda tai tunani ganin sunan wajan da kuma jami,an dake zagaye da wajan yasa ta amince masa tabi bayansa bayan, ƙara gyara mayafinta sosai.
Shikam ledar kayan tarkacen kwakinta da sauran kayan data siyo ya dauka, bayan ya sakale ƴar bindigarsa a gefe suka jera kamar sun san juna, yana janta da hira itakuwa jikinta banda tsuma babu abinda yake, sakamakon yadda taga securty's ɗin wurin suna zagaye tare da bindiga suna faman zare jajayen idanunsu zat rashin mutunci.
Baƙaramin mamaki bane ya kamata, ganin yadda taga wasu mahaukatan manyan tsala tsalan motoci fake acan gefen wajan , waɗanda idan ba idanunta kemata gizoba tabbas, wasu ƙartin guards tagani suna zagaye wajan motocin.
Sake jin gabanta tai yay wata irin faɗuwa wanda batama ji me wannan securtyn ke gaya mataba.
"Ƴammata idan kin fito ki sameni anan, ki amshi kayan ki, tacan kofar zakibi".
Amsa masa tai sannan tai masa godiya kana tabi ta inda yace mata ɗin.
Bakaramun kauyanci tai ba awajan dan seda ta tsayama tana karewa wasu turawa kallo waɗanda suke kan wasu kujeru suna buga game.
Sannan ta isa gaban kofar ta danna wajan, kana ta kutsa kanta babban wajan wanda seda tai danasanin shigowa sabida tasan batada kuɗin siyayya awajan.
Anyama kuwa nan umma ta gaya mata gaskiya ba nan bane dan wancan ance bashida wani girma sedai ko maybe mistake na suna tayi.
Jin wani irin sanyin ac ya ratsata yasa ta saki kofar wadda ta koma ta rufe ruff sannan ta shiga ciki tana faman baza idanunta a waje.
Gaban saitin wasu mayuka ta nufa tare da kura masu idanu, tundaga nan ta soma raina kanta sabida baki ɗayama kayan wajan babu na kuɗinta, saboda ita abinda ya rage a jakarta 3500 ne baki ɗaya.
Tsiru tai da idanunta tana neman ta faki idanunta tabar wajan ta samu ta fita tin kafin kunya ta kasheta.
Tafe take kanta ƙasa tana faman danne ƴar jakarta sam bata san tai karo da mutum ba seda ta ɗago kanta.
Sannan tai maza ta sunkuya ta tayata kwasar kayan da suka zube na cikin kwandon.
Seda suka kwashe tass.
Sannan ta ɗago ta dubi matar datake gaban nata wadda ta bigewa kayan hannun nata.
Farace ƴar gayu mai kyau.
Cikin karkarwa tace.
"Anty dan ALLAH kiyi hakuri ban kula ba".
Da kallo wadda aka kira antyn ta bita batare datace mata komai ba ta juya tana kokarin barin wajan bayan ta ɗan jefeta da kallon raini kaɗan.
Da mamaki nafisa ta juya baya zata bar wajan tana mamakin wannan lamari watakan wasu mutanan ma da kallo kaɗai suke hukunta mutum koda basuyi magana ba.
Ganin wata yar farar budurwa wadda taci kwalliya tare da ƙaton mayafi ajikinta tana nufo wannan wadda ta buge ɗin yasa nafisa ta bata waje tare da kokarin tama bar wajan baki ɗaya ganin wannan budurwar, tana son ta bigeta bata saniba.
Saukar muryar budurwar nafisa taji cikin kunnenta.
"Anty Hidaya kizo ga cream ɗin da kike nema canfa".
Ɗan waigawa nafisa tai ashe wannan wadda taɗan buge ɗin itace anty hidayan, kafin ma taja kofar tabar cikin wajan saboda hartaje kofa taji saukar muryar wannan matar.
"Are you serious shaheedah" bata tsaya jin mai budurwar zata faɗa ba ALLAH ya taimaketa tabar wajan bayan tasha gwagwarmayar danna abun bude kofar da ƙyar.
Ganin kamar kofar data biyo akwai yawan masu tsaro yasa tabi tawani dogon hanya wanda yabi ya mike yay samɓal.
Babu yawan mutane wajan illah yalwar bishuyu gami, da tsirarun securty's waɗanda suke kaiwa da komowa,.
Cikin tsoro tare da fargaba take tafiya gakuma ilahirin jikinta yana tsuma, gefe guda wata mahaukaciyar faduwar gabace ta rusketa.
Jan kafarta kawai take tana ALLAH ALLAH tabar wajanma baki ɗaya.
Adai dai saitin wasu manyan bishuyu taja ta tsaya tana kallon wajan tana kuma danasanin bin hanyar sabida tama manta inda zataga wannan na ɗaxun ta karbi kwakinta da ragowar ƴan kayanta.
Wata ƙwallace ta zo mata bayan ta saka mayafi ta share sannan tacigaba dabin hanyar.
Tunda ta biyo ta bakin wata ƙofa mai ƴar raga taji gabanta ya cigaba da faduwar fiye dana baya.
Kirjinta har wani dokawa yake kamar ze fito.
Ambaton sunan ubangiji ta somayi cikin zuciyarta.
Adai dai wani katon fili wanda  zakabi ta bayan wata baƙar mota ka fita hanyar taji wani mahaukacin mugun daddaɗan ƙamshin mayen turare wanda ya saka seda ta dakata ta shaƙi ƙamshin sosai sannan tafara cigaba da tafiyar nata.
Jan burki tai ganin wani mutum tsaye a saitin kofar hannunsa wanda ya cika da gargasa rike da waya mai uban tsada yana faman magana wadda bazakaji miyake fadiba.
Sosai gabanta ya fadi tana tuna inda tasan wannan kamshin.
Tare kuma da kurawa bayan nasa idanu wanda yake sanye cikin wani mayen bakin boyel mai uban sheki kwantacce wanda daga kallo guda zakasan anci uwar nera a siyansa.

Raɓewa tai kaɗan tana kokarin kare kanta daga kallonsa tana son tabar wajan.
Adai dai saitin kofar karaf kamar ance ta waiga sukai idanu huɗu dashi.
"Wayyo ummah sahura" tafaɗa bayan ta tattare skirt ɗin jikinta tana kokarin guduwa.
Bakaramin tsoro firgici gami da tsantsar kyau yay mataba.
Ganinshi tai tamkar jinin larabawa sabida tsantsar kyawun sura dana xati da ALLAH yay masa.


Shikam kamar star ya tsaya yana bin yarinyar dake ɗan gabansa da kallo wadda ko a ina ya ganta baze gaza ganetaba.
Bakinsa ya gasa furta komai illah nunin dayake mata da hannunsa wanda yaji uban manyan zobunan azurfa masu uban sheki da kyau.
Jan ƙafafunshi yake yana kuma kokarin saita tsinin mashin kaifin idanunsa acikin nata wadda ita kuma jikinta yake faman karkarwa yana rawa kamar mazari wadda take kokarin sakin fitsari.
Yana zuwa gab da ita  yay saurin saka hannunsa ya damƙi tsintsiyar hannunta da ƙarfi wanda seda hannun nata ya amsa dan tsabar yadda ya damƙar mata shi.
Itakuwa tsugunnawa tai awajan daɓar tana sakin numfashi, tare da sakin kuka!.
Tsintar muryarta yay mai kama data yara dan tsabar sirarantar ta.
"Malam ka sakeni kona maka ihun kwarto"!!!
Tsananin mamakin furucinta, yasa ya sakar mata hannun nata gami da biyota kasan shima ya tsugunna agabanta irin tsaiwar yan ƙwallon ƙafar nan.
Yana tsirata da kaifafan idanunsa yana binta dasu yakuma gaza furta kalmar komai agareta.
Cikin dabara take kara tattare skirt ɗinta tana kuma ja baya batare data bari ya ankara da hakan ba.
Allah kuma ya bata sa,a tai wata irin hantsilawa wadda tai matukar basa mamaki..
Cikin zafin nama tai tsalle ta fice daga kofar.
Shikam cikin tsananin tashin hankali yay wani irin juyi, yabita yana faman yiwa guards ɗinsa magana alamun su bita.
Yarinya kamar aljana, kafin suyi wani yunkuri nafisa harta sami nasarar barin wajan baki ɗaya.
Ganin kamar hankali ya fara yowa kanta yasa tabi bayan wani ɗan tudu ta boye tana share hawayenta tana, kuma saita nutsuwarta jin kirjinta yana dokawa kamar ze ɓallo.
Sunkuyawa yay ya dauki bakar ƙaramar jakar dake gabansa.
Wadda yake tunanin ta,tace.
Cikin alamun ɓoye damuwarsa yake kallon ɗaya daga cikin securtyn waɗanda su suke tunanin kamar sata tayine awajan.
"Mai gida wannan yarinyarfa inaga ta ƙware a satar nan dan wallahi har waje mun duba, bamu gantaba,".
Yana kaiwa nan yay kasa da kansa cikin ladabi yana kallonsa.
Sega wanda ta bawa ajiyar kwakinta ya karaso yana huci.
Cikin ladabi ya dubi, na kusa dashi.
"Oga yarinyar nan kukace barauniya ce aini na shigo da ita, dan ga kayanta can ma ta bani ajiya garin kwaki ne da ƴan tarkacan sabulai, amman batai kama da ɓarauniya ba sabida naga nutsuwanta".
Jin zasu ɓata masa rai yasa yabar wajan batare daya tankawa kowansu ba.
Cikin motar gabansa ya nufa nan da nan guard ɗinshi ya bude masa ita ya shiga ya zauna.
Dafe kansa yay yanajin ninkin tausayin yarinyar ya kamasa.
Acikin zuciyarsa yake jimamin yadda ta saka gudu a yayin datai idanu biyu dasu, wanda da alamun kamar bata son ganin sanne ma baki ɗaya.
"Malam kasakarmun hannuna ko nai maka ihun kwarto"!
Yake nanata maganar tata cikin ranshi, tare da bude yar jakar dake gabansa.
Kirjinsane ya buga.
Ganin ƴan ɗari biyar-biyar  na dubu uku da dari biyar acikinta.
Se ƴar guntuwar takarda an saka.
_Kwaki da madara da sabulu da omo da carbin da comflaks da milo da pad_ da dai sauran tarkacen kayan masurufi acikin takardar.
Nan da nan wani mugun tausayi ya kamasa tabbas tana bukatar taimako.
"Ina zan ganki?"
Kalmar daya furta kenan tare da maida kansa saman seat ɗin kujerar dayake kai, yana jin zuciyarsa na zafi akan furucin da akai mata.
"Ɓarauniya ce" wanda tsabar takaici ya hanasa tanka masu komai.
Yafi gaban mintuna goma  haka yana takaicin sakacin dayay na barinta ta kufce masa.
Har hidaya ta shigo motar oska ya riko mata siyayyar tata haka suka motocinsu suka bar wajan.
Kumashi tunda ya kifa kansa bai ɗagoba har suka isa gida.


Aɓangaren nafisa.

Tana ganin waɗan nan guards ɗin sun gama dube-dubensu sun koma ciki taja jikinta cike da tashin hankali tabar wajan.
Saboda tsabar kiɗima batama san ta yada jakartaba seda sukai nisa akan mashin ɗin sannan ta lura.
Kukane ya kufce mata.
Kifa kanta tai tana kuka, ahaka tai masa kwatancen gidansu walida.
Abakin gate ta dakatar da mai mashin ɗin ta shiga gidan, a tsakar gida ta tarar da hajiya maman walida tana bawa masu aikin gidan umarni.
Adaddafe suka gaisa sannan hajiya ta ɗaga murya ta kira walidan.
Bata tsaya jan hiraba ta amshi kudin mai mashin ɗin takai masa sannan suka shiga cikin katafaren dakin walida wanda yaji kayan alatun rayuwa kala kala.
Sosai ta saka walida agaba tana mata kuka.
"Walida wannan mutumun kasheni zeyi".
Cikin kidima walida ta furta "waye hakan nan kawata?"
"Ɗan majalissarku walida anan ta zayyanewa walida komai amman ta saya mata rike mata hannun dayay tadai nuna mata cewar ita ganinsa datai yasaka ta gigita harta yada poss ɗinta.
Dariya walida tai.
"Hmm da har kina cewa bakya sonsa, kintaba ganin daman wanda yace so ya dawo yace ƙi? sedai a ɓacin rai, ahaka kike kinsa haryasa kika gigita kika kasa siyayya har kika yada kudi, ALLAH yasoki yarinya yaya nura daman jiya ya bani kudi yace na baki, seki mike na tambayi hajiya muje muyi siyayyar".
Godiya tahau yi mata bayan sun fita sunwa hajiya sallama.
Seda suka futa zuwa waccan super market ɗin sannan nafisa ta gane, cewar ashe mistake tai ba ɗayar zatajeba wannan umma take gaya mata.
Sosai sukai siyayyar sannan suka fito dan harma kudin yay ragowa.
Mai napep suka tara ya sauke walida agida ita kuma ya nufi gida da ita.....!


*AUTAR MANYA*
40 - 41


Bata shiga gida, ba se goshin magariba, seda ta shigar da dukkan siyayyar datayi cikin zaure, sannan ta koma ta miƙawa mai napep ɗin kuɗinsa sannan ta soma shigar da kayan da ƙyar.
Abakin rijiya ta tarar da umma tana kokarin zira guga zata ɗebo ruwa, seda ta kammala jera kayan a tsakar ɗakin sannan ta fito waje, ta ƙarasa gaban umma wadda tama dibi ruwan tana alwala, sannu da zuwan fitar datayi tai mata sannan itama ta ɗibi ruwan tai alwala tabi umman ɗaki.
Atare suka tada sallah, bayan sun idar umma sahura ta dubeta.
"Nafisa amman kin zauna kodai lafiya?" ɗan muskutawa tai sannan ta dubi umma sahura "wallahi umma wajan ne akwai cikowa seda na jira sannan na samu na kammala komai". takai maganar tana ɗan jan numfashinta! kaɗan.
Da kallo umma sahura ta bita cike da gamsuwa kana tace.
"Miƙe kije kicin ki ɗauki abinci".waro idanunta tai waje "lah wai harkin gama abinci?".dariya umma tai sannan tace "aikinsan bazan tsaya jiranki ba na riga dana gama tuwone tun ɗazuma dana shigo". mikewa tai  cikin zumuɗi har jikinta  yana rawa sabida rabonta da abinci tun wannan ruwan shayin data sha ɗazu daman kuma aƙasan zuciyarta maitar tuwon takeyi.
Kicin taje ta zubo tuwon kana ta bude marufin miyar zata ɗiba, ƙamshin daddawar dataji, shine ya bugar mata hanci da sauri ta maida ta rufe tare da  ɗibar man gyaɗa kaɗan daɗan maggi ta fita, batai wajan ummaba ɗakin dayake matsayin nata ta nufa.
Zama tai abakin gadon tare da ɗora kwanon a tsakiyar cinyarta,
Tana cin tuwon tana tuna haduwar dasukai dashi kamar a mafarki, wanda ta ɓoyewa umma sahura hakanne sabida gudun zargi.
Lumshe idanunta tai tana kuma hasko yadda taga kyakykyawar fuskarsa ta kuma yin wani irin sahihin kyau mai dakar zuciyata, kallo guda zaka masa kasan baya tare dawata matsala ko damuwa.
Wata zazzafar ƙwallah ta share da hannunta tare da furta "ALLAH ka yayemin abinda nakeji akirjina" sannan ta mayar da hankalinta gun cin abincinta.
Koda ta kammala ta mike ta koma waje ta wanke bakinta da hannunta sannan ta komo ɗakin ta miƙe ta dauko ragowar lemon tsaminta na ɗazu tacigaba da sha, tana mamakin yadda test ɗin bakinta ya koma kamar ɗaci-ɗaci.
Dahaka wani shegen bacci mai dadi ya dauketa bata kuma barkaba se can dare sannan tayo alwala tai sallar dake kanta.
Ta kuma komawa baccinta.


             ƊAWISU   G.R.A

Tunda suka koma gida ya haye sama ɗakinsa ya shiga tare da faɗawa kan lafiyayyan gadonsa na alfarma.
Ya lumshe manyan idanunsa batare daya budesuba.
Nan danan ya fada duniyar tunanin neman mafuta.
Ta ƙara masa girma a idanunsa fiye da da! ta ƙara haske amman bai kalli fuskarta sosai ba sedai kallo guda zaka mata kasan cewa tana cikin damuwa.
Daɗa gyara kwanciyar tasa yay tare da hasko yadda take sarrafa ɗan mitsitsin bakinta.
"Malam kasakar min hannuna idan ba hakaba nai maka ihun! kwarto" ya kuma mai maita hakan a fili tare da kwai kwayon yadda take maganar wadda kallo guda zaka mata kasan tanacin tsananin tsoro da furgici yayin datai wannan maganar ne.
Wani haushin kansa da kansane ya kamasa ta yaya ma yabarta ta gudu! ba tare daya san ta ka mai mai inda ze kuma ganinta ba "Ya ALLAH ka kawo mana mafita" abinda ya fadi kenan tare da watsar da lamarinta agefe dan kadama ta shiga zuciyarsa harya  shiga damuwa danshi mutum ne wanda idan yasaka abu aransa da wuya ya fita ta daɗi.
Ganin magariba tayi yasa ya miƙe ya zame kayan jikinsa.
Tare da shiga bathroom yaɗan watsa ruwa sannan ya fito ya shirya kansa cikin wata blue jallabiya mai yankakken hannu tare da fesa turare mai sanyin kamshi, ya dauke yar jakarta ya adana acikin wani ɗan akwatu dake cikin kayansa, sannan ya  fita waje jin anata kwaɗa kiran sallah.
Baiga hidaya a falo ba, dan haka yay saurin zira slipers ya sauka ƙasa.
Da sauri ya karasa cikin masallacin jin anata kokarin tayar da sallah.
Allah ya taimakesa ya samu jam'in dan haka ana idarwa yay saurin fita gudun kada wani ya tsai dashi.
Part ɗin maamah ya nufa tundaga bakin kofa yaji tashin hirarsu da alama ma harda su anty.
Dan gashinan duk suna zaune a falon ƙasa 
Cikin nutsuwa ya tura kofar tare da sallama cikin bakinsa.
Mamakin yadda yaga harda hidayama da sakina matar imran duk suna zaune anata hira da alamun sallah ma suka idar, gefe guda mamah ce zaune bisa abin sallah tana lazumi anty kuma tana sakawa zuby dutse a kitsonta.
Sakinace ta fara gaisar dashi sannan shahida wadda take gaban filet ɗin abinci, sannan hidaya itama ta gaisar dashi.
Gaban anty yaje ya  zauna yana kallonta sannan ya gaisar da ita yana ɗan jan gashin zuby.
"Waini kam anty wannan ƴar taki gashin watayine agidan nan naga kullum kara yawa yake?" yakai maganar cikin barkwanci baki ɗaya kallo suka bisa dashi sanin sa mutum marar magana shi dariyama bata damesa ba bare magana.
Amman ita anty dayake sun saba shiyasa bataji wani mamaki ba sema dariyan datai kana tace.
"Naka tayo mana aikai inda macene bamu san irin kuɗin dazamu ciba" murmushi ya saki yana makawa shahida harara wadda take guntse dariyanta.
Bai kuma cewa komai ba ganin su shahida da matar imran a wajan sema matsawa dayay can gaban maamah yana jiranta idan ta kammala su gaisa yay waje.
Wayansa ya zaro tare da faɗawa face book yana dan karan ce karancen sa anan kunnensa ya jiyo masa muryar shahida.
"Lah wai nikam ɗazu anty hidaya a plazar nan miya haɗaki da wannan mai bakin mayafin?" tafada tana dariya.
Jin an fadi hakan yasa yay tsai da ransa yay kamar yana danna wayan amman hankalinsa na kan hiran nasu, domin tabbas yasan wannan yarinyar suke nufi sabida daga wajan yaga ta fito kuma mayafin jikinta baƙine shara shara, dan iskanci ita bata san yana fitar mata da sura wajeba.
Sekuma yaɗan ja tsaki.
Tare da ayyana baze taɓa barin matarsa ko kanwarsa fita da kamar wannan gyalenba.
Jin muryar hidaya yasa yay tsai da ransa.
"hmm shahida nima ban santaba amman haka kawai dana ganta naji gabana ya fadi wallahi wannan dalilin yahana ni na iya bata amsar hakurin data bani, seda nai gabama naji tabani tausayi". takai maganar cikin nadama.
"Nima anty senaga kamar ma a tsokarce take, amman miya hadaku ne?".
Takai maganar cikin sigar jin taci miya hadasun.
"hoho shahida ƴar jarida" cewar sakina matar imran wadda itama haihuwarta koyau ko gobe ne.
Dariya hidaya taɗanyi sannan ta dubi shahida da kyau.
"Bigeni tai amman bata kulaba dan naga kamar yarinyar tana cikin wani matsalar ne, duba da yadda jikinta yake rawa seda na kalli idanunta da kyau kuma nagama kamar kuka take". takai maganar tana dariyar wannan tambayanta shahida.
"ALLAH Sarki anty hidaya nima wallahi tabani tausayi sosai kuma daga gani talakace sabida kwata kwatama inaga kamar ba wajan zatazoba tai ɓatan kai, dannaga farkon shigowarta miyau ne abakinta wai dan tsabar kauyanci ta shiga wajan da ake biyan kudi zata zubar a wajan shine naga wani ya daka mata tsawa to ina tunanin wannan firgitarne ya saka ta bigeki bata saniba".
Kirjinsa ne ya buga dumm!! miyau? name abakinta? innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraju'un, ya furta acikin zuciyarsa.
Muryar hidaya yaji tana dariya.
"Oh yar jarida shahida, nima wani zubin senaita tara miyau abakina musamman idan ina dattin ciki kinsan kowa da halintar sa amman wannan yarinyar ma ai daga gani bata da aure bare tai abinda kike tunani". takai maganar cikin son ta ganar da shahida.
"Eh kam anty nima wani zubin inayi wallahi".
Jin haka yasa ya saki wata wawiyar ajiyar zuciya wadda harta bayyana fili.
Tare da furta may be dattin cikine danshi kam baiga wani sign na wani abu agaretaba shiyasama yaji hankalinsa yayi ɗaya, kuma tinda yaganta hartana da kafar gudu yasan cewa lafiya ta samu shiyasa yaɗanji dadi.
Da wannan tunanin yaji zuciyarsa ta aminta.
Ganin mamah ta idar yasa yaja jikinsa gabanta suka gaisa baiga fuskar zama ba dan haka yay ma su anty sallama ya koma part ɗinsa.
Daman wani zubin mama bata basa fuskar zama gabanta musamman idan taga jama,a.
Tea ya haɗa yasha sanna ya wuce bathroom  yay brus tare da wanka ya kuma dauro alwala yazo ya tayar da nafila.
Akan sallaya hidaya tazo ta tarar da shi itama bathroom ɗinsa ta wuce ta dauro alwalan tazo tabi bayansa suka kuma jera wata sallar daman sun sama duk daran juma,a basu fiye yin bacciba suna tsayawane sosai sukai kukansu wajan ubangiji akan ya dubi lamarinsu sannan suna barar neman haihuwa a taskarsa.
Basu bar kan sallayunsu ba seda akai kiran sallar farko sannan suka kuma mikewa suka bada raka'atul fijir, shikam masallaci ya wuce ita kuma tai sallar ta agida.
Bayan ya dawo daga masallacin ya kuma biyawa part ɗinsu mama ya gaisa dasu sannan yay nasa part ɗin.
Tunda yay azkar yay abinda zeyi na al,adar rayuwarsa ya kwanta bai tashiba se da zetafi  masallaci yay wanka ya shirya cikin ɗanyen farin yadin boyel.
Oska ne yazo ya daukesa daga sallar juma,a kuma, yay gidan mai girma governor acan ya ɓata manyan lokuta sabida gayyatar da governor ɗin yay masa, cike da yunwa ya dawo gida haka ya zauna yaci abinci ya ɗan huta sannan suka dauki hanyar can hayin rafi inda zega aikin daya bayar.
Haka rayuwa ta cigaba da gudana a wajan mai girma mas! sam baya da lokacin kansama bare na wani kullum baya nan baya can yana wajan sabgogin gabansa.
Cikin watan kuma ya ɗaga ƙasa mai tsarki dan yana son yay umara daga nan kuma zeje london. bayan ya damƙawa babban yaronsa oska wasu daga cikin hidindumin  dake  gabansa.
         

                     WANNAN KENAN.


  

                      Ɓangarenta.

Kwanci tashi babu wuya awajan ALLAH yau nafisa tagama shirinta cikin uniform ɗinta masu kalar fari tas, tana tsaye  gaban rijiya tana zira guga zata dibi ruwan dake rijiyar ta zuba a jarka wai ita yanzu tafison ruwan rijiyar akan na ko ina.
Umma sahura kuma tana zaune tana goge mata jakarta sega walida ta shigo cikin sauri.
Gaisawa sukai da umma sahura sannan ta dubi nafisa ta ce mata gafa ya nura can yana waje dan ALLAH tai sauri.
Seda ta ɗura ruwanta sannan tazo ta dauki jakarta tunda walida ta shigar mata da kayanta mota tuni.
Nan kuma idanunta sukai ƙwal ƙwal zataiwa ummah kuka!
Seda umma sahuran tai mata da gaske sannan ta mayar da ƙwallar tata, suka jera harda baba mijin umma sahura suka rakata wajan mota, amman anan ɗin seda tai kuka!
Itama umma sahura kukan ta saka tare da shiga cikin gida tana jin kewar ɗiyar ɗan uwan nata sabida sunyi muguwar shaƙuwa ba kaɗan ba.
Haka su nafisa suka shiga mota ya nura nata tsokanarta itakam ta sada kanta a kasa tana mayar da ƙwallar ta, tana tuna ƙaddarar data faɗa mata wadda ta ɗora rabin laifin akanta.
Muryar ya nura taji yana magana da walida.
"Kinji kuwa mai gida yau yabar saudi ya nufi london nan da sati biyu ze shigo 9ja". walida tai maza ta dubi ya nura.
"Eh ɗazu inajin hajiya tana gayawa baba a falo dasuna break wai anfada a labarai".Se kuma ta juyo ta dubi feenah" kefa mutumin mu yana out side" tafaɗa cikin far'a itakam feena juya kanta tai side na window tana shakar iskar datake kaɗo musu cikin jin ɗaɗi.
Seda walida ta kuma mai mai tawa.
"feenah nace maki mai girma mas! yana out side fa" to anan taɗan juyo tare da furta "Barkansa" ta kuma juyawa tabar walida cikin jimanta maganar.
Sun kusan kaiwa bakin gate ta ƙyallah taga wani koren mangoro, cike da zalama ta kaiwa walida cakuma.
Hartana karkarwa wajan tsaida ya nura kafin ta ɓalle marfun motar ta fita waje.
Gaban wanda ke bakin bishiyar ta nufa cikin tausayi take masa magana "assalamu alaikum babana dan girman ALLAH kasammun mangwaronka wallahi raina ya bita" cikin mamaki tsohon ya dubeta "ayya yarinya babu nu nanne fa se ɗanye ɗazunnan ƴan sari suka kwashe".yakai maganar bayan ya mayar da ƴar redionsa kunnensa.
"Baba ai daman ɗanyen nake so koda kudina nane baba katai makamin". bai ce mata ko ƙala ba ya nufi ciki mintuwa kusan biyar segashi nan ya taho da wata bakar leda ya cika mata ita da mangoron.
Karba tai gami da masa godiya sannan ta nufi motarsu cikin mamaki walida ta dubeta "kekuma mizaki da wannan kayan ciwon cikin keda ko mai nuna bakki sha" bata bata amsaba illama bude ledar tadai ta dauki ɗaya tahau sha duk yadda yake da tsami.
Haka suka karasa cikin harabar makarantar abakin office ɗin p.c ya nura yay parking nafisa ce ta futa wajan p.c taje tai singing kasancewarta shugabar makarantar sannan ta komo taiwa ya nura godiya, kana walida ta samo masu yara suka daukar masu kayansu har hostel, itakam wajan sauran ƴan uwanta masu ɗan kwali ta koma suna taya sauran malamai chakking na provision ɗin student's na yaji da sauran abinda hukumar makaranta bata yarda a shigo mata da shiba.
Bata bar wajan ba se can yamma koda ta koma cikin hostel ma kwanciyarta tai jin kanta na ciwo.


Duk yadda walida taso dasuci abincin data dafo masu agida ƙitai sema, mangwaronta data dauko ta zuba a kwano ta yayyanka sa ta zuba masa yaji zallah da mai tahau cinsa ahaka.

Seda akai magariba sannan ta nufi bandaki tayo wanka ta sauya kaya kana ta dauko farar shinkafa bata saka miyaba tahau cinta ahaka.
Sosai walida take shan mamakin wannan lamarin.

Haka rayuwar feenah ta cigaba da gudana acikin makarantar su kusan kullum sesunyi rigima da walida akan rashin cin abincin dabatayi ga yawan bacci langwai kala kala.

Kusan kullum da zafin jiki take kwana wataran haka zatai ta murƙususu akan gado ita bamai zazzaɓi ba ita bamai ciwon ciki ba.

Al amarin daya faru kuma agaba shine, yawan hararwa duk abinda taci seta amayar dashi wanda walida bata yarda tai agaban jama,a haka xata  bata fonsu dasuke fitsari acikinsa idan dare yayi tai aman aciki idan ta gama ta tayata ta wanke jikinta sannan ta zubar dashi.

Sosai feenah ta rame tai wani irin haske watansu kusan biyu a school bata gane komai hatta karatunsu bata wani mayar da hankali akansa kullum tana bacci abinta ko dutynsu bata fita, haka zata kwanta sedai walida tai masu komai wadda kanta yagama kullewa da lamarin f.k


Yau kimanin watansu uku cif a school  da faruwar lamarinta da mai girma mas! wata huɗu kenan kallo guda mai hankali da nutsuwa zema nafisa yasan da shigar ƙaramin ciki ajikinta wanda yanzu sam ta daina wannan hararwar sedai bacci da yawan kasala da zazzaɓi.
Ta murje ta kara fari kirjinta yay wani irin cika sedai kuma cikinta a shafe yake babu ta yadda yay alamun tasawa bare a gane cewar mai ciki ce.


Sedai ƴar ramar data ɗanyi.               yau suna zaune ita da walida suna cin kwaɗon kwaki bayan sun dawo daga area class karatun yamma.

Da kallo tabi walida sannan tace "Nikam walida yaufa wajan wata hudu ban ga al,adata ba,". cikin ɗan firgici walida ta dubeta "kuma ai baki taba yin hakaba ko?".

"Inafa inda inayi aida kin sani ni wallahi tsoro nakeji". takai maganar idanunta na kawo ruwa.

Cike da kuruciya walida tai dariya "ke dallah share kima godewa ALLAH ai kita addu,a kuma ALLAH sa ba gamo kikaiba kinsan jinnu nasaka haka". nan kuma seta gasgata maganar walidar tare da kudurar niyar yawaita addu,a sosai.

Kwanci tashi babu wuya wajan ALLAH yau su nafisa suka fara zana jarabawarsu ta karshen term wadda kuma daga hutun idan suka dawo zasu fara zaman extension daga shi kuma ze final exam se kuma candy.

Wannan karon tai kokari babu laifi domin ta watsar da lamarin wani anwarr sam ta manta dashi arayuwarta sedai idan ta tuna rabata da kimarta dayay setaji komai ya fitar mata aranta.

Sam yanzu baya gabanta musamman dataji labarin ma bayanan ya dawo daga london ya kuma ɗaukar matarsa sun bar kasar setaji komai ya kuma fita akanta na lamarinsa.

Haka hutu ya zagayo, tun safiyar hutu suke faman koke koke itada da walida kamar bazasu kuma haduwa ba wai dan kawai wannan karon baxatabi motar tasuba habu wanda zata aura ɗan kawune zezo ɗaukarta.

Sabida daman sunyi da ummah sahura akan wannan karon can gidan kaka zata wuce bazata zo gidantaba.

Haɗa yan tarkacan su sukai sukai can area class inda ake zankun zuwan daukar dalibai.

Da habu taci karo yasha yadinsa mai suna ishurunka maraya ya ƙwama ƙaton gilashi baki shi bai yarda ba yay kyau.

Dariya dukkansu suka guntse sannan walida ta dubeta "a,a kalli mijin naki yasha gayu kamar gobe kuke aure". tsaki taja kana tabar wajan fuww tai gaba abinta.

Cikin takun na isa habu ya ƙaraso gabanta "ke baki ganni bane xaki wani koma can baya kamar baki san da mutum ba?" yafaɗa irin ni na isan nan, tsaki taɗan ja kaɗan tare da furta ko yashawo kayan mayen nasane aka san ranta.

Amman gudun abin fada yasa ta basar tare da gaisar dashi sannan ta hau bayan babur dinsa dayazo dashi wanda seta ta karanta addu,a kafin ta hau.

Itakam walida mika  mata kayan ta tai sannan suka kumayin sallama kana suka rabu.

Gudu sosai habu yake da ita wanda take yin   addu,a ALLAH ya kaisu gida lafiya.

 ta hanyar ɗawisu expiress sukabi wanda kuma aka sanar ayau mai girma ɗan majalissar taraiya ze biyota dan ya shiga cikin sabuwar kasuwar wayar daya bude ta ɗawisu expiress sannan kuma ya duba aikin dayay acikinta.

Wannan dalilin yasa babu mahalukin daya biyo wannan hanyar ayau domin tunda waɗan nan mutane suka kai masa hari watannin baya yasa yanzu duk inda zebi yake zama safe babu yawan jama,a wannan dalilin yasa, aka baza labarai akan zezo tawajan inda ze duba aiyukan daya zuba kafin tafiyarsa kasar waje wanda kuma ya dawo jiya da safe ayau din kuma ya fito shawagin duba waɗan nan ayyukan dayay.


Sosai kuma aka zuba jami'an tsaro atawajan gudun faruwar wani abu marar kyau.

Tunda suka biyo hanyar nafisa take baza idanunta ganin kota ina an zuba securty's zagaye da manyan bindugu.

Habu wanda danasani ta rufe da tsoro shiya cigaba da falfala gudu kamar babu gobe daman seda abokinsa illah yace masa kada ya biyo ta hanyar amman tsabar taurin kai yasaka yabiyo ɗin.

A farkon hanyar idan jami'an sukaga da nafisa da uniform suna barinsu su wuce amman da sukazo tsakiyar hanya seda habu yadan dakata sabida yadda karar motoci da jiniya suka cika hanyar nan da nan idanun habu suka fara raina fata.

Itakam nafisa ganin lokaci na kuma ja yasa ta bude jakarta ta dauko carbin tahau ci sabida yunwar data addabeta, ita kuma bata da masaniyar wani ɗan majalisa zezo wucewa kawai ita tai mamakin yadda taga motocin jami'ai sunyi yawa a hanyar yasaka take tunanin ko sabida tsarone yasaka akai hakan.

Abakin wata bishiyar mangwaro suka tsaya sabida yadda motocin securty's suka kunno kai sannan kuma gawasu tsala tsalan motoci na kata'i da suka biyosu a baya.

Habune ya dakata ita kuma ganin haka ya saka ta sauka daga mashin ɗin ta nufi gaban bishiyar tai tsallan albarka ta tsinko guda biyu.

Habu baya da vakin magana datasha masifa amman ganin securty's yasaka bakinsa ya mutu.

Haka tayo wajan mashin ɗin hannunta dauke da mangwaronta tana sha.

Kuma adai dai nan motar mai girma mas!

Ta kawo kai suna kokarin shiga cikin sabuwar kasuwar wayar da akai ta ɗawisu expiress.


Jikinsa sanye da wasu irin kaya masu kyau se farar jacket daya ɗora a samansu sabida ɗan sanyin da ake agarin.

Hannunsa rike da waya suna magana da mai kamfanin sardauna construction akan aikin daya basa, magana suke cikin harshen turanci inda yake fitar da dukkan kalmomin cike da ƙwarewa kamar bai taɓajin hausaba.

Sosai suke magana da Alhajin sardauna wanda sam bai san da labarin yaronsa wanda yake ha'inci akan harkokin kamfanin nasa daya bar masaba, seda yanzu anwarr ɗin ke masa bayanin hakan wanda alhajin yaketa basa hakuri tare da ce masa ya kwantar da hankalin sa nan da karshen wata ze zo ƙasar.

Kamar ance ya ɗago idanun sa ya ɗan sauke glass ɗin motar karaf idanun nasa ya sauka akanta.

Jikinta sanye cikin uniform fari wanda ya bayyana masa daga makarantar data fito, sedai ɓacin ran daya riskesa yadda yaga tana  yagar mangoro kamar wata akuya, bai gama shan mamakinta ba seda yaga ta tsallako aguje tare da ɗanewa babur harda dafa mai mashin ɗin sannan suka bar wajan dawani matsiyacin gudu.

Dafe kansa yay cikin takaici sannan yaɗan ja ƙwafa, ya mayar da hankalinsa kan wayar dayakeyi.

Abinda yasa hankalinsa ya kwanta yadda yagane uniform ɗin school data  fito wanda ya kudura aransa zesa akira masa p.c ɗin makarantar ya tambayeta akanta.

Da wannan tunanin yabar maganar ya fada sabgarsa.


Itakam tana hawa mashin suka cigaba da tafiya hankalinta kwance domin sam batama kula dashi ba.

Basu sukaje gidaba se wajan la,asar.

Wanka ta farayi sannan ta haye gado se bacci kaka tun tana saka ran tashi suyi hira har abin ya fassakara.


Yau kimain satin ta guda acikin gidan kaka, wanda baki ɗaya bata fita koda waje se ɓanɗaki shima seya zama dole.

Sabida yadda tafarajin rade radin cewar wai bazata koma makaranta ba auransu da habu za,ai acewar kawu yagaji da zaman ta agidan haka babu aure.

Aranar dataji maganar kasa bacci tai haka tai ta kuka kuma adaren ta kira walida ta shaida mata sannan ta kira umma sahura wadda tayi ta bata hakuri kana ta ce mata ta kwantar da hankalinta tanan bisa hanya gobe.


Washe gari wajan goman safiya sega ummah sahura mijinta yayi mata rakiya koda suka samu baffa da wannan maganar kicin kicin yayi da ransa harda cewa umma sahura dan bata san ciwon haihuwa bane ba shiyasa take fatana nafisa ta zauna agida salon taja musu wani mummunar masifar.

Abin dai babu wani daɗin ji haka umma sahura tai ta bawa nafisa haƙuri sannan ta koma gida rai babu daɗi.


Kwanci tashi yau aka kawo kayan lefen nafisa akwatu 1 wanda seda walida tazo sannan tai ta rarrashinta harta samu tai shuru anan walida ta kwana sannan, washegari ta nufi gida sabida bikin sati uku aka saka kafin nan zata kuma dawowa.


Kullum nafi bata da aiki sena kuka ko abinci da ƙyar take cikinsa ga kuma wani ikon ALLAH tunda ta dawo gida cikinta ya soma girma kamar ana hurosa.

Kusan kullum tana ɗaki jikinta sanye cikin hijabi wannan dalilin ya saka sam babu wanda ya kula.


Yau takama asabar umma sahura tazo tun safe da nufin kwana sabida bikin nafisa da habu daya kawo kai yau sauran kwana biyar ciff.

Itama walida gove zata shigo garin tunda nafisa ta kwanta yau take kuka yau ga bikinta yazo amman babu dangin mahaifiyarta ko ɗaya wanda inda akwai su da bazasu bari ai mata wannan aureba.

Umma sahura kadai ke rarrashinta ita kuma amfi karfinta baxata iya ita kaɗai ba.

Yanxuma ita ke kanta tana tashinta ta mike ta shiga wanka.

Sabida yadda takejin jikinta ya saka ta mike tabar dakin ta nufi can tsakar gida ko mayafi babu ajikinta.

Rigar jikinta mai santsice wadda ta baiwa ɗan cubur bur ɗin cikinta damar bayyana a fili.

Ta fito daga wankan tana kokarin shiga ɗaki matar baffa mai suna safiya ta jawo hannunta cike da izgilanci.

"Munafukar ALLAH yau dai semun ganki tunda akai maganar aurenki kike zaman ɗaka bakya futa ko ina muna fukar ALLAH" tana fada ta kum jawo hannunta izuwa wajan cikin sauran matan kawunan nata.

Caraf harira ta mike "yauni ƴasu menake kallone anan " takai maganar tana kaiwa cikin dake jikin nafisa duka kaɗan wanda yasa nafisa sakin ƙara! kaɗan ta sauka ƙasa tana rintse idanunta.

Baba safiya ita ta tayar da ita anan fa matan gida suka hau shewa yau mizasu gani cikin buɗa harira tace "ikon ALLAH ai wannan ko makaho yasan ciki gareta baremu damukaga jiya mukaga yau" aikuwa kafin wani lokaci tuni sun zagayeta caaa!

Nan danan magana ta baza gidan aikuwa abinka da gidan yawa nan kowani sashi sukaita yowa kanta masu murna nayi masu jaje nayi.

Kafin wani lokaci magana taje kunnan kaka sosai nafisa ta shiga tashin hankali hatta hawayenta seda ya ƙafe sam bata kawo ciki gareta ba se yanzu da wannan masifar ta faɗa mata yasa ta tuna rabonta da priod 4mnth.

Kuka babar habu tahauyi tana cewa da za,a cuceta a ƙaƙa bawa ɗanta jaraba wannan lamari ya firgita kowa amman umma sahura tafi kowa shiga tashin hankali sabida wasuma cewa sukai sabida bata san ciwon ɗaba yasa ta dauki nafi hutu ta turata karuwanci.

Wannan lamari ba,a barsa anan ba har seda aka dangana da asibiti anan likita ya bada shaidar cikin dake jikin nafisa cikin koshin lafiya harna wata huɗu.


Bayan sun dawo gida kaka harda suma tana gunjin kuka! shima habu haka yazo yagama tijararsa yabar gidan al amarin dayasa su baffa sukai meating akan a ɓarar da cikin.

Wannan lamarin ya saka nafisa ta ɗago idanunta da sukai jajur ta dubesu "kawu kuyi hakuri kubarni  naji da wannan ƙaddarar data sameni amman banajin zan iya zubda cikin jikina wanda kaddara ta samar dashi gwara na bar maku gidanku" tana fadin haka ta saka kuka tana mikewa tai cikin dakin kaka.

Tana kuma jin tausayin kanta "ina zan ganki ummata?" takai maganar tana kifa kanta akan gado tana kuka "na cuci kaina soyayya ta kaini ta baroni gashi ina cikin tashin hankali kai kana can cikin farin cikinka" bataji shigowar umma sahura ba sejin maganarta tai a bayanta.

"Babu komai nafisa nina ɗauki ƙaddara kamar yadda sukace nina kaiki harkikai ciki nikuma zan daukeki na kaiki gidana ki haifarmin shegen na goya idan ALLAH ya saukeki lafiya ni zan dauki ɗawainiyarki data abinda zaki haifa saboda haka ki maza ki haɗa kayanki mubar musu gidansu ni baxan wulakantaki ba".

Cikin mintuna kadan suka hada kaya suka bar gidan wutsiya a zage wanda kaka har lokacin kuka take sukam su baffa yi sukai kamar basusan nafisa ba harda cewa kila halin uwarta tai sukam zuriyarsu babu mai abun kunya.

Haka umma sahura tasakata agaba sukai gidanta nafisa na kuka har suka kai.

Yau kwanan ta kusan biyu agidan umma wanda babu abinda take se tsabar kuka seda mijin umma sahura ya fara yi mata tofi sannan ta soma iya magana ma.


Cikin tsananin tashin hankali walida ta fito daga gidansu kaka bayan tagamajin abinda ya samu feena daga bakin harira wadda ta tsaida ita tundaga soro bayan walidar tazo da shirin biki.

Da kuka walida ta fito tana haɗa hanya ta dauki mashin ya nufi unguwar da ummah sahura take......!



IDAN KUNGA TYPING ERROS KUYI HAKURI....



*AUTAR MANYA*
42 - 43


*MASU FIDDAMIN DA LITTAFI KUJI TSORON ALLAH*



Cikin lokaci kaɗan walida ta sauka a ƙofar gidan ummah sahura, mai mashin ta tsaya ta bawa kuɗinsa sannan ta shiga cikin gidan a gigice.

Da baba mijin umma sahura taci karo yana kokarin fitowa daga cikin gidan, ɗan ja da baya tai kana ta tsugunna ta gaisar dashi sannan ta ƙarasa shiga tsakar gidan.

Sallama  ɗauke abakinta ta ƙaraso ta samu umma sahura a tsaye tana bushe gyaɗa, cikin ladabi walida ta rissuna ta soma gaisar da umma sahura.

Kana ta dubeta batare da wani jinkiriba tace "Umma daga can gidansu kaka nake naje akan maganar bikin nafisa, amman senaji wani magana daban marar daɗi. anan ta zayyanewa umma sahura dukkan abin da harira matar ƙanin baban nafisan ta gaya mata.

Sannan ta cigaba tana mai share hawayenta "Umma idan har wannan lamari da gaskene to waye yay mata wannan zaluncin daman wallahi ninaga alamunta tun a school bata cika lafiya ba?" takai maganar cikin sigar tambaya.

Umma sahura bata iya cewa komai ba illa aje gyaɗar hannun nata datayi sannan tajawo hannun walida sukai cikin ɗakin da nafisan take.

Zaune suka tadda  ita, ta rafka uban tagumi kallo guda zaka mata kasan tana cikin tashin hankali.

Da ɗan sauri walida ta saki hannun umma sahura ta karasa gabanta tana sakin gunjin kuka! ganin haka yasa umma ta juya tabar masu wurin.

Itama nafisa a wannan karon kukan take sosai mai cike da ma'anoni da dama.

Cikin shashshekar kuka! walida  ta soma mata magana "Feeanah garin yaya wannan mummunar ƙaddarar ta samemu waye ya maki haka! waye ya zalunci marainiyar ALLAH wallahi koma waye se ALLAH......." Tai saurin saka hannunta ta rufe mata bakinta.

Sannan ta dubeta da jajayen idanuwanta daman kuma gasu sun ƙara yin wani iri  na alamun ciki atare da ita.

"Walida nina jawa kaina wannan ƙaddarar gwara da kika kirata da mummuna domin kuwa mummunar ce agareni. walida soyayya tajamin  na kai kaina ga bala'in dayafi ƙarfina a yau zan gaya maki abinda ban gayawa wani mahaluki ba...........sedai ina son ki ɗaukar min alƙawarin da bazaki taɓa shaidawa wani mahaluki wannan maganarba".


Tafaɗa tana tsare walida da idanunta.


"Nai maki alƙawari ƙawata bazan taɓa tona wannan sirrin namuba insha ALLAHU".

Tai maganar cikin gamsuwa....


"Walida na tafka babban kuskure acikin rayuwata.................ta gama bawa walida labarin kaf abin da ya faru a tsakanin ta da mai girma mas! batare data rage kota ƙara ba.

Jan numfashi walida tayi sannan kuma ta ɗan dubi nafisa cikin tausayawa.

"Tabbas kawata kaddararku ce a haka dagake harshi amman bamu san mai ALLAH ya ɓoye acikin wannan lamarin ba kuma nai maki alƙawarin bazan taɓa gayawa kowa wannan maganarba amman tabbas senai dalilin da mai girma mas! yasan da cikinsa ajikinki, amman ko ze kasheni bazan taɓa nuna cewar nasan inda kikeba".

Waro idanu nafisa tai sannan ta dubi walida.

"Walida kada kimin hakan ki tuna matsayinsa da girman sa nasan bazeso wannan abun kunyar daga wajansaba dan ALLAH kada ki futar da wannan sirrin wataƙil ma yasaka a koremu daga wannan garin kokuma yace azubar da cikin wanda nakejin gwara na rasa raina akan a rabani da wannan cikin najikina".

Takai maganar tana kuka!

Dafata walida tai sannan tace.

"Sam ba wannan tunanin daga wajena amman meze hana a zubar dashi ɗin tunda kina gudun matsala gakuma makarantar ki?".


Da baya taɗanja kaɗan sannan ta dubi walida.
"Walida na hakura da karatuna matukar wannan cikin nawa ze rayu, nasan ba lallai nai aure a duniyaba saboda wannan abun kunyar danai dan haka zan zauna na raini ɗana ko ƴata cikin amincin ALLAH sabida bani da wani sanyin idaniyata idan ba wannan cikin ba a yanzu".

Sosai wannan furucin nata ya saka walida kuka haka suka hade kansu sunata kuka abin gwanin tausayi.

Seda umma sahura ta shigo hannunta dauke da kwanon dambun da nafisan ta kafe akan shitake so sannan suka ɗan tsagaita da kukan nasu.

Aje masu shitai a kasa sannan ta dubi walida..
"Walida ni abin da yafi ɗagamun hankalina yadda ita kanta nafisan ta kasa shaida wanda yay mata wannan aika aikan amman babu komai akwai ubangiji".

Umma sahura takai maganar tana share hawayenta itama.

Walida ce ta dubeta sannan tace "tabbas umma inaganin wani azzaluminne yay mata wannan zaluncin alokacin da bata hayyacinta kinga kuwa bazata gane kowaye ba amman koma waye shida ALLAH".

Walida takai maganar cike da son bagarar da umma sahura wadda ta amsa mata cikin gamsuwa tare da barin ɗakin.

Ganin walida yasa tadanci dambun kadan sannnn walida ta taimaka mata sukai sallah.

Har isha walida tana gidan se wajan taran dare sannan ta masu sallama ta wuce gida itama.

Cikin dare nafisa idanunta ya ƙanƙance babu abinda take se faman tunanin wannan lamari daya kunno mata kai tsaye..

Washe gari da safe segasu baffa nan sun diro gidan umma sahura.

Irin tashin hankalin dasu nafisa suka gani se wanda yagani, sabida cin mutunci sukaiwa umma sahura tas akan lallai ta kori nafisa ta, tafi bariki su bazasu hada zuriya da wadda tai cikin shegeba.

Seda mijin umma sahura yay tsaye akan babu inda nafisa zata tunda su basu rike zumuncin ALLAH sun ɗauki ƙaddara ba to su sunji sun aminta babu kuma inda nafisa zata tafi zasu riketa muddin rai.

Haka su baffa suka juya baram-baram abun babu kyawun ji. kaka dataji wannan maganar seda tai gudawa dan tsabar kidima haka ta tara yayanta ta saka masu kuka akan kada wanda ya kuma zuwa gidan umma akan nafi tunda su bazasu riketaba to subar su suɗin su riketa.

Abin da yasama kaka bata tsaya kan nafi ta dawo nan ɗin ba sabida tasan zaman nan ɗin baze mata dadi ba gwara gidan umma sahura duk tsiya.

Gidan umma sahura kuwa nafisa tai kuka kamar ranta ze fita seda ƙyar suka samu tayi shuru sannan kuma baba mijin umma sahura yayita bata baki yana kwantar mata da hankalinta.


Yau kimin sati biyu da faruwar komai haka zalika sosai nafisa ta murje tai kyau amman ta ɗanyi rama kaɗan, sabida damuwar data saka acikin ranta.


Yau tun wuri suka fita ita da umma zuwa asibiti sakamakon maleria ɗin da nafisa take fama da ita kuma anata faɗakarwa akan kada abar masu ciki da maleria domin tana cutar da jaririn dake ciki ɗin.

Fafur nafisa taki amincewa dasuje wannan clinic ɗin se general hospital sukaje bayan ammata gwaje gwaje da sauransu aka bata magani sukayo gida.


Walida na yawan ziyartar ta har hutun su ya kare sosai kuma walida tai kuka yayin dazata koma school taga fina bazata koma ba sabida cikinta ya girma dan ya fito sosai.


Haka rayuwarta ta cigaba da tafiya agidan umma kwanci tashi babu wahala yanzun cikin nata ya shiga wata na bakwai kuma idan ka dubeta seta baka tausayi sabida yadda cikin yay wani irin girma wanda yasa ko fita batai indai ba zuwa awo ba wanda ada bata zuwa amman baba yace dole ta dinga zuwa kodan lafiyar ta.


Al amarin mai girma mas! kuwa sosai ya mayar da hankalinsa akan aikace-aikacen dake gabansa wanda a yanzun haka baya da wani lokacin zaman kansa bare yay wani tunani akan wata nafisa.

Sam yama manta da ita ko hidaya iya hakkinta na aure ke haɗasu sabida baya son shiga hurumin hakkinta amman bada ban haka ba seta wuni bata gansa ba sabida aikin dake kokarin shigar masa dukkan wasu lokutansa.

Yau kimanin wajan wata ɗaya kenan daya bada aikin babbar makarantar ƴammata ta ɗawisu.

Tun safe ya shirya motocinsa domin fita ran gadin aikin daya nuna kamar yama manta daya bada shi.

Yana sanye cikin wasu irin suite bakake masu kyau yana daura tie hidaya ta shigo dakin nasa.

"My dear inada magana dakai".

Agogon hannunshi ya duba tare da ɗaukar glass ɗinsa.


"Kibari na dawo tukunna sabida kinga anata jirana".

Cikin gamsuwa hidaya tace.
"Ba damuwa ALLAH yadawo min dakai lafiya".

Dayake gidan nasu akwai taron ƴan uwa sabida matar imran ta haihu wannan dalilin yasa bai tsayaba sabida yasan yace ze tsaya za,a ɓata masa lokacine dan haka kawai ya wuce abinshi.

Cikin zafin namansa ya nufi wata farar motar ƙirar wannan zamanin wadda oska yay saurin bude masa ita ya shiga driver yaja sauran guards ɗinsa suka mara masa baya acikin sauran motocin nasa.

Wajan titin expiress ya soma zuwa tare da duba ayyukan da aka masa.


Sannan ya wuce makarantar daya bayar a gina wadda za,a wareta domin koyon karatun aikin  jinya.


Adai dai wannan saitin bishiyar mangoron ya ɗaga kansa anan dukkan tunanin ta daya mance sabida uzurin gabansa ya faɗo masa.

Dafe kansa yay wanda yakejin yana barazanar sara masa.

Atake ya umarci oska daya saka driver ya juya kan mota zuwa dawisu bording school.

Cikin mintuna arba'in karar sautin jiniyar motocinsa ta cika cikin babbar makarantar ƴammata ta ɗawisu, wadda zuwa lokacin tuni cikin p.c ya ɗuri ruwa.

Dakatar da kowa yay tare da bude marufin motar da kansa ya nufi office ɗin p.c wadda sabida tsabar kidimar datai hatta jikinta rawa yake.

Cikin katafaren office din yaja kujera ya zauna gefensa oska yana tsaye wanda shi kaɗai ya amincewa daya biyosa.

Malaman makarantar kowa jikinsa rawa yake da wannan on expected visit da mai girma mas! yay masu cikin wannan rana.

Cikin mutuntaka da sanin kima da mutunta ɗan adam kamar yadda ake son kowani mutum ya kasance. mai girma mas! yake amsa gaisuwar da malaman school ɗin ke shigowa ɗaya bayan ɗaya suna kawo masa.

Seda ya kammala dasu, sannan ya dubi p.c wadda take gurfane gabansa duk jikinta ya jiƙe da zufa kamar wadda taiwa sarki ƙarya.

Cikin ƙwarewa da sigar siyasa ta yadda ƙaryarka ka gane miya kawosa ya dubeta.

"Ina buƙatar ganin students ɗinki nafisa kabir" yafaɗi hakan a dake batare daya bar wata kofar daza,a gane mike ransa ba.

Sannan kuma karyarka ka gane wani abu agame dashi sannan kuma ga mamakin yadda ya rike sunan tamau kodan ya kasance mutum mai rike abu acikin ransa.

Ɗan jimm p.c tai kana ta kirawo baba vise wanda yake tsaye abakin office ɗin.

Cikin azama baba vise ya kunno kai office din bayan ya rissuna masa.

"Amm vise ka shaida nafisa kabir acikin wannan makaranta?" cewar p.c kenan wadda baki ɗaya ta gama tsurewa.

"Eh mana wadda kwana kifa sukai magana dashi akan haurowar gardi shugabar ɗalibai fa hajiya".

Baba vise yakai maganar cikin ƙwarin gwiwa.

Se a lokacin p.c ta gane domun ada ta ɗan tsorata wanda dalilin hakan yasa  ta kasa shaida wayake nufi.

"Ayyah ƴar aljanah ashe yake nufi, sannan ta dubi mai girma mas! wanda yay kamar bai san mi sukeba amman a ransa yana nanata ƴar aljannah!.

"Ranka ya dade wallahi bata dawo ba wannan term ɗin kuma muma mun kasa gane transper sukai mata ko wani abu daban na kasa fahimta yarinyar tana da brain sosai rashin sanin gidansu yasa banjeba".

Takai maganar cikin ladabi.

Waro manyan idanunsa yay alamun bai damu da lamarin ba sannan ya ɗan kaɗa kafaɗarsa alamun ko ajikinsa a ƙasan ransa yana cewa. may be ko aure za,a mata dan daga ganinta villager ce.

Yana kokarin mikewa ya fita baba vise ya dakatar dashi cikin son nuna gwaninta da ƙwaɗayin ɗan na cefane.

"Ranka ya dade, inaganinta da wata yar baƙar yarinya habiba usman mai lafiya, ƙila tasan ta yi hakuri bara naje na turo maka ita".

Ɗan tsaki! yaja cikin ransa sannan ya koma kamar an masa dole ya zauna shikuma baba vise ya fita a 360

SS3A Ya shiga ya kira habiba usman walida wadda ta koma kamar marainiya a school sabida rashin feenah ta dago ganin baba vise yana kiranta.

Jikinta yana rawa tabiyisa seda taga manyan motoci sannan kuma jikinta ya koma yay sanyi dan da ita tai tunanin yan gidansune sukazo dan yanzun jinta take kamar a cell rabuwarta da feenah.

Cikin sanyin jiki ta bisa har cikin  office ɗin p.c wanda baba vs yace ta biyosa ita dai mamakin rawar jikin dayake taketa yi a ranta, amman ganin yadda malamai sukai tsitt a falon farko na office ɗin yasa tasha jinin jikinta.

Sallama tai cikin nutsuwa kana ta lumshe idanunta jin wani sassanyan kamshi yana ratsata.

Gaban walida ne yay wata irin faduwa ganin wanda batai tsammani ba agabanta yana zaune ya dora kafa ɗaya kan ɗaya yana danna wayansa.

Duk yadda taso ta basar da kallonsa hakan bai yuwuba se a wannan lokacin tasan dole nafisa tai hauka akansa lallai ALLAH yay halitta awajan nan  tsarki ya tabbata ga wanda ya halicci wannan mutumi ALLAH kenan sarki ɗaya gagara misali.

Da kallo yabita ta ƙasan idanunsa minta ɗaya ya ɗago ya umarci oska dasu p.c su basu wuri.

Cikin rawar murya walida ta fara gaisar dashi wanda shi daga lafiya bai kuma amsa mataba.

"Ina ƙawarki take?"

Tambayar daya cillo mata kenan kamar ba shiba.

Cikin dakewa itama nafisa wadda tai ta maza ta basa amsa da.

"Nima ban san kotana ina ba a yanzun haka".

Cikin daure fuska ya dubeta tare da kallon ke bana son rainin wayo sannan ya kawar da kansa.

Lallai ana mulki anan bayan sarauta walida ta furta sanin mi kallonsa yake nufi gareta ta soma magana bayan ta karo sautin kukanta na sharri.

"Ni dai ranka ya dade bani da wata ƙawa se nafisa duk da dai ban sa wakake nufi taka mai maiba  amman kawata ɗayace nafisa wadda mummunar ƙaddara ta faɗa mata wadda tai dalilin rashin dawowarta school ɗinnan wadda wannanɗ kaddarar ta saka ni kaina bansan ina ta shiga ba a yanzun".....bata jirasaba ta cigaba "nafisa kawata ce marainiyar ALLAH marar cikakken gata babban kuskuren danai da ban nemi gidansuba tsahon shekarun da mukai da ita amman ita kam tana zuwarmin sosai kuma nasan dukkan halin datake ci..kawai wannan hutun mun koma segata ta zomun da sassafe da ɗaurin kayanta akanta akan itafa tafiya zatai sabida ayanzun haka tana dauke da ciki wata hudu wanda bazata taba yarda ya zauna ajikinta ba zubar dashi zatai ta koma karatunta ko ALLAH yasa tazama wata nan gaba amman tai alkawarin bazata taɓa gayan wanda yay mata cikin ba  sabida danginta duk sun ƙita bata da wanda zata nufa da wannan abun kunyar,ina kuka tana kuka tabar gidanmu dan wallahi cikin  harya fito amman daga ranar ban kuma ganintaba har yau ɗinnan......!!

*KUJI DAƊINKU ƳAN P.C DA WANNAN PAGE ƊIN NAKUNE KU ƊAYA*

_Typing  9:36 pm 2apr 2021_




Walida takai maganar tana kuka! sosai wanda ya fito fili.

Ɗan ɗago kanta tai a sace jin yayi ɗuff kamar babu halittar ɗan adam a office ɗin.

Bata kuma ji komai ba illah karar takalminsa alamun ya mike zebar office ɗin.

Cikin tsoro walida ta wara idanunta sosai tana kallonsa, sekuma tai saurin yin ƙasa da kanta.

Ƙaryar mai nazari ya gane a wani hali yake ciki sedai ƙwayar idanunsa data fidda zallan ɓacin rai wadda ta sami damar kaɗawa tai jajur kamar wadda akaima aman jini.

Daga saman kansa kuwa wata irin jijiyace ta miƙe tai wani irin shata layi kamar zata faso waje. har wani irin huci mai fitar amo! numfashinsa ke fitarwa amman sabida tsabar zurfin ciki gami da zafin rai tare da tsantsar miskilanci wanda ya game masa jini ya kasa bawa walida damar fuskantar a wani irin yanayi yake ciki.


Bai kuma ce mata ci kanki ba illa barinta dayay da mugun ƙamshinsa yabar office ɗin.

Saranda walida tai "anya feenah da gaske take shi yay mata wannan cikin?" walida ta furta hakan a fili cikin wani irin yanayi..

"Banga alamun firgita ko rikita agaresaba, naso naga yadda ze gigice amman ko alamar hakan ban ganiba, anya feenah ba sharri zatai masaba".

Ganin malamai suna kokarin kutsowa yasa tai saurin watsar da batun ta fita daga office ɗin ma baki ɗaya.


Cikin tangaɗi mai kama dana wanda yay maye ya nufi wajan motarsa jikinsa na wani irin tsuma, da sauri oska ya bude masa marufin mota ya faɗa baya tare da sada kansa a ƙasa.


Bai kula guards dinsa dake zagaye da motarba illa hannu daya ɗagama oska alamun su tafi kawai..

Duk sauran malamai da p.c dasuke tsaye suna jiran tsammani sakin baki sukai ganin yadda motocin suka barsu da ƙura.

Hannunsa ɗaya ya ɗora saman gorar ruwa ya ɓalle marufin tare da ɗorata a saman bakinsa bai kuma cireba harseda ya shanye tass sannnn ya cillata waje.

Sunkuyar da kansa yay ƙasa yana nanata kalmar "hasbinallahu wani'imal wakil" a bayyane amman cikin sautin ƙasa-ƙasa yadda babu mai jinsa.

"Zaka zama sila ta shigar rayuka biyu a ɓacin rai sannan zaka zama waraka garesu tare da warwara daga karshe ina umartarka daka ambaci kalmar hasbinallahu wani'imal wakil insha ALLAH komai ze warware da izinin ALLAH"!!!!

Sautin muryar wannan dattijuwar matar data taɓa gaya masa kenan ayayin dayaje zagayen aiki.

Tabbas wasu mutanan koba aljanu ba akwai waɗanda ALLAH yake wankewa idanunsu suga abinda kai baka ganiba shiyasa kada ka raina mutum duk ƙanƙantarsa gareka.

Duk da bai yarda da maganar tataba alokacin amman a yanzu yana ganin akwai lauje cikin naɗi acikin maganar tata.

Tabbas wannan yarinyar ƙaddarace ta kawota gidansa, harya mata ciki domin yasan shi kaɗai ya soma saninta a mace dan haka babu tantama wannan cikin nata gudan jininsane!

Daɗa ƙasa yay da kansa yana murɗa ƴan yatsunsa suna bada sautin ƙas! ƙas!! yana kuma cije lips ɗinsa kamar ze ciresu daga jikinsa baki ɗaya.

Babu abinda yake se aikin tunanin mafita wanda duk dai mafitar tasa ɗaya ce tak.

Har suka isa gida sautin karar horn ɗin motarsu yasa ya ɗago birkitattun idanunsa yana kallon window dasu har suka karasa cikin compound din gidan.

Da kansa ya bude marufin motar ya fice kamar wani kumurcin zaki batare daya tsaya jinta bakin su oskaba wanda atake oska yafara tunanin miyasamu ogansune hakan? ganin baya da mai basa amsa yasa ya bawa sauran guards ɗin umarni akan zasu iya wucewa shima ya kama hanyarsa kansa a kulle.

Cikin yanayi mai wuyar misali,  ya ƙarasa cikin ɓangaren nasa bai samu hidayaba may be kotana ɓangaren su mama ne domin sakina matar imran ta haihu.

Dan haka se kawai ya wuce bedroom ɗinsa daman kuma a yadda yake a wannan yanayin yafi buƙatar ya kaɗaice  shi ɗaya tak batare dawani ya damesaba.

Takalmansa da suka kasance rufaffu kawai ya tube tare da fara kai komo acikin katafaren bedroom ɗin nasa, iska yake faman kaiwa naushi kamar wani taɓaɓɓe.

"Babban kuskuren dazaki tafka shine kiyi gangancin zubdamin da ciki. wallahi! tallahi!! kika zubarmin da cikin dani kaɗai nasan yadda nasha walahar yi maki shi sena daure dukkan zuriyarku sekin ƙare rayuwarki agidan yari"!!

Yakai maganar kamar tana gabansa tamkar wanda ya taɓu ya zube a ƙasa tare da ɗaga hannunsa sama.

"Ya ALLAH nagode maka ina kuma sake gode maka ya ALLAH ka yafe mani abisa aikata laifin da ƙaddara ta haddasa man shi, ya ALLAH  kabani kyauta ina kuma godiya gareka na kuma amsa! ya ALLAH kada ka nufi wannan baiwa taka data zubarmin da gudan jinina ya ALLAH ka tserarmin da ɗana ko ƴata ya ALLAH ka sadani dasu cikin sauki".

Yakai maganar yana shafawa tare da zubo da wata zazzafar ƙwallah.

"Bayan kin haihu zan karbi abinda kika haifa na bawa hidaya ta shayar man dashi zan biyaki iya abinda harki gama rayuwar ki bazakiyi talauciba, na yarda na rasa kimata martaba kujerata muddin zaki haifamin wannan cikin dole na nemoki a duk inda kike".

Abayyane yakai wannan maganar wadda hatta hidaya wadda take kawo kai taso taji sautin muryar tasa amman kuma bata ji ainihin mai yake  faɗa ba.

Duk yadda taso gane kansa kasawa tai daga karshema ya mike yay bathroom anan ya ɓata lokaci sannan ya fito ya shirya kansa cikin coffee jallabiya, ya nufi part ɗin mama.

ALLAH yasoshi part din maamah babu kowa dan haka ya sami space na sakata agaba kamar wani karamin yaro yau babu zancen wata kara haka ya sakata agaba yan kuka da idanunsa kamar ba jarumin mazaba.

"Maamah kimin addu,a" yake shaida mata hakan bayan ya gama zayyane mata yadda sukai da walida.

Sosai maamah ta firgita da wannan bayanin nasa amman dayake akwaita da ilimin addini yasa ta soma rarrashinsa gami da kwantar masa da hankali itama kuma ta aminta da maganar tasa akan suna son duk abinda aka haifa din.

Bazasuki baiwar da ALLAH yay masuba wannan ma kuma wata jarabtace daga ALLAH wanda babu wani bawa daya isa ya ketare tasa kaddarar.

Sosai mamah ta zauna ta cire kunya tana kwantar masa da hankali sabida taga baki ɗaya tamkar baya cikin nutsuwarsa ma.

Bai baro part din ba se wajan sallar magariba abin mamaki yana idar da sallah ya fice a sace, bakaramin wuya yashaba sabida bai fiya dogon tuki kamar hakaba wasu daga cikin layukan gari ya soma bi koze ganta sedai ko alamunta, haka ya dawo gida yana faman bakin rai.


Koda yazo bacci seda yay ƙwalla.

"Ni ina nan cikin A/C ko ina my  unborn child ntake kwance oho?" yafaɗi hakan kamar wani zautacce.

Da ƙyar bacci ya kamasa amman tsakiyar dare nayi ya mike yafara kaiwa ubangiji kukansa.


                          HOSTEL.

Cikin sauri p.c ta nufi wajan baba vise akan ya kira mata walida taji mike faruwa.

Koda walida tazo bata bar wata ɓaraka ta fita daga wajantaba illa ma kawo wata karyar datai ta maka akan cewar daman akan maganar haurowar gardine wanda yay ma nafisa alkawarin ze dawo wai yaji mi ake ciki kuma da ya manta amman se yanzu wai yazo rangadi ya tuna shine ya viyo dan yaji yaya ake ciki kasan cewarsa mutum mai son cika alƙwari da waɗannan ƙaryaryakin walida ta shigar dasu.

Dayake sun san da wannan maganar shiyasa basu wani jata da tsahoba suka sallami walida tabar wajan.

Haka walida ta zauna tana faman nanata maganarta wadda take tan tamar anya nafisa ba sharri tai masa ba dan babu ta yadda zaka gane akwai wani abu agame dashi.

Amman koma dai maye ta barsa har suyi candy ta koma ta kara jin wata wainar suke toyawa tare da bawa feenah lavarin yadda sukai duk da tana tunanin kafinma lokacin ai ta haihu.
 WANNAN KENAN.

   Ɓangaren anwar kam.

Sosai ya rage walwala wataran ma ayyukansa se yaransa amintattu ke kular masa dasu.
Baya da lokacin wani abu se addu, sadaka ya dorata akan tada taimakon alumma akullum kuma yana kwana da tunanin ɗansa ko ƴarsa batare dayay tunanin feenah ba dan bata gabansa fatansa a kullum yay tozali da ita ta basa abinda ta haifa idan kuma ayanzu suka hadu ze riketane harta haihu tukunna. ( son kai com. )

Tun hidaya na damuwa da yadda ya koma harta kuma dangana sabida daman can ya lafiyar kura.

Haka rayuwar ta cigaba da tafiya kuma kusan kullum seya fita zagaye batare da sanin kowaba ko ALLAH ze saka yay tozali da ita.

Ɓangarenta.
Kimanin watanni takwas da samun cikinta yau ranar juma,a wadda tai dai dai da ɗaya ga watan azumin ramadan wanda ya rage sauran wata guda ta haihu.
Babu yadda umma batai da itaba akan kada ta dauki azumi amman taki gashi antashi da rashin wuta ga rana dole seka samu kankara zaka zauna lafiya.
