                  *JIN DAƊI GAREKU MY S.P FANS MUSAMMAN  AMINA NADAMA, HAUWA A CHINDO,MAMAN BAFFA, ALHAJI KABIRU, NAFISAT BASHIR,ANTY HAMIDA, DA SAURAN DABAN LISSAFOKUBA KUSANI INA MUKU SONSO DOMIN ALLAH* 💅


Tawani baje a tsakar gida akan tabarma tana tsintar shinkafa ƴar hausa, wadda da gyar tagama tsince baƙi-baƙin Cikin nata sannan ta busheta kana ta mike da gyar tana dafe saman cikinta sabida yadda cikin nata ya tutso gaba sosai kamar yau zata haihu.

Ta dafe wani ɗan tudu na gefen wajan inda suka adana turminsu, kana ta soma tafiya kamar zata faɗi ƙasa, zaman daɓaro tayi abakin rijiya bayan ta debo ruwan da gyar ta zuba a saman shinkafar wanda ya baiwa wannan ɗan mataccen ƙuntun damar watsuwa zuwa sama.

Sannan ta wanke hannunta na dama, ta soma yamutsa, shinkafar ma,ana tana cuɗa dattin cikinta, seda tai mata ruwa mai kyau har wajan biyar sannan ta zubata a ƙwalanda, domin taɗan sha, kasancewar shinkafar hausa tanada saurin damewa idan ba,a kulaba.

Takai wajan minti goma a wajan sannnn ta ɗibi, ruwan dake cikin wani bokiti, ta watsa a saman kanta jin kan nata ya dauki zafi. sannan ta mike da ƙyar, ta dauki wannan shinkafar tai hanyar kicin da ita a hannunta.

Leƙawa ɗakin umma sahura tayi bayan ta aje shinkafar a saman bencin dake adane acikin kicin ɗin, ganin tana kwance tana hutawa yasa ta jamata labule ta koma bakin aikinta wanda sam umma sahura bata so amman itan ta kafe sabida dokar da likita ya bata akan tana ɗan yawan motsa jikinta sabida koda naƙuda tazo mata kan ɗan baze wahalar fitowaba, wannan dalilin yasa duk bayan magariba ma take ɗan tattakawa zuwa bakin titin buxuwar ɗawisu, da ƙafa ta kuma dawo da ƙafa.

Kicin ɗin ta shiga ta zauna a saman kujera ƴar tsugunno sannan ta saka hannunta ta bude marufin tukunyar, wadda taketa zaɓal,ɓala alamun ta tafasa. kana ta soma zuba wannan shinkafa cikin nutsuwa.

Sannan ta rufe bayan ta saɓa marufin saboda, in case koda ze tafaso ya watso sama.

Zafine ya dameta abinka da kicin ɗan mitsitsi ta fito waje tare da ɗauko ledar salak, ta soma fito dashi tana gyarasa silla-sillah.seta da gama tsaf sannan ta miƙe ta dauko, gishiri da wuƙa ta nufi gaban rariya seda ta wankesa tass sannan ta koma ta zauna ta fara yankawa bayan ta gama yankawa nan ma seda ta kuma ɗaurayesa sannan ta aje acan saman bancin dake kicin.

Alokacin shinkafar ta fara watsowa sama tana kumfa, kwando da wani ɗan bokiti ta dauko ta fara kwasheta bayan taɗan saka hannunta ta latsata alamun taji yanayin laushinta. sannan ta kwasheta ta zuba a kwandon kana ta ɗauraye wannan ruwan mai kamar sitati. kana ta mayar da ita tukunyar domin ta turara, anan ta zauna tsahon wasu ƴan lokuta har shinkafar ta ƙarasa kana ta kwasheta ta zuba bisa kula, sannan ta soma kiciniyar haɗa miyar jajjage wadda ta wadata da kifin da mijin umma ya kawo ɗazu.

Seda ta kammala komai wanda ya shafi hadin miyar sannan tai kasa da rishon kana ta komo waje ta  zauna akan tabarma tana firfita da maficin gabar dake kusa da ita, jin jikinta yana wani irin ɗigar da zufa wadda tunda cikinta ya tsufa take masifar jin wannan gigitaccen zafin mai haka ka.

Tana nan zaune aka soma gabatar da tafsirin da ake a masallacin dake kusa da gidan lokacin take tunanin karfe biyar ɗin yammaci tayi, cikin dauriya ta koma kicin ta kashe rishon sannan ta nufi ɗakinta wata doguwar rigar atamfata ta dauka ta sauya sanan ta dauki katon hijabi ta zura kana ta ɗauki ƴar jakar datake aje ƴan kuɗinta,wanda baba yake bata a yayin daze fita kasuwa.

Ta zira wani bakin takalmi marar dubu, hannunta ta saka ta kare cikin nata sabida kunyar fita  haka take gashi yay wani irin girma wanda ya kasa buya acikin hijabin nata, duk da ALLAH ya taimaketa anan ɗin babu wanda yasan cikin bana halak bane ba sabida unguwar kamar kazo nazoce babu ruwan wani da wani, kauye kuwa gidansu wajansu kaka daman tunda akai wannan rikicin babu mai zuwa nan ɗin bare taji wata magana marar daɗi gwarama kaka tazo sau biyu, wanda da kuka suke rabuwa idan tazo ɗin.

Kuma duk zuwa takan zo mata da ƴan kayayyakin amfani, sabida acewar kaka hannunka baya ruɓewa ka yanke kayar, duk tsiya itaɗin jinin tace.

Dawanan tunanin ta share hawayen daya zubo mata kana ta fita tsakar gida.

Da umma sahura sukai kiciɓis tana kokarin fitowa daga ɗakinta.

"A,a nafi ina zakuma kije kina fama da kanki, nifa bamma so kikai azumin nan ba amman kin kafe yaya zanyi?". matsowa tai gareta sannan tace.

"Zanje babban titi na samo kankara dan wallahi bazan iya shan ruwa babu sanyi ba umma sahura bakiji yaddq cikinnan ke mini ba".

"A,a ki bari malam yazo seya je ya samo kekam kina fama da kanki ina zakije a haka".
Murmushi tai sannan ta kuma kallonta.
"Tabb nikam garau nake hajiya duba ma kiga babu abinda ban makiba kawai koma ki kwanta" takai maganar tana dariya dayake daman wataran kamar jika da kaka suke idan suna wata dariyar ma seka rantse batada wata damuwar wadda ita umma sahura tana hakan ne domin ganin ta mantar da ita bakin cikin dake cikin ranta.

Itama umma sahura dariya ta saka sannan tai saurin shiga kicin,  ɗin da sauri tana duba tukwanan cike kuma da ɗumbin mamaki ta fito waje.

"Ke ƴar albarka wadda aka haifeta a yayin da tsiya ta kwanta ubangiji ya baki mijin arziki yadda kikai min kema rabbana yay maki fiye da hakan yakuma rabaki da abinda yake cikin jikinki lafiya". takai maganar cikin tsananin murna tare da yaba ƙoƙari irin na nafisan.

Itakam dariya take sosai a wannan karan sannan ta dubi umma, "kai umma wannan addu,a haka to nagode ALLAH ya amsa sena dawo".

Tafaɗa tana jan kafarta wadda ta ɗan soma kumburin ɗaitar haihuwa, tai waje ganin garin ya soma lumshewa da alamun hadarin daya haɗo tun asubahi yana shirin zubar da ruwa, kuma tasan muddin umma sahura ta ankara da wannan hadarin bazata barta ta fitaba.

Cikin tsannin dauriya gami da hakuri irin nata take taka kafafunta.

Har ALLAH ya kaita bakin titi lafiya sannnn ta samu mai napep.

"Malam babban titi?"

"To naira talatin zaki bada hajiya".

Ɗan murmushi tayi "Tom babu damuwa ALLAH ya kaimu lafiya".tafaɗa bayan ta shiga cikin baburin.

Tafiyar mintuna goma ta kaita babban titi ta sauka ta basa kudin sannan ta nufi wajan ƴan ƙanƙara wadda ake zubota a irin katon buhun nan wadda ba,a yarda ashataba sedai a zubata acikin ruwan sanyi ya sanyaya da ita.

Abakin wajan ta tsaya "Assalamu alaikum malam kan kara nakeso ƴar naira ɗari da hamsin". tafaɗa tana zaro kudin ta mika masa ya amsa ya bata bayan ya saka mata a bakar leda sannan ta karɓa ta fita daga wajan.

Wajan masu saida kayan marmari ta nufa sabida tana bukatar ayaba zata haɗata da kwakwa ta markaɗa ta zuba mata kankara haka kawai yau take marmarin yin hakan.

Tana tsaye a wajan tana bada kudi aka soma wata irin iska mai ƙura gami da duhu, a gurguje ta amsa sannan ta nufi hanyar titi ganin har an soma yayyafi kaɗan-kaɗan.

Kafin takai titin ruwa ya kece wata yar rumfar kwano ta samu ta laɓe cikinta ga ruwan duk ya jika mata jikinta.

Ganin jama,a kowa yanata kama gabansa masu shaguna suna rufewa guri ya soma tsit! yasa tai ta maza tanajin gwara ta sai maganin mura akan ta zauna anan ita ɗaya, ga tsohon ciki bata son umma sahura ta ɗaga hankalinta.

Tafiya tai kaɗan ta isa bakin titi tana aje kayanta a gefe guda taja ta tsaya tare da tsirawa ƴan motocin da suke wucewa tsilla tsilla idanu.

Yau tunda ya tashi baya jin daɗin jikinsa sabida kwana biyun nan da baije wajan training ba seyake jin kamar ana masa duka acikin ƙashinsa, yau an tashi da rana ga azumi hidaya bata nan tun jiya tana wajan mahaifiyarta wadda bata ɗan ji daɗiba, shiryawa yay cikin red j.s waɗanda sukai masa matukar kyau tare da farin kwambos, sumar kansa ya gyarata tai wani irin kyau mai burgewa, ga kayan jikin nasa sunyi masa mugun kyau abinka da farin mutum.

Rigar j.s ɗin mai kajeran hannun ce seya saka fara mai dogon hannu ta cikinta wadda take da tsukakken wuya, dogon wondon j.s shima ya zomasa har ƙasa kasancewarsa mutum marar son fidda jikinsa waje.

Cikin sauri yake fita daga part ɗin nasa, ya nufi harabar gidan ya faɗa mota ya nufi  A & A ƊAWISU TRAINING PLACE. wajene da aka zuba kayayyakin motsa jiki kala kala harda ma wajan da aka ware domin mai buƙatar buga ƙwallon kwando kota ƙafa da dai sauran kayan motsa jiki, wanda wajan ya kasance na se wanda ya amsa sunansa acikin wannan gari ze halarcesa duba da yanayin tsaro da kuma tsadar wajan.

Bai wani jima ba kasancewar garin ana azumi ba kowane ma ze iya motsa jikinsa a wannan lokacin ba, shima sabida wannan nukurkusar ƙashin nasa yasa ya fito a wannan lokacin.

Ya fito daga wajan yana tsane kansa da wani farin towel wanda baki ɗaya jikinsa ya gama jika da zufa. sannan ya bude motarsa ya wurga towel ɗin kana ya fito da ruwan gora, ya wanke fuskars dashi sannan ya tube rigar sa ya wurga baya, ya rage dagashi se wannan farar ta ciki wadda ta fito da ainihin surarsa ta cikakken namiji maji karfi da lafiya.

Hanyar babban titi ya ɗauko sabida itace privacy seyafi tafiya cikin salama sabida karancin jama,ar wajan.

Ganin wani irin baƙin hadari ya haɗo a garin yasa yaɗan sassauta tuƙin nasa yana bi cikin salama.

Ruwanne ya kece kamar da bakin ƙwarya.

Tundaga nesa yake hango, kamar mutum a tsaye ruwa na faman dukansa ga hanyar kamar babu kowa.

Harya kai can gaba sekuma ya fara revars yana dawowa da baya, daga can ɗan nesa da ita ya tsaida motar dayake glass ɗin mai tintak ne shiya basa damar gane macece.

Cikin tausayi ya kashe motar sannan ya bude marufin motar ya fito waje duk da ruwan yana jikasa amman shi burinsa ya taimaketa, saboda wannan ruwan shi namiji ma yana jinsa bare mace wadda yake ganin kamar ma tana daukene da juna biyu.

Ita kam nafisa harta gaji da tsaiwar ga ƙafarta ta ƙara yin yimm sosai.

Sam bataji ƙarar tawowar mutum ba sedai kawai ganin sa tai tsaye akanta kamar hoto.

Shikam tsananin mamakin daya kusan kaisa ƙasa yaja ya tsaya yana kallonta jinsa yake kamar a mafarki sosai yaja ya tsaya yana binta da kallo ga ruwan na dukansu duka su biyun wanda daga nasa ɓangaren ma hatta sumar kansa ɗigar da ruwan takeyi.

Jin kamar anan kallonta yasa ta ɗago kanta cikin tsananin tsoro..


MANAGE🙏
48 - 49


Cikin wani irin tashin hankali firgici da tsantsar tsoro mai cike da wani irin rawar jiki, feenah ta ɗago tana kallonsa cike da ɗunbin mamaki.

Bakinta har yana rawa wajan furta kalmar "WA'INNAHU SULAIMANU WA'INNAHU BISIMILLAHIRRAMANIR RAHIM". Tana kuma dire kalmar ta soma jan ƙafafunta dasuka soma wata irin karkarwa kamar zasu ɓalle.

Gam-gam taji saukar hannunsa akan nata ya riƙe tamau rikon tsauri rikon da bata isa ta ƙwaceba.

Bakinta ya riga ya gama bushewa haka zalika idanunta babu alamun ruwa ko guda bare asaka ran zasu zubar da hawaye.

Ɗago kanta tai tare da tsira masa idanunta tana kallon yadda ya kafe cikin jikinta da ido kamar mai son gano wani abun.

Baki ɗaya jinsa yake kamar ba,a cikin wannan duniyarba, sabida tsabar yadda ya kaɗu da wannan lamari saka ɗayan hannun nasa yay yana goge idanunsa gani yake kamar suna masa gizo, tare da ɗago idanunsa ya dire akan ƴar ledar dake gabanta, tabbas ƙanƙarace aciki domin gata nan tana faman zubo da ruwa.

Wani irin ɓacin rai gami da tausayi sune suka tsirga masa,yanxun macen datake dauke da ɗansa ko ƴarsa itake tsaye anan ruwa na duka ita ke tsaye anan tare da wannan kazamar kankarar wadda kilama idan tasha tai masa asarar ɗansa.

Hannunsa har yana karkarwa wajan dagosa ya ɗora a saman tsinin cikin nata yana son ya taɓa yaga yes da gaskene cikin ne kokuwa.

Cikin wani irin hargitsats tsan kallo tare da zafin naman gaske, tai saurin saka hannunta ta doke masa hannun nasa sannan ta fara kokarin fincike ɗayan hannun nata daga cikin nasa, amman duk yadda taso yin hakan ta kasa.

"Malam wannan wani irin rainin wayone da rainin hankali zakazo kana kai hannunka kan cikina" takai maganar a dake batare dataji tsoro ko shakkarsaba.

Bai kulataba har seda ya ida nufinsa ma,ana ya shafi cikin nata kana ya ɗago jajayen idanunsa waɗanda nan take suka sauya kamanni saboda wannan kalmar data gaya masa sam baya kaunar rainin wayo haka zalika baya shiri da marar kunyar mutum.

"Ni bake na shafaba ajiyar danai anan na shafa wadda  naketa nemanta tsahon watanni ban sameta ba se yau".yakai maganar yana dubanta ta ƙasan idanunsa, yana mamakin ƙarancin shekarunta.

"Ni bansan kaba malam kuma ban gane wata ajiyarka ba ka iya sakarmin hannuna na wuce dan nagaji da dukan ruwa".
Takai maganar tana murguɗa masa bakinta.
Dariyar kin rainan wayo yay sannan ya kuma damƙe hannunta tamkar zata kufce masa.

"Bani da lokacin baki wata doguwar amsa amman ki sani wannan cikin nawane baya da mahaifi seni, cikin da kikai kokarin zubar dashi tunkafin ya bayyana amman tsabar addu,ar mahaifa data ƴan uwa ALLAH ya tserarmin dashi, idanma bakya bukatarsa ni a yau zan maki viza zuwa waje aje aciren ɗana ko ƴata ki tafi abinki nagode ƙwarai". yakai maganar cikin daure fuska babu alamun wasa.

A tsorace  ta ɗago da kanta tana kallonsa kamar yau ta soma ganinsa sekuma ga hawaye kamar famfo sun zubo mata.

"Kasani koda zaka kawo dukkan mutanan dake cikin wannan ƙasa akan naje aciren cikin nan wallahi sedai su kasheni, miye halaƙarka da cikina, tunda naje gidanka ka turmusheni ka makamin ciki ka san dashi, se yau daga ganina sama taka zakazo kana min wani magana kaje can da juyar matarka, idan fitsari banzane kazama tayisa mana, haka kurum baka san wani hali na shigaba baka san da yaya na raini cikin ba kawai samataka zakawani ce na baka to baza,a bada ba kai duk abinda zakai" mtswwwwwwwww! taja tsaki tare da fincike hannunta,

Awannan karonne ya ɗago idanunsa ya dubeta tare da sakin murmushin takaici, aransa yana aiyana tun yanzu tafara bani ciwon kai.

Cikin rashin damuwa da furucin nata ya dubeta a karo na biyu, shifa kurari ko firgici bai fiye tsorata shiba, yasha jin Barazana daga manyan ƙwarin dasuka fita waɗanda ma su ta ransa da lafiyarsa suke amman bai taɓa damuwaba bare ya saka hakan aransa hasalima idan suka haɗu awaje yana kokarin jansu ajikinsa ya nuna kamar bai san komaiba bare wannan ƴar wadda bada ban albarkar cikin jikinta  dataciba da meze sa ya tsaya da ita.

"Amman dai kinsan bazaki ma kanki cikiba ko?" bai jira amsartaba.

"Niba mugu bane akanki bazaki saka na soma muguntaba, salin alin kibani ɗana nikuma zan gina maki rayuwar da har abada bazaki nadama ba".

"Wannan cikin tunkafin ya fito kowa ya gansa yasan nawane sabida daga zafin naman yadda yake motsi ajikinki da yadda yay girma ai ni yayo babu ko tantama". yakai maganar yana binta da idanunsa.

Ɗan ɗaure fuska tai kana ta ƙwaɓeta kamar xata kurma ihu cikin ranta tana kuma mamakin yaya akai yasan tanada ciki oho?.

"Kaifa babbane agarinnan malam abun kunya bai karɓekaba kabar ni nizanji da dukkan wata matsalar dazan fuskanta banson sunanka ya ɓaci akan abinda nina jawa kaina dan haka kamanta daka taɓa haɗuwa dawata nafisa nina amince zan rike duk abinda ALLAH ya bani cikin nagarta".

Takai maganar tana saka ɗayan hannunta tana sharce ruwan dayake ɗigo mata.

Kallon mahaukaciya yay mata.

"Baki da wayo akan wannan cikin zan iya bada rayuwata bama kujera ba, mutuncin da kike faɗa ze bani ɗane? koko tunda nake kan kujerar ita ta taɓa bani ɗa? to kowa datasa ƙaddarar ni anan tawa take kuma na amsa bana kuma shakkar nunashi aduk inda nake, dan haka kima cire wannan gangancin acikin ranki" yagama faɗin hakan tare da fincikarta ya nufi mota da ita cikin zafin nama.

Ya buɗe gaban motar ya sakata tare da rufowa ya zagaya shima ya shiga.

Hanyar babban asibitinsa ya nufa da ita.

Babu abinda take se kuka harda majina"amman wallahi kai azzalumine kuma duk abinda kai min se ALLAH ya sakaaaaaaaa......"bata karasaba ya bige bakinta da hannunsa cikin ɓacin rai ya cigaba da driving ɗinsa bayan ya kure motar da karar redio.

Dafe bakinta tai kana ta ɗago idanunta "Ina zaka kuma kaini kaimin ciki na farko wani cikin xaka kuma min? dan ALLAH kabarni hakan nan yanzu nasan.............................

Duk inda marikiyata take hankalinta atashe yake".

Murmushi yay mai sauti kana ya ɗan dubeta ji shirme daman antaba ciki kan ciki lallai wannan ɗanyace.

"Inda ALLAH ma ze taimaken na kuma maki cikin kan cikin aida nazama ɗan gata". kawai taji saukar maganarsa a dake akanta.

Wani kukan ta kuma kecewa dashi"na rantse da ALLAH wancan ma tsautsayine amman yanzun da hankalina, daman lokacin ma soyayyar danake maka ita takaini yanzu kuma tunda ina da cikinka nasan ko banza zan sami mai kama dakai ko babu kai atare dani matukar ina kallon abinda zan haifa zanji dadi.

Kallon kuruciya yay mata "ato dakuma kince cikin banawa bane bakima sanni ba, amman gayanan kin tonawa kanki da kanki asiri".mtsw yaɗanja tsaki ganin surutun nata ya soma hawar masa kansa.

Har suka ƙarasa cikin wani katafaren asibiti wanda yaji kayan more rayuwa kamar babu gobe anata zabga kiran sallah, suka ƙarasa ciki. parking ya gyara yana kokarin fita daga motar bayan ya kasheta.

sekuma ya juyo ya tsareta da idanunsa,batare dakuma yace mata komaiba, ya zira hannunsa ya ɗauko gorar ruwa ya miko mata, seda ta karba sannan se  kuma ta kallesa.....!

MANAGE.
50 - 51




Taɗan dubeshi tare da kau da kanta gefe batare data amshi ruwan dayake miƙo mataba.
"Ki amsa malama zanje nai sallah kafin nan nai abin da zanyi anan ɗin semu wuce" yafaɗi hakan cikin kaushin murya!.
Tura baki tai gaba tamkar karamar yarinya sannan ta saka hannunta taja ruwan, cikin ranta tana masa ALLAH ya isa.
Kafawa tai abakinta tana faman sha kamar zata haɗa da gorar, shikam ya zuba mata idanunsa yana binta dasu cikin tausayawa batare daya, yi fitar dayakeson yay ɗinba.
Harta gama shan ruwan kana ta dire gorar ruwan tana lumshe idanunta.
Can kuma seta ɗago ta dubesa tare da yatsina fuskarta.
"Ina ayaba ta da ƙanƙarata tunda bazaka kaini gidaba kabani, abuna nasha yunwa nakeji". tafaɗa cikin tausayin kanta.
Shikam tsare ta yay da idanu kamar wani tsohon maye batare dayace mata ci kanki ba.
Can kuma setaga ya bude marufin, motar ya fita kamar, wanda yay mantuwa, tana ganin ya fita ta soma kiciniyar bude motar ta fice, amman ta makaraso sabida ya saka mata key, haka ta kifa kanta akan cinyarta tana kuka! gefe guda kuma tana tunanin yanxu awani hali umma sahura take oho??.
Mintuna kusan biyar segashi  nan ya dawo cikin motar amman a wannan karon fuskarsa daɗan ƙasa daga gani sallah yaje yayo ya dawo.
Hannunsa  rike da wata ƴar leda mai sheƙi ya miko mata, cikin tsananin, buƙatuwa da yunwa tai maza ta karɓi ledar, kamshin soyayyan dankali mai hade da nama da ɗan taruhu shiya daki hancinta, cike da zalama abinka ga mai bukata ta fara daukar guda ɗaya ta saka abakinta tana kuma kallonsa ta gefen idanunta shikam ko ruwama da alamun bai shaba, sema wayansa dataga ya rike yana faman daddannawa mintuwa kusan biyu taji yana magana.
"Hellow safiyan nifa zan wuce inada abubuwa agabana, kawai ka gayamin drugs ɗin sena, siya base kazo ba naga time ɗin yaja da yawa, gayanan maamah tana ta min waya tajini shuru" bataji amsar dana wayar ya basaba tadai ga ya katse wayar tare da ɗan  jan tsaki kana ya saka hannunshi ya yamutsa sumar kansa wadda taci uban kuɗi.
Duk yadda taso ta danne wannan dankalin ta kasa, daman wannan matsalar take da ita, ma,ana ita cikin nan nata yanada wani irin salo, duk abinda ta ɗora ranta akansa tofa idan bata cisaba babu lafiya, wannan dalilin yasa tunda ta dauki wannan dankalin ta saka abakinta takejin amai na taso mata, kamar ze fito mata ta hanjinta.
Tun tana daurewa har idanunta ya fito waje sosai tana kuma zubar da hawaye.
Kakarin amai ta somayi.
Cikin daure fuska taga ya juyo yana maka mata harara!
"Ke bani son sakarci wallahi kikai min amai amota sekin lashe abinki" yafaɗa cikin taking serious batare data alamun wargiba.
Kafin ma yaja wani lokaci ta soma zubo da aman dukkan dankalin dataci ɗin tare da saka hannunta ta dafe wuyanta wanda takejin kamar ze tsinko ya fito, wannan feshin aman kuma har rigarsa ya samu inda ma ALLAH ya taimaka bata zuba masa sosaiba.

Babu abinda take se uban haki da nishi!
Tana firfito da idanunta waje.
Shikam fasta yay yana kallonta baki ɗaya ta gama basa tausayi.
Daman haka masu cikin ke fama jiba daga cin abinci harta maido dashi yana tunanin ma ko ruwan bai zauna a cikintaba.
Seda ta gama aman tsaff sannan ta ɗago idanunta jajir! tana kallonsa kana ta ciro ɗan kwalinta mai kyau ta soma goge wajan hardama kan cinyarta.
Saukar hannunsa akan nata shiya kwance mata dukkan notikan da sukai saura ajikinta.
"Ke bani son ƙazanta da ɗankwalin naki kike goge aman?" yakai mata tambayar yana kallonta.
Bata kulashiba harta kammala sannan ta buɗe, glashin motar ta jefa kallabin kana ta komar da kanta ƙasa tana ƙyafe bakinta.
Can kuma ta dauki, sauran dankalin ta jefa a waje.
"Mizakici?" yafaɗa mata maganar yana mai kusanto da fuskarsa gareta sabida yadda yakejin wani irin mugun tausayin yarinyar daga gani wannan cikin nasa yana bata wahala.
"Ƙanƙarata zaka kawomin".
Takai maganar kamar zatai kuka!
"A,a bazaki sha kankara ba salon ki sakamin yarinya tai mura" yakai maganar still yana binta da ido.
Azuciyarta tana ayyana yadda yake kamar maye yawani tsirata da idanu kamar ze cinyeta domin acikin jikinta tana jin yadda idanun nasa ke yawon kallonta.
"Nidai ita zansha wallahi idan ban shaba bazan iya cin komai ba" takai maganar kamar zata kurma ihu!!
Hannunsa guda ya saka ya ɗago fuskarta yana binta da lumshashshun idanunsa masu mugun kaifi gami da dafi.
Baki ɗaya ta daburce ta susuce jin saukar hannun nasa bisa fuskarta, nishi taɗan ja kaɗan kana ta soma lumshe idanunta tana zubo da ruwan hawaye waɗanda shi kansa yakejin saukar ɗuminsu acikin tafin hannun nashi.
Sam bazata taɓa iya kallonsa ba, shiɗin wani irin mutum ne mai mugun ƙwarjini karyar ka,kaiya hada idanu dashi koda na minti ɗayane bare ka sami damar yi masa kallon kurulla tabbas bazaka iya ba.
Jikinta yana wani irin karkarwa ta saka hannunta guda,ta ture hannun nasa sannan ta cusa fuskarta cikin tsakiyar cinyarta tana sakin gunjin kuka!!.
"Miyasa mutanan gari da kowa ke maka kallon mutum nagari? amman ni miyasa kazama mugu agareni ka cutar dani da yawa sabida kaganni marainiya? koko domin kaga zuciyata mai rauni ce akanka?"
Takai maganar tana kuka sosai.
Tsit cikin motar tai harna tsahon mintuna biyu sannan taji amon muryarsa acikin kunnenta.
"Naannahhhhhhhhh, taji saukar muryarsa pls ɗago muyi magana mi kikeson nai maki?" yakai maganar yana jan Numfashi kamar mai cutar asma.
Mamaki fal ranta,duk duniya malam liman kakanta ɗaya ke ce mata nanah kuma tunda ya rasu babu wanda ya taɓa gaya mata wannan sunan sedai nafisan ta amman shi yau ya kirata da wannan suna dan haka cikin wani irin birkitaccen yanayi ta ɗago fuskarta fall hawaye tana dubatan sa da ita.
Hannun nasa still ya kuma kaiwa kan fuskarta a wannan karon goge mata hawayen dake zubo mata yay sannan ya mayar da kansa saman kujerar motar ya jingina.
Kamar kuma an tsikareshi taga ya sunkuyo zuwa kasa, ƙafarta ya ɗago ya ɗora akan cinyarsa, tare da saka hannunsa yana ja mata yatsun kafafun nata, waɗanda sukai wani irin kumburi, kamar sunja ruwa, wannan dalilin dama yasa ma ya biyo da ita cikin wannan asibitin dan ya karba mata magani lura dayay kamar kafar tata tana bukatar hakan.
"Niba mugu bane ba kamar yadda tunaninki ya baki hasalima ni ciki kawai nasan nai maki amman bani na kaiki gidanaba, amman sabida adalci irin nawa naji na amince da komai amman shine zaki kirani da wannan suna? mi kikeso ai maki? idan wannan cikinne bakya bukata nagaya maki zamuje a cireshi ki huta na huta ni kuma nai maki alkawarin baki kudin dazaki gina rayuwarki ta har abada batare dakin sha wahalaba".
Yakai maganar yana ɗan jan numfashi kaɗan kaɗan.
"Idan har kai mani haka tabbas sunanka ze sauya daga mugu agareni ya juya zuwa AZZALUMI!! saboda kai nasarar rabani da abu biyu masu matukar mahimmanci acikin rayuwata. nafarko soyayyarka wadda tabi jinin jikina ta lafe, na biyu gudan jininka wanda yanzu shine bugun zuciyata tabbas idan ka mani haka zan kashe kaina na huta kabarni naji da abu guda pls pls pls" takai maganar aburkice tana gunjin kuka,   wanda tasaka  bai san lokacin daya jawota zuwa kirjinsa ya rungumeta tsam tsam ba. 
Shafa mata gadon bayanta yake zuwa saman gashin kanta, yanajin kuma tsinin cikin dake jikinta tare da bugawar zuciyarta mai sauri.
Gabaki ɗaya ta riga ta gama basa tsoro ba,a son mace mai juna biyu tana waɗan nan abubuwan sabida hatsarine, wannan dalilin yasa ya hadata da jikinsa domin ya bata nutsuwa ta hakan amman bawai yana nufin yana sonta ba.
Sun kai mintuna kusan biyar ahakan harya samu ta daina kukan.
Wayarsa ce tai ƙara! safiyan ke kiransa kuma yasan halin safiyan da bin diddigi and kuma yasan wannan motar idan yaga bai ɗaukaba ze iya karasowa kuma shi bai son agansa da mace a motar sabida gudun zargin abin da yafi tsana.
Wannan dalilin yasa ya janyeta daga jikinsa tare da jinginar da ita a jikin kujera, sannan ya ɓalle marufin motar harya saka kafarsa guda a waje ya juyo yana kallonta.
"Bani minti goma please"!.
Batare daya jira amsarta ba yafita baki ɗaya.
Ɗagowa tai tana kallonsa cikin shaukin so.
"Tabbas kai ɗin nagari ne kamar yadda ake faɗa ka kasance mutum mai wa naƙasa dashi adalci tabbas kana da saukin kai kana da daɗin zama, duk da banga ko ƙwayar sona a idanunka ba amman na aminta da tausayinka gareni ƊAN BAIWA".
Tana kuma kai maganar ta duba side nata taji a rufe dajan jiki ta bude side nasa daya manta baiwa key ba ta fice daga motar tana share hawayenta.
Cikin zafin nama tabi ta karamar kofa ta fice, tana jin iska na kadata ta ko'ina kana ta sami napep ta haye......!!



*KAIIII NAFI KIN TARO PILLARS*😳😳😳😳😳😳😳
52 - 53


"Hajiya ina muka nufa naga garin yana kuma narkewa?"
Cewar mai napep ɗin da feena ta hau kenan bayan ya soma tafiya da ita.
Cikin wani irin yanayi ta dubesa sannan tace.
"Nan bakin hayin ɗawisu zamuje".
Takai maganar bayan ta sada kanta a ƙasa tana tsoron kada ya biyota hanya.
Har sukaje unguwar feenah bata dawo dai dai, ba gani take kamar ze iya biyota nan ɗin ma, sauka tai tare da ɗan sinkuyowa da ƙyar.
"Malam ɗan bani mintuna uku zanje na amso maka kudi a gida".
Takai maganar tana kallonsa.
Ɗan murmushi ya ƙaƙalo sannan ya dubeta.
"Haba baiwar ALLAH aiko da bani da imani na ganki a wannan yanayin zanji wani abu, wallahi ki bar kudinki nagode".
Yana kaiwa nan yaja baburinsa zebar wajan.
Ɗan jimmm tai kana taɗan ƙaƙalo murmushi.
"Nagode bawan ALLAH ubangiji ya biyaka da mafificin alkairinsa Ameen".
Takai maganar tana murmushi kanq taja kafafunta tai cikin layin nasu.
Ɗan ja tai kaɗan ta tsaya tana sakin murmushi ita ɗaya.
"Tabbas muhammad anwarr kai mutum ne nagari miyasa tundaga lokacin dana ɗauki cikinka ALLAH bai sa na wulaƙanta ba,mai yasa jama,a da dama suke tausayamin aduk sanda zan gamu da wani tofa seya nuna tausayinsa gareni.......tabbas wannan ma wata baiwa ce daga ALLAH kaiɗin mutum ne nagari wannan dalilin yasa jininka baze taɓa tozarta ba, domin ka rufawa mutane da dama asiri ka tausayawa mutane, kaima shiyasa,ALLAH yake tausayawa naka".
Takai maganar tana sakin murmushi kaɗan akan fuskarta haka kurum take hasko movement ɗinsu na ɗazu ahaka harta karasa cikin gidan umma sahura.
Tundaga bakin kofa take jin kamar alamun shashshekar kuka! cikin azama taja jikinta tai ciki hartana hardewa, sekuma ta gyara tafiyar tata azuciyarta tana, fina kula wannan mutumin baze taɓa ɗaga maki kafaba idan wani abun ya sami ɗansa ko ƴarsa ya ɗaureki a iska, da wannn tunanin ta karasa tana hango umma sahura zaune tana kuka! baba mijinta yana gefe yana mata nasiha.
Jin sallamarta yasa umma sahura ɗagowa da sauri.
"Nafi daga ina kike kin ɗagamin hankalina! nace daga gidan ubanwa kike tun yamma har yanzu kina ina newai?".
Rage tsahon ta tai sannan ta dubeta.
"Umma kimin alfarma wallahi ganin ruwa yasa na fake a hanya wanda na hadu da marasa imani.........." ta lafto mata karyar kare kai dan bata son umma ta gano komai.
Bayan umma tagamajin komai sekuma tahau hamdala tana fama sakin hamdalar sosai! "Allah nagode maka ni sahura inda waɗan nan mutane sun far maki dayaya xakiyi maza wuce daki ki sauya kaya kizo muci abinci danni ma na kasa kai komai bakina kedai barka, yau babu inda banjeba nemanki ba" takai maganar tana murna shikam baba mijinta fita yay yana dariya.
Itakam nafisa ɗakin tai tana dariyar itama dan umma ta bata dariya wannan gigitar haka.
Sauya kayan jikinta tai amman still kamshin mayen turarensa yana manne cikin kayan nata rungume kayan tai tana lumshe idanunta tanajin wata irin kasala na saukar mata wadda ta kasa gane kota maye.
Adaddafe ta sauya kaya, kana ta fita seda tai alwala tai sallar magariba sannan ta fita suka fara shan ruwan wanda seda baba ya siyo mata ayabar sannan aka zauna lafiya.
Da ɗaya a hannunta tai cikin ɗaki ta kwanta bisa gado tana kallon rufin ɗakin da haka aka kawo nepa da ƙyar ta tashi ta kashe ƙwai sannan ta kwanta tana jin yadda cikinta ke motsi tamkar ze bito duniya.
"Watakan kaima kaji hannun dadynka ko shine xaka damen" tai wannan maganar kamar cikin nata naganin ta tanayi tana cin ayabarta hartai bacci ahakan.



"Wannan zata shafa a ƙafar wannan kuma zata sha kafin ta kwanta, Amman but gaskiya wannan fa na masu cikine ALLAH sa dai hidaya ta samu karuwa ne".
Dr safiyan yakai maganar bayan ya zuba masa maganin a ledar.
Shikam kamar ze kaiwa safiyan maka haka yakejin kansa waftar ledarma yay kana ya saka kai ya fita a office ɗin tamkar ze tashi sama.
"Ikon ALLAH Nifa idan wannan yana wani abu wataran har dariya yake bani komai cikin zafi". dr safiyan yakai maganar yana dariya kasa kasa ganin bai gama fita ba karya dawo ya sauke masa kwandon
Wayarsa ke ringing! ganin mai kiran yasa yaja ya tsaya ya rabe awani kwana gudun kada wani ya gansa, sannan ya dauka maamah ce cikin alkunya gami da kara gefe guda kuma tsantsar damuwa dashi, take tambayarsa ina ya tafi tun ɗazu bata gansaba? amsa ya bata dayana asibiti amman gayanan zuwa gidan yanzu.
Cikin zafin nama ya mayar da wayar cikin aljihun jc ɗin kana ya cigaba da tafiya.
Abakin mota ya tsaya yana kokarin ya bude.
Yana kuma ayyana yar rigmar nan tai bacci may be, ko yaya zata masa kwatancen inda take oho? gashi ba,a son tashinsu idan suna barci, amman dole daman yaje yaga inda take ayau sabida zuwa gobe asan abunyi.
Wani irin duka gabansa yay lokacin daya bude kofar motar yaga ashe babu key, gashi kuma bai ganta ba.
Haka yayta faman lalube tamkar wani zautacce har bakin gate yaje yana faman baza ido kamar ze hauka.
"Lahhhhh wanake gani kamar ƊAN MAJALISARMU" yaji saukar muryar wasu dandazon samari a kunnensa cikin axama ya koma da baya ya fada mota ya fice daga asibitin ganin za,a tara masa mutane.
ALLA kaɗai ya kaisa gida lafiya amman tsananin tashin hankali ya gansa.
Part ɗin mama ya yada zango ya zauna tamkar yaro karami yana mata bayani yana ƙwallar takaici.
Seda yagama mata bayani sannan ya dubeta.
"Wallahi mama dana ganta kota haihu ko bata haihuba sena karbi abuna"......!
54 - 55

Ɗan nisawa maamah tayi kaɗan cikin zulumin wannan cakuɗaɗɗan yanayi da ɗan nata yazo mata dashi sannan ta ɗago ta dubesa.
"Kai yanzu idanma ta baka ɗan ko ƴar mizakai dashi ɗanda ba akan sunna aka sameshiba?".
Ta kai masa wannan tambayar tana mai tsareshi da idanunta.
Wani irin ɗaci yaji acikin zuciyarsa jiyake tamkar zuciyarsa zata faso a wannan karon.
Kuma yanajin itaɗin ce kaɗai zata iya kallonsa ta gaya masa hakan batare daya ɗauki matakin komaiba.
Ɗan cije ƙasan laɓɓansa yay kana ya ɗago rinannun idanunsa waɗanda daman tun can da kalar hakan ya shigo gidan dasu, tare da zuba mata batare daya tanka mata ba illah hucin dayake fesarwa kaɗai.
"Magana nake maka idan baka da amsa ni wuce kabani waje". 
Takai maganar bayan ta maka masa wata katuwar harara.
Bai ce mata komai ba illah mikewa dayay tsaye tare da barin ɗakin nata cikin wani irin yanayi.
Itakam mamah tai masa hakan ne badan bata karɓi ƙaddararsu ba tai masa hakan ne domin ta nuna masa cewarfa ba komai ake samu ta sauƙi ba koda yay nasarar amsar ɗan daga wajan yarinyar tofa akwai wani ƙalubalen agabansa.

Tamkar bugagge haka yake haɗa hanya adai dai kwanar dazata sadashi da kofar fita, sukai kiciɓus da anty, cikin zafin nama, anty tasha gabansa tana mai tsareshi da idanunta.
"Ɗan gidana lafiya naganka ahaka?"anty takai masa wannan tambayar tana jinta acikin matuƙar damuwa.
Zama yay ragwajaf akan ɗaya daga cikin Royal ɗin falon tare da sada kansa ƙasa yana yamutsa sumar kansa, ganin haka yasa anty tai maza ta shiga kicin, ruwa mai sanyi ta ɗibo masa, acikin glass, cup tare da zuwa gabansa ta miko masa.
Batare da yayi musu ba ya amshi cup, ɗin ya kafa a bakinsa bai kuma direba seda ya shanyesa tass.
Sannan ya sada kansa a ƙasa yana jinsa acikin wani irin bakin ciki marar misaltuwa acikin xuciyarsa kuwa yana ayyana ALLAH kai ka jarabcen da wannan ƙaddarar kuma na amsa da hannu biyu koda duk kowa ze juyamin baya akan wannan kyautar dakamin to nifa na amsa kuma ina godiya gareka ubangijina.
"Azubo maka tuwo?" 
Yaji saukar muryar anty kunnensa tana yi masa magana cikin damuwa, amman yasan duk damuwar dazata shiga bazata taɓa takurashi akan ya gaya mata meke damunshi ba sabida tasan halinsa mutum marar son tambaya.
Ɗagowa yay ya dubeta  sannan ya girgiza kansa alamun a,a.
Itakam anty zama tai sabida tasan tunda yace baya bukata tofa hakanne.
"Muhammad meyake damunka?"
Takuma tambayarsa akaro na biyu.
Numfasawa yay kana ya ɗago ya dubeta batare daya ɗauke idanunsa akantaba ya soma labarta mata dukkan abinda ke faruwa.
Sosai ta kaɗu da wannan bayanin nasa amman seta basar tare da ɗagowa ta maka masa harara.
"Amman shine baku taɓa sakani acikin wannan maganar ba koko kuna zargin zan muku ba dai dai bane ba aciki?"
Awannan karon ranta ya ɓaci sosai.
Lumshe idanunsa yay kana ya ɗago ya dubeta.
"ALLAH ya huci zuciyar anty na duk a tunanina maamah zata gaya maki wallahi ban san baki saniba ni ayy".
Yafaɗi maganar iya bakin gaskiyarsa dan duk tunaninsa maamah ta shaidawa anty komai.
"Nikam bata gayamin hakanba, amman miyasa maamah zata faɗi hakan bayan ita da kanta tasan ƙaddara bata bar komaiba, mukam har suna zamuyi insha ALLAH ban da ALLAH waze baka ɗa".
Takai maganar tana jin mai farin ciki aranta tare da jin tausayin sa sosai yadda taga yaɗaga hankalinsa akan wannan haihuwar tasa.
Zama sosai anty tai tare da dubansa tana gaya masa maganganu masu sanyi tare da bashi shawara sosai akan hakan.
Kuma babu laifi yaji ya sami rangwamen wasu tarin damuwar acikin ransa sannan ya miƙe yay mata sallama ya tafi part ɗinsa.


Anty yana fita ta kwantar da zuby wadda tagama rigimar bacci sannan tai part ɗin maamah.
Sallama tai mata cikin nutsuwa sannan suka gaisa kamar ba kishiyoyiba abin sai san barka.
Anan anty ta zayyanewa maamah komai sannan ta ɗora da nuna ɓacin ranta akan yadda basu gaya mata maganarba.
Itama maama acikin ranta mamakine fall na yadda anawar bai gayawa anty ba lallai ta yarda ɗa da mahaifi se ALLAH.
Seta ɓoye laifinsa ta nunawa anty ita kunyar sanar matan take wanda ita ai yadda take ganinsa shida anty suna shiri duk atunaninta yariga daya gayawaa antyn tuni amman shi ashe bai gaya mata ba.
Sannan ta ɗora da cewa.
"Umman zubaida". ( sunan da mama ke kiran anty dashi kenan )
Abinda yasa na gaya masa wannan furucin na ɗazu naga sam wannan yaron baya tuna yarinyar nan data ɗauki rainon cikin harta haifesa shikam kawai a kullum burinsa ya karɓi abunda ta haifa wanda hakan sam banga adalci acikin lamarin ba wannan dalilin yasa na soma nuna masa rashin amincewata tuni sabida nasan ɗacin raba ɗa da mahaifi kasancewata uwa".
Maamah takai maganar tana kallon anty.
Itakam anty sada kanta tai ƙasa sannan ta ɗago ta saki murmushi kana tace.
"Adai sassauta mana maaman imran dan ALLAH kada abin nan ya hadewa ɗan gidana shi ɗaya ki barsa da tashin hankalin neman wannan yarinyar, kina fa kallo duk san sa da aiki amman yanzu bai fiye fita ba sedai ya saka yaransa pls mamah muhaɗu mukwantar masa da hankalinsa ALLAH ya shige mana gaba baki ɗaya".
Takai maganar tana duban mama. wadda tai burus kamar bata jinta, amman sarai tana jin antyn itadai tsarin daya ɗaukone bai mata ba akan mi zaka nemi raba ɗa da mahaifi.
Daga wannan maganar kuma suka faɗa wata hirar daban.
Se can dare anty tabar part ɗin mama ta nufi nata part ɗin.


Shikam yana zuwa part ɗinsa ya hau sama direct bathroom ya nufa cire kayansa yay kana ya sakarma kansa ruwa na tsahon mintuna 30 Sannan ya fito daure da tawul, agurguje ya shirya kansa cikin farar jallabiya tare da komawa toilet  ya ɗauro alwala, yazo ya tayar da sallah, ko acikin sallar tasama addu,a guda yake nanatawa ALLAH ya bayyana masa ita cikin koshin lafiya.
Sanin hidaya bata nan yasa ya shiga kicin da kansa ya haɗa ruwan lipton zallah yazo ya zauna a falo yana sha.wanda da iya shi kaɗai yay buɗa baki,Sannan ya kuma kowama bedroom ɗinsa ya tube jallabiyar ya haye kan gado yana ayyana yadda suka haɗu ɗazu jiyake tamkar yay tsalle ya gansa agaban ɗansa ko ƴarsa.
Cikin haka baccin gajiya ya kwashesa.


Kimananin sati biyu kenan yana faman zaryar neman nafeesah agarin nasu amman ya nemi ko mai kamarta ya rasa.
Hatta hidaya data dawo ta kasa gane kansa haka zalika su oska sun kasa gane kan uban gidan nasu.
Damma ALLAH yayosa mutum mai barin abu arai ahakanma yana aje neman nata yay abun dake gabansa amman ana ce maka magariba tayi tofa dabino da ruwa kaɗai yaka iya sha daga nan se yawon neman nafi.
Baya dawowa gida seyay asham daga nan ya wuce part ɗin mama wadda yanzu ya koma mata tamkar bebe daga gaisuwa yake jan bakinsa ya tsuke wanda yake matukar bata tausayi ba kaɗan ba.
Ahaka har azumin yazo karshe karshe anwar yana faman bulayi amman babu nafi babu alamunta.
20 ga ramadan dai dai, suka ɗaga umara dukkansu harda su anty hidaya da sauran ƴan gidan.


Ɓangarenta.....!!
56 - 57


Ɓangaren nafisa.

Sosai ta mayar da hankalinta akan ibadunta sakamakon ƙaratowar ƙarshen wata mai daraja.
Kuma bata  fasa yin azumin nata ba, duk da umma sahura ba son tai ɗin take ba amman ita kam bata, fasa ba kullum setayi azumin nata.
Gefe guda kuma ga watan haihuwar ta ya ƙarato sosai cikin nata yake sake yin wani irin girma na fitar hankali, yanzu idan ka dubeta sau ɗaya seka tausaya mata sabida yadda cikin nata ya turo gaba kamar wadda zata haifi ƴan uku.
Ga ƙafafunta sunyi wani irin kumburi tamkar zasu faso gaba,amman idan ka dubeta ta rame sosai sedai girman cikin kawai amman babu ita sam.
Sabida itafa a kullum ta kwanta babu abinda take tunani se yadda zata fara nakuda sabida taji ance akwai wahala abun babu sauki ko guda.
Haka aka cigaba da gudanar da azumin dukkan alummar musulmi sun mayar da kai akan ibadunsu domin rabauta da ramahamar ubangiji.
Haka zalika umma sahura ta samu ta kammala dukkan ƴan ƙananun siyayyarta ta haihuwa hatta bawon wanka ta saya hatta rezar daza,a yankema jaririn cibiya seda ta kammala siya ta aje waje guda.



           Saudiya.

Tunda suka sauka acan ya tare a masallaci babu abin da yake se faman bautar ubangijinsa, babu dare babu rana hatta su maamah sedai su haɗu dashi a bakin harami  haka lokaci yayta tafiya har ALLAH ya kawo karshen azumi ana ya gobe sallah jirginsu ya dira a kasarmu nigeria.

Sosai ya sami yan tarba da yawa duk da zuwan nasu na darene haka suka tattara sukai gida daga airport.

Yana kuma ji acin ransa ALLAH ya amsa addu,arsa domin tunda yaje yake addu,ar akan ALLAH yasa komai yazo masa cikin sauki agame da lamuran wannan yarinyar.

Itama mamah addu,ar tata kenan haka zalika anty tana wannan addu,ar dama wadda ta dameta.
Itakam hidaya addu,arta ALLAH ya bata haihuwa itama duk da ba wannan ne zuwansu na farko ba amman suna kokari duk ramadan idan sunje addu,arsu tafi yawa saboda garaɓasar watan.

            WANNAN KENAN.

Yau ta kama ana yi gobe sallah tunda yamma baba ya kawo kajinsa guda biyu manya, tafasasu umma tai sannan ta soya, lokacin nafisa ita kuma tana ɗaki tana tsifar kanta sabida taci burin koda azaune ne setaje sallar idi.

Tana gama tsifar ta fito waje ruwa ta ɗiba tare da saka shamfo ta wanke kanta tass sannan ta gyarasa tare da shafa mai kana ta saka ribom.

Ɗaki ta koma ta tattare sannan ta fito waje gaban umma taja ta zauna wadda take zaune tana gyara kayan miyar taushen dazasuyi.

"Umma baba ya kawomin ɗinki?" takai maganar tana kallon umma sahura.
"A,a sedai zuwa dare har hijabin ze taho maki dashi sabida ki samu kije idin tunda kin matsa, amman ni senaga kamar nakudar tsaye kike?" umma takai maganar tana tsareta da idanunta.

"Wallahi umma ni bana komai ki yarda kawai dai ina ɗanjin ciwon mara ne amman shima ɗin ba sosai ba?'.

Murmushi umma sahura tai sannan tace "Ai alamun kamun gida kika soma ALLAH dai ya kawo ta lafiya".mikewa tai da ƙyar tana cije vakinta batare data kuma magana ba tai cikin ɗakinta.

Zuwa wajan sallar magariba umma ta kammala aikin komai har lokacin da baba ya dawo daga kasuwa, bayan ya huta ya mikowa nafisa sabuwar atamfar daya sai mata ta sallah wadda aka ɗinkata zuwa doguwar riga, kuma babu laifi ɗinki yayi kyau se katon hijabinta fari tas har kasa.

Sabida tsabar murna nafisa har kukan daɗi tayi na ganin yadda bayin ALLAHN suke kula da ita sannan tai masa godiya kana ta dibi kayan tai cikin ɗakinta dasu.

Bayan shan ruwa ta samu ta lallaɓa umma ta fita zuwa bakin layin tasiyo lalle awani gida sannan tazo ta haɗa ta ƙunsa a hannunta, ita dai umma dariyar nafisa kawai take tana mamakin yadda take abu tamkar yar yarinya karama.

Haka dare ya tsala garin ALLAH ya waye, wajan karfe takwas dukkan wani aiki umma sahura ta kammala shi tass, hatta tuwon gidan ta kwashe ta share ko ina.

Itakam nafisa tana gado tana barci bata ma san gari yay haka ba seda umma sahura taje ɗakin ta tayar da ita sannan ta mike tana mamakin yadda lokaci yaja.

Bayi ta nufa tai wanka sannan tazo seda taci tuwo sannan ta shirya cikin farin ciki.

Tana kammalawa umma sahura ta fito, itama tai shirin masallacin sannan, suka hadu da nafisa baba ma ya shirya sabida baki ɗayansu zasu wuce babban masallacin garin.

Karfe takwas da rabi suka fito kowa yaci gayunsa baba ne ya kullo gidan sukuma suka wuce zuwa titi domin tarar baburin dazasu hau.

Yau take sallah dukkan alummar musulmi suna kokarin shiryawa domin fita yin sallar idi, daga gefe ɗaya kuma mata suna gida suna faman haɗa girki, wannan lamarin shiya kasance dasu umma sahura da baba da nafisa wadda take taka kafarta daƙyar haka suka ɗunguma suka shiga napep zuwa babban masallacin dake cikin garin ɗawisu.

Koda suka sauka ran umma sahura ya ɓaci sabida yadda mutane sukai tai mata magana akan yaya tabar nafisa ta fito da wannan tsohon cikin haka.

Abinda yasa ranta ya ɓaci sabida ai seda tace mata kada ta fito amman ta kafe aikuwa sunsha magana wajan jama,a wasu na mata sannu wasu naiwa umma fada sabida yadda cikin ya koma yay wani irin mahaukacin girma.

Haka baba ya nufi bangaren maza sukuma sukai na mata wadda sallar ma se a zaune nafisa tayita.

Ana idarwa ta shafa addu,arta sannan ta soma kokarin ɗagowa.

Cikin mamaki taga wata farar mace mai kyau ga yarinya agefenta tana kallonta.
"Sannu kona tai maka maki?" cewar matar wadda taketa faman bin nafisan da kallo.
Da murmushi asaman fuskarta tace.
"A,a Umma zan iya bara ma kig.....kafun ta karasa taji mararta tai wata irin murɗawa cikin azama wannan matar tayo kanta tare da fara ce mata sannu cikin kiɗima umma sahura tana can gaba ga jama,a sunata tururuwar fita ganin haka yasa matar ta kwaɗawa ƴar budurwar dake gabanta kira.
"Shahida! maza ki ɗaukarmin zuby ki kama afra ku fita wajan su maamah ganinan zuwa".
Takai maganar tana tallafo nafisa jikinta wadda baki ɗaya tafara fita a hayyacinta dan da alama nakudar ce tazo ganga ganga.

Da dubara ta kamata sukai waje can wajan wata bakar mota ta nufa da nafisa a tallafe ajikinta tana faman zubda ƙwalla.
Itakam umma sahura tana can tana faman bulayin neman nafisa tana kuma tambayar mutan wajan ko sunga yarinya mai tsohon ciki.
Da ƙyar ta sami wadda tace aikam taga wata mata da mai ciki ajikinta sunyi waje.
Tun kafin ma matar ta gama fada umma sahura tai wajan aguje.
Wanda kuma a dai dai wannan lokacin maamah tabawa driver umarni akan asaka nafisa a mota su tafi asibiti.
Anty wadda take tallafe da nafisa data soma jijjiga kamar mai iska ita ta tallafamata suka shiga motar driver yaja sukai gaba cikin sauri.
58 - 59


Turus umma sahura tai tamkar wadda tai losing control ta ɗora hannu aka zata zuba ihu!

Cikin wannan yanayin sega shahida tare da hidaya sun nufo gaban ɗaya motar zasu shiga.
Cikin azama umma sahura tayo garesu ko gaisuwa bata tsayaba ta soma rokarsu akan suyiwa ALLAH su kaita wajan waɗannan mutanan.......tai masu bayanin komai kuma cikin sauki shahida ta gano komai saboda agabanta akai dan haka suka sakata a cikin motar tasu driver yaja suka tafi, amman a gida aka aje hidaya dan itakam tace bazata jeba tana da aiki agabanta

Shahida da umma sahura sune suka wuce asibitin.


Yana sanye cikin wata dakakkiyar farar shadda tass wadda tasha ɗinki sarauta ya zuba hula wadda ta dace da kayan jikin nasa daga kafarsa kuwa takalmine na zallar fata rufaffe, hannunsa yana ɗaure da agogon zallar azurfa mai uban kyau, gaskiya bakaramin kyau yay ba abin se son barka.
Fuskarsa babu abun da take illa daukar idanu tare da sheki da walwali,gefensa jama,a ne  fal haka zalika ga ƴan jaridu dama sauran masu kafafen yaɗa labarai sunata faman daukar fitowarsa daga sallar idi tare dashi akwai mai girma governor, bayan sa mai martaba sarkin ɗawisu, damasu faɗa aji acikin wannan garin babu abin da ake awajan face gaisuwar girma da mutunci.

Sun ɗauki manyan lokuta suna faman gaisawar wadda hatta ciyamomi seda suka halarci wajan haka zalika shugaban jam'iyarsu shima ya sami halartar sallar wadda wannan gaishe gaishen shine ya cinye masa manyan lokuta a wannan masallaci.

Seda tawagar mai girma governor, tabar wajan sannan shima ya samu ya yakice jikinsa da ƙyar ya nufi mota, bayan ya gama.wa jama,ar wajan ɓarin naira.

Dagashi se oska acikin farar motar tasa kirar wannan zamanin ko driver bai daukaba yau haka kawai yakejin gavansa yana faɗuwa.
Guards ɗinsa ya dakatar tare da ce masu kowa yaje ga iyalansa yau take sallah suyi zarafin gabansu.
Bayan ya bawa oska kudi masu kauri ya rarraba masu.

Haka ko wannensu ya  nufi gida yana yaba halin mai girma mas! na mutuncinsa da adalcinsa da kuma sanin ya kamatan sa.

Yana daga bayan motar yana jingine da jikin kujerar, oska yana gaba yana driving.

Wayarsa ya zaro daga cikin aljihun wondon farar shaddar dake jikinsa ,yana son yaje can family hause ɗinsu ya gaisar da kakanninshi wannan dalilin yasa ya shiga contact ɗinsa ya lalubo number maamah dan yay mata barka da sallah kafin ya dawo gidan.

Cikin kasa da murya kamar ko yaushe ya dubi oska.
"Kawuce dani gidan innah"
Cikin ladabi oska ya rissunar da kansa yana kuma sauya akalar motar tasa zuwa unguwar kakannin na uban gidan nasa wanda, aka haifi mahaifinsa acan.

Wayar mamah hartai ringing! kusan uku bata ɗaga ba hakan yasa ya kira ta hidaya wadda take zaune anci uban ado na kece raini tana faman jiran mijin nata.
"Hellow dee" 
Hidaya ta faɗa a yangance, cikin ɗan saurin bakinsa kaɗan wanda ya zamar masa jiki ya furta.
"Na,am kinga zanje gidan inna na kira layin maah bata  ɗauka ba, ki haɗamun abinci da ALLAH"  Yakai maganar cikin alamun bata umarni.

Ɗan nisawa hidaya tayi kana tace.
"Eh ai gidan ma babu kowa nice kaɗai nima natahone domin nahaɗa maka abinci".
Waro manyan idanunshi yay tare da cewa.
"Suna ina kenan?" 
Yay mata maganar cikin sigar tambaya.
"Nikam ban sani ba amman kamar dai naji sunje hospital".
"What"!!!
Yaɗan faɗi maganar cikin kaɗuwa kafin ma yace wani abun ta karasa maganar.
"Ka kwantar da hankalinka fa bawani serious problem bane bafa, but kasan maamah kusan halinku guda da ita, anty ce ta gamu da wata mai ciki a masallaci wai tana naƙuda shine fa suka sakata zuwa can ɗin". takai maganar cikin halin ko in kula.
Tun kafin ma tace komai ya datse kiran tare da shafar kansa.
"Ya salam yaya maamah zatai man haka tasan yadda garin ke fama da rashin tsaro gashi na kirata bata dauka yanxun haka idan wani ya kula dasu se a cutarmin dasu cikin sauki ko ai amfani dasu aci galabata tunda babu securty's atare dasu". yakai maganar a bayyane.
Tare kuma da umartar oska akan ya sauya akalar motar zuwa asibitin sa dan yana da yakinin nan zasuje.

Oska na driving shikuma yana try number mamah amman still ba,a dauka ba.
Ahaka suka isa cikin asibitin wanda kafin lokacin ya kira layin maamah yafi nawa.
Duk yadda yakai da dauriyarsa seda yaji babu dadi ahaka suka karasa special place, wanda acikin asibitin an waresa ne dominsu isu isu familyn su sabida asibitin daman nasane.
Kuma wajan babu wata hayaniya ta kofar baya oska yabi har suka samu suka karasa cikin wajan.

Tun kafin ma oska yay wani yunkuri anwarr ya balle marufin motar yafice zuwa cikin wajan.

Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke yayin dayaga motar gidan afake daga can ciki bai tsayaba haka yayta zuba sauri kamar bashi ba har zuwa reciption ɗin. da shahida yaci karo tana jijjiga zuby a kafadarta wadda take kuka!

Bai tsaya jin gaisuwarta ba yay ciki a zaune ya tarar da maamah akan wata kujerar karfe daga gefenta wata mata ta rafka uban tagumi.
Sallama yay masu cikin kamilar muryarsa, cikin nutsuwa kuma ya rissuna yanawa mama magana wadda se a wannan lokacin ta ɗago tana amsa gaisuwar sannan itama umma sahura wadda duk tawani hargitse ta ɗago idanunta tana kallonsa cikin mamaki tana ayyana miya kawo wanda take gani a t.v nan kuma.,?
Amman kallon mama yasa ta ɗan hasko wani abu game dasu ɗin tunda akwai kama ta jini.
"Maamah mike faruwa ne kizo na kaiki gida baze yuwu ku zauna anan babu cikakken tsaro ba"
Yakai maganar cikin ladabi.
Cikin tsananin tausayi mama ta juyo tana kallonsa.
"A,a itakam antynku ma tana ciki bazan iya tafiya ba domin idan shahida ko hidaya ce anan babu inda zani inaga wannan yarinyar se dai a yankata a cire ɗan sabida tana wahala antyn ka na wajan doctor" takai maganar tamkar zatai kuka!

Numfashi yaɗan ja tare da mamakin wannan hali na maamah haka kurum baki san mutum ba amman ku dage akansa.
Kallon umma sahura yay sannan yace.
"Baba ina mijin ta yake, kinga mamah baze yuwu ai mata wani abu batare da permission na mijinta ba".
Yakai maganar yana kallon umma sahura wadda tai wuki wuki nan da nan kuma idanunta ya kawo ruwa.
"Mijinta ya rasu nice kuma mahaifiyarta idan har kunyi niyyar tai makonmu to kuyi dukkan abin da ya dace domin ALLAH".
Takai maganar tana share ƙwallar ta, sannan ta ɗora.
"Bawan ALLAH nasanka tunkafin na ganka a yanxu Alkairinka yake xuwa kunnena amman ban sha mamaki ba seda nagani yanzu da idanuna ashe abun ajininku yake kaiwa ALLAH ka taimakemu ka saka hannu aiwa nafisa aikin nan wallahi tunda ta sami cikin nan muke walaha" takai maganar cikin kuka.
60 - 61


Babu abin da ke tashi a wajan face kukan! ummah sahura wadda ta karyawa maamah ta,ta zuciyar itama nan da nan ta soma share ƙwallah da tsadaddan farin babban mayafin dake jikinta
Shikam mas! sunkuyar da kansa yay yana nanata Nafisahhh!! acikin zuciyarsa atake shima tasa zuciyar ta karye tunanin tasa nafisar yay masa kaaaa sannan tsananin tausayin matar ya ratsa masa jikinsa dama zuciyarsa baki ɗaya.
"Katashi kaje ka saka hannu ai mata c,s" mamah ta fada cikin bada umarni.
Cikin damuwa gami da kasala ya mike duk jikinsa yana amsa kuwa! yanufi cikin wajan.
Katafaren office ɗin doctor hassan wanda ya kasance babban likita a ɓangaren ya shiga.
Da anty yaci yakaro tana kokarin fitowa duk jikinta ajike da zufa, da kallo ya bita sannan kuma suka juya ciki atare da ita.
Cikin mamaki doctor ya mika masa hannu suka gaisa sannan yay masa bayanin dukkan abin da ake ciki sannan ya umarcesa daya saka hannu domin har yanzu takasa haihuwa sannan kuma tana faman jijjiga wadda ba,a son mai nakuda tayita.

Ɗan numfasawa yay kasancewarsa shima ƙwararren doctor kana ya dubi doctor hassan sannan yace masa, baza,a iya bata taimako ta wata hanyar ba dole sedai an yankata? murmushi doctor hassan yay sannan ya mike tsaye ya dauki kayansa na aiki sannan ya umarcesa daya biyoshi can ɗakin dan yaga halin datake ciki idan da taimakon daze bata sabida yafishi sanin wasu abubuwan.

Ɗan jimm yay sannan ya dubi doctor wanda ya mike tsaye itama anty ta mike baya da zabi face binsu zuwa levour room ɗin..wata kwana sukabi bayan, sun fito daga office ɗin.


Adai dai bakin kofar yaja yatsaya yanajin kirjinsa yana bugawa kamar ze ɓalle ya fito waje.
Dafewa yay sannan ya aro jarumta yabi doctor.
Tamkar gizo ya hangota tana faman wani irin abu bakinta yana fitar da dafara, tana faman ciccije bakinta, tsananin mamakin abun shiya daskarar dashi a wajan ya kasa gaba balle yay baya.
Itakam daman anty a bakin kofar aka dakatar da ita. haka itama ta tsaya cikin zulumin lamarin.
Seda yay tamaza sannan ya ƙarasa shiga cikin ɗakin baki ɗaya nurses ɗin wajan sun gama jigata bayyanar doctor hassan yasa suka ɗan ragu aka bar sister naja ita ɗaya tana rike da kayan aikin.

Nafisa kuwa iya wahala ta ganta tun tana kuka! da hawaye har seda tazo tana kuka babu hawaye bata gane komai hatta kanta.
Ikon ALLAH kuma da shigarsa tamkar an karo mata nakudar haka ta kawo kai kan jiki kan karfi.

Baya da wani zaɓi face ya bata iya taimakon da ALLAH ya nufesa domun yanzu babu lokacin tsayawa doguwar hira.

Cikin dabara ya sallami doctor hassan shine ya tsaya akanta sister naja tana taimaka masa har ya samu yay mata wata allura wadda ta tsayar mata da wannan jijjigar kafin kuma cikin amincin ALLAH faya ta fashe wadda nan da nan kuma nakudar ta dawo sabuwa.

Cikin amincin ALLLAH kafin wani lokaci karar kukan jaririyar ya cika ɗakin, cikin azama ya tarota bayan ya sabule safar hannunsa, mikawa sister naja yay yarinyar sannan ya koma ga nafisa wadda ta sume taimakon gaggawa ya bata kana ya saka naja ta kirawo nurses suka wuce da ita ɗakin hutu.

Itama yarinyar an samu nasarar gyarata tass cikin kayan sanyi farare wanda aka samosu a office ɗin doctor.

Gava ɗaya rigar jikinsa ta jike da jini haka ya tattara ya nufi waje cikin tsananin murna marar kwatance.

Anty ya mikawa yarinyar yana gaya mata itace wadda yake nema.  

Cikin tsananin mamaki anty ta dubi yarinyar katuwa wadda se a hade yara biyu saboda tsabar girman yarinyar.


Wadda hatta yatsar hannunta irin na mahaifinta ne babu abin da ya raba kamanninta dana sa sak suke kama da mai girma mas!

"Anty kimin addu,a ALLAH ya rayamin ita, na sameta a yayin danake tsananin bukatar ganinta, sedai abin da yasaka nai hawaye shine ƴata aka haifa a yau amman an rasa rigar daza,a saka mata se ata ƴan sadakar asibiti". yakai maganar cikin kunar rai.

Itakam anty mamakin wannan lamari da kuma tsananin farin ciki sune sukai mata dirar mikiya ashe wannan yarinyar ita ɗan nasu yaketa faman nema yanxu wannan yar ta hannunta mallakin anwar ce?.
Haka anty tai ta nanata maganar har zuwa sanda taji yana mata magana akan suje wajan su maamah danshi kam ze koma gida ya gyara jikinsa.

Anty ce ta rungume bebyn ajikinta suka nufi wajan dasu mamah suke cikin tsananin murna.

Take mikawa mamah bebyn batare data ce mata komai ba.

Shikam jayay ya tsaya akansu yan faman sakin murmushi.

Cike da mamaki umma sahura take duban jinjirar kana tace "Alhamdulillahi mungode ALLAH sannunku bayin ALLAH".

Cikin farin ciki anty ta ɗago kanta ta zubawa umma sahura idanu tana doka far,a.

Cikin rawar baki umma sahura ta furta.
"Ƴar fulani" duk da abin akwai sauyi amman hakan baze saka ta kasa gane taba.

Cike da mamaki anty ta ɗago kana ta dubi umma sahura da kyau sannan ta furta.
"Lahhh adda sahu!!! takai maganar da ƙarfi tana kuma dubanta d kyau.


Kallon kallo aka shigayi a tsakanin umma sahura tare da anty inda kuma mai girma mas! da mama suka zama ƴan kallo.

Kuka anty ta saka tare da tsugunnawa wajan tana zaman dirshan tamkar taɓaɓɓiya.

"Adda sahu daman kuna duniya naje garinku yafi sau dubu ban gane ko ina ba adda sahu ina jaririyataaaaaa......?"

Itama umma sahura share hawayen tai kana tace.
"Labari mai tsaho ƴar fulani amman ina maki albishir da cewa jaririyarki ita ce ta haihu yanxu kuma wannan ƴar da kika ɗauka jikarki ce ɗiyar wajan nafisa wadda....."
Bata karasa ba kukan anty ya cika wajan.
"Adda sahu kina nufin wannan mai nakudar jaririyata ce kina nufin ɗiyatace?" tafaɗi maganar tamkar ta zare tare da saurin kai hannu jikin mama ta warci jinjirar ta rungumeta tsamm tana faman kuka!!

Cikin tsananin kuka umma sahura tace. "Tabbas yar fulani baki kyautawa rayuwar ɗiyarkiba tindaga ranar da kabiru yabar duniya baki kuma waigenta ba yanxu gaya nan har kaddarar....." batai maganar ba taji saukar muryar mai girma mas! akanta.
"Baba ba kaddara bace kaddara da warwara zakice domin nine mahaifin wannan jinjira.....yakai maganar yana tsugunnawa agaban umma sahura tare da bayyana mata komai.

Sannan ya mika hannu ya amshi yarinyar ya kara ajikinsa yana zubda kwalla.

Sosai mama ta kadu da wannnn lamari haka zalika umma sahura ta kadu lallai nafisa tacika munafuka dabata taɓa bayyana mata komai ba, amman koda yake rabon saduwarta da mahaifiyarta yasa ta ɓoye.

62 - 63.


Babu abin da mama take faɗi se kalmar.
"Alhamdulillahi! Alhamdulillahi!! Alhamdulillahi!!.

Kana ta ɗago kanta ta kuma sauke idanunta akan anwarr wanda yake tsaye da ɓatattun kaya ajikinsa ga kuma bebynsa rungume a kirjinsa.

Cikin wani yanayi wanda yake cikin sarƙaƙiya ta dubesa tare da yi masa alamu da hannunta akan yazo.

Cikin ladabi da biyayya ya karaso gareta tare da ɗora mata bebyn akan cinyarta.

"Maza kiramin uncle ɗinku" ta gaya masa hakan.
Cikin mutuwar jiki.
Cike da ƙwarin gwiwa ya zaro wayarsa ya fara laluben, layin uncle sa'idu abokin mahaifinsa kuma ɗan uwa agaresa wanda suka kuma taso tare da marigayi alhaji sameer.

Bugu uku uncle sa'idu ya ɗaga wayar bayan sun gaisa anan ya mika mata wayar magana sukai da mama cikin harshen fillanci wadda auta ta kasa fahimta.

Bayan kamar mintuna biyu ta katse kana ta baiwa anwarr wayar tasa, sannan kuma suka mike zuwa cikin ɗakin hutun da aka warewa nafisa dukkansu.

Da ƙyar aka lallaɓa anty ta zauna anan falon ɗakin wanda aka ƙawata shi harda kayan, kallo cikinsa kuma nafisa ke kwance an jona mata ƙarin ruwa.

Seda umma sahura tai wa anty nasiha sannan ta daina kukan datake wanda baki ɗaya ta kasa zama tana zama take kuma mikewa ta nufi cikin ɗakin ganin ko nafisa ta farka amman ko motsi.

Umma sahura wadda itama ta ɗagu abasu sallama su wuce gida kodan ta sanarwa da ƴan uwa wannan lamari itama kanta ta kasa zama.

Daga kan kujerar gefe kuwa mama ce zaune rike da ƴar bebyn se shi uban gayyar a tsaye yana faman danna waya yana daukar bebyn a hoto.

Babu ko kunyar idon mama wadda tai kasa da kanta ta kasa magana sabida  yadda yake bata mamaki.

Cikin haka sukaji an turo kofar sallamar uncle sa'idu ce ta saka suka mayar da hankalinsu kofar.

Bayansa baba Ali ne kanin abban anwarr mai bi masa.

Daga bayan baba ali kuma akwai inna mahaifiyar abban anwarr.

Waɗanda duk suka shigo ɗakin cikin kyakykyawar shiga ta kayan sallah.

Bayan gaishe gaishe kuma anan hira ta ɓarke sosai kuma su uncle suka karbi yar jaririyar sukai mata huɗuma da suna khadija kana suka mikawa mama ita.

Ɗakinne ya dauki tsit..

Mama ce ta juyo ta dubi inna mahaifiyar mijin nata sannan ta soma yi mata bayani tundaga haduwar anwarr da nafisa cikin daya mata da komai da komai.

Har zuwa yau da suka tabbatar da yarinyar ɗiyar anty ce ashe.

Sannan mama ta ɗora da cewa.
"Abinda yasa na nemeku tun anan shine ina son ai komai cikin sirri sedai babban abun da ya dauran kai ga wannan lamarin shine, wasiyar alhaji wadda ya bayar a gabanki". ta nuna inna da kuma baba ali da kuma uncle sa'idu.

Sannan ta cigaba..


"Lokacin wannan yaron baya garin nan, sannan antynsu ma bata wajan, Alhaji ya baka wasiya sa'idu dan ALLAH kamar yadda na rokeka a waya akan kazomin da wannan akwatun da alhaji ya baka yace kada a fito dashi se anga ƴar wajan antyn su nakeson ka nuna mana".
Takai maganar tana haɗa hannun ta waje guda.

Cikin zulumi uncle sa'idu ya fito da akwatin yar karama wadda tun a waya mama ta roki dayazo da itan sannan ya mikowa baba ali.

Baba ali ya karba ya budeta wata takarda ce ta bayyana.

_Assalamu alaika ya ɗana Muhammadu anwarr hakika ni mahaifinka nai maka wani ɗan abu kaɗan wanda ban saniba ko zakaji dadinsa ko bazakaji ba, ni mahaifinka wanda nai sanadin kawoka wannan duniyar a yau asabar 2/ 2 nida aminina sa'idu munje wajan mahaifin tsohon mijin matata hafsatu mai suna liman nane mamaka auran ɗiyar wajanta nafisa kuma alhmdh an bamu kuma mun bada sadaki wanda yazama abokina sa'idu shine shaidata kuma haka zalika mai gari abokin malam liman shine shaidar mu domin abokina sa'idu shine waliyinka haka zalika mai gari shine waliyin wannan yarinya.nayi wannan haɗinne domin na wankewa hafsatu kuncin abinda dangin mahaifin yarinyar suka mata, haka zalika na boye wannan lamarine domin gudun ɓacin rai. wanda na boyewa hafsatu hakan domin na nuna mata laifin yin burus da ƴar tata datai, naje wajan malam liman domin na amso nafisatu ta dawo wajanmu sedai na tarar rai yay halinsa haka na nemi wani daga cikin danginsu domin muyi magana amman ban samu ba, naje wajan mai gari baya nan, dan haka na dawo gida kuma babu dadewa nima wannan lalurar ta ajali ta saukar min wadda bana da tabbacin ko zan tashi, ina rokonka daka gaggauta karɓo matarka domin na hango sarkakiya daga dangin mahaifinta,idan sun musa maka ka nemi kawunka saidu da kuma mai garin domin sune shaidar komai, ina fatan zuri'ata da dukkan wanda ya raɓeni yaywa wannan takardar kyakykyawar fahimta_

Baba Ali yagama karanta wannan takardar jikinsa duk yana rawa.

"Innalillahi wainna ilaihirraju'un yanzu sa'idu inda anyi gangancin aurar da yarinyar nan da mai kenan akai? ai wannan ba hujja bace mai yasa kai sa'idu kuka ɓoye mana hakan yanxu bada ban wannan kaddarar ta miko ba dayaya akai kenan?" inna takai maganar tana share hawayenta.

Gyaran murya uncle sa'idu yay sannan ya dubeta "daman inna ina da niyyar bayyana wannan kulli sabida halin rai da mutuwa,domin na fuskanci sam ita hafsatu batama san cewar marigayi yana zuwa can wajan tsohon surukin nataba, sannan ita kuma tace bata gane wajan kotaje sakamakon surkullen da mahaifinta yay mata domin ya dauke hankalinta daga kan ƴar tata sabida zafin wulakancin da aka mata".

Yakai maganar yana murmushi.

Mama kam kuka ta saka haka zalika anty umma sahura babu mai bakin magana se kuka, shikam uban gayyar bazaka tantace wani hali yake cikiba illah sujjadar dayay  awajan yana godewa ubangijinsa abisa wannan rufin asirin daya masa cikin sauki abisa tserar dashi dayay daga samun ƴar fari ta zina.

Muryar anty ce ta karade wajan.

"ALLAH nagode maka! hakika wannan itace isharar ubangiji, Anwarr hakika ALLAH yanawa bayinsa rufin asiri tare da rahama alokacin da yaso tabbas ALLAH ne ya wankeka ya kuma duba lamuranka bai kuma nufa dakaji kunya a idanun makiyankaba masoyanka kuma suyi kuka ba........ALLAH ya kaddara maka kaddara ka amsa daga karshe kuma ya wankeka acikinta......ashe duk wannan abun wannan yarinyar matarkace ta sunna kai ka kuma godewa ALLAH tabbas wannan shi ake kira da ƘADDARA DA WARWARA!!...............
Hakika tsakanina da alhaji se addu,a yadda yaymin ALLAH yay masa ALLAH yakai haske kabarinsa ALLAH yasa  dashi da annabin rahama, hakika nidaman jikina yana bani akwai ɓoyayyen kulli acikin wannan lamari, nagartaccen mutum kamarka anwarr baze aikata abunda ALLAH baisoba duk da ALLAH babu wanda baya jarabta amman ni naji ajikina Anwarr bazaka aita zina ba sabida ka fito daga tsafta taccen family cikin tsarka,kakkun iyaye.

Anty takai maganar tana kuka!

"Nima tabbas Babu abinda zancewa da ALLAH se godiya, wallahi tun sanda yazo ya shaidamin bana bacci kullum cikin rokon ALLAH nake akan ya yafe mana laifukan da mukai har ya ƙaddara wannan yaron ya samu rabo ta hanyar da ba,a so, ashe shi ubangiji akwai nufinsa dayin hakan lallai ALLAH ALHAKAMU!".

Kowa godiya yakewa ALLAH acikin wannan lamari, musamman umma da mama da anty.

Ita kanta umma sahura wannan lamari ya kaɗata so kawai take asamu faraga ta fice oh yau ina su baffa masu kuri suzo suga ikon ALLAH.....!!
