ANAIWA AUTA UZURI🙏🙏🙏🙏
64 - 65



Hoho umma sahura bakinta kam ya kasa rufuwa se uban far'a take faman saki tamkar babu gobe.

Cikin nutsuwa inna kakarsu ta dubi mama sannan ta soma magana........
"Hadiza ina ganin idan an basu sallama ku wuce gida can da ita, idan yaso baza,a shaidawa kowa cewar ga abun da ya faru ba. illah za,ace masu kawai matarsa ce, tunda ya aureta bata cikin gari ya ajetane acan garinmu sabida ta sami ƙaruwa, gudun cutar da ita yasa aka boye maganar".
Inna takai maganar tana kallon mama sannan ta juya ta kalli sauran tana jiran miza,a suce.
"Eh kam inna maganarki tayi tunda ALLAH yasa matarsa ce ta sunna ai ba wata matsala se a faɗi hakan kodan gudun bakin mutane".
Cewar baba Ali kenan kanin marigayi.
Kowa yay na,am da maganar illa mama wadda bata ce komai ba hasalima sunne kanta tai a kasa tana jan carbinta, kuma daman kusan hakan ne gareta bata fiye tsoma kanta a cikin sabgar Anwarr ba.
Ita kuwa umma sahura suna gefe da Anty suna  tattauna hirar yaushe gamo.
Cikin dubara kuma ta ari wayar anty ta kira, baba mijinta wanda ya gama firgitar rashin ganin ko ɗaya acikinsu wanda tsananin firgicin nasa yasa har ya dangana da gidan kaka, ya sanar dasu halin da ake ciki.
Yanzunma haka yana wajan kakar wadda ta saka ƴaƴanta agaba tana faman kuka!
Cikin nutsuwa umma sahura tai masa bayani atakaice.
Sannan ta ɗora da idan yana da hali yazo da kaka gidansu na nan zuwa anjima zata taho daga asibitin.
Tunda ta boye musu zancan auran nafisan.
Fafur su baffa sukace bazasu je barkar shege ba itakam kaka babu yadda ta  iya haka ta biyo baba sukayo gidan umma sahura.
Bayan umma ta kammala wayar ta juyo ta dubi anty tare da mata godiya sannan ta bata wayar.
Sukam su inna sunyi sallama dasu mama tare da barin asibitin bayan inna ta shaida masu cewar a yanzu base anjima ba zata tara dukkan zuriyar tasu ta shaida masu wannan lamarin.
Haka suk fice daga ɗakin wanda yay saura daga shi se mama tare da umma da anty wadda take abakin kofar tamkar tai tsalle ta dauko nafisa.
Cikin kwantar da murya mama ta dubesa.
"Yakamata kaje gida ai kodan matarka sannan ka sauya kayan jikinka".
Wata kunyace ta kamasa shi sam yama manta dawasu kayan dake jikinsa.
Shafa sumar kansa yay tare da furta mata "tom" cikin kasan laɓɓansa sannan ya mannawa ɗiyarsa kiss bisa goshinta kana yay ma su umma sallama yabar ɗakin.
Tun sanda ya hau mota yake faman ɗora hotunan yarinyar tasa cikin dukkan kafafan sadarwarsa.
Wanda zuwa wannnan lokacin zancen haihuwar yacika gari.
Tuni kuma ƴan jarida sukaima kofar gidansa caa suna jira daga garesa.
Tsananin yadda ake faman doko masa waya yasa yama kashe wayar tasa baki ɗaya, bayan ya sanar da  oska akan yaje yayma jaririyar siyayya harma da mamanta.
Tundaga bakin kofar gate din gidansa yake arba da jama,a da ƙyar securtyn gidan suka taimaka ya wuce a falon, kasa ya tsaya yay wanka a gurguje sannan ya shirya cikin kananun kaya, tare da fita waje danya warware ƙullin dake gabansa.
Tundaga bakin kofar farko.
"Yallaɓai waishin da gaske an maka haihuwa  a yau wanda iya sanin duniya ba,a san kai wani auren ba, sannan kuma ba madam ta gida bace, shin mezaka iya faɗa danka warwarewa mutane wannan ƙulin wanda yacika kowa da mamaki amman babban abunda yafi bawa kowa mamaki yadda akaga kai da kanka kake watsa wannan bayanin?"
Cewar wani ɗan jarida dake faman karo masa abun magana bakinsa.
Lumshe idanunshi yay kana ya bude.
"Alhamdulilahi ya ALLAH, abinda zan iya gayawa masoyana dama maƙiyana shine ayau takama ɗaya ga ranar karamar sallah ALLAH yay min kyautar Nana Khadija, ta wajan amaryata wadda mukai aure da ita bada jimawa ba abin da kuma yasa na ɓoye wannan auren harma cikin sabida yadda makiya sukai min caa duk da bana tsoronsu Amman ance a ɓoye nema a Bayyana samu, kodan gudun matsala".
Yana gama fadin hakan kuma ya juya part ɗin nasa baki ɗaya yabar taron yan jaridar rike da abun daukar rahoto suna watsawa duniya.
Cikin awa ɗaya zancan haihuwar amaryar ɗan majalisa ya karaɗe garin baki ɗaya, wasu na mamaki wasu alajabi wasukuwa tsorone ya kamasu 
Kuma cikin kankanin lokaci inna ta tara dukkan wasu iyalan mai carbi ta sanar masu da maganar.
Kamar yadda ta tsara tun a asibiti wanda tsananin mamaki yacika wannnn zuriyar amman murnar haihuwar yasaka kowa ya kasa zama.
Haka kuma mutan gari sukaita ɗora tayasa murna akan kafafan sada zumunta.
Hatta mai girma gwamna zuwa wannan lokacin ya kira wayar sa dan yamasa barka yafi sau nawa amman akashe haka zalika ƴan jam'iyarsu da sauran jama,ar gari.
Cikin asibiti kuwa ilahirin ƴan gidan sun halarci asibitin hatta su imran waɗanda har kukan murna seda sukai haka zalika shahida da sauran kannansa dasu sakina matar imran duk sun hallara a asibitin wanda shelar inna tazo musu daga gidan innar sukayo nan suka kuwa kama yar jaririyar suka rike tamau suna faman yi mata hotuna.
Duk wanda yaga mahaukaciyar siyayyar da oska yayo.seya zabura sabida siyayya akai tamkar kanti sabida haka anty tace shahida tai gida da wasu kayan.
Acan cikin gidan kuwa tuni labari yakai ga hidaya wadda kawarta ta kira tana mata barka cike da mamaki take sauraran kawar tata.
Cikin haka ya shigo falon.
Cikin nutsuwa yake dubanta danya mata bayani.
Amman abunda tai ya basa mamaki matuka harda kiransa ɗan iska, itafa sam bata yarda da wannan munafurcinba 
Duk yadda yaso ya fahintar da ita ya kasa haka ya tattara ya watsar da ita yay cikin ɗakinsa danya shirya ya fita.

Acan asibiti kam mama waya ta kuma ɗagawa ta kira baba Ali dayake yana kusa ya kuma zuwa anan ta roki daya dauki anty da umma ya kaisu can  wajansu kaka, sabida tafison a gama komai kafin  tashin nafisan dan ta fison suyi gida kai tsaye.

Haka kuwa akai ya dauki umma da anty A gidan umma aka dauki kaka da baba suka nufi can gidansu kakan wanda daga nan akai wajan mai gari harda su baffa.
Sosai baba ali yaywa mai gari bayani wanda tunkafin ma ya karasa mai gari ya cafke anan fa aka soma kallon kallo, xuwa wani lokaci zancan ya cika wannan zuriya ta liman wanda tuni wasu suka soma dana sani, anty kam zama tai tamkar tauraruwa awajan su baffa sabida yadda ta koma.
Haka aka gama tattauna komai kana sukai masu sallama suka barsu cikin zulumin wannan lamari wanda hatta matan su baffa mamakin lamarin ya hanasu sakat.
Kaka kam kuka take tana kuma godewa allah sukam su baffa mamaki kunya sune suka masu caaa!

Mijin umma sahura kam harda kukan murna.

Bayan kammala komai cikin sauki haka baba Ali yayo asibitin da kaka da umma da baba da anty wadda kaka taketa zumuɗin ganin nafisan.
Wadda duk suka shaida yanzu matar ɗan majalisarsu ce kuma tai masu zarra.

Lokacin da suka nufi asibitin.
66 - 67



Shigowar su umma kuma yayi dai-dai da farkawar nafisa.
Cikin azama nurse ɗin dake kanta tana kula da ita, ta fito waje cikin farin ciki take sanar dasu cewar mai jego tafa farka.
Cikin murna Anty wadda daman bata zauna ba, ta nufi ɗakin bayanta kuma umma sahura dasu, kaka.
Zaune suka tadda ita a bakin gado jikinta sanye da doguwar riga redmate wadda oska yazo dasu.
Cikin nutsuwa Anty ta nufeta tare da rungumeta tana mai sakin kuka!.
Cikin rashin fahimta feena take duban Antyn, batare data iya furta koda kalma guda ba.
Ilah idanun ta dake rarrabawa tana binsu da idanunta, kafin kuma komai ya soma dawo mata daki-daki.
Tasan dai a masallaci ta soma naƙuda, kuma wannan matar data rungumeta yanzu ita ta taimaka mata, kuma tundaga nan bata kuma sanin komai daya faruba.
Shafa cikinta tai taji wayam da sauri taɗan zame jikinta, daga Anty tare da tsirawa umma sahura idanu cikin tuhuma.
Maganar kaka ita ta saka kowa ya soma magana kuma.
"Haba kekuwa nafi baki ganemu bane kin zubawa kowa idanu tamkar wata wawiya".
Muryar sakina matar imran ita ta sauka a bayan ta kaka.
"Haba iya kimata ahankali mana, shahida mikamata bebyn ta ganta dan naga alamun yadda take baza idanu".
Anan kam kowa seda ya dara amman banda nafisa wadda komai ya tsaya mata, kallon shahida take cikin mamaki wannan ai kwanaki ta ganta a wani plaza, kuma tasan dai daga cikin familyn sa take tomeya kawota nan.
Saukar ɗiyarta akan cinyarta shine ya dawo mata da hankalinta,.
Inda Anty ta kuma gyara mata ita cikin so da ƙauna.
Kasa daurewa dai Anty tayi seda ta kamo zuby ta ɗorata akan cinyarta.
Sannan ta dubi nafisa, "Kinsan wannan?".
Ta nunawa feenah zuby.
"A,a" tafaɗa cikin rawar murya.
Cikin sauri kuma umma sahura ta nufo anty, "Kinga hafsatu kibari har mu.koma gida yanxun bazata gane komi ba".
Da wannan Maganar yasa anty yin shuru.
Inda kuma ta bar nafisa da kallon zuby, wadda su shahida suka zo da ita.
Duk hasashenta ta kasa gane komai dan haka taja ta watsar da lamarin illah ita fatanta abarsu da umma sahura taji wannan lamari dan duk idanta ruwa yake ta kasa fahimtar miya kawo waɗan nan nan kuma.
Cikin haka doctor hassan ya shigo zema jinjirar allura, ya umarci dukkan ƴan ɗakin su fita.
Hakan ce ta faru kowa ya fita waje domun zaman jira.
Anan suka tarar da mama a zaune wadda ko motsi batai, ba tana nan inda suka barta, daga karshema seta mike taiwa imran magana akan su zo su wuce gida.
Hakance ta faru, sakina imran da mama da sauran kannansa harda zuby suka tarkata sukai gida aka bar Anty kaka da umma anan ɗin, daman kuma sun san mama bazata zauna anan ɗinba mace mai alkunya da kara kenan.

Kuma cikin ikon ALLAH basufi minti ɗaya da barin asibitin ba sega motarsu mai girma mas! nan ta kutso kai cikin asibitin shida oska da wani abokinsa.
Wanda tun sanda hidaya ta gama haukanta tai ɗaki tana kuka bai kuma gamuwa da itaba hakan yasa ya shirya ya fito danya kara duba lafiyar bebynsa.

Seda motar tai parking sannan suka firfito daga ciki.
Turo kofar sukai tare da sallama shine na karshen shigowa ɗakin, wanda tsananin mamakin ganinsa yasaka kaka zamewa daga kujera tabbas wananan shine ɗan majalisarsu.
 
Agurguje suka gaisa sannan sukai hanyar ɗakin datake.
Shiya fara tura kofar bayansa kuma salim da oska ne.
Hannunshi rataye bisa bayansa haka ya karasa cikin ɗakin.
Sam ita feena bata ma san da shigowarsa ba.
Illah kamkame yarinyar datai tsamm tana faman zubda ƙwallah.
Sukam su oska.
Ganin likita akanta yasa suka ja da baya suka koma tare da rufo kofar, shikam karasawa yay tare da tsayawa ya mikawa doctor hassan hannu.
"Aikam gwara da ALLAH ya kawoka, madam dai taki aiwa bebyn allura wai ai mata ita abakacinta".
Batare dayay magana ba ya saka hannunsa yaja yarinyar wadda ita feena sam batada masaniya akan shine cikin kuka tace.
"Nidai dan ALLAH karku mata wannan ƴar yarinyar mita sani nikam gwara ai mani".
Cikin dakakkiyar muryar dabatai zaton jiba taji yace.
"Dallah malama ki cikata ana gudun lafiyarta kinwani kamkameta".
Cikin tsananin tsoro feena ta ɗago kana ta saukar da idanunta akansa cike da tsoro ta saki yarinyar.wadda cikin zafin nama yay saurin tarowa tare da ɗorata a kafadarsa yana jijjigata domin wani kukan data saki.
Da wannan damar doctor ya tsirawa bebyn allurar tare da hade kayansa yabar masu ɗakin
Ya jima yana jijjiga ƴarsa ita kuwa nafisa kanta yana kasa jikinta yana faman rawa cikin zulumi da tsantsar mamaki.
Ganin kukan yay yawa yasa ya juyo gami da ɗora mata ƴar akan cinyarta kana ya tsareta da idanunsa.
"Ko yau ɗinma kwarton zaki cemin, dalla bata mamanta tasha bana son ɓata lokaci, kuma wallahi wannan shine na karshe dazaki sake yimin wasa da ɗiyata".
Yakai maganar yana maka mata harara!.
Kamar bada ita yake magana ba haka tama juya masa baya kana ta saka hannunta ta toshe kunnenta kuma bata dauki ɗiyar bama baki ɗaya.
"Magana nake".
Yakai maganar cikin yar tsawa kaɗan.
"Bani da abinda zan bata ai".
Itama ta gaya masa maganar kai tsaye.
Wani shegen killer smile ya sauke sannan ya tallafo yar kana ya direta a gefe.
Tattare hannun rigarsa yay kana ya haye kan gadon shima.
Tsananin kusancin da sukai da ita ya saka kowannensu yaji faɗuwar gaba ta xo masa.
Cikin jarumta irin ta karfafan maza ya tarairayota jikinsa, tare da ɗora hannunsa saman ƙugunta ya murɗa.
Wata irin zillewa tai tare da kwantowa ajikinsa batare data saniba.
Ƙamshin daddaɗan turarensa yasa ta cusa kanta a tsakiyar kirjin nasa tana kuma saka hannunta ta zagaye masa bayansa dashi.
Cikin rawar murya ta soma magana "ALLAH ya hana hakafa ka tuna hakan haramun kake kokarin aikata min". sabida ta gane nufinsa wannan dalilin nema yasa tai masa wannan cakumar dan karya aikata mata hakan.
Bai tsaya jin daga gareta ba ya ɓanɓare ta daga jikinsa kana ya harɗeta da kafafunsa ya kifata saman cinyarsa tare da saka hannunsa guda cikin saman rigarta.
Saukar hannunshi kirjinta shiya kuma sakawa tai wata irin xilliya wadda ta saka dole ya saketa ta mike tana faman zubbo da hawaye inda shi kuma ya maka mata wata uwar harara kana yace.
"Ina wasa dake nannaaahhhh" 
Turo baki tai gaba tamkar yarinya sannan ta juya masa baya kana ta fiddo da abincin yarinyar waje.
Da baya ta juyo da hannunta zata dauki yarinyar.
"Wallahi zan ɓata maki rai ahaka zaki daukar man ɗiyar".
yakai maganar yana jan ƙwafa, jin saukar muryarsa yasa tai maza ta juyo tare da daukar ɗiyar ta ɗora akan cinyarta.kana taja bedshit ta rufo kirjinta.
Sauka yay daga kan gadon jin alamun su oska zasu shigo......!!
68 - 69



Bedshit taja ta rufe jikinta kana ta saka hannunta ta tallafi bebyn tata, batare data kuma fara feeding ɗinta ba, ahaka su oska suka ƙaraso Cikin ɗakin.
Salim ke gaba daga bayansa oska, dukkansu cikin shiga ta kamala.
Da kallo feenah ta bisu cikin rashin fahimta sedai kuma taso shaida oska kamar taso ta gansu tare lokacin dayaje school ɗinsu akan gardi issue.
Cikin sakin fuska tare da   Mutuntawa su oska, suka shigo cikin ɗakin tare da zama akan ɗaya daga cikin dogayen kujerun dake ɗakin.
Muryar salim ita ta katsewa feenah hanzarinta.
"Congratulation madam ALLAH ya raya mana bisa sunnah".
Shima oska amshewa yay kana ya mike zuwa wajan mai girma mas! ɗin wanda yawani ja ya tsaya a tsaye yana faman sakar mata harar rashin shayar masa da beby da batai ba.
"Mai gida sannu da hutawa zamu ɗauki yarinyar tamu".
Oska ya kai maganar cikin tsananin ladabi da biyayyah.
Tare da komawa wajan zamansa ganin mai girma mas! bai tanka masa ba.
Har zuwa wani lokaci sannan ya karaso gabanta.
Tare da miko mata hannu.
Daman kuma ta basa haushi na yadda tai burus dasu oska ko gaisuwa Babu. tamkar ma bata san da halittarsu awajanba.
Da ƙyar ta amsa musu barkar da sukai mata ma.
Cikin halin ko in kula ta dunguro masa yarinyar saman hannun nasa tamkar ta saka ihu!
Haka kurum take faman jin haushin kowa sabida yadda ake nan nan da ƴar tata wanda ita hakan bai mataba, taso anbarta da ƴarta sunje can sunyi rayuwarsu cikin kwanciyar hankali.
Shi kansa dubi yadda yawani tsaya mata tamkar ze ƙwace ƴarma baki ɗaya.
Ƙwafa! yaja a fili cikin sautin ƙasa-ƙasa wanda ba lallai su oska sujisa ba sannan ya tallafi ɗiyar da farin showwel ɗin da aka rufeta ya mikawa oska wanda yay zumbur ya mike tsaye.
Karbarta oska yay tare da nufar wajan salim, da ita cikin zumuɗi salima ya saka hannu ya amsheta yana faman daga wayarsa yana ɗaukar ta a hoto.
"Kamar kumbo kamar katanta" cewar oska wanda yake tsaye bakinsa ya kasa rufuwa.
Salim ne ya amsa masa da cewar...
"Gaskiya nima dai ban taɓa ganin carbon copy tamkar wannan ba, kasan ALLAH hatta ɗan sajen nan nata irina na mahaifinta ne".
Dariya oska yay tare da furta "ALLAH dai ya raya mana little mamman mu".
Shikam miskilin yana tsaye a inda yake yana faman turawa hidaya massege.
_Ayi haƙuri madam a ɗoramin ruwan coffee tun safe banci komai ba, ina asibitine dana fito kuma zanje gidan mai martaba in masa gaisuwar hawan sallah pls komai ya zama redy kafin nazo_
Abinda ya tura mata kenan tare da sakin smile bayan ya kammala tura matan kana ya mayar da wayarsa cikin aljihun wandonsa ya naɗe hannunshi akan kirjinsa  ya tsaya ahaka tare da sunkuyar da kansa a ƙasa.
Har zuwa wani lokaci kafin salim ya mike tare da nufar gadon ya miƙa mata ɗiyar yana dariya tare da mata fatan alkairi.
Cikin rashin far,a ta amsa masa.
Bandir ɗin ƴan dubu- dubu ya fiddo sabbi gadagau daga aljihun wondonsa ya dire mata agabanta.
"Asai mata madara".
yafada tare da barin wajan, salim abokin anwarr ne tunna kuruciya yanzun haka yana aikine a matatar man fetur kuma yana can, da xama shida matarsa.
Wallahi murnar haihuwar da anwarr ya samune yasa ayau ɗin ya biyo jirgi ya nufo garin cike da ɗauki, domin ya taya abokin nasa farin ciki.
Yadda ya aje kudin haka ta barsu a banzace batare datai koda godiya ba wanda sauran kaɗan anwar ya nutse dan tsabar kunya.
Tamkar kububuwa haka ya marawa su salim ɗin baya.
Abakin kofar fita yaja ya tsaya yana mayar da ɓacin ransa.
"Osk ka kai salim hotel ya huta sannan kazo ka daukeni muwuce emir palace,".
Yafadi hakan cikin bada umarni.
Sannan ya dubi salim cikin nutsuwa.
"Abokina zanzo by tmr idan ALLAH ya nufa akwai magana".
Ɗan murmushi salim ya saki shima tare da kaimasa duka kaɗan irin na abokai sannan yace.
"Gaskiya nima akwai zance abakina sedai goben dannima na ɗagu nai balance duk jikina ciwo yake mani".
Sallama sukai anwarr ya juya ɗakin sukuma suka nufi cikin falon inda su umma suke zaune domin su wuce.
Da ɗan karfi ya tura kofar, wanda tunma kafin ya shigo mayen daddaɗan turarensa ya mata sallama.
A ciki ciki yay mata sallama tare da tsayawa akanta.
"Ni babu macen data isa ta bani ciwon kai, Alhamdulillahi ALLAH ya wadatani da mace tagari, and kuma iyayena sun bani tarbiya mai kyau, dan haka idan kinsan wannan rashin ɗa'ar zakina min wallahi zan karɓi ƴata dan bazata taso cikin wannan gida danci ba,............"
Dan tsabar rashin tarbiya kinsan waye salim? a ƙasar nan ma baki ɗaya!! ayau ya taso dan ya tayani murnar samun karuwa shine zaki masa wannan kauyancin nonsense! kawai".
"Nifa........" "Rufemin wannan ƙazamin bakin naki" yay saurin tarar maganar dazatai sannan ya ɗora da bala'insa kamar ba shiba haka zalika itama nafisan ta kasa gane ma mai yake fada sakamakon saurin Bakin dayake faman yi.
Seda ya wanke ta tass da masifa tamkar ze ari baki har wani hucin zafin rai yake idanunsa yay jajir haka jijiyar kansa ta mike tsaye goshinsa yay wani irin ja!.
Wanda kallo guda tai masa tai kasa da kanta tana share ƙwallar data zo mata.
Ɗaukar ƴarsa yay daga kan gadon ya ɗorata akan cinyar nafin agabansa ta zaro maman ta turawa ƴar abaki, yana faman zabga mata harara danyaga idan ya tsaya sanya wannan yar rainashi xatai.
Iya zafi feena tana shansa sabida yadda ɗiyar take zukar nonon gashi kuma wannan shine na farko gareta haka dai ta daure ta shayar da ita cike da azaba sannan ta kwantar da ita ganin tai bacci.
Se alokacin ya ja kafafunsa yabar mata ɗakin yana amsa wayarsa wadda duk kusan bayan mintuna uku setaga ankirasa kuma kalma guda taji yana fada watakan Amin Amin.

Futarsa da mintuna uku su umma sahura suka kuma dawowa dakin kuma still da wannan matar data taimaka mata a masallaci wadda ita matarce ma ta hada mata tea mai kauri ta sakata agaba tasha duk da bakinta babu daɗi.

Ahaka doctorn ya kuma dawowa tare da ce musu zuwa dare ze basu sallama domin yanzun haka komai yay normal dan bata ma karuba duk da girman ɗiyar amman babu ɗinki ko guda ajikinta.
70


Doctor yana gama masu wannan bayanin anty ta dauki waya ta kirasa akan ta shaida masa abinda doctorn ya fada.
Sedai user busy hakan ya saka ta nemi layin imran bugu ɗaya ɗauka anan ta shaida masa bayan sallar isha yazo zasu wuce gida.
Fatan alkairi yay masu tare da amsawa akan babu damuwa ALLAH ya nuna mana lokacin.
Ɗakinne ya dauki hira wadda kaka take bawa umma sahura akan yanzu habu bama ya zaman gida kullum yana kan hanyar bibiyar matan banza da dai sauran abinda ba,a so.
Nafisa wadda tai tsai da ranta tana sauraran wannan lamari mai ɗunbin alajabi ita ta numfasa sannan ta dire cup ɗin shayin hannunta kana ta dibi kudin gabanta ta mikawa umma sahura tare da gaya mata abokinse  ya bata godiya umma tai cikin mamaki tare da adana kudin awaje guda sannan ta cigaba da sauraran kaka wadda tai stop tana faman kallon damin kudin cike da tsoro.
Haka suka cigaba da hirarsu ita kam nafisan tana ɗan kishin giɗe tana sauraransu.
Ana kiran sallah kowa ya mike yay wajan alwala kaka falo ta fita itakam anty tana fashine.
Dan haka umma sahura ita ta shiga na nan ta dauro alwalarta.


Yana fita daga reciption ɗin asibitin ya fada mota wadda oska tuni ya dawo daga kai salim.
Direc emir palace suka wuce wanda anan suka gamu da mai girma gwamna shima yaje anan sukai salla sannan yayo gida bayan ya gama karbar barkar karuwar daya samu daga bakuna masu daraja.


Aparking space oska ya dakata tare da fitowa da sauri ya bude masa kofar cike da nutsuwa yake fitowa daga cikin motar tasa.

Batare dayay ma oska magana ba ya nufi sashensa.

Ganin sashen su maama akwai taron jama,a.

Sashensa ya nufa cikin rashin kuzari yake taka step ɗin harya samu ya karasa cikin falon nasa.

Hidaya na zaune ta harde ƙafa tana game a wayarta ko kallonsa batai ba haka ma kuma bata amsa masa sallamar saba.

Zama yay agefenta abinda bai saba ba waishi rarrashi tare  da kama hannunta.

"Haba my queen yaya ina sallama ma baza,a amsamin ba ko bakiga sako na bane ba?".

Tureshi tai da hannunta tare da tashi daga wajan tai hanyar ɗakinta.

"Naga sakonka niba baiwa bace ba kaje karuwarka tai maka mana".
Tana kai maganar tabar wajan baki ɗaya.

Harga ALLAH maganar ta sokeshi atake kuma ya kumai wa ALLAH godiya tabbas inda nafisa ba matarsa baceba yau dayaji haushin komai ma amman cikin rahamar ALLAH yanzu yana jinsa free itama hidaya yasan kishine amman da zarar ta sakko ze kuma mata bayani yadda zata fuskanta kuma abinda yasa yake mata uzuri tabbas yasan kowa akaima hakan baxeji daɗiba.

Da haka ya mike yay bedroom ɗinsa tare da rage kayan jikinsa ya faɗa bayi wanka yay sannan ya fito bayan yay sallah ya kwanta akan gadonsa tare da fiddo da wayarsa yana kallon hoton diyarsa.

Sosai mama ta saka masu aiki suka kuma gyara part din anty sannan suka gyara ɗayan dakin na kusa dana anty wanda ya sami sauyin dukkan kayan ciki wanda aka jerawa feena nata aciki dana bebynta domin anan zatai jegonta.

Bayan sallar isha i imran yaje asibitin bayan mintunan da basu wuce gomaba suka fito tare da shiga motar da imran din yazo da ita wadda take mai ɗan girma.

Bebyn tana hannun anty umma da kaka sune suka daddauko sauran kayan suka nufi mota.

Tare da barin aibitin wanda tunda taga sun dauki hanya daban bata gidan umma ba take faman turo baki gaba tare kuma da mamakin ina zasuje wanda bata sha mamaki ba.........


BACCI YACIN😴
71


Tundaga yanayin tsarin anguwar da yadda anguwar takasance umma sahura da kaka harma da nafisa suka soma rarraba idanunsu cike da tsoro da fargaba, lallai kuɗi karshen zance ne.
Basu sha mamaki ba seda sukaga bakin ƙaton mahaukacin gate ɗin da imran yay parking wanda aka kusan haɗe girman wata anguwar.

Cikin sauri masu gadi kusan su biyar dukkansu da uniform ajikinsu suka rugo zuwa wajan gate ɗin tare da wangale masa, cike da nutsuwa imran ya fara tafiya cikin gate ɗin inda harya samu damar parking acikin katafaren  parking space ɗin gidan.

Yakai kusan mintuna biyar yana gyara parking ɗin nasa gami da kashe motar kana ya bude ya fita bayan yaywa Anty magana akan zasu iya fitowa suma.

Cike da nutsuwa Anty ta bude marufin motar ta fito sannan umma kaka, da kuma nafisa, baki ɗaya sakin baki kaka tai wadda har jikinta ya soma rawa ganin tarin manyan motocin da suke zube awajan yanayin tsarin gidan kuwa bakaramin kaɗa kowannensu yayba.

Cikin lokacin da bai gaza mintuna ukuba, masu aikin gidan suka zo suka ɗauki kayayyakin da sukazo dasu sannan Anty ta basu umarnin akan dasu zo su wuce ciki.

Bangaren mama suka nufa kai tsaye tun daga bakin part ɗin su umma suka soma kallon yadda aka narka dukiya kafin daga bisani suka karasa babban falon maman wanda tsananin haduwar dayay abin ma baze faɗu ba.

Falon mama cike yake da ƴan uwa,  da abokan arziki da suka zo mata barka, ita kuwa tana daga can kan wasu irin tum-tum azaune gabanta wani flate ne mai ɗan karen kyau an ƙawata shi da kayan marmari.

Bayan sallamar dasu umma sahura sukai basu damar zama akan luntsuma luntsuman kujerun falon akai sannan aka soma gaishe gaishe wanda yawanci dangin Abba mahaifin Anwarr sunfi yawa a falon se ƴan uwan mama harda Anty aisha nurse wadda tsananin mamakin ganin umma sahura ya hanata tai koda motsin kirkine.

Ita kuwa nafisa tana daga gefe akan wani pillow da sakina matar imran ta saka mata a ƙasa ta zauna akan sa bebyn kuwa anata mikawa jama,a suna daukarta.

Da ido anty taiwa nafisa sigina akan taje wajan maama kuma cikin ikon ALLAH ta gane mai antyn take nufi, cike da sanyin jiki ta mike tana gyara hijabin jikinta.

Gaban wata yayar Abba ta fara tsugunnawa ta gaishe da ita sannan ta nufi gaban mama wadda kake hango tsabar kamanninta da Anwarr wanda ko shakka babu kana kallonta kasan itace mahaifiyarta.

Itama mama ta ƙasan idanunta take kallon, nafisan tare da yaba nutsuwar yarinyar tare da rashin kazar kazar ɗinta wanda atake kuma tai na'am da ita harma ta bata gurbi a zuciyarta a matsayin ƴarta kuma surukarta.

Saukar muryar nafisa wadda ta tsugunna agabanta itace ta katse mata tunanin nata.

Cikin siririyar muryarta ta soma gaishe da mama, itama kuma maman cikin sakin fuska take amsa mata gaisuwar daga karshe kuma ta mata yaya jiki sannan taja bakinta tai gumm.

Wadda ke kusa da mama, nafisan ta ɗaga kanta ta gaisar da ita, wadda da alamun bata ma jitaba seda mama tai ɗanyi gyaran murya sannan tace "Umman hidaya ana gaisar dake" cike da far,a matar ta juyo tanawa nafisan alamun data zo gareta sannan ta soma amsa mata gaisuwar cike da tsantsar kulawa wadda kana kallo kasan akwai tsabar dattako awajan matar nan.

Ita dai nafi duk tajita a takure haka ta zauna gaban matar kafin kuma taga matar ta ɗagota tare da kama hannunta suka nufi kofar falon da ita.

Wani ɓangaren umman hidayan ta kaita kuma da kanta ta haɗa mata ruwan wanka da kanta kuma tai mata wankan wanda seda nafisa tai kuka dan tsabar azaba, sannan ta bata wasu kaya ta sauya tare da pad kana taja mata kofar tabarta akan ta kwanta wai ta ƙara hutawa tunda dare yayi.

Godiya nafisa tai mata kana ta ɗan gyara jikinta ta kwanta akan gadon.

Acan falo kuwa tsabar mamaki bai bar anty Aisha nurse zama ba seda ta tambayi umma sahura akan lamarin anan kuma umma sahura tai mata bayani akan cewar daman can nafisa matar Anwarr ce kawai sun ɓoyene sabida tsaro.

Bayani tai mata kamar yadda inna ta tsara cikin gamsuwa Anty aisha nurse ta dubi umma tare da cewa "haba ashe surukata ce shiyasa naji na damu da lamarinta, kai Amman wannan yaro bai kyautaba dayaje mata da ƙarfi haka" ahaka sukaita tattaunawa har zuwa sanda aka kawo masu abici na alfarma mai dan karen daɗi.

Umma sahura da Anty aisha a flate guda suka zuba suna ci kaka ma nata daban haka zalika sauran kowa dana sa bayan cin abinci da hutawa anty ta umarci su, umma akan suzo su wuce zuwa part ɗinta.

Fafur umma sahura taki yarda akan itafa bazata kwana anan ba zata wuce gidane da safe sa dawo driver aka umarta daya kaisu gidan umma sahura bayan an basu kuɗi masu yawan gaske.

Suma sauran ƴan barkan sallama sukai, suka wuce gidajansu, inda anty taima mama sallama ta wuce part ɗinta itama.


Falon ya rage tsitt babu kowa se shahida da mama tunda daman anty aishama mijinta yazo ya dauketa sakina tai part ɗinsu.

Cike da mamaki mama ta dubi shahida "Nikam yau ina antynku ne?"
Cikin ɗan nazari shahida tace.
"Wallahi nima rabona da ita tun safe, amman da ummanta tazo ɗazu naga ta shiga part ɗin kuma data rako umman bata zo nan ba naga ta koma".

Murmushi mama tai batare datace komaiba ta ɗauki flate ɗin gabanta tai sama.

Ita kuwa anty ta jima a falonta tanawa, yayunta waya sabida bata gayawa danginta ko ɗayaba, cike da mamaki sukace mata gobe zasu kamo hanya zuwa nan ɗin.

Zuby da afra takai daki ta kwantar sannan tai ɗakin da aka warema nafisa, zaune ta tarar da ita ta zabga tagumi ga ƴar jinjirar cikin net tana faman bacci.

Zama anty tayi agefen nafisa tare da kama hannunta kana ta soma mata bayani "Nafisa nice mahaifiyarki!"! dum dum! gabanta ya yanke ya fadi tare da zaro ido tana kallon antyn,.

"Kuma ina son ki saka aranki Muhammadu Anwar mijinki ne na sunna kuma mahaifin ɗiyarki" awannan karon fina kasa zama tai seda anty ta kamota jikinta sannan ta soma bata labari tundaga kan haduwarta da kabiru mahaifin nafisan har zuwa rabuwarsu.

"Bayan baffana yazo wajan kakanki liman danya masa gaisuwa anan su baffanki sukai mana cin mutuncin dabaxe faduba daga karshe suka karbeki.ta karfin tsiya daga wajena ina kuka ina komai muka nufi rugarmu tundaga lokacin na soma cuta kamar zan mutu ga nonona yay sake sake sabida babu mai sha,haka naita jinya ko abinci bana ci ganin haka yasa baffana yaje da kansa wajansu domin su dubi ALLAH su bamu ke amman daga karshe harda jifansa, wannan dalilin yasa baffana yay kaura daga garin baki ɗaya kinsan zuciyar fillo...


Tunda muka koma cikin burni nake satar futa naxo nan garin naku amman fafur bana gane gidanku ahaka har muka dauki shekaru ban taba kwana batare danai kukaba cikin ikon ALLAH baffana ya fara cutar aljali harya rasu dayake daman kuma innata ta jima da rasuwa ita, daga nan se rikona ya koma hannun hammana wanda daman mu biyu baffa ya haifa dagani seshi..........
Kuma gidanmu guda tare da shi tunda  daman daga innata baffa bai kuma samu yay aureba, cikin haka wannan bawan ALLAH baban anwar yazo wajan hammana domin ya sai shanu da sallah, anan ALLAH ya haɗamu batare da ɓata lokaciba mukai aure dashi na taho nan Alhamdh bana da matsala da ƴaƴansa da kuma matarsa sannan kuma mun samu rabon haihuwa dashi ga sucan ƙannanki su biyu...........".
Daga nan kuma ta soma baiwa nafisa labarin aurenta da anwarr wanda bata san anyi maganar ba wadda aka yita a asibiti a ɗazu dasu uncle sa'idu sukazo.

Sosai nafisa ta kaɗu daman mijinta ne wani kukane yazo mata batare data shiryaba atake kuma tafara godema ALLAH akan tsallakar mata da ɗiyarta  dayay akan fitowa batare da aure ba.

Sannan anty ta fara mata bayanin komai daki daki.

Tundaga kan matsayin mama, sakina anty aisha, sosai feena tasha mamakin ashe matar data mata wanka ɗazu babar matarsce kuma matar kanin mamansa, lallai wannan familyn akwai zaman lafiya acikinsa.

Itama feena cikin kuka ta soma baiwa anty labarinta har zuwa sanda ta soma school da yadda ta fara ganin Anwarr har sanda ta basa taimako a wajan kai masa hari, amman kunya ta hanata ta gayawa anty, fyaɗen daya mata daga nan ta soma gaya mata yadda umma sahura ta riketa wanda anan wajan anty har kukanta fitowa fili yake ita tana nan tana shan ac ɗiyarta na can cikin bala'i rungume juna sukai cikin so da ƙauna suka cigaba da kukansu.

har zuwa wani lokaci kafin anty ta mike ta zubo mata farfesun data zo mata dashi har wajan karfe ɗayan dare suna hirar yaushe gamo sannan anty tai mata sallama ta wuce ɗakinta.

Nafisa kam ta jima batai bacci ba gani take tamkar a shirin film ALLAH kenan mai yadda yaso a lokacin daya so ya jarabceta da ƙalu bale kala-kala daga karshe kuma yazo ya warware mata shi acikin sauki.

Ahaka wani bacci mai daɗi yay gaba da ita.

Amman cikin dare ƴar jaririyar tace bata san zance ba haka ta dinga rigima wanda tsabar tsotso bai bar nafi ta rintsa ba.....


IN KUNGA ERROS KUMIN UZURI🙏🙏🙏
72


Tun nafisa tana shayar da ita cikin kulawa hartazo ta fara ƙosawa sabida babu laifi yarinyar masha ALLAH badai tsotsoba.

daga ta juya seta kuma saka ihu haka nafisa tai ta faman tura bakinta gaba tamkar wani na wajan tana ayyana daman haka iyayenmu mata, suka sha wahala damu lallai jinjina ga mahaifiya.

Seda Anty ta shigo ɗakin jin kukan yay yawa, tare da ɗaukar jaririyar ta zura mata harshenta a kunne tana hura.mata aikuwa nan da nan tai shuru har bacci yay gaba da ita.

Juyowa tai ta dubi nafisa kana tace, "Kingafa kume take cikinta gaya nan ya kumbura kekuma se ɗura mata nono kike". tafaɗa tana miko mata yarinyar tare da barin ɗakin.

Kwantar da ƴar tai akan ruwan cikinta tana shafa bayanta.

Ahaka wani wawan bacci ya kwasheta, batare data farkaba seda asubahi lokacin anty ta kuma dawowa ɗakin ita dai mikawa anty ɗiyar tai sannan ta koma baccinta. 


Goyota anty tayi tare da barin ɗakin dan nafisa ta samu ta ɗan runtsa.

Shikuwa anasa ɓangaren baƙaramin bacci ya samu jiyanba, dan yau yama so ya makara a sallar asubahi.

Cikin sauri ya shiga bathroom ya watsa ruwa sannan ya zira jallabiya ya wuce masallaci.

Bayan an idar da sallah a ƙa'ida yana zuwa wajan maamah ya gaisar da ita amman yau bai iya zuwa ɗinba kansa tsaye part ɗin nasa ya koma.

Ɗakinsa ya shiga tare da tube jallabiyar jikinsa sabida akwai wani aiki dayake son yaje ya gano wajan karfe bakwai.

Shirya kansa yay cikin wani milk ɗin yadi mai yankan sukari, tare da murza dakakkiyar hula wadda ta zauna akan nasa tai ras.takalmi da agogo masu yanayi da shigar jikin nasa ya saka tare da fesa turare mai daɗin kamshi.

Dayake da oska da p.a zasuyi wannan tafiyar shiyasa bai dauki makullin motaba haka yaja, kafarsa yabar ɗakin yana zuba uban kamshi.

Ɗakin hidaya ya wuce kai tsaye, sallama yay mata a bayyane, amman bai sami arzikin koda amsa mata ba balle ya saka ran gaisuwa.

Cikin mamaki da danne abun acikin ransa yaja stool ɗin madubi ya zauna gaf da kafarta.

Tana zaune akan gado cikin shigar kayan bacci masu santsi kanta ko ɗan kwali babu rike da wayarta wanda daga gani kasan chart take.

"Amincin ALLAH ya tabbata agareki".
Yafaɗi maganar cike da kulawa.

Ya tsine fuskarta tai alamun ko ajikinta kana ta juya masa baya tare da ɗaukar pillow ta rumgume batare data tanka masa ba.

Sakin ƙaƙƙarfar ajiyar zuciya yayi tare da danne ɓacin ran daya taso masa sannan ya kira complete name ɗinta.

"Amina magana nake maki tun jiya kinyi min banza kinsan ni arayuwata bana son irin haka ai wannan ma rainin wayone".
Yakai.maganar yana jan ajiyar zuciya da sauri.

Juyowa itama tai cike da rashin kunya gami da fitsara tare da furta..........


"Ahayee su rainin wayo manya ai kaine babban ɗan........"Amina!!!!!!!!!!!!!! ya kira sunanta da karfi tare da mikewa tsaye ya daki bangon ɗakin da karfi.

"Niba lusarin namiji bane ba kirike wannan aranki wallahi ki kama bakinki tun kafin ki tsinci hakoranki a zube".!

Yakai maganar ilahirin jikinsa yana faman rawa.

Tsit tai tana zaro idanu, cike da zafin rai ya fice fuwww! yabar mata ɗakin.

"Aikin banza aikin wofi ai wallahi zaman lafiya babu shi acikin gidannan ba dai ni za,a yaudara ba muzuba nida kai har itama tsakuwar dakake ƙiƙirarin matarka ce wallahi sena hana kowa sakat".

Takai maganar tana kuka! jefar da wayar tata tai ma baki ɗaya tai ruf da ciki tana gunjin  kuka!.

Kamar ze tashi sama haka ya nufi part ɗin mama babu kowa a kasa sabida safiyace dan haka ya wuce sama.

Ɗakin baccinta ya nufa kai tsaye sallama yay a kasan ransa.

Mama wadda take zaune kan sopa da kur'ani a hannunta ta juyo ta dubesa tare da amsa sallamar tasa.

Tsugunnawa yay ya gaisar da ita cike da ladabi tare da ɓoye damuwarsa.

Itama amsa masa gaisuwar tasa tai gami da binsa da kallo tana noticing ɗinsa.

Bai tsaya ma sosaiba sabida yana sauri dan haka ya tashi yay mata sallama yabarta da tunani tare da auna damuwar data gansa aciki.

Da har ze nufi parking space sekuma, ya juya zuwa part ɗin anty.

Tun daga bakin kofar daze shiga falo yakejin kukan yarinyar har wani canyara taka kuma yi.

Jikinsa na tsananin rawa tamkar ze fadi ya nufi cikin ɗakin dayake jin kukan nata bayan ya shigo cikin falon baki ɗaya.

Sallama yay Anty wadda take zaune tanawa bebyn wanka ita ta juyo tana amsa masa sallamar tasa.

Gaishe da Anty yake amman idanunsa da hankalinsa yana kan ɗiyar har sanda anty ta cirota daga bahon, tare da naɗeta a towel, tare da miko masa ita, tana cewa karbeta karka ƙwarar dani tafadi hakan tana dariya tare da daukar ruwan tabar masa dakin.

Ɗagota yay tare da ƙura mata idanu yana kallonta cike da so da ƙauna.

Tunda yake baita ɓa ganin kama da kama sak kamar shi da yarinyar tasaba.

Sakata yay a kafadarsa yana kiran sunanta daya saka mata shi ɗaya "Miemah tah" ki bar kuka dady bai so" haka yaketa faman fadi tare da rungumeta amman fafur taki shuru wanda da alamun fa wannan ƴar yarinyar acicice.

Ahaka nafisa ta fito daga toilet jikinta ɗaure da guntun towel tare da ɗan mitsitsi akanta.

Ko kallonsa batai ba taɗanja tsaki akasan ranta kana tai gaban madubi tana kokarin jayo hijabi.

Shikam binta yake da kallo tundaga kanta har zuwa kafafunta...........!

_TABBAS MUNA TAMU SHI KUMA ALLAH YANA TASA, AMMAN TA ALLAH ITACE GASKIYA, WALLAHI BAN TSARA WANNAN LABARIN ZE KAI MIN BAYAN AZUMI BA AMMAN TUNDA ALLAH HAKA YA TSARAN BABU YADDA ZANYI, DA WANNAN DAMAR NAKESON IN BAWA MASOYANA MASOYAN WANNAN LITTAFI HAƘURI,ABISA JINKIR TASHI DAZANYI HAR ZUWA BAYAN SALLAH IDAN ALLAH MAI KOWA MAI KOMAI YA KAIMU DA RAI DA LAFIYA, KU SANI INA MUKU TANADI ACIKIN WANNAN LABARIN TAFIYAR MIKAKKIYACE WADDA BATA DA KWANA BANA SON NAI MAKU KODA TSALLAKEN KALMA GUDANE NADA CIKIN WANNAN LABARIN SABIDA HAKA KUYI HAKURI AKWAI ƘURA A SHAFUKAN GABA WAƊANDA SE MAI HAKURIN BINA NE KAƊAI ZE GA ƘWAL UWAR DAKA_ !!! 👊
73

*Dubun godiya ga ALLAH ubangijin halitta daya bani damar sake dawo maku da cigaban wannan littafi ya ALLAH kabani ikon kammalasa lafiya amin*

_Inayiwa ɗaukacin musulmi barka da sallah ya ALLAH ya karɓi ibadunmu yasa muna cikin bayinsa ƴantattu amin_

*Masu fidda min da littafi waje kuji tsoron ALLAH kudaina domin harga ALLAH kuna shiga hakkina kuma duk wadda ta karanta min littafi batare data biyaba taje na barta da ALLAH*

                          ********

Ɗan kauda kansa yay kaɗan yana mai lumshe mayun idanunsa tare da sake matse ɗiyar tasa asaman kafaɗarsa,yana kuma jin kukan! yarinyar har cikin ransa.

Itakuwa nafisa yitai tamkar bata san ALLAH yay ruwan halittarsa ma a wajanba.

Cikin hukuncin ALLAH ta samu ƙaton hijabi wanda ya rufe mata dukkan jikinta, ta tafka tare da jawo kujerar gaban madubi tana kokarin zama akai.

Duba agogon hannunsa yay tare da ɗan jan tsaki kaɗan cike da jin haushi.
Yaja gyaran murya tare da furta.
"Malama bakkijin miemah tana kuka! ne?".
Yay maganar cike da muskilanci tamkar yana magana da wani cannn......,.

Sarai ta jisa amman setai mai kunnen uwar shegu, ma,ana tama juya tare da ɗauko cream zata fara gogawa a fatar jikinta.

"Naanah! kada na kuma mai mai ta maki miemah tana kuka!".yakai maganar a kaushashe.

Jin saukar muryarsa mai ɗan sauri bisa cikin dodon kunnenta yasa taɗanji tsoro kaɗan tare da ɗan ɗago wutsiyar idanunta taɗan saci kallonsa kaɗan.

Sannan ta aje man hannun nata sannu a hankali ta tako kafafunta har zuwa gabansa tana ɗan tura baki gaba kaɗan.

Awulakance ta saka hannu ta dafe bayan yarinyar ma,ana tana son ta dauketa.

Wani kallo ya watsa mata wanda babu shiri tai saurin saita kanta tare da maida hankali wajansa ta amshi yarinyar ta nufi gaban gado da ita tana jin kamar tai ta dukanta sabida haushin data bata yarinyar akwai ci masha ALLAH.

Zama tai abakin gado tare da ajeta sannan ta gyara cikin hijabi take kokarin zirya bebyn dan ta samu ta bata abincin nata.

"Malama kada ki zurmuƙamin ɗiya cikin wannan katon hijab sallon kimin asarar ta".

Taji saukar muryarsa yana tsaye idanunsa manne bisa screen ɗin tafkekiyar wayarsa yana kallo sam bazama ka ɗauka yana kallonta ba.

Tura bakinta tai gaba tare da ɗan janye hijabin nata kaɗan sa'annan ta saita kan yarinyar tare da fara bata abincin nata.

Cike da zalama yar ta cafki maman ta soma sha babuji babu gani, ita kuwa nafisa rintse idanunta take tana faman cije bakinta sabida wata irin azaba data keji acikin ƙirjinta sosai takejin zafin yadda yarinyar ke tsotsar jikin nata.

Tari miemah ta soma alamun ta ƙware cike da zafin nama naga mai girma mas! ya mayar da wayarsa cikin aljihun rigar yadin jikin nasa har yana tuntube wajan ƙarasowa wajansu.

Hannu na rawa ya kai jikin nafisa ya ɗago miemah yana bubbuga bayanta sosai take tari seda gyar yasamu tarin ya lafa, cike da masifa! ya juyo yana mai kallon feenah da manyan idanunsa tare da ɗan cije laɓɓansa na ƙasa batare daya ce mata komai ba ya dire mata miema akan  cinyarta yabar ɗakin sabida yadda yaji wayansa yana faman ringing! ko ba,a gaya masa ba dai yasan su oska da p.a ne suka karaso suna jiransa akan fitar dazasu yi.

Cike da mamaki ya ƙarasa wajansu oska akasan ransa yana ayyana anya baze nemi wadda ta ƙware a raino ba domin yana kallon yanayin sanyi wannan yarintar tamkar bazata iya wani abun kirki akan ɗiyar tasaba.( kuji fa ).
    

Cikin girmamawa oska ya rissina yana gaisar da uban gidan nasa kafin ya mike ya nufi bayan mota domin ya bude masa.

Shima p.a rissunawa yay tare da furta "barka da safiya mai gida, yaya kwanan mammarmu?" batare da walwala akan fuskarsa ba ya furta kalmar,"Lafiya". abinda ya iya fadawa p.a kenan tare da zira hannunsa cikin aljihunsa yabar wajan.

Cikin tararrabi p.a yaja jiki yabi bayansu, akusa da oska ya zauna shikma mai girma mas! abayan motar ya samu wajen zama ya baje yana kwasar raɓa, tare da ɗaukar wayarsa ya shiga cikinta yana danne dannensa wanda rabi akan duba wasu ayyuka da aka turo masa yanzun yake.

Har suka ƙarasa cikin yalwar ɗawisu bai ɗago fuskarsaba kasancewar motar mai tintack ce wannan dalilin yasa babu wanda yasan da zuwan sa cikin wannan anguwa.

Gangara motar oska yay har cikin gidan yarin na yalwar ɗawisu wanda mai girma mas! ya bawa kamfanin A.A.SALIHI constract kwangilar ginin wasu sabbin ɗakunan guda, ashirin sabida ƙarancin ɗakunan da mazauna cikin gidan ke fama dasu.

Cike da azama shugaban gidan, ya ƙaraso wajan motar su mai girma mas! wadda ta kasance ita ɗaya jall sabida wannan fitar ta musamman ce shiyasa bai sanar da excort ɗinshi ba.

Da sauri shugaban wajan ya saka hannu ya bude masa motar tare da jada baya yana rawar jiki.

Seda ya dauki mintuna kusan goma sannan ya soma zuro da kafafunsa kafin daga bisani ya fito baki ɗaya.

Batare da jin shiɗin mai mukami bane ba ya mikawa Alhaji atiku, shugaban gidan yarin hannu sukai musahaba, sannan Alhaji atiku yay masa barka da samun karuwa kana suka jera su huɗu har zuwa cikin, ɓangaren sabbin ɗakunan da aka gama aikinsu a jiya ranar sallah kenan.

Cikin jin dadi Alhaji atiku yake zagayawa da mai girma mas! wajan wanda tuni Anwarr ɗin ya soma ware idanunsa yana bin wajan da kallo sabida yadda wajan yay masa mugun kyau bai taɓa baiwa wani kamfani aiki yaji dadi ba tamkar na A.A.Salihi sabida anmasa har abun da yafi wanda yace ɗin.

Cike da jin daɗi ya dubi oska, "Kai gaskiya osk naji dadin aikin nan wallahi nikam bantaɓa jin daɗi aiki tamkar wannan ba". yakai maganar yana kuma duba manyan kofofin  da aka zuba waɗanda suke tamkar na glass da glass.

Cikin rawar jiki oska ya furta "tabbas mai gida ai Alhaji ALIYU (  RABON WANI  ) tsayayyen mutum ne sosai yake tsaye akan dukkan aikin da aka baiwa kamfaninsa mutum ne jajurtacce tsayayye yadda ka ganka" oska yakai maganar yana dariya, shima Anwarr ɗin dariyar yay amman marar sauti sabida bakaramin dariya oskan ya basa ba. 

Sun ɓata lokaci sosai anan ɗin kafin suyi sallama su kuma ɗaukar hanyar sabon garin ɗawisu domin kaiwa kamfanin sardauna mamayar aikin daya basu batare dasu ƴan sardaunan sun sani ba.

                         *********


Cikin takaici ta mike ta nufi gaban madubi tube hijabin jikinta tai tare da ƙarasawa bakin gado.

Ta dauki wata yalluwar atamfa wadda anty ta kawo mata ta saka, riga da zani ne seda ta soma saka under wears kafin ta soma saka rigar wadda tai mata ciff sabida nafisa bamai jiki bace ba.

Ko ɗaurin kallabi batai ba ta koma wajan miema ta zauna tare da saka hannu ta zabga tagumi acikin ranta take mamakin halin mutumin nan gaba ɗaya lamarinsa mamaki yake bata tunda ta haihu yake mata faɗa ai koda ace miema badaga jikinta ta fito ba ai baxe dinga mata wannan faɗan, shi ko tausayinta ma baiji kenan.

Rintse idanun ta tai waɗanda ruwa yaɗan tarar masu kaɗan sannan ta furta "seda so ake tausayi" takai maganar cikin jin tausayin kanta tare da ɗago ɗiyar tata ta tsira mata idanu tana kallon tsantsar kamaninta da shi,  afili ta furta "ALLAH yasa kada kiyo faɗan abbanki" takai maganar tana ɗan jan laɓɓan yarinyar waɗanda suke jajur dasu tamkar an saka jan baki.

Sallamar shahida ta saka tai saurin janye yar daga cinyar cikin sauri sabida kunya tare da jan kallabinta tabar wajan ta nufi can bakin madubi tahau dube duben rashin makama, muryar shahida wadda take faman gaisar da ita itace ta katse mata tunanin nata.

"Maman miema barka da safiya yaya kwanan miemarmu?" shahida ta faɗa tana dariya gami da daukar miema wadda take faman cilla kafa sama zatai rigima.

"Lafiya ina mama dafatan tana lafiya?" cewar nafisa cike da kunya, zama shahida tai akan gadon tare da bata amsa "tana lafiya Alhamdulillahi tace ma na gaisar dake sosai kafin ta shigo". cewar shahida wadda taketa faman kallon ɗiyar yayan nata.

Ahaka anty aisha nurse wadda zuwanta kenan itama tayo sallama cikin ɗakin, wadda take tare da yayar mammah tare da matar dr ƙanin mammah, cikin alkunya wadda ta rasa inda zata saka kanta ta karaso wajan suka gaisa sannan ta koma can kurya bayan shahida ta zauna cikin rashin sabo dasu.

Tamkar kace mata ket ta arce daman can ita feena bamai son shiga jama,a bace ba.

Babu abinda take faman haɗawa se zufa haka ta zauna tanajin hirar ƴan uwan mai girma mas! ɗin dasuka zazzauna waɗanda yawancin ƴan uwan mammah ne mahaifiyarsa wanda taga kama tamkar an tsaga kara.


Ahaka suma su umma sahura suka shigo ɗakin tare da matansu baffa harma da kaka waɗanda sukai tamkar kace masu ket suma su arce bakaramin mamakin gidan suke ba watakan akwai gidaje kamar waɗan nan a duniya.


Koda suka zauna acikin ɗakin sosai suke mamakin yadda wai wannan ɗakin ƴar fulani ne matar kabiru, lallai inda ranka kasha kallo yanzu ƴar nafi ce take auren ɗan majalissarsu yarinyar da suka ɗauki karan tsana suka ɗorawa amman ita ALLAH ya daukaka haka.

Kafin wani lokaci masu aikin anty sun shake gaban kowa da kayan sanyaya maƙoshi tare da snacks waɗan da tuni ɗakin ya kaure da kamshin kayayyakin.

Cikin nutsuwa nafisa ta karasa gaban umma sahura tamkar zata shige mata cikinta take gaisar da ita kafin ta juya wajan kaka dasu inna hanse matansu baffa dukkansu ta gaisar dasu waɗanda suka zuba mata idanu tamkar wata tauraruwa.

Kaɗan-kaɗan suke hirar su da umma sahura wadda tsananin shakuwarsu tai yawa da nafisa, ahaka anty ta shigo cikin ɗakin bayanta,su zuby da afra ne waɗanda akaiwa shiga ta kaya iri ɗaya, da kallo feenah tabi ƙannen nata tana mamakin yadda taga suna ɗan yanayi da yaran musamman zuby wadda hatta sanyin nasu kusan iri gudane ma.

Hannunta ta saka ta jawo yarinyar wadda a dubara nafisa tabar ɗakin da zuby rike a hannunta sabida daman jinta take a takure.

Ɗakin anty zuby takai feena tana ɗanyi mata surutu kaɗan-kaɗan zama sukai akan sopa ahaka wani bacci mai ma,ana ya dauke batare data shirya ba.

Acan ɗakin kuwa sosai ya cika da ƴan uwa da abokan arzikin dasuketa zuwa barka amman sam basuga mai jegoba, wadda suka samu labarin tai bacci a ɗakin anty daga bakin zuby wadda ganin feena tai barci yasa ta baro ɗakin.

Dangin anty waɗan da sukazo daga rugar ɗawisu, sune suka sauka yanzun nan waɗanda suka yada zango a babban falo suna huce gajiyar da suka kwaso.

Cikin zumuɗi anty ta shige cikin dangin nata itama tana mai jin dadi acikin zuciyarta marar misaltuwa, matar hammanta wanda suke ciki guda ita ke dauke da ƴar jaririyar a hannunta tana gasa mata cibi, gabansu kuwa ancika sa da kayan ci dana sha.

Ahaka su kaka da matan kawu, suka fito daga ɗakin sukayo falon anan fa aka soma kallon kallo a tsakanin dangin anty da matan kawu, atake anan kaka ta saka kuka! tana mai bawa dangin anty hakuri, sekuma hira ta ɓarke tamkar babu abinda ya faru sabida saurin fahimtar junan data faru a tsakaninsu.

Anty ce ta koma ɗakinta ta tarar da nafisa kwance tana faman bacci, tada ta tai tare da haɗa mata ruwan wanka kana, ta fiddo mata da kayan dazata saka, cikin jin dadin yadda mahaifiyar tata ke bata kulawa ta faɗa bayin tana kuma godiya ga ALLAH.

Ita kuma anty cikin ranta tana kuma ayyana yadda zata baiwa nafisa kulawa sosai sabida yadda ƴar tata take bata tausayi domin sam ita bata samu kulawa ta mahaifiyaba shine yasa yanzun take kwatan ta mata.

Harta fito anty na ɗakin seda ta soma kiciniyar saka kaya sannan anty tabar ɗakin.


Cikin sanyinta ta kammala shirin nata tare da zira slippers ta fita zuwa falo.

Sosai jikinta yahau rawa idanunta ya kawo ruwa tana dafe zuciyarta ganin yadda dangin mahaifiyar tata suka cika wajan tare da dangin mahaifinta.

Ahaka ta karasa gwagwgwaji matar hamman anty ita ta aje jaririyar akan cinyar wata matar kawun anty ta mike da azama zuwa wajan feena tare da rungumeta tana sakin hamdala tofa anan fillancin ya motsa domin sosai, falon ya karade da murna kowa ka duba farinciki fall aransa idan ka cire matar kawunta wadda daman can bata kaunar nafisan.

Bayan komai ya lafa anan kowa ya hau cika cikinsa da  kayan abincin da masu aikin suka jajjera.

Itakam gwagwgwaji miƙewa tai tsam rike da miema a hannun tare da kamo hannun nafisa tai ciki dasu.

Abakin gado ta aje miema tare da sakin hannun nafisa sannan ta koma falo tare da shigowa da wata babbar jaka, wasu kayayyaki ta fiddo sannan tai cikin toilet da kanta ta kuma haɗa wani ruwa a baho sannan tayo waje dashi, ta dauko wani ƙullin magani tare da wani ruwa a gora ta juye acikin bahon sannan ta ɗauko miema ta tube mata kayanta ta tsomata cikin ruwan.
Aikuwa miema ta tsanyara ihu! "Yimin shuru dole mu tsuma ku da maganin tsari wannan sakon tundaga baffankune".mijinta kenan yayan anty, sosai ta ma miemah wanka ta naɗeta a tawul sannan ta dauke ruwan taje ta zubda.

Itakam nafisa bata ce komai ba kuma batai gigin daukar miemah ba sabida itafa rigimar wannan yarinyar ta soma isarta.
Ahaka gwagwgwaji ta fito tare da ɗaukar miemah tana jijjigata sannan ta fita waje da ita.

Bayan kamar mintuna biyar ta kuma dawowa tare da shigewa toilet ɗin.

Nafisa ta ƙwalawa kira tare da umartarta akan ta shigo cikin toilet ɗin.

Wani irin ruwa mai ɗan zafi kaɗan ta haɗa mata tare da zuzzuba magungunan sannan ta fice tabar nafisa bayan ta bata umarni akan ta shiga cikinsa sosai ya ratsa mata ƙasanta.

Cikin tsoro feena ta shiga cikin katon bahon bayan ta tube sutturar jikinta kuma sosai taji ruwan yana ratsa mata jikinta sannan kuma tana jin dukkan nukurkusar da kashinta ke mata na gajiya babu.

Takai kusan mintuna talatin sannan ta ɗauro tawul ta fito, daga toilet tana kuma fitowa taga katon kaskon wuta wanda aka cikashi da garwashi a tsakar ɗakin saman tiles yana faman ruruwa.

Tsorone ya kamata ganin ana kuma gasawa miema cibiya ganin gwagwgwaji bata kulata ba yasa taja hamdala sannan tai gaban wadrov ɗin anty.

Jin muryar gwagwgwaji tai akanta tana cewa kada ta saka kaya tazo ta shiga cikin zani ta turara jikinta har ƙasanta maganin tsarine tare dana sanyi sannan kuma yana gyara mace.

Zaro idanun ta tai waje cikin tsoro ta karaso kasancewar nafisa yarinya mai biyayya da alkunya sam bata da musu a rayuwarta amman bada ban haka ba da baxata je gaban wutar nan ba.

Hawaye ta share a fakaice sannan taja kaskon ta tsugunna tanajin yadda zafin wutar ke ratsata tare da hayaƙin maganin mai hade da kamshin turare kaɗan.

Jin muryar gwagwgwaji tai akanta tana faman mita "Keko am nafisa bakya magana ne hala?" itakuwa nafisa tana cikin zani tana faman haki da dafe kirji tare da zubda hawaye sam ta kasa magana ma bare tai yunkurin wani abun har gwagwgwaji ta gama shirya miemah kana ta yaye zanin tana bin nafi da kallo.

"Kekam wace irin ɗiyace ai tun ɗazu ya gama konewa amman baki fitoba jiba fuskarki yadda tai jajur" tafada tana mamakin halin wannan ɗiya sannan ta dauke gaushin tabar dakin.

Da ƙyar feenah  taja ƙugunta zuwa wajan wadrov ta dauko zani ta daura ta zame tawul ɗin tare da zama abakin gado tana faman haki.

Bata jin zata iya koda daga yatsanta sabida azabar yajin da idanunta da kirjinta ke mata, ga fuskarta tai jajur abin tausayi.

Gwiwarta ta sauko ƙasa tare da kifa kanta a saman gadon miema kuma na gefe tana faman cilla kafa sama.

Ahaka taji wani mayataccen kamshin turare ya kunno kai cikin ɗakin wanda take tunanin a wajan wanda ta sanshi.

Shikuwa mai girma mas! harya karaso cikin ɗakin sallama dauke a bakinsa, baiji ta amsa ba, daman dawowarsa kenan shima duk acikin gajiya yake amman nacin son yaga miema yasa ya kasa daurewa seda ya biyo ta nan ɗin.

Koma part ɗinsa baije ba amman ya tsaya  afalo sun gaisa da ƴan uwan anty wanda yaji dama bai biyo tanan ɗinba sabida kunyar dayaji ta kamasa ganin yadda suke binsa da kallo musamman dangin baban nafisa waɗanda basu taɓa ganin saba seyau inda ba ajikin pasta ba.

Ta kalman ƙafafunsa ya tube da kansa cikin sanyin jiki ya tako ya karaso cikin ɗakin.

Akusa da nafisa ya ɗan zauna kaɗan a gefen gadon tare da daukar miemah yana cillawa sama sannan yay saurin komawa ƙasan carpet ya zauna sabida yana jin babu ɗa'a ya zauna a gadon surukar tasa, sam hakan bai dace ba a tarbiyarsu.

Ganin tamkar bata san da wanxuwarsa a ɗakin bane ba yasa yaja gyaran murya kaɗan tare daɗan saka kafafunsa ya gogi jikinta.

Firgit ta ɗago tare da kallonsa da jajayen idanunta waɗanda shi kansa seda suka basa tsoro.

Ɗanja baya yay kaɗan cikin ƴar basarwa ya dubeta "Mike damunki?" abinda ya iya fada mata kenan tare da maida kallonsa ga miemah.

Cikin jin haushin abinda ya mata ta mike tana dafe ƙugunta harta karasa gaban wadrov ta jawo doguwar riga tare da ƴan tarkacen su pad tai bayi.

Shikam dariya ya guntse kaɗan tare da furta "Raguwa" sabida yaga alamun kamar daga wanka take babu mamaki ko taji ruwane yasakata hakan.

Harta fito wannan dariyar bata bar kan fuskarsa ba "Sannu ALLAH kare gaba". abinda ya faɗa mata kenan tamkar bai damu ba.

Tura baki tai gaba tare da ɗan gunguni kaɗan sannan tabar masa ɗakin ma baki ɗaya.

Shikam mamakin halintane ya kamasa ita watakan haka ALLAH yayota bata magana kamar bebiya, kujifa har kura ce zata cewa kare maye.

Yafi karfin mintuna goma yana wasa da ƴarsa sannan ya mayar da ita kan gadon yana kokarin barin ɗakin.

Itakam nafisa kunyar ganin yadda ƴan falo suke binta da kallo yasa ta koma da sauri hartana tuntube.

Karo suka kusan yidashi baya tai zata faɗi yay saurin tallafota da hannunsa.

A karo na farko a rayuwarsu da suka taɓa kallon kallo a tsakaninsu, sosai mamakin kuruciyarta ya kamasa lallai ALLAH mai kyauta da kari idan ba idanunsa ke masa gizoba wannan shahida ma ta girme mata nesa ba kusaba.

Seda kallo guda zaka mata ka hasko shuru shurunta gami da sanyinta tare da raunin da ALLAH ya mata baiwarsu.

Itama kallonsa tai kaɗan, tare da saurin kauda kanta sabida baiwar kwarjinin da rabbi ya basa na cika idanun mutane.

Cikin dabara ya mannata a saman kirjinsa yana jin wata iriyar nutsuwa tana shigarsa tare da jin tausayinta yana ratsashi.

Da ƙyar ya ƙaƙalo maganar datake bakinsa "Mi aka maki ɗazu?" abin da ya faɗa kenan yana bin saman fuskarta da kallo.

Kirjinta ke faman bugawa tamkar ze faso ya fito, ALLAH ALLAH take ya saketa sabida baxata iya jure yadda kaifin dafin ƙwayar idanunsa ke yawo a jikintaba ga kuma yadda kirjinta ke faman dokawa tamkar ze faso wata iriyar sahihiyar kaunarsa ke sake bijiro mata jitake tamkar ta zauce ta haukace ta lalace akansa.

Cikin sanyi tare da karkarwa take kokarin zamewa daga jikinsa shikuwa yaki bata damar hakan sema sake azabtar da ita dayake kumayi da kaifafan ƙwayoyin idanun nasa.

Jin kamar za,a shigo yasa tai saurin zamewa ta faɗa daki aguje.......................

Murmushi ya sauke a saman kamilalliyar fuskarsa tare da ɗan cije bakinsa kaɗan sa'annan yaja ƙafafunsa yabar part ɗin.
Itakuwa nafisa aguje ta faɗa cikin ɗakin tana jan numfashi tamkar mai cutar asma, hartana ɗan dungurin miemah kaɗan batare data sani ba.
Lumshe idanunta tai tana tariyo yadda tajita acikin lafiyayyen kirjinsa mai uban laushi da ƙamshi.
Cikin wani irin yanayi take lumshe idanun nata ahaka umma sahura ta shigo ta sameta.
Cikin mutuwar jiki ta mike ta zauna tana ɗan ƙaƙalo far'a kaɗan sannan ta dubi umma sahura kana tace.
"Umma se yanzu kika  shigo wajan ƴar taki kenan ni wallahi umma senake ganin kamar kin daina sona". takai maganar tana tura bakinta gaba kaɗan.
Cikin dariya umma tace "faɗi ki ƙara nikam yanzu ta ƴar jikalleta nake bata taki ba". tafaɗi maganar tare da saka hannu ta dauki miemah wadda taketa faman cillah kafarta sama.
Dariya itama nafisan ta saki tare da duban umma sahura a karo na biyu tare da ce mata.
"Umma nikam wai ina labarin waleedah har yanzu suna can zaman extention ɗin ni wallahi na matsu na ganta har yanzu shuru mun juma bamu haɗu ba".
Cikin dariya umma sahura ta dube kana tace" aminan juna aiseki jeki kai mata ziyara ai kafin candyn naku yazo". cikin zolaya umma sahura tai wannan maganar amman ita kam nafisa ta dauka gaskene shiyasa tace.
"Aikam umma bara komai yay dai dai wallahi zanje ɗin". takai maganar tana dariya.
"Hmm nikam badani ba wannan katoɓarar"cewar umma sahura ta faɗa tana dariya, tura bakin ta tai gaba tana kallon umma sahura tamkar zata saka kuka!

Cikin haka anty  ta shigo cikin ɗakin bayanta wasu matane ƴan gayu na ƙarshe cikin azama anty ta jawo masu wani lallausan carpet suka zauna sannan ta karasa gaban miema ta daukota gami da naɗeta awani shegen tawul mai uban laushi ta mika masu ita.

Itama nafisa saukowa tai daga gadon ta zo gabansu ta tsugunna tana gaisar dasu.
Sun jima acikin ɗakin suna faman ɗaukar miema a camera kafin su masu sallama tare da aje manya manyan bags ɗin da suka shigo dasu.
Har waje anty takaisu sannan tazo
Seda ta dawo take gaya masu cewar matan ƙannan mamaah ne , cikin mamaki nafisa take kallon anty tana kuma jimamin wannan yawa na zuriyarsu amman batace komai ba.

Seda maganar antyn ta katseta akan ta shirya zata leka part ɗin maamah su gaisa da ita.
Cikin nutsuwa umma sahura ta kintsa miemah duk da dai daman can ɗinma yarinyar a shirye take tadai daɗa gyarata ne sosai kana, itama nafisan ta shirya kanta cikin wani pink hijab mai kyau da anty ta dauko mata.
Fita falo sukai suka wuce su gwagwgwaji  a yashe da almun baccin gajiya ya kwashe sune.

Daga umma sahura se feena suka karasa cikin part ɗin maamah domin ita anty tuni ta koma sabida bakin data kumayi.
Cikin haɗaɗɗen falon maamah suka shiga wanda ya cika da wani irin azababben kamshi tare da sanyi mai shiga jiki.
Tundaga kofar falon jikin feenah ya soma rawa har suka karasa ciki.

Wasu mata suka tadda zaune bisa carpet ɗin falon. se mama wadda take zaune akan wani irin tum tum tana dauke da casbaha a hannunta kamar koda yaushe.
Kai tsaye gabanta feenah ta nufa umma sahura tana bayanta seda ta nemi gu gaban mamar ta zauna sannan ta dago tana gaisar da sauran matan dasuke zaune waɗanda da alamun abokan arzkine sukazo mata barka.
Umma sahura itace ta soma gaida maamah cikin sakin fuska.
Itama mamar ta juyo fuskarta cikin hasken annuri take amsawa kana ta dubi nafisa da kulawa tana sakin far'a cikin sauri feenah ta rissuna  ta soma gaida maamah cikin ladabi da biyayya ganin haka yasa umma ta dire mata miemah akan cinyarta tabar wajan ta koma can kan kujera ta zauna.
Taƙasan idanu feenah take kallon maamah tsananin kamarsu dashi tayi yawa kamar an tsaga kara haka take kama dashi.
Bata sha mamaki ba seda taga yadda mamar ta tsirawa miemah idanu tana kallonta cike da soyayya, itakam feenah mamakin wannan alkunya ta mama takeyi,wadda gatanan akan fuskar matar ta bayyana.
Ahaka shahida suka shigo cikin falon tare da wasu ƴammata se shahid mai bin shahidar shima, ai har ana rige rige wajan zuwa ɗaukar miema wanda daga karshe ALLAH yabawa shahid sa,a ya dauketa.
Cikin murna yake jijjigata yana cillata sama.
Cike da rawar jiki sakina matar imran wadda take shigowa falon tai maza ta nufi wajansa ta karbeta tana dariya.

75.

Cikin sauri tuwon data saka abakinta ya soma ƙoƙarin fitowa sabida ƙoƙarin amsa sallamar hidayan datake son tayi.

Da kanta ta dafa masa tea mai kayan kamshi sannan tai ɗakin da nafisa take kwance

Karbar kayan tea ɗin tai tare da nufar haɗaɗɗen part ɗin nasa wanda numfashinta seda yakusan daukewa ganin yadda aka narka uwar dukiya acikinsa.

Harta shiga cikin ainihin main falon jikinta bai bar bariba dan dai itadin yarinyace marar musu amman da babu abinda zesakata sake binsa, sabida tsorone fal acikin ranta.

Falon ƙasa wayam se uban manyan kujeru tare da katuwar t.v plazma, rau rau tai da idanu zatai kuka! ganin step ɗin bene awani hanyar corridow shiya bata damar bin hanyar harta karasa wajan cikin sanyin jiki da tsoro tahau ta soma tafiya, wani sanyin AC mai shiga jikine ya buleta a yayin data murda handle ɗin kofar glass ɗin data kare babban falon saman, harta samu ta shiga rantsatstsen falon nasa wanda yaji saitin manyan kujerun royal ƴan gasken masu kyau da tsari se katuwar t.v infact  ma dai komai na more rayuwa yaji acikin wannan falon babu karya.
Ƙwai guda ɗayane kawai a kunne wannan dalilin yasa sam bata hangesa ba ganin tana bata lokacinta ga dare nayi yasa tai hanyar daining domin ta aje tana ayyana ko suna ina oho?

Cikin sauri ta karasa domin idanunta sun nuna mata cewar yes shiɗinne cikin kiɗima ta jefar da kayan tea ɗin hannunta har ruwan tea ɗin na zubo mata a ƙafafunta amman hakan baisaka ta tsayaba harseda ta karasa gabansa.
Cikin zubar ƙwalla ta ɗora dukkan hannayenta saman cikin nasa tana shafa masa.
"Miyake damunka?" tafaɗa tana zubar da hawaye, azuciyarta tana ayyana yanzufa suka rabu lafiya amman mike damunsa? ganin babu mai bata amsa yasa ta kai face ɗinta dai dai saitin tasa tana kallonsa.
Idanunsa a lumshe suke cikin zaƙuwa tare da zalama ya tallafota baki ɗaya yamata masauki asaman ruwan cikinsa wanda rigarsa tuni ta tattare sabida yadda ya dageta yana dafe marar tasa 
Cikin zafin nama tare da zaƙuwa ya matseta tsam! asaman ruwan cikinsa yana jan numfashi.
Tsuru tai tana neman dauki aranta tana ayyana ta shiga ukunta! mima ya kawota nan din baki daya kuma maganar innatu ta faɗo mata aranta shi wannan wani irin namijine, kafin tunaninta ya dawo mata taji saukar ɗumin bakinsa cikin nata yana zuba mata miyoun sa yana kuma tsotsar cikin bakin nata tamkar ya samu alawa, kukane yake kokarin zuwar mata amman sam, yaki bata damar yin haka.
Ɗagata yay cak ya ɗorata bisa ruwan cikinsa jin tudun halittarsa yay mugun bata tsoro cikin wani irin yanayi mai fitar hayyaci yake furta "kitaimaken zanmutuuuuuu! yaja maganar kamar wanda yaci yaji atake anan yay ƙasa da ita, sosai yafara gudanar da abinda yake muradin yi domin ya samu nutsuwa, jin ɗaya hanyar tasa babu waje yasa yabi ta ɗaya hanyar dayake ganin ze samu relief, jin saukar ɗumi cikin bakinta tare da ɗan wani irin ɗanɗano na daban ya bata tabbacin abin da take tsorone ya auku musamman lokacin dayake budurin nasa wanda hatta numfashinta seta wahala take fesar dashi.
Seda yakai kusan mintuna masu yawa yana budurinsa kafin ya sarara mata yaja gefe yana mayar da numfashi, ta cikin ɗan hasken falon yake kallon yadda idanunta suka kumbura kaɗan sabida kukan datai, itama cikin wani yanayi take kauda kanta domin gudun kallon halittar tasa wadda har wani irin rawar jiki take sabida gujewa kallon nasa.
Sakinta yay daga jikinsa wanda yabata damar tattare ragowar karfinta tabar wajan aguje!.
Bataga anty ba dan haka tai cikin part ɗin duk ilahirin jikinta yana faman rawa, toilet ta wuce tare da tsugunnawa a ƙasa tana share hawayenta gani take tamkar wani ya gansu.
Takai mintuna masu tsaho a haka sannan ta haɗawa kanta ruwa mai ɗumi tai wanka dashi ta ɗauro tawul a ƙugunta, bayan ta fito kai tsaye ta nufi gaban madubi ta shafa ɗan mai sama sama sabida yanayin garin ana ɗan sanyi kaɗan doguwar riga ta zira mai laushi tare da hijabi tai kangado tare da kwanciya.
Tana lumshe idanunta tana hango al amarin ɗazu wanda idan ta tuna har gabanta faduwa yakeyi.

Lumshe idanunsa yake tare da ɗan fesar da iska mai sanyi jin yadda jikinsa ya samu gamsuwa sosai, jikinsa ya gyara tare da tashi da ƙyar gaban daining yaje ya yago tissue, gaban wajan yazo ya goge duk inda ya baci kana yay cikin bedroom ɗinsa, yana jinsa sakayau, kai tsaye bathroom ya wuce bayan ya zuba kayan jikinsa cikin washing machine, dafe bangon tiles ɗin bath ɗin yay domin baya jin ze iya shiga bathtube  dan haka ya sakarma kansa showwer sosai sannan ya ɗauro tawul bayan ya ɗauro alwala, agaban katon dressing mirrow ɗinshi ya tsaya tare da ɗaukar Cum yana sharce kansa, bayan nan ya ɗanyi abinda ze kana ya feshe jikinsa da spray masu sanyin kamshi sannan yay gaban katon wadrob ɗinsa domin ya saka kaya, shigar three quarter yay baƙi da white amrless, bayan ya kammala yay gaban gadonsa yaɗan kishingiɗa kaɗan, cikin mintuna biyar sega hidaya nan ta turo kofar da kallo ya bita sannan ya amsa mata sallamar tata.

Baccine mai dadi  ya kwashesa wanda bakaramin bakan tawa hidaya yay ba amman hakan ta daure ta rabasa ta kwanta tana gunguni.

Washe gari da wuri guard's ɗinsa sukai masa caa acan bakin gate sabida yau ɗin zeje fadar mai girma shugaban ƙasa, domin su tattauna akan wasu ayyuka wannan dalilin yasa bai je yaga miemah ba, tunda kuma yatafi bai dawo ba se wajan magariba a part ɗinsa ya tare ya addabi hidaya da jarabarsa har akai sallar isha'i sannan ya samu ya fita.

Sosai nafisa ta damu da rashin zuwan nasa dan hakama koda yazo bata ɗaga ta dubesa ba haka ya gama wasanshi da ƴarsa ya fita shima bai wani damu da hakan ba karewa ma shi yadauka barci take.

Kuka tasaka tace daman tasan ba sonta yake ba.

Acikin kwanakin kuma baya samun zama sosai sabida ayyukan dayake gudanarwa, har ranar suna tazo wanda fadin irin karyar abin duniyar da akai ma ɓata bakine  nadai bar mai karatu ya hasko sunan da kansa, anyi abubuwa sosai wanda seda ya girgiza yan garin na ɗawisu, wanda aranar babu abinda ake haskowa se wannan shagalin na suna wanda nafisa har kuka tai dan murna sannan tai takaicin rashin ganin walida akusa da ita.

Anyi taron suna lafiya angama lafiya yarinya taci suna nana khadija, wanda kuma har zuwa  wannan lokacin feena bata samu kansa ba sabida yadda baima cika shigo musuba sabida baya zama idan kuma ya dawo da gajiya yake dawo wa seya tare part ɗin hidaya watarana ma se tai bacci yake lekowa.

Wannan abun fa bakaramin........!
*79*


Wannan abun nasa daya ɓullo dashi a kwana kinnan ba ƙaramin ɗagawa nafisa hankali yayiba, sannan kuma ranta yana mugun ɓaci zuciyarta tana tafasuwa da irin yadda ya ɓullo ɗin.
Yauma tana zaune a tsakar ɗaki bayan ta fito daga wanka, zaune take akan wani irin pillow wanda aka ƙawatashi da ado mai burgewa, hannunta guda ɗauke da ƙaton frame na hoton suna wanda ɗazunnan shahida ta shigo masu, dasu wai ankawo daga ɗawisu photos gefenta kuma wani ɗan madaidaicin kwano ne na silva wanda aka cikoshi taf da soyayyan naman rago na suna, wanda wannan aikin na innatune tunda akai sunan yau kimanin kwana biyar kenan kullum seta ɗebo mata naman waitaci ta more acewarta.
Hotunan tashiga buɗewa waɗanda dukkan ninsu babu na banza acikinsu, domin bakaramin kyau sukai acikin hotunanba,musamman ita da batai wata kwalliyar kirkiba, sedai ƴar powder data shafa se gazal, shaddace ajikinta mai kalar green wadda taci uban kuɗi ita kuma ta hidaya yelluwa domin taga duk wasu ɗinkunan data saka wanda shahida ta kawo mata adaran sunan kowannensu ta gansa ajikin hidaya sedai bam-bancin kala, dakuma tsarin kwalliya domin sosai hidaya tai make up ɗan ubansu wanda idan ka dubeta seka rantse ma itace mai jegon, haka ta dauki dogon lokaci tana faman duba hotuna tana cin namanta wanda sam bata jin ɗan ɗanonsa ko kaɗan sabida yadda bata cikin walwala a kwanakin nan sabida rowar ganinsa dayake mata.

Cikin haka anty ta shigo bayanta zuby da afra ne dukkansu sunyi kyau cikin ƴan kanti jajaye, zama anty tayi akan sopa tana mai duban nafisa, sannan tace mata"waike mike damunki ne nafuskanci kwanan nan kamar bakya jin daɗi?" takai mata maganar da kula, caraf innatu wadda take fitowa daga cikin banɗaki ta amshe maganar "kema kya faɗa har ina cewa wannan ƴa da baƙin hali take kotafi son dangin ubanta ne akanmu wannan ƙunci yay yawa hansatu sefa ta tashi daga gaisuwa bata kuma min magana".innatu takai maganar tana yarfe hannunta.
Cikin nutsuwa anty ta mayar da hankalinta kan ɗiyar tata kana ta juya  ta dubi innatu sannan tace"kiyi haquri innah wallahi nima abun ya soma isata dan ubanta idan zaman danine bata so wallahi sena haɗa mata kayanta tabarmin gidana, dan tsabar rashin hankali da nutsuwa ace shekara nawa rabona dake amman ALLAH yaso mun haɗun shine bazaki kwantar min da hankalina ba" anty takai maganar cikin jin haushinta.
Kukan miema wadda ta barka daga bacci yasa innatu mikewa tana mita ta karasa ta daukota tazo ta dire mata ita akan cinyarta sannan ta dora da tara masifar itama "Nima dai abin da nake gani kenan kamar tafi sonsu akanmu mutanan dasuka rabaki da uwarki" share hawayenta tai tare da turawa miemah mama, cikin bakinta tanajin zuciyarta tana rawa sam bata son taga anty na ɓacin rai akanta itafa ba akan haka bane ita tata damuwar akan sa ne amman tayaya zata nuna musu hakan?tayaya zata nuna musu shiɗinne garkuwa agareta sam bazasu fahimci hakanba se ita da zuciyarta.
Se kawai ta fashe da kuka batare data iya furta komai ba, miemah yarinya ƴar kimanin kwanaki goma sha biyu a duniya, itama sakin maman tai ta saka kukansu na jarirai wanda wannan lamari yabaiwa kowa mamaki ba kaɗan ba abu kamar a shirin film.

Sosai nafisa take kukan nata batare data ce masu komai ba tunda batasan yadda xatai ta fahimtar dasu ba ɗin,Wani mayen ƙamshin turarene yacika ɗakin wanda tun kafin ya mayar da marufin kofar hancinta ya shaida masa shine ɗin.
Sallama ce abakin radau wadda kana jinta kasan acikin nishaɗinsa yake.
Tsugunnawa yay akan kafafunshin tare da gaisar da innatu wadda take faman dokawa feenah harara sannan ya gaisar da anty, babu ko kunya ya karasa gabansu yana mai mika hannunsa yana amsar miema tare da kallon anty "miya samesu naga har maman tata ma kukan suke hala lafiya?" yakai tambayar cikin nutsuwa yana mai ɗora miema bisa kan kafadunsa yana rarrashinta.
Tunma kafin anty ta furta wata magana innatu ta rigata da cewa........."Hmm wannan matar taka anwaru mun kasa gane mata kullum wani baƙin halin natake sake fito mana yanzufa harta kai nafisa idanta farka da safe daga gaisuwa bata kuma tankawa kowa acikinmu gatanan har uwar tata, wadda ko tausan hansatu bataji".takai maganar tana sharce hawayenta.murmushi yayi wanda ya bayyana masa da ainihin baiwar kyawun da ALLAH yay masa sannan ya dubi anty "Allah ya baku haquri baxamu kuma ba". yafadi hakan yana dariyar innatu acikin ransa ya fuskanci rayuwar yarinyarce kamar a haka ta taso a tsangwama shine yasa har yanxu bata cika sakewa da jama'a da wannan dalilin shima yake mata uzuri "Hmm nikam Muhammad  nakasa gane kan kanwar taka senaga kamar tamafi son can gidansu akan mu nan".Anty takai maganar cikin tsananin damuwa.
Alokacin feena ta dago tana kallon anty tana share kuma hawayen nata itama kana tace "Dan ALLAH kidaina faɗin haka nikam ba haka nake nufi ba".takai maganar tamkar zata kurma ihu! "Kimana shuru munafukar banza kai anwaru ka sawwaqe mata kawai tai can gidan uban nata ta karaci tsiyarta".innatu takai maganar cikin barkwanci dariya kurum yay baice komai ba sema fidda wayarsa dayay yana dubawa.
Kifa kanta tai tana cigaba da kukanta tayaya za,ai su fuskance ta su gane akan dalilin kukan nata?

kaɗan ta sauke dukkan ilahirin jikinta ya ɗauki rawa jin yadda yake faman goga mata sajen nashi ajikinta, hannunta tasaka ta ture shi ajikin nata tare da kifawa a kan gado kuka mai cin rai ta saki, ita kaɗai takejin yadda zuciyarta ke fama akansa, hannunshi duka biyun ya ɗora a saman west ɗinta cikin wani irin salo yake murza mata shi, wani irin zillo tayi tare da mirginawa tai baya tamkar zata faɗi ƙasa, gwiwarsa ya ɗora a saman gadon tare da tarairayota baki ɗaya ya dawo da ita kan cinyarsa ya aje yana binta da idanunsa waɗanda suka sauya kala zuwa launin jah! Tsira mata idanunsa yay yana kallon yadda take rintse idanunta alamun bata son kallonsa, kwantota yay a jikinsa tare da ɗora fuskarsa a saman tata gami da fito da harshenshi ya ɗora akan bakinta yanajin yadda bakin nata yake da wani irin laushi, cikin dubara ya sami damar tura harshen nasa cikin bakin nata sosai ya lashe dukkan miyoun dake cikin bakinta, sannan ya ɗagota"Kibuɗe idonki kice bakya sona" yafadi maganar yana kallon saman idanunta wanda ya kumbura kaɗan, tura bakinta tai gaba jitake kamar ta miqe tabar wajan, ɗora bakinsa yay a saman idanunta ya tsotsa kaɗan, sannan ya mayar da idanunsa saman rigarta wadda saman yaɗan bullutso kaɗan na shanunta ya ɗan fito wanda yake ganiyar sa a yanzun, hannunsa ya ɗora a saman wajan  yana ɗan shafawa kaɗan, tureshi tai tare da tashi aguje! tai cikin bayi ta danna key, tana jin jikinta yana amsa dukkan sakonnin nasa amman tuno da nasihar innatu ya fargar da ita a lamarin nasa.

Cikin wani irin yanayi ya ɗan saki killer smile kaɗan tare da  sauka daga gadon yay hanyar waje jin wayarsa tana ringing!

Afalon anty babu kowa hakan ya bashi damar saka kansa yabar wajan kai tsaye parking space ya nufa gaban wata bakar prada ya tsaya yana amsa wayar tasa, hidaya wadda take faman jefa masa ƙorafi acikin wayar itace ta fito cikin shirinta bayanta afra ce da shahida duk sunci kwalliya cikin shiga ta alfarma.

Kansu tsaye wajan motar suka nufa suka bude back seat suka shiga ita kuma hidaya ta bude front seat ta zauna tana faman tura bakinta gaba sabida ya ɓata masu lokaci.

Seda ya gama abinda ze ya bude driver seat ya zauna yana ɗaura belt ajikinsa, da sauri sarkin gida ya rugo gabansa "Mai gida nace naga zaka fita yau babu su oska kenan amman mai gida baka ganin fita a haka kai ɗaya babu matsala?" sarkin gida ya faɗi maganar cikin ɗan damuwa kaɗan.
Murmushi ya saki acikin ransa sannan ya dubi sarkin gida "Karka damu sarki insha ALLAH babu matsala kaga yau week end kowa yana cikin iyalinsa baxan taso suba, nan nan zamuje unguwar goba gidansu madam sister ɗinta ce ta haihu". yakai maganar cikin kulawa. murmushi sarkin gida yay cikin jin daɗin yadda uban gidan nasu yake kulawa dasu tare da furta "ALLAH ya tsare oga" da ameen ya amsa tare da yin addu,a ya kunna motar yay revars tare da nufar gate. da gudu masu gadi suka bude masa gate ɗin. motar tasa ta halba kwallata ɗoɗar...............
