Cikin mintuna talatin suka isa anguwar goba, gidan ƙanin maamah mahaifiyarsa kenan,kuma gidan mahaifan hidaya matarsa, parking yayi abakin ɗan madai daicin gate ɗin gidan, batare daya ƙarasa wajan masu gadi ba, buɗe motar yay ya fito suma su hidaya fitowa sukai suka taka da ƙafafunsu har gaban gate ɗin, bude ƴar ƙaramar ƙofar da ake shiga yay tare da yiwa masu gadin sallama, cikin nutsuwa batare daya tuna shiɗin wani bane ba, ya miƙa masu hannu sukai musabaha, Cikin matuƙar farin ciki masu gadin sukaita washe baki, batare daya ɓata lokaci ba ya zaro bandir ɗin ƴan onek  ya mika masu sannan yasa kai yabar wajan ya nufi cikin gidan magan ganun masu gadin gidan ne ya karade kunnuwansa "ALLAH yaja daran hajiya hadiza ALLAH yajikan Alhajin mai carbi gado se ɗan gado sukari bakai farin banza ba! ALLAH ya raya zuriya insha ALLAHU sekai shugaban ƙasa" murmushi kawai ya saki tare da nufar ciki, da saurinsa, babbar ƙofar sitroom  yaja ya shiga ta wajan saukar baki wanda daman tun asali idan zeje gidan anan yake zama, acikin ɗaya daga cikin kujerun sitroom ɗin ya zauna tare da sada kansa a ƙasa yana jin wani iri acikin jikinsa tunda ya rabeta yakasa samun sukuni acikin jikinsa, tabbas baya sonta so ɗayane tak! yana kuma wajan hidayansa, amman kuma yana ishirwarta tabbas idan bai da gaskeba ze iya yin abin kunya ma,ana ze iya jawowa kansa raini, uwa uba kuma idan bai nisanta kansa da itaba tabbas ze shiga matsala sabida jegon datake, taune ƙasan laɓɓansa yay tare da jan numfashi kaɗan, sallamar Abba ƙanin maamah baban hidaya ita ta dawo dashi daga tunanin daya faɗa, cikin sauri ya diro daga saman kujerar tare da tsugunnawa ya gaisar da abban sannan suka soma ɗan tattaunawa wanda rabi akan abubuwan dasuke addabar wannan ƙasar tamune musamman cikin jaharsu ta ɗawisu, zaman abba da ƴan mintuwa umma itama ta shigo gaisawa suka kumayi kafin ƙannan hidaya su shigo tare da kayan abinci,cikin mutuntawa suka gaisa da kannen hidaya sannan suka fita suka barshi shida abba da umma wadda itama tana gama gaisawar dashi tabar wajan, mai jego ma tazo yay mata barka yaga ɗan nata harya mata kyautar kuɗi masu yawa sannan abba ya mike ya fita yana ce masa ya daure yaci abincin koda kaɗanne,acikin gida kuwa, ɗakin umman hidaya cike yake da ƴan uwanta waɗanda suke ciki guda, umma ce ta dubeta "Hidaya ina son magana dake".cikin sanyin jiki ta furta ina sauraranki".da kallo umma tabi sauran ƴan uwan hidaya waɗanda hakan ya shaida masu mai umman take nufi, sum sum suka fita zuwa falo inda su shahida ke zaune suna kallo, sosai umma ta mayar da hankalinta kan hidaya "Kinfi kowa sanin tarbiyar dana baki sabida haka bana goyon bayanki akan kishin hauka irin na dabbobin mata idan har kikai abinda zumunci mu ya ɓaci ko kikai abinda za,ai tirr da ke wallahi!tallahi! ban yafe maki ba".cikin zaro idanu hidaya ta furta "Wallahi umma sam tunani na bai kai haka ba, ada tabbas na fito da wasu halaye na kirsa da riya wanda, ƙawata shafa tai min huɗubarsu amman dana zauna nai nazari sena gane ba kowace ƙawa bace takeson ganinka a ni'imah! wata ƙawar a kullum burinta ta sauya maka farin cikinka zuwa bakin ciki wata ƙawar kullum burinta ta fitar dakai daga rahamar dakake ciki domin tana bakin ciki da rahamar da ALLAH yay maka".ta numfasa tana kallon umma "Amman wallahi umma duk na watsar zan rungumeta tare da ƴarta kuma zan rungumi mijina domin bai sauya min ba wallahi umma yadda yakemin ada yanzun ba bata sauya zaniba tabbas shiɗin adaline sabida haka zanyi kokarin ganin na so ƴarsa domin shine tukwicin ƙaunarsa gareni, amman kuma umma kema kinsan kishi ajininmu yake mu mata zanyi dai iya yina insha ALLAH dan ALLAH kema ki sakani acikin addu,arki". takai maganar tamkar zatai kuka!
Shafo kanta umma tayi cike da tausayawa sabida daman duk cikin ƴaƴanta hidaya tana da saukin kai tare da sanin darajar mutum sam duniya bata dameta ba, "Zan miki addu,a hidaya kema ALLAH ya baki ɗan kanki sabida haka kema ki kuma ɗorawa akan wadda kike fatana ALLAH ya baku zaman lafiya ALLAH kuma ya ƙara nisataki daga sharrin zuciya dana muyagun ƙawaye". murmushi tayi tare da share hawayenta sannan tace "Ameen Umma nagode".Anan kuma hira ta karade a tsakanin ta da mahaifiyar tata tabbas taji 80daga cikin 100 na damuwarta sun ragu a ƴar tattaunawar da sukai da ummanta, kuma acikin ranta tana addu,ar ALLAH ya bar mata ummanta sabida uwace nagartacciya mai tsage gaskiya sam bata damu da duniyaba, wannan dalilin yasa suke aminai ita da maamah mahaifiyar mijinta. Sun ɗauki lokaci mai yawa suna hira cikin haka Aminu mai binta ya shigo ɗakin dayake yana da barkwanci sosai suka zauna ana hira har zuwa sanda sakon sa yazo mata akan tafito, zasu wuce gida.Koda suka tashi tafiya seda umma ta kuma bata shawarwari masu kyau daga ƙarshe kuma tace mata "Komai zakiyi kidinga tunawa da cewa da mahaifiyar mijinki da mahaifinki cikinsu ɗaya komai zakiwa matarsa ko ƴarsa kidinga tuna wannan dangantakar wadda nasan ko babu aure a tsakaninku keɗin mai iya rike masa sune, ALLAH ya tsareki daga sharrin shaiɗan, ban hanaki kula da mijinki da riritashiba amman komai zaki kiyi sa domin ALLAH kada kiyi domin ki bakantawa marainiyar ALLAH rai".share hawaye hidaya tai tare da kallon umma tana murmushi "Umma senaga kamar ma kinfi sonta naji ance ranar farkoma keki ka mata wanka".dariya umma tai "Idan banda abinki hidaya aikome lauje ya yayo ciyawa ai anwarr ɗana ne koda baya aurenki kituna yadda mahaifiyarsa take sona itafa tai silar kawoni gidan nan ita ta haɗa aurena da abbanku, bayan haka kuma nafisa abar tausayice kituna ke kina dani kinada abbanki ita fa? daga fa mahaifiyarta bata da kowa". dariya hidaya ta kuma saki tana rataya jakarta tace "tana da abban miemah ai wallahi umma bara kiga setai alfahari dashi sabida na yarda da ya anwarr managarcin mutum ne".ahaka ta fita daga falon mahaifiyar tata tana jin ranta sakayau. ko a mota anwarr yay mamakin yadda ta saki jikinta tanata sabgarta bakamar fitowarsu ba, awani store suka tsaya bayan tsabar takurar data masa da sauri ta fita bayan ta ɗan leko da kanta "Afuwan dadyn miemah ina zuwa kabani mintuna uku" da kallo ya bita tare dajin wani sanyi aransa "dadyn miemah! ya maimaita a fili tuni wani farin ciki ya kamasa dama zega wannan rana, Cikin kamala ta karasa cikin tsore ɗin ɓangaren beby's dream ta nufa, Atm ɗinta ta fito dashi wanda daman indai zata fita tare dashi take fita sosai ta jibgi kayan beby kala kala na dubunnai sannan ta koma kan kayan shafarsu nan ma tai siyayya bata wasa ba, ta kaso iyayen kuɗi bayan an mata bill aka shigar da kayan cikin leda masu kula da wajan suka ɗaukar mata kayan har zuwa mota, a boot aka zuba mata seda tai masu alheri dayake itama hannunta a bude yake sannan ta bude motar ta shiga tana basu haquri da kallo ya bita batare dayace mata komai ba yatayar da motar suka nufi gida, yawan amsa wayan dayake yasaka basu wani yi sauriba ana kiran sallar isha'i suka karasa a bakin gate ɗin farko ya tsaya tare dayin parking ya ƙarasa gaban mutanan dake jiransa dayake daman baya rabuwa da mabukata ya sallamesu sannan yazo yaja motar ya karasa ciki, shi baima shiga part ɗinsu ba alwala yaje famfo ya dauro ya nufi masallaci seda yay salla sannan ya nufi part ɗinsu, direct bedroom ya nufa rage kayan jikinsa yayi tare da fadawa bathroom, yayi wanka sannan ya fito ya shirya kansa cikin farar jallabiya, lokacin daya fita falo daining yay redy sabida haka zama yay hidaya tai serving ɗinsa itama tai nata suka soma cin abinci wanda rabi wasanninsu suke cike da burgewa.
Seda komi ya kammala sannan ta miqe tsaye hijabi ta ɗoro asaman  ƴar sleeping dress ɗin dake jikinta sannan tazo gabansa manyan ledojin daka shigo mata dasu ɗazu ta ɗauka guda biyu ta tura masa ɗaya gabansa, "Dady kazo kaimin rakiya" da kallo ya bita tare da lumshe idanunsa "Kinsan dai matsala bazan iya fita a haka ba".yafaɗa yana nuna mata yadda abar tasa ta miƙe acikin jallabiyar tasa, Murmushi tayi tare da aje ledar gabanta kana ta miqa hannunta kan abar taɗan ja kaɗan! "Afuwan beby ta kimin uzuri yanzu zamu dawo na baki kulawa". Tafaɗ cikin wani irin salo mai rikitarwa, tunma kafin takai aya.ya jawota jikinsa ya matseta yana fesar da zazzafan huci! tare da lalubar bakinta yafara tsotsa seda yaɗan rage zafi da ita sannan ya miqe yana ɗan dafe rigarsa, ahaka yaja ɗaya ledar suka fita.
Ganin ta nufi part ɗin anty ya basa mamaki amman dayake miskiline bai ce mata komiba, a falo suka tarar da inna tu tana gasawa miema cibiya cikin barkwanci suka gaisa da ita, sannan hidaya ta tambayi anty? cemata tai tana cikin ɗakinta tanawa afra wanka, ita kuma nafisa tana ɗaki, tashi hidayan tai shima ya miqe suka karasa cikin dakin da nafisa ke ciki. Sallama hidaya tayi,tare da tura kofa zaune take agaban madubi tana sharce kanta jin sallamar bai saka ta rufeba ta juyo tana tsare kofar da ido tare da amsa sallamar cikin muryarta mai karkarwa.
Ganin hidaya yasa taɗan kame kanta sabida bata san dame tazo ba, gogan yana baya ganinsu su biyu yasa ta miqe ta jawo mayafin data aje a gefe ta yafa tare da karasowa kan center carpet ɗin ɗakin taja pillow ta zauna, hidaya itama zama tai a kusa da ita shikuma yana tsaye ya harde hannunsa a saman kirjinsa azuciyarta tace oh jibi namiji kenan kamar bashi ɗazu yagama yamutsataba.
Cikin sakin jiki hidayan take mata magana "Zuwa nai dama na kawo miki tawa ƴar tsarabar babu yawa dai asakawa miemah ta" takai maganar tana jawo ledar gabanta sosai nafisa tasha mamakin kayan mai ɗa wawa nan danan ta saki jikinta tana mata godiya shima kansa ganin kayan bakaramin mamaki ya bashiva aidaman duk kudinka kana son kaga anyi maka, sosai kuma yaji hidaya ta kuma shiga ransa.
Hira sama sama suke ita da hidayan inda atake anan nafisa taga sauyi sosai a fuskarta bakamar rannan ɗinba.
Koda wasa batai gigin kallonsa bare yasaka ran gaisuwa, wannan lamarin ya bashi mamaki sema kara washewa hidayar baki take sunata hirarsu kamar ma sun manta dashi awajan.cikin ransa yake tuna wani lamari daya taɓa ji wai idan mata suka hade kansu gasa miji suke, ganin kamar ya tafi tunani yasa hidaya ta dubesa "Dady hirarfa ta ɗau daɗi koza zauna ga waje" bai tanka mata ba sema sake gyara tsaiwar sa dayay ita kuwa nafisa batama kalleshi ba tacigaba da sabgarta, itama hidaya atake anan ta gano nafisa yarinyar tana da saukin kai fiye da zato.
Turo kofar akai innatu ce riqe da miema tana ta far,a bayanta wasu mazane su biyu.
"A,a haba sekace baki ku shigo mana tana ciki".cewar innatu sanin hali yasa hidaya ta kame kanta waje guda bayan ta amshi miema ta koma can gefen gado ta zauna tana gyarawa miemah parmpas, ita kuwa nafisa zama ta gyara tana washe bakinta tare da furta "Gabakin dare ai wallahi nayi fushi ace yayye guda se dare zasu zo".Cikin barkwanci babban cikinsu ya kai mata ranƙwashi da sauri ta goce tana dariya tare da furta "Wallahi yaya baffa har yau halinka yana nan bai sauyaba". bummmmmmm!! sukaji ƙarar rufe kofarsa da ƙyar yake taka kafafunsa wannan wani irin haukane da sakarci.........

*82*



Wannan wani irin sakarcine, ace ƙatunan garada su shiga har cikin ɗakin matar aure ta sunna, abin baima tsaya a hakanba harseda wancan mai fuskar alalan yake ƙoƙarin kai mata ranƙwashi aka, bai san ina yake taka ƙafafunsa ba har yakai cikin falonsa, a tsakiyar carpet ya baje yana mayar da ajiyar zuciya, kai menene na damuwa da hakan bayan ba sonta kake ba? cewar zuciyarsa kenan, shafo sumar kansa yay tare da furta "Ina kishin aure na ne" yabaima kansa amsa a bayyane, cikin sauri ya miqe jikinsa haryana tsuma kai tsaye fita ya kumayi a hanya suka gamu da hidaya tana kokarin shigowa ko kallonta bai ba ita ma kanta haushin take basa duk maganar datake masa bai kulata ba, cikin mamaki tabi bayansa da kallo, kansa tsaye part ɗin antyn ya koma jikinsa har yana rawa, koda suka gamu dasu a hanya suna kokarin fitowa baiko dubi inda suke ba, hasali ma wani mugun kallo ya watsa musu tare da banka kofar ya faɗa tamkar wani zararre, tana kokarin gyara gyalenta domin raka ƴan uwan nata, su ya baffane ƴaƴan yayan mahaifinta su a birni suke xaune da matansu kuma babu laifi sukam bata da matsala dasu, domin sosai suke sonta dan duk wata matsalar ta idan ta tashi ta kirasu a waya babu laifi zasu taimaka mata dai dai ƙarfinsu, hankaɗa ta yay tai taga taga zata faɗi, cikin basarwa tai maza ta miqe batare data kulashi ba takuma yunkurin fita, da ƙafa ya kwarfota ta faɗi akan tiles kanta yayo ganga ganga, cikin tsoro taja baya tana dafe bakinta, wanda ya fashe saboda buguwar datai akan tile ɗin, cikin wani irin zafin nama ya ɗagota cak! ya tsaidata a gabansa jikinsa yana rawa bakinsa ma haka Saurin bakinsa yafi nada, "Ke watakan baki san darajar aure ba shine har kike barewa waɗancan garadan baki" yafaɗa cikin ƙaraji! tureshi tai da iya ƙarfinta tana mai dafe bakinta "Ƴan uwana ne maye dan na sakar masu fuska, kai xan iya sauya ka amman sukam bazasu sau......"  batakai maganar ba ya kife bakin nata da tafin hannunsa "Ƴan uwan banza ƴan uwan hofi waɗanda basu san darajarki da ciwonkiba, to wallahi bara na gaya miki na tsani danginki bana ƙaunarsu bana bukatarsu a rayuwarki waɗanda suka kasa rikeki alokacin da gudan jini na ke jikinki"!!! yakai maganar cikin tsawa!   "Kaima bana ƙaunar ka bana buƙatarka a rayuwata" fuwwww ta wuce cikin fushi ta yayo miemah wadda take cikin nate tana barci, gabansa tazo ta dire masa ita "Gata nan indai ƴarkace gatanan ina ita ta haɗani dakai to ga abarka nan nima kabarni na rayu da ƴan uwana, wannan wani irin bala'ine Anwarr kai ɗayane namiji ko ance maka dole seda kai zanyi rayuwa bana auren kabani takarda ta ga ƴarka nan kowa ya kama gabansa, kuma wallahi a yanxu kabani sena bar maka wannan kaddararran gidan na......." bata kai karshe ba ya bata wani kyakykyawan mari mai shiga jiki, kafin ya sauke hannunsa taji wani bakon marin a ɗaya side ɗin nata, kafin kuma daga baya taji duka kota ina acikin jikinta, muryar anty ce ta karade ɗakin  "Matsa Anwarr kaban waje karna  sameka ɗauke miema daga ƙasa, maza, Dan ubanki ni yau sena illataki  haka tarbiyar gidan naku take to wallahi ba,a gabana ba wani shegen ne yabaki tarbiyar mayarwa da mijinki magana,billahillazi ko mahaifiyarsa kishiyata ban taɓa mayarwa da maganaba bare mahaifinsa akan biyayyata aka cucen aka rabani dake, mijina koze yanki naman jikina wallahi bazan masa abinda kikewa anwar ba". sosai take jibgarta kota ina, ganin abun yay yawa yasa yay maza ya aje miema a gefen gado yajanyota jikinsa yana bawa anty hakuri "Anty dan ALLAH kiyi hakuri laifina nane wallahi, dan ALLAH anty kiyi haquri". Abinda yaketa faɗa kenan Cike da xafin rai anty tai waje fuww tabar ɗakin jitake kamar tai kuka ancuceta an ɓata mata tarbiyar ƴa.
Tureshi tai ajikinta tare da zaro masa idanunta tamkar mai cutar aljanu "Dalla ni kasakeni aida kabari ta kasheni karewar kiyayya kenan".takai maganar tana kuka sosai.
Tallafota yayi zuwa jikinsa yana bubbuga mata bayanta, "Ni kasakeni wallahi seka rabu dani bana sonka bana kaunar ka indai haka soyayya take batai min adalciba dan ina sonka zakana gaya min dukkan maganar datazo bakinka banasoooooo" wata iriyar murya tamkar ta rodi ce ta cika masa dodon kunnensa kafin yaji kuma ɗuf! tamkar gawa ta kife ajikinsa dawani irin karfi taja dogon numfashi tare da bankarewa baki ɗaya, a nitse ya fuskanci komai wata iriyar ƙara! ce takaraɗe ko ina na ɗakin kafin yaji dirin sautin wani irin kuka! wanda yasa da gudu anty da innatu harma da su afra kunnowa cikin ɗakin, wata irin banƙara take tana haniniya tana wata iriyar dafara, kafin wani lokaci tagama jigata jikinta ko ina na jikinta rawa, yake haka zalika ko ina na halittar jikinta ta sauya idanunta sun koma wasu iri, sosai anty ta gigice tana kuka! kafin wani lokaci maganar taje kunnen maama wadda zuby taje ta sanar da ita, ganin tana kokarin illata yasa yay ta maza ya isa gabanta, da ƙyar ya samu ya rike hannunta saboda wani irin karfin dabai taba jiba shiyaji acikin ƙashinta, duka take kaimasa ta kota ina, cikin ƙwarewa tare da gwancewa yafara karanto (suratul baqra) yana yi yana tofa mata mamah wadda itama jikinta ke rawa tana gefensa tana karanto dukkan addu,ar datazo bakinta, sun jima suna karatun kafin ta fara sauke ajiyar zuciya sekuma kuka!  mai ƙarfi ya zo mata tashi tai tsam ta hade kanta da gwiwa tana kuka! "Anwarr bakai mana adalci ba! Anwar bakai mana adalci ba!! wata iriyar murya mai kama data yara ce ta karade ɗakin, Cikin dakewa yake tsareta da idanunsa baki ɗaya tausayinta ya gama kamasa, "Muba macuta bane ba hasalima mu ƴan gadone duk wanda yasan gidan liman yasan akwai mu acikin gidan, mukanzo jikinta idan ranta ya ɓaci, tabbas a yau zuwanmu yazo maka acikin alkairi domin yadda muke tafasa mata zuciyarta a ɗazu zaku iya samun matsala, da ita, Anwarr ka tuna matar data xo maka lokacin dakaje zagaye cikin makarantar kwana? toba wata bace ba seni tun alokacin na baka labarin dukkan abinda yake faruwa a cure nace maka zaka zama warwara zaka xama sila na fitar da rayuka biyu daga cikin matsala ka kuma zama sila na shigar da rayuka biyu cikin damuwa, to rayuka biyun daka warware daga matsala sune waɗannan! ta nuna anty da nafisa, rayuwakan daka jefa cikin damuwa sune! ta nuna miema da nafisa, Cikin ƙudurar ALLAH kuma komai yazo ya wuce meze saka kuma yanzu kazo kana jefa mana jaririya cikin matsala? zamu ɗauketa zamu rabaka da ita kawai!!!! wannan maganar ita ta kuma karade ɗakin, awannan karon karo sautin karatunsa yay sam bai saurari maganganun nasu ba, yacigaba da karatunsa mama tana ɗorawa cikin kuka suka soma bashi haquri "Wallahi zamu fita zamu fita kada ka kona mu" "Sena kona ku domin niba jahili bane ALLAH shi kaɗaine masanin gaibu duk abinda kuka faɗa ban yarda dashiba domin komai ze faru a ƙaddararmu yake babu wanda ya isa ya sauya mana ita".sosai ya cigaba da karatun har sanda suka soma rantse rantse akan bazasu kuma haurowa ba har zuwa sanda sukai atishawa suka fita, zaitun anty ta miqo aka goga mata ajikinta mama ta rungumeta tana share mata gumin dayake keto mata.
Baccine ya kwasheta da ƙyar aka tada ita aka ɗauro mata alwala sannan taxo ta kwanta.
Tagumi yayi ya tsira mata idanu yana kallonta.
"Katashi kaje ka kwanta dare yayi".cewar anty wadda take tsaye duk tausayin ƴar tata ya kamata ashe ɗazu harda sharrin iska a lamarin nata wato damuwar data mata yawane yasaka take shiga wannan hali, Shima anasa bangaren wata kunyar anty da tausayin nafisan ne ya kamasa, duk akan soyayyarsa ƴarta take cikin wannan hali? ashe damuwa tana kusantowa mutum shaiɗanu su dinga kusantar sa, yana ji aransa kodan anty ze tilas tama ransa ya dinga kula da ita sabida gudun faɗawarta halaka duk da dai yana da yakinin seya miqe tsaye da izinin ALLAH ze kawo mata karshen lalurar tata.
Shafa kansa yay tare da furta "Kije anty ki kwanta zan kula da ita kafin anjima".murmushi tayi, tare da kallon su mama waɗanda suke kokarin fita daga ɗakin.
Itama juyawa tai tabar ɗakin tabi ayarinsu.
Ya jima yana kuma tofa mata addu,a yana jin zuciyarsa ta karye akanta musamman idan ya kalli miemah seyaji komai ya kunce masa.
Kamar gawa haka ta koma koda miemah ta farka tana kuka duk iya dubararsa ya kasa bata mama tasha haka ya ɗorata a kafada yana bata ruwan fida harta koma.
Da kallo yabi agogo karfe ɗayan dare harda kwata alokacin kuma ta tashi tare da hade kai da gwiwa tana kuka! maganar duniya yay mata tai masa banza haka ya ɗora mata miema a cinyarta yabar ɗakin alokacin innatu ta shigo.
Harya koma part ɗinsa yana juya lamarin aransa, hidaya tai barci shima alwala yay tare da kwanciya washe gari daya koma wajanta ko kallonsa batai ba gaisuwa ma da ƙyar tai masa daya matsa mata da tambayar meke damunta yanzu seta saka masa kuka! karfe goman safiyar laraba ya nufi can kauye wajan malamin sa sosai ya karbo mata taimako ya kawowa anty babu laifi yanzu tana ɗan sakin jiki dasu anty amman shikam daga gaisuwa bata kuma tanka masa, kuma Alhamdulillahi yanzu jikinta ya warke sedai kuma aranta ta kudira ta daina shiga sabgarsa indai hakan zesaka taja mutuncinta, kwanci tashi babu wuya gashi yau har sunyi kwana arba'in koda tace masa zataje gidansu wajansu kaka bai mata musuba sabida shima yana ta shirye shiryensa domin zaɓe yazo.
Taje gidansu kaka tai kwana uku wanda kusan kullum seyay waya akan ta dawo sannan taje gidan umma sahura tai kwana biyu, tabi innatu can wajan dangin anty tai kwana biyu.
Dawowarta da kwana biyu anty ta soma mata shirye shirye na gyaran jiki akan zata koma can part ɗin su hidaya, duk jikinta babu wani sukuni take karbar gyaran anty.
Hidaya ma tana zuwa dan tare ma sukaje gidansu hidayan da ita, kuma tare sukaje danginsa, shikuwa sosai tadau zafi dashi sarai yanzu ta warke bata da wata matsala dan gane da mutananta sedai ita da kanta taɗau alwashin karɓarwa kanta ƴanci ta hanyar fita daga duk wata sabgarsa, koya shigo bata kulashi,  daga gaisuwa take bar masa yarsa ta fice falo wannan abun yana mugun kona masa rai amman sabida shima ɗin miskiline yasa yake basar da ita.
Yau tunda suka tashi ake gyarata mai gyaran jiki musamman anty ta ɗauko wadda ta zage tana gyara ko ina na jikinta sabida anjima kaɗan za,ai walima tare da saukar alqur'ani zuwa dare kuma zata koma can part ɗinsa.......!
*83*



Ba,a gama gyara mata jikin ba se wajan ƙarfe biyu da rabin rana, ruwa mai ɗumi mai gyaran jikin ta haɗa mata a bathroom tare da zuba mata wasu irin turarurrukan wanka masu uban ƙamshi, bata umarni tayi akan ta shiga tai wanka, ita kanta taji daɗin yadda jikinta yake wani irin sheƙi tare da fitar da wani irin sihirtaccen ƙamshi mai ma'ana, ta jima tana gasa jikinta,acikin bathroom ɗin kafin ta gama ta ɗauro babban tawul ajikinta ta kuma yafo wani ƙarami asaman kitson kanta wanda anty ta zauna jiya tai mata, mai uban yawa ƙanana mai kyau, alwala ta dauro sabida kiran ƙarfe uku da rabin rana da akai, tana fitowa ta zira doguwar riga ajikinta seda ta fara rama sallar da ake binta sannan ta ɗora ta la'asar, bayan ta idar ta miqe tare da tube rigar jikinta ta ɗauro zani, wani kaskon wuta ke gefe ancika shi da turare da wasu irin kayan gyara waɗanda suka saje da turaren cikin kaskon, tsugunnawa tayi akai matar ta yafa mata wani katon zani ta rufeta ruf takai kusan minti goma a haka, ko ina na jikinta yana ɗaukar wani irin kamshi kuma tana jin yadda ƙasanta, ke wani irin yamm yana fidda wani irin gumi wanda daga ji kasan an kuma kunno mata wani ruwan ni'imar, bayan anɗauke kaskon zama tai a gefen gado ta soma shiri cikin doguwar rigar shadda wadda aka mata paint work, tana ƙoƙarin ɗaura ɗankwali, mai gyaran jikin ta kuma shigowa cikin  sauri hannunta rike da wani kwano na samira na ainihin da! miko mata tayi "Aida baki soma shiri ba sabida bamu gama ba ai".tura bakinta tayi gaba aranta tana itafa tagaji da wannan ɗurar ga mararta se ciwo take mata komai na jikinta ya sauya, amman dayake bata da musu karba tai tare da godiya, ɗora zani tai akan rigar ta bude samirar, dafaffun ƴan shilane waɗanda aka masu haɗi na musamman, babu wani ɗan ɗano haka ta cinye da ƙyar, ta shanye romon, amman matar bata barta ta taune ƙashinba, tana gama ci aka miko mata kofi wanda aka cikashi da haɗin madara da zuma hardasu kankana da dabino kayayyaki dai kala kala, na mata haka ta shanye shi tass ta miko mata kofi,"Yauwa koke fa ai wallahi idan oga ya shiga hannunki da wuya yasan asuba tayi" ita dai bata fahimci komai ba haka tai murmushi ta miqe ta soma shirinta, daman tun ɗazu miema tana wajansu hidaya suna mata shiri, shiyasa ta samu ta shirya a nutse faɗin yadda tai kyau ma bata bakine,ta fito falo sosai sauran jama,ar da'aka gayyata waɗanda yawanci ƴan uwane da abokan arziki suke yabata harda masu mata hoto. Anty Aisha nurse ce ta mata jagora part ɗin maamah anan ta kuma gaisawa da sauran danginsa ashe dai su taro suke sosai, kai tsaye sama suka nufa ɗakin maamah bayan tagama gaisawa da ƴan falon, wasu saitin akwatuna guda biyu ta gani a jere masu kyau a ɗakin maamah bayan sun gaisa da maamah da kanwar ta wadda Anty aisha nurse kebi anan maamah ta umarci anty aisha data bude mata kayanta tagani, wai ɗazu ya aiko oska ya kawo mata ita ɗaya hidaya ɗaya, miemah ɗaya to ita hidayar an bata nata wannan nasune ita da miemah, wanda atake anan tunaninta ya bata cewar a madadin lefen da bai mata bane ba yayo masu wannan uwar siyayyar domin faɗin irin dukiyar da aka, lafta cikin waɗannan akwatunan bata bakine, seda anty aisha tagama nuna mata kayan, sannan ta dubi mama tana godiya, sannan ta miqe ta fita, waje koda ta koma can part ɗin anty su kaka da sauran matan kannen babanta da yayyensa duk sun zo haka zalika su umma sahura, ma duk sunzo, zama tai suka gaisa kowannen su yana binta da kallo.
Lokacin da mai gabatar da wa,azin tazo a lokacinne hajiya mahaifiyar walida tare da yayyenta da walidar suka shigo suma, wani tsalle tai ta ɗane walida harda kukanta na murna, sosai take farin ciki tana ce mata walida wai munyi candy ban dani se hawaye sharr, kowa dariya yake mata, cikin haka sega masu aikin maamah nan ɗauke da akwatunan da aka nuna mata acan part ɗin anan fa kallo ya koma kansu ita dai jan walida tai suka bar wajan.
Kowa ya dubi kayan cikin akwatunan seya san harkar ta manya ce, sosai matan baffanninta suka tsorata da dukiyar da suka gani nan danan jikinsu yayi sanyi, bayan angama kallon lefe anan masu aikin anty suka soma shigo da kayan ciye ciye da shaye shaye waɗanda seda su kaka suka tsorata, anan fa suka soma Fakar ido suna zubawa a jaka.
Wajan da aka ƙawata ya cika da taron jama,a harda ƴan waje sosai mai wa,azin ta zauna tana bayyana komai daki daki bayan ta kammala akai addu,a tare da soma gabatar da abubuwan da aka tana da domin rarrabawa jama,ar wajan, bayan komi ya kammala, aka soma hotuna waɗanda duk abin da ake miema tana hannun hidaya cikin dangi suna faman hotuna.
Se magariba aka kammala daga nan kaitsaye part ɗin maamah aka nufa da ita, sosai maamah tai mata nasiha mai ratsa jiki sannan dangi suka, wuce da ita, can part ɗin nasa.
Wanda akwai tazara a tsakaninsu dana su mamah sosai.
Sama aka kaita corridow ne ya rabasu ita da hidaya se babban falo wanda nan ne general falo kowa ze iya zama, ɗakuna ne jere acikin ƙaton falon nata guda uku, se kicin tare da store, sosai aka narka dukiya acikin dakunan nata waɗanda ko tsinkensu babu duk maamah ce tace su barshi shi ze mata kowa yana yaba wannan tsari na ɗakunan nata musamman dangin mahaifinta waɗanda suka gane cewa ashe bayan wuya se daɗi a rayuwa.
Nasiha mai ratsa jiki umma sahura tayi mata sannan suka watse suka barta ita da walida sabida miema tana wajan hidiya.
Hirar yaushe gamoce ta ɓarke a junansu seda walida ta gama bata labarin yadda sukai dashi a school sannan itama ta gaya mata komai daya faru wanda walida har kukan daɗi tayi.
Tashi sukai sukayi sallah sannan suka zauna a falo, mintuna kaɗan hidaya ta miqo mata miema itama ta zauna suna hirarsu.
Ganin lokaci yaja yasa walida yimata sallama ta wuce akan gobe ko jibi zata dawo.
Har akai isha bai shigoba.......
*84*


Har akai sallar isha'i bai shigoba jefi-jefi  suke ɗan taɓa hira da hidaya wadda rabi akan miemah ne wai yarinyar tana da haƙuri, amman kuma bini-bini tana lura da yadda hidayan ke ɗaukar wayarta tana danna wata number wadda aka rubuta MY CWT MAN wadda take zaton tasa ce amman yadda hidayan bata nuna mata shitake kira ba yasa taja bakinta tai gum dan ita ALLAH ALLAH ma take tabar mata part ɗinta tai sabgar gabanta.
Har wajan 9pm bai shigoba lokacin taɗanja pillow ta kwanta tare da rintse ido (hoho ke hidaya baki san feena bamai son shiga jama,a bace😂) tun hidaya tana hirar harta gaji ganin feena na bata amsa sama sama, kanta a likafa taja ƴam matan ƙafafunta tabar falon,Bayan tayi mata sallama, hidayan  tana fita tajawo miema saman ruwan cikinta ta kwantar tana ayyana rashin mutuncin dazata shuka masa tabbas! yana sane yay tafiyarsa sabida baya ɗaukin ganinta a part ɗinsa, ai amarya kota buzuzu ce, Ana ɗaukin ta bare kuma ita da martabar ta.
Cikin zafin rai ta ɗago miema tare da nufar bedroom ɗin da aka fara ajeta acikinsa kwantar da ita tayi ita kuma ta faɗa toilet ta ɗauro alwala, tana fitowa ta shinfiɗa darduma ta fara gabatar da sallar isha'i, tana shafawa taji anturo ƙofa bata ɗago ba sabida jikinta ya bata shine, seda ta kammala komai na dangane da ibadarta, sannan ta nade sallayar, ta aje a gefe guda.
Hijabin jikinta ta rage wata mahaukaciyar yunwa takeji, dan haka tana aje hijabin ta juyo domin fita kicin ta sami abin da zataci.
Karaf idanunsu suka sarke dana juna, zaune yake akan sopar ɗakin yasha farar shadda mai ɗinkin tazarce se bakar hula wadda tai marching da zaran aikin gaban shaddar, babu abinda fuskarsa take se ɗaukar haske gami da annuri, kana ganinsa kasan babu  wata damuwa a game dashi 0 tension, kallo guda tai masa ta zare idanunta anasa, ciki ciki tai masa sannu da zuwa tamkar ammata dole shima kuma anasa ɓangaren da ƙyar ya amsa mata, sabida shifa ya soma kosawa da wannan miskilancin data ke masa a kwanan nan yarasa gane inda ta dosa. kafin ya ɗago harta bude kofar ta fice.
Iska ya kaiwa naushi tare da dafe kansa yanajin wata iriyar damuwa akan abin da ta masa.
Ita kuwa kicin ta nufa na cikin falonta ganin babu komai na kayan abinci yasa, ta fita babban falon gidan kanta tsaye kicin ɗin dake cikin falon ta shiga wanda komai yake a dire,kofi ta dauka ta wanke a sink kana ta zubo ruwan tea tare da sugar, biscut ta ɗauko sabida bata shan madara ta fito, waje zata shiga falon ta taga hidaya na kokarin fitowa a wannan karon fuskarta babu walwala ta juya ciki batare data kula hidayanba domin tana hango kamar hidaya irin mutanan nan ne masu bin diddigi tare da son shiga kamar dutsen niƙa.
A falo ta zauna tana sassaita shayin cikin yunwa ta soma sha abinka da mai jego,
Shikuwa wani bacin rai yaji mai tarin yawa acikin ransa da kimarsa da komai wannan yarinyar take wulaƙantashi, wallahi babu macen data isa yabita ɗakinta amman ita sabida mama tai masa nasiha yasa ya biyota amman xata masa banza.
Cike da bacin rai mai yawa ya kwashe wayoyinshi, ya nufi waje bayan ya mannawa miema kiss yana jin ko hidaya bata taɓa masa wannan cin kashin ba.
Ko kallonta baiba haka ya buga mata kofar ya fice.
Tsaki taja aranta tana ayyana kafi ruwa gudu sannan tacigaba da sabgar gabanta.
Zaman kallo ta zauna har wajan 12 dan haka anan ta soma bacci secan kuma ta miqe kamar an tsikareta tai cikin ɗakinta, wani tsorone ya kamata jin shurun tai yawa ga miema tana sharar barci, dan  haka ta juya tai falo tattare kayan datai amfani dasu tayi kana ta fita waje domin baxata iya, zama da dattin kayan data sha tea dasuba.
Tana murɗa handle ɗin kofar ɗakin ta tahango abinda ya dakar mata zuciya, hidayace zaune bisa cinyarsa da alama ma kamar basa cikin wannan duniyar tamu, da karfi taji zuciyarta tana rawa nan da nan hawaye masu ɗumi suka soma gudana akan fuskarta kofin hannunta, ne ya faɗi wanda karar! sa ta dawo dasu duniyarmu cike da sanyi jiki ta koma ɗakinta direct kan gado ta faɗa tana gunjin kukan kishi.
Ɗan zame hidaya yayi yana mayar da ajiyar zuciya, cike da kirsa da barikin sanin kan miji hidaya ta dubesa "Haba my man kaima dai kasan baka mata adalciba, nidai kaje na baku sati guda" tafaɗa cikin jin daɗi saboda a yanzu ta tabbata cewar ita kadai ce mai guri a zuciyarsa ganin yadda ya haukace mata.
Shikuwa cikin jarumta da ƙwarewa wajan nuna shiɗin namijine ya dubeta yana sakin murmushi domin baya san tagane akwai wani abun a tsakaninsa da yarinyar "Au bakki san in miki na ƙarshe kenan?" yafaɗa yana tsareta da jajjayen idanunsa ɗan hade rai tai kaɗan "Ban gane na karsheba sekace mutuwa,"   "Eh mana na karshe bagashi kin bamu sati guda ba" yafaɗa yana gyara rigarsa tare da miqewa daga wajan dan kar ta gane barakarsu yasa ya nufi cikin ɗakin nafisa yana danne dariyar sa.
Da sassarfa ya tura kofar bedroom ɗinta jin yadda take faman sakin kuka, farar rigar jikinsa ya rage tare da hayewa kan gadon baki ɗaya.
Birkito da ita yay zuwa saman cinyarsa yasa hannunsa yana ɗage mata gashin kanta wanda jelar kitson ta kare.
Cikin fusata ta ɗago da rinannun idanunta "Dalla malam ni sakeni wallahi wajan anty zan tafi" tafaɗa tana sakin kuka! sosai take gaya masa maganganu waɗanda babu komai cikinsu se tsabar kishin tsiya, shi dariya ma ta soma bashi, ganin miema zata farka yasa ya hade bakinsu waje guda.
Ɗan zamowa yay kaɗan tare da tsirata da idanunsa jin ya kashe bakin tsiwar.
"Bara nai maki abinda naiwa matata tunda shi kikewa kuka" wannan kalmar ta matarsa bakaramin bata mata rai take ba, ganin tana son ta zame yasa ya ɗora dukkan hannayensa a kafaɗunta ya haɗeta da kirjinsa ya rungumeta tsam.
Birkitaccen turarenta shiya soma kwance masa notikan dake kansa, babu abinda yake se fesa mata hucin sassanyar iskar cikin bakinsa mai kamshin mint.
Tsam ya miƙar da ita tsaye tare da furta mata taje ta dauro alwala, sam bata kawo komai aranta ba ta koma toilet ta dauro alwala shima danata yay using atare suka kammala shiri ya shinfiɗa masu sallaya,sallah raka'a biyu sukai ya jima yana masu addu,a kafin ya miqe da kansa ya dauke miema a gadon ya kaita kan ɗan karamin gadonsu na yara can nesa dasu.
Zame rigar jikinsa yay lafiyayyen gashin dake kwance bisa kirjinsa ya bayyana, babu abinda fatar jikinsa keyi se fitar da kamshi da haske.wajanta ya nufa kai tsaye cikin zalama, tana zaune gefen gado duk idanunta sun firfita waje dan tsabar tsoro.
Kamota yayi da kansa ya zame hijabin jikinta, rigar jikinta ya zame  tai saura daga ita se skirt ɗan karami, jawota yay ta faɗa samansa ya saka hannunsa ya rungumeta tsam tsam, tanajin sautin saukar bugun zuciyarsa wadda take gudu da sauri da sauri.
Hannunsa ya ɗora a tsakiyar ramin gadon bayanta yana shafawa kamar mai tsoron kar wani abun ya sameta, ita kuwa hannunta duka biyun ta ɗora a saman kirjinta sabida daga ita se bra, sannan kuma tana kare idanunta daga ganinsa a yadda yake dagashi se guntun wando na maza.
Ɗorata yay bisa gefensa yay mata filet yana binta da kallo ta cikin ɗan hasken dake cikin ɗakin wanda yake fitowa kaɗan kaɗan.
Tureshi tai tana goge hawayenta.
"Ni dan ALLAH ka rabu dani kazo kana ta min abubuwa kamar wani........."Shiiiii Ya faɗa a dai dai saman bakinta tamkar wani tsohon maye kafin ya cafko bakinta yana faman tsotsa tamkar ya samu swt, hannunta ya ɗauke daga kirjinta ya mayar danasa akai yana yi masu wata iriyar sahihiyar shafa, babu abinda take se sauke ajiyar zuciya shima anasa bangaren babu abinda yake se sauke zazzafar ajiyar zuciya kamar wani maye.
Bakinsa ya ɗora asaman dokin wuyanta yana goga mata sajen dake kwance a saman fuskarsa yana ɗan jan fatar wajan kaɗan, alokacin kukanta ya tsananta shima kuma alokacin yake jin ya kasa controlling kansa, sosai yake sucking dokin wuyanta kamar ya samu cwt harya gangaro da bakinsa saman kirjinta yana ɗan ja kaɗan kafin ya mayar da bakinsa baki ɗaya awajan yana musu wata iriyar zuƙa, kuka take tare da kokarin turesa sabida ta kasa gasgata abinda yake yi mata, cikin sauri taja baya tana kuka! "Kabarni na huta na gajiiiiiii" ta furta tana kuka! shima a wannan karon hawaye yake amman hawayen daɗi cike da ƙwarewa ya bita ya jawo jikinsa "Nooooo bazan barki bahhhhh" yafaɗa tamkar wanda yaci yaji!
Mazauni yay mata a saman kirjinsa yana wasa da duk inda yaci karo ajikinta da hannunsa mai uban laushi baki ɗaya ya rikiɗe mata tamkar ba wanda ta saniba jin hannunsa a ƙasan mararta yasa tafara sauke ajiyar zuciya, ganin yadda ya gigice ya zauce wasan nasu ya sauya,yasa naja ƴammatan ƙafafuna nabar ɗakin

Nadai jiyo ihu da sumbatunsa kaɗan kafin na karasa ɗakin madam hidaya.

Sosai take kukan kishi da ƙyar bacci yay gaba da ita wanda rabi na kaɗaicin mijinta ne.

*85*


Jinta take cikin wani irin bakin ciki marar kwatance tana jin kewar mijin nata tare da jin kunci ya kuma ziyartar ranta.
Ganin  abun ze bata matsala ya kuma haifar  mata da damuwa yasa ta miqe tare da nufar bathroom alwala ta dauro gami da shinfida sallaya ta soma kaiwa ALLAH kukanta, ta jima tana addu,a tare da korar shaidan da kuma neman tsari dashi sannan ta rage kayan jikinta ta kwanta nan da nan nutsuwa ta sameta tare d bacci mai daɗi.

Yajima yana gudanar da sha'aninsa akanta bai sarara mata ba se can cikin dare, jawota yayi cikin wani irin yanayi mai wuyar fasaltuwa rungumeta yayi tsam a cikin faffaɗan kirjinsa yana shafa mata gadon bayanta har zuwa ƙwanƙwasonta, sosai take kuka ƙasa ƙasa tana jan ajiyar zuciya, sabida yadda yaje mata ɗin be mata zuwan wasa ba sosai ya murji gajiyarsa ajikinta ya zubar mata da dukkan wani sinadarin sa acikin jikinta.
Ɗago fuskarta yayi yana share mata hawayen dake gudu a saman fuskarta da harshensa, sannan ya tarairayeta zuwa bathroom jinginar da ita yayi asaman top ɗin ciki, da kansa ya tarar musu ruwa mai ɗumi acikin bathtube zuwa yay gabanta tare da zare mata ƴar guntuwar rigar dake jikinta, bata da ƙarfin kare kirjinta haka ta barshi ya rungumeta ya haɗa ta da fatar kirjinsa, ya matse har ya kaisu cikin tube ɗin, zaunar da ita yay akan cinyarsa shikuma ya zauna acikin tube ɗin yana matsa mata jikinta da tattausan hannunsa, soso ya ɗauka ya zuba shower gel yana murza mata jikinta, seda ruwan yay sanyi sannan ya kuma tarar masu wani mai ɗumin yana zuba mata shi har cikin down ɗinta, sabida wajan yaɗan samu sukuni, barinta yayi tayi wankan tsarki bayan ya gama nasa, sannan ya naɗota acikin bathrube suka fito waje, da sauran lokaci kafin ashiga masallaci, zaunar da ita yayi a saman sopa da kansa ya ɗauko mata riga mai saukin nauyi ya taimaka mata ta saka, wanda a garin ya saka matan ma seda ya mammatse mata breast ɗinta, kwanatar da ita yayi tamkar yana tsoron wani abun ya sameta, tare da manna mata kiss a saman goshinta, fita yayi jikinsa daure da tawul, minti biyu ya dawo jikinsa sanye da jallabiya, tsugunnawa yayi agabanta tare da rikota zuwa jikinsa, yana fesa mata numfashinsa mai kamshi, taimaka mata yayi suka koma toilet ya haɗo mata ruwa tai alwala sannan suka fito
Lokacin har ansoma tayar da sallah cikin sauri, ya shinfiɗa mata sallaya tare da daukar mata hijabi ya bata.
"Pls bara naje kada nai missing jam'i ki kulamin da kanki".
Cikin sauri yabar ɗakin, yana ɗan haɗawa da gudu-gudu, cikin ɗunbin mamaki ta tayar da sallar harta idar tana jimanta abinda yamata a ranta baki ɗaya ya zauce a jiyan ya koma tamkar zautacce sumbatun daya dinga mata shiyafi bata mamaki, harda dan ALLAH kada ta guje shi, tun ranar daya soma ganinta arayuwarsa, ranar data soma zuwa gabansa acikin asibitinsa da kuma lokacin dayaje,, school ɗinsu lokacin dayaje zancen haurawar gardi da fyaɗen da akama yaran makarantar yaji ya kamu da sonta, har zuwa sanda ALLAH ya haɗasu, a wajan taro data kai masa taimako wajan kareshi daga harbi, har zuwa lokacin da komai ya faru dasu a gidansa, kuma yace mata tindaga lokacin bai kuma samun sukuni ba har lokacin daya ganta da cikinsa, har umara yaje akan ALLAH ya bayyana masa ita, Wannan dalilinne yasa bayajin koda itan ba matar sabace ba ze iya rabuwa da cikinta ko itan, daga karshe ya fara rokarta akan dan girman  ALLAH kada tai amfani da soyayyar daya ke mata ta wulakantashi domin yanajin wata iriyar muguwar soyayyarta wadda yakejinta har cikin kowani gaba na jikinsa, "I love you nafisahhhhhhhhh" haryanzu wannan kalmar taƙi fita daga cikin dodon kunnenta domin wannan kalmar ita kaɗai yake ambata mata a lokacin dayake ratsa jikinta, har zuwa lokacin daya samu nutsuwa ya karasa da "ALLAH yayi miki albarka ina sonki farin cikina"!
Firgigit! ta dawo daga duniyar tunanin lokacin dataji tattausan hannunsa asaman kafadarta yana matsa mata, cikin tsoro ta juyo shikuwa dariya ya saki kamar ba shiba tare da ɗagata cak, ya zare mata hijabin jikinta tare da nufar gado da ita, rage hasken ɗakin yayi tare da rabata da rigar jikinta, hannunsa ya ɗora akan breast ɗinta masu uban laushi tare da cikar jego yana matsasu kaɗan, cikin kunnenta ya lalubo tare da furta "Wallahi nafiki shiga damuwa akan sonki kawai na danne miki ne, amman wallahil azim tun ranar dana ganki acikin hospital ɗina ban kara samun sukuniba, idan baki manta ba har tsawa na daka maki"..yafaɗa yana zira harshensa yana lasar cikin bakinta, wani irin dadi take ji har cikin ranta tura baki tai gaba.
"Amman kaita wahalar da zuciyata?" tafaɗa ruwa na zuba daga idanunta.
"Plsssss ki yafeni wallahi tawa zuciyar tafi taki shiga damuwa akan ƙaunarki" Yakai maganar yana ɗora harshensa akan hawayenta tas! ya lashesu tare da tsotse saman idanun, "Ammann............,Shiiiii" yaƙi bata damar kuma wata maganar sabida yadda ya hade bakinsu waje ɗaya.
Sumbatar bakinta yake cikin wani irin yanayi, hannunsa yakai ƙasanta tare da shafo wajan, jinsa acikin wani irin ni'ima yasashi kuma zaucewa yana kokarin kuma faɗawa cikin rijiyar tata mai uban ni'ima, kuka take sakar masa ƙasa ƙasa wanda yakejin kamar ta kara kunna masa wata wutar buqatuwar tane, kwantar da ita yayi a saman gadon tare da binta yana wasa da duk wani waje dayasan ze ƙara masa wuta, kafin wani lokaci ya kuma komawa ruwa, bai sarara mata ba se wajan wani lokaci nan ma kukan ƴarsune ya farkar dasu.
Wanka sukai tare da fitowa zama tai bayan ta shirya tana bawa mima abincita, shikuwa fita yay mintuna goma ya shiryo cikin ɗanyen boyel yana zuba uban kamshi ango zam idan ka dubesa, zama yay kusa da ita yana yiwa mima wasa, ya jima a dakin tare da ƴarsa sannan ya mata sallama ya fita sabida ana jiransa, ɗakin uwar gida ya shiga, zaune take taci uban ado na birgewa ganin sa yasa ta miqe tare da rungumesa tana masa barka da safiya, ya jima a ɗakin ganin bata da wata damuwa yasa yabar dakin yana mata sallama, part ɗin maamah yaje ya gaisar da ita, tsintar kansa yayi da kin karasawa part din anty sabida kunyar dayake ji, 
Wajan mota ya nufa, kai tsaye abinda yake gabansa yaje ya gudanar wanda yaja manyan lokuta, ma'ana gidan kakannin nafisa yaje wajansu baffa gaishesu yayi tare dayi musu alkairi cikin mutuntaka sosai aka wadata tasu da kayan abinci, bayan ya baiwa ƴan kwangila damar gyara gidan, daga nan aka rakashi gidan umma sahura wadda yake jin wata iriyar kaunar matar har ransa, kallon gidan yay yana ayyana anan matarsa da yarsa sukai rayuwa, gida mai kyau yay wa umma sahura albishir dashi tare da kujerar makka itaa da mijinta da innatu da kaka dasu baffa harma da nafisan tunda bata taba zuwa ba, sosai su kaka umma sahura dasu baffa suke kuk! dama wannan daɗin zezo musu  lallai ɗan hakin daka raina.....yau gashi arzikin nafisa yazo musu har gadon kwanansu.
Bai shigo gida ba se yamma, wanda har labarin abinda yay ayau ɗin yazo kunnensu ita dasu anty sabida yadda ƴaƴan baffanninta mata suka zo tun ɗazu suna faman godiya harma da baffa musamman shida yafi takura mata,kukan dadi nafisa take sabida ita duk abinda sukai mata baisa taji haushinsu ba fatan Alkairine kullum a tsakaninsu, suma kukan suke suna kuma neman yafiyarta, ita kam jinta take fes bata fita daga cikin wannan mamakinba sakon waya daga walida yazo mata wai ya aika oska da abun alkairi ya kai mata ita dasu hajiyarsu, ranar nafisa wuni tayi cikin farin ciki har ta matsu ta gansa domin tai masa godiya, koda ya dawo rungumesa kawai tai tana kuka! shikuma daga rarrashi ya buge da kashe arna.
         *******************
Bayan shekara guda.
Abubuwa masu dadi da marasa daɗi sun faru, domin kuwa har su nafisa sunje saudiya sun sauke umara wanda a wannan lokacin aka yaye mima tana wajan maamah ta riqeta, daga cikin marasa daɗin kuwa shine rasuwar kaka wadda ta zauta nafisa dan seda anwar yay da gaske ta saki jikinta.
A yanzu haka idan ka dubeta kasan hutu da jin dadi sun gama aikinsu sabida yadda komai ya sauya ta zama babbar hajiya kowa adangi sonta yake ji suke kamar zasu lasheta.
School ce kawai yaqi yarda ta koma amman komai wanda akewa mace dan taji dadi tana samunsu.
Zamansu da hidaya kada ran kada hakan basa fada basa gaba amman ba kamar daba sabida yadda yaƙi yay control na sonta agaban kowa.
Shikuwa wani irin nishadi farin ciki tare da kwanciyar hankali yakejinsa aciki tamkar bashiba, wajan jama,a se son barka, yanzun haka an takura masa akan ya fito takarar shugan kasa babu yadda ya iya haka ya karba har an soma kamfain shiyasa baya samun zama.
Anyi bikin walida harma da shahida da ɗayan ƙanin nasa mai bin imiran haka zalika sakina matar imran ta kuma haihuwa.
 Yau tinda ta, miqe take jin bata jin daɗi shima yau bai fita ba yana gida zaune a falo ya saka tab a gabansa yana tura wani sako.
Fitowa tai daga ita se wani, mini skirt tare da ƴar best ture tab ɗin gabansa yay tare da yi mata alama da hannunsa akan, tazo gareshi, cikin yauki ta karaso tare da zama akan cinyarsa, jin jikinta zafi yasa ya rungumeta yana hade ta da jikinsa, ganin kamar bata jin dadi dauketa zuwa master room da kansa yay chakking ɗinta binciken farko ya nuna tana da ciki harna tsahon wata biyu, kasan cewarsa babban likita.
Wani irin sumba yake kai mata yana kuma godiya ga ALLAH, sosai yake farin ciki harda daukar waya ya soma kiran dangi yana shaida masu babu ko kunya.
Wajan hidaya ya nufa yana murna tare da sanar mata.
Bakaramin farin ciki tai ba rungumeshi tayi tana tsalle wani irin jirine ya dauketa babu shiri ta zube agabansa tana mayar da numfashi.
Cikin kidima ya dauketa da kansa ya kaita daki yana kwantar da ita akan gado.
Ganin kamar bata jin dadi yasa ya dauko abin bada tai mako na gaggawa binciken farko ya nuna hidaya tana dauke da ciki harna tsahon wata uku!
Wani irin kuka ne yazo masa tare da godiya ga sarki ALLAH watakan rabon nafisa shine agaba kafin nata, ita kanta hidaya tai kuka tare da farin ciki marar misaltuwa.
Kafin wani lokaci labarin wannan abin dadi ya cika danginsu shekaru bakwai da aure se yau ALLAH ya bata,  rabonta ita kanta nafisa anan ɗakin hidaya ta tare tana ta nan da ita sabida ita nata baya bata wuya kamar hidaya.
Mama kam murna harta ki boyuwa fatan alkairi taketa ma ɗan nata, haka zalika umma mahaifiyar hidaya itama har kukan dadi tai.
Kwanci tashi babu wuya har cikinsu ya tsufa lokacin kuma zabe ya zo dan haka ko zama baya samu, cikin wannan rintsin hidaya ta haihu bayan tasha doguwar nakuda ta haifo ɗiyarta mace mai kama da ita sak.
Sosai aka murna tare da farin ciki kuma duk abinda akewa mace yayi kokarin yima hidaya na bajinta.
Anyi komai lafiya an gama lokacin kuma itama nafisa cikinta ya tsufa ko tafiya bata fiya yiba sabida girman cikin, ranar da akai zabe yana gida a zaune suna hira da miema wadda take gwaranci sabida yanxu ankaita school.
Nafisa tazo ta zauna da Amira ƴar gidan hidaya mai sunan anty wato hafsat, karbar amira yay yana mata fada akan ta daina yawan jijjiga kanta sabida matsala.
Hirarsu suke akan zabe har yana bata labarin gobarar da kamfanin sardauna yayi akan ha'incin dayake sannan kuma ashe wannan harin da aka taba kawo masa harda hadin bakin sardaunan da kuma wasu daga cikin ƴan adawarsa,shi abin da yace mata koda aka kirasa kotu cewa yay ya haqura, tunda shi wannan ƙaddarar ta haifar masa da farar kaddara tunda a dalilin sune ya kuma gamuwa da nafisa har ya mata ciki daga karshe kuma ashe matarsa ce dan haka shikam yabarsu da halinsu.
Dariya take ita kam tana mamakin yadda yake zaro zance ahaka hidaya ta shigo ta samesu itama  aka cigaba da hirar da ita.
Ranar da aka sanar da zabe tun safe ake sanar da cewar mai girma mas! yaci zaben shugaban kasa ko ina ka duba agari babu  abinda ake se murna da farin ciki, zuwa wannan lokacin kuma zancen yagama karade ko ina a garin, wanda yay dai dai da lokacin da nafisa take dakin nakuda mai girma mas! da kansa yake bata taimako acikin asibitin nasa, duk murnar cin zaben da ake yayi bai bashi dadi ba fatansa kawai yaga ta rabu da cikin jikinta lafiya.
Lokacin da kan ɗan ya taho tana kuka! yana kuka! cikin tsananin azaba ta zan kato ƴara maza kyawawa har guda uku! cif.
Idan ka dubeshi a wannan ranar seka rantse zeyi hauka dan murna, bayan wani lokaci aka gyara yaran da mahaifiyarsu aka miqasu dakin hutu.
Fadin farin cikin da familyn nan sukai bata bakine.
Abu goma da dari kenan haka akaita shagali wanda ya hade biyu.
Ranar suna yara sunci sunan baban nafisa baban hidaya baban anwarr, masha ALLAH mai jego ta fito shar da ita.
BAYAN WANI LOKACI.
sun tashi daga wannan gida sun koma can gidan shugaban kasa rayuwa mai kyau suke gudanarwa yanzu nafisa tana da yara, biyar, biyu mata uku maza, hidaya tana da ɗaya, rayuwarsu suke mai kyau sam basa nuna wani kishi na jahilci sedai kowacce tana kokarin ganin ta kara wutar kaunarta  azuciyar mijinta.
Shima anasa bangaren yana godiya ga ALLAH daya bashi mataye nagari duk da akasan ransa feenah ce gaba, amman a fili yana nuna adalcinsa ga hidaya wadda ta kasance matarsa t farko uwar ƴarsa kanwarsa ƴar uwarsa.
Sosai yake jida iyalin nasa basu nemi komai sun rasa ba.
Haka zalika ƴan uwa jama'ar gari suna son barka da mulkin nasa.
